# Kẻ Sống Sót: Bóng Ma
## Kịch bản bản 3.1 — cuộc trao đổi bị phản bội

**60 đơn vị sản xuất × 6 clip × 10 giây = 60 phút hình nguồn.** Mỗi clip một cú máy, nhịp diễn thay đổi theo hành động; không giữ hình để đủ phút. Thời lượng bản dựng phụ thuộc phần trim và mix âm thanh thực tế.

Nguồn duy nhất: `PHAN_CANH_V3.json`. Hành động/camera viết tiếng Anh cho prompt, ý đồ cảnh và thoại giữ tiếng Việt. Tái sinh bằng `python3 build_full_60_scenes.py`; kiểm tra bằng `python3 build_full_60_scenes.py --check`.

Sơn có bảy ngày trước chuyến tàu chuyển Hạ đi. Anh hợp tác với Mây, giao bằng chứng cho điều tra và chấp nhận trả giá. Kiên muốn Sơn im lặng, Bà Hai muốn lấy lại quyền kiểm soát. Cuộc trao đổi ở cảng bị phản bội; Dũng dùng chất Tê hư cấu giữ Sơn sống, cả hai phe đều nhận biết thuốc. Hạ để lại dấu và mở đường, Mây dẫn việc cứu người. Sơn cứu cả Kiên khỏi ngã rồi tự nhận còng.

### CẢNH 01: Ngày Thứ Nhất
*Thời gian truyện: D1 dawn*
Sơn tới Hòn Sóng và tự đặt hạn chờ.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn đang tìm nơi bắt đầu lại hay chỗ để biến mất?
**Móc vào:** Anh để chuyến xe rời đi rồi mới bước về thị trấn.
**Điểm bẻ:** Một người xuống xe biến thành người tự đếm ngược bảy ngày.
**Gieo / trả:** Lựa chọn chỗ ngồi, đồng hồ đóng và vạch đầu gieo cho 03, 34, 52, 60.

**Clip 1 · OPEN · CROSSROAD · W**
- Đầu: Coach door open, Sơn still inside.
- Hành động: Sơn steps down once with his canvas bag, lets the coach pull away, then commits his weight toward the town only after it clears his sightline.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Tôi phải tìm dấu cô ấy trước khi quá muộn.
- Cuối: Sơn alone at the crossroad, bag in left hand.

- Ý đồ: Make arrival feel like a deliberate loss of escape, not a travel postcard.
- Bố cục: Keep the open coach door left of center and empty coastward space beyond Sơn.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He clears the step once; bag stays in his left hand as the coach leaves.
- Diễn xuất: His eyes follow the coach longer than his body does, then settle toward town.
- Điểm nét: Keep Sơn and the coach edge readable in the established wide depth.
- Chất liệu: Fine dust moves naturally around worn boots; jacket fabric lifts only with sea wind.
- Âm nổi: Engine weight recedes continuously into exposed sea wind; narration remains dry and close.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the departure space to finish before his commitment becomes visible, without a frozen tail.

**Clip 2 · J · STREET · T**
- Đầu: Sơn approaching the coffee stall from screen left.
- Hành động: He catches a crate rolling toward the road and returns it to Bảy.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Cảm ơn chú. Cần tìm ai?
- Cuối: Bảy holds the recovered crate, Sơn beside the stall.

- Ý đồ: Earn Bảy's later kindness through an ordinary helpful act, without making Sơn heroic.
- Bố cục: Track at pedestrian height with the road edge and rolling crate visible together.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn intercepts the crate within his walking line, returns it, then stops beside Bảy.
- Diễn xuất: He treats the recovery as routine; Bảy's surprise softens into curiosity rather than gratitude theatre.
- Điểm nét: Favor the returning crate and both upper bodies; keep the road legible behind.
- Chất liệu: Scuffed crate corners and sun-faded awning establish a working town rather than a polished set.
- Âm nổi: One scrape, restrained footfall and Bảy's breath precede his thanks; no triumphant accent.
- Nhịp diễn: Let catch and return form one continuous motion, then allow the question to land.

**Clip 3 · CUT · STALL · M**
- Đầu: Bảy behind his counter, Sơn facing him.
- Hành động: Sơn asks for a quiet room, Bảy turns toward the dune.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Chú có chỗ nào yên không?
- Cuối: Bảy pointing uphill, Sơn following his eyeline.

- Ý đồ: Show Sơn choosing isolation while Bảy offers practical shelter without demanding a confession.
- Bố cục: Frame the counter as a modest boundary, both faces readable in the medium setup.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn stays on the customer side; Bảy's pointing hand opens the route toward the dune.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn asks quietly without selling a cover story; Bảy answers from habit, not suspicion.
- Điểm nét: Hold both eyelines and the pointing hand at a usable shared depth.
- Chất liệu: Condensation on existing glasses and weathered counter grain give the exchange everyday weight.
- Âm nổi: Keep low stall ambience below the line; let the sea remain present but unromantic.
- Nhịp diễn: Leave a fraction after the question for Bảy to understand, then follow only with eyes.

**Clip 4 · L · DUNE · W**
- Đầu: Both men already halfway up the dune path.
- Hành động: They reach the cabin porch, Bảy passes Sơn the key.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Nhà trống. Gió thì không.
- Cuối: Key resting in Sơn's open right palm.

- Ý đồ: Make receiving the key feel like accepting a temporary place, not finding safety.
- Bố cục: Keep cabin, last stretch of path and the two men in the same wide geography.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They finish the climb before the key passes into Sơn's open right palm.
- Diễn xuất: Bảy is lightly winded and matter-of-fact; Sơn receives without the ease of a vacationer.
- Điểm nét: Maintain readable figures and the handover zone; do not cut to a separate key insert.
- Chất liệu: Pine shadows cross the sand; the small metal key looks used rather than ceremonial.
- Âm nổi: Retain their breathing under the pine wind; Bảy's dry joke should not invite a music cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Spend the first beats on arrival, then let the key settle into his palm once.

**Clip 5 · MATCH · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: Key set beside a small carving tool.
- Hành động: Sơn cuts the first short mark into the bare doorframe.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Bảy ngày. Để tìm ra sự thật.
- Cuối: One fresh mark, tool lowered out of frame.

- Ý đồ: Turn a vague stay into an exact private deadline that the audience cannot yet explain.
- Bố cục: Use the existing detail angle on tool, clean wood and the newly cut single mark.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: His hand makes only the first short tally, then lowers the tool completely from the mark.
- Diễn xuất: Express the decision through a steady wrist and controlled pressure, not a violent flourish.
- Điểm nét: Keep the cut and adjacent untouched grain sharp; the room remains unimportant beyond.
- Chất liệu: Fresh pale wood contrasts with the dark old frame; a few loose fibers catch daylight.
- Âm nổi: One brief wood scrape sits beneath the voice-over; no clock tick is added here.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow recognition of the first mark before the tool leaves, while wind keeps the image alive.

**Clip 6 · CUT · PORCH · M**
- Đầu: Sơn seated on the porch with his closed watch.
- Hành động: He turns the engraved lid toward the light without opening it.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Closed watch in his hand, sea beyond his shoulder.

- Ý đồ: Attach the closed watch to a withheld emotional truth rather than explaining its contents.
- Bố cục: Keep the watch at chest-to-lap height inside the medium frame, sea soft beyond his shoulder.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He rotates the engraved lid into existing light without touching the release clasp.
- Diễn xuất: His face should resist tears; one contained breath carries more weight than a mournful stare.
- Điểm nét: Share useful focus between his fingers and face, never revealing the hidden watch interior.
- Chất liệu: Worn silver catches a narrow natural glint; jacket cuffs and salt air remain tactile.
- Âm nổi: The watch is silent in the present; only porch wood, wind and his breathing remain.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the small rotation finish, then stay on the consequence without a second turn.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Let the porch sea wind fall under rain on the silk-shop awning. Cut to the existing silk curtain, not to another coach arrival.

### CẢNH 02: Quá Khứ: Hợp Đồng Cuối
*Thời gian truyện: PAST one month earlier night*
Bà Hai giao việc cuối và Dũng lộ sự ghen tức.

**Câu hỏi:** Ơn nuôi dưỡng của Bà Hai có cho Sơn quyền rời đi không?
**Móc vào:** Dũng đóng cửa nhẹ hơn mức khán giả chờ ở một lời mời ngồi.
**Điểm bẻ:** Tấm vé xin nghỉ bị phủ lụa, món việc trở thành món nợ.
**Gieo / trả:** Sự ghen ghế của Dũng trả ở 18–24; lụa che vé báo trước quyền sở hữu con người.

**Clip 1 · L · SILK · W**
- Đầu: Silk curtain at left, three figures around a teak desk.
- Hành động: Sơn stops before Bà Hai, Dũng quietly closes the rear door.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Ngồi đi con.
- Cuối: Sơn standing opposite Bà Hai, Dũng by the shut door.

- Ý đồ: Introduce a household invitation whose geometry already limits Sơn's freedom to leave.
- Bố cục: Keep Bà Hai behind the desk and Dũng near the rear door within one wide frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn stops at the desk edge while Dũng closes the door softly behind his path.
- Diễn xuất: Bà Hai's warmth is practiced and genuine enough to unsettle; Dũng avoids a theatrical glare.
- Điểm nét: Keep all three positions legible, with the door not swallowed by excessive blur.
- Chất liệu: Silk absorbs the warm lamp light while the desk retains a hard reflective edge.
- Âm nổi: Door latch is quiet but distinct; her invitation stays conversational, without a sinister sting.
- Nhịp diễn: Hold the small delay between door closure and invitation so the room's control becomes apparent.

**Clip 2 · CUT · SILK · I**
- Đầu: Bà Hai's left hand beside a face-down photograph.
- Hành động: She slides Kiên's portrait toward Sơn, jade bracelet visible.
- Thoại: Bà Hai, ngoài khung hình: Kiên giữ sổ gốc. Má cần lấy lại.
- Cuối: Kiên's portrait beside Sơn's black glove.

- Ý đồ: Let a single sliding photograph define the transaction more clearly than exposition can.
- Bố cục: Place the bracelet, portrait and Sơn's black glove within the same stable detail plane.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Her left hand slides the photograph toward his glove and stops before touching him.
- Diễn xuất: The hand is unhurried and assumes compliance; his glove remains still long enough to register refusal.
- Điểm nét: Keep the photographed face readable as a picture, not an extra living person in the room.
- Chất liệu: Jade and paper move against polished wood; the glove's matte leather interrupts both reflections.
- Âm nổi: A soft paper drag and faint bracelet contact support her off-screen sentence, not compete with it.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the portrait reach its final position before the last word, leaving its offer unanswered.

**Clip 3 · CUT · SILK · M**
- Đầu: Sơn facing Bà Hai across the desk.
- Hành động: He leaves the photograph untouched and sets a return ticket down.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Xong việc này, con nghỉ.
- Cuối: Ticket between the two, Sơn's hands still.

- Ý đồ: Give Sơn's wish to leave a concrete cost by putting his return ticket between them.
- Bố cục: Use the medium composition to include his face and the ticket landing near the desk edge.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He sets the ticket down once and leaves Kiên's portrait untouched where it was offered.
- Diễn xuất: His voice is restrained; the stillness afterward should look rehearsed, because he expects resistance.
- Điểm nét: Keep the ticket shape visible without readable print while his eyes remain the emotional center.
- Chất liệu: The thin ticket lies exposed against heavy wood and dense folded silk nearby.
- Âm nổi: A small paper tap should be heard before the sentence ends; keep room tone steady.
- Nhịp diễn: Deliver the line without rushing into defense, then let Bà Hai own the silence he leaves.

**Clip 4 · CONTINUE · SILK · M**
- Đầu: Ticket between the two, Sơn's hands still.
- Hành động: Bà Hai covers the ticket with a folded strip of silk.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Con đi thì ai giữ nhà cho má?
- Cuối: Her hand rests on the silk covering Sơn's ticket.

- Ý đồ: Show Bà Hai answering departure with possession through an almost affectionate domestic gesture.
- Bố cục: Keep the exact prior medium setup, positions and lens; the desk remains the shared boundary.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Her folded silk covers the ticket once, leaving her left hand resting on top.
- Diễn xuất: She asks as though appealing to family; Sơn's jaw tightens without a visible argument starting.
- Điểm nét: Preserve the previous depth and eyelines instead of inventing a new close-up on her hand.
- Chất liệu: Soft fabric settling over paper makes the ticket disappear through an ordinary, reversible act.
- Âm nổi: Silk rub replaces the paper sound; her gentle line should remain more troubling than a raised voice.
- Nhịp diễn: Use a measured cover and a brief still response; nobody repeats the ticket placement.

**Clip 5 · CUT · SILK · O**
- Đầu: Dũng holding the rear door for Sơn.
- Hành động: Dũng looks at the untouched chair, then blocks the doorway briefly.
- Thoại: Dũng (@Dũng), nói trong hình: Má để ghế cho mày hoài.
- Cuối: Sơn and Dũng separated by the door edge.

- Ý đồ: Make Dũng's jealousy specific to the place Sơn receives in the household.
- Bố cục: Frame over Sơn's near shoulder toward Dũng's three-quarter face and the rear door edge.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng glances at the unused chair, then occupies the doorway just long enough to matter.
- Diễn xuất: His resentment should arrive through an almost casual remark; Sơn does not reward it with a taunt.
- Điểm nét: Keep Dũng's mouth and the blocking door edge clear, with the chair only a secondary cue.
- Chất liệu: The narrow opening compresses bodies against hanging silk and a scuffed door jamb.
- Âm nổi: Door wood shifts under his hand; the spoken complaint carries no added threat effect.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the chair glance precede the line, then release the doorway only after its meaning settles.

**Clip 6 · L · ALLEY · P**
- Đầu: Sơn already outside, silk door closing behind him.
- Hành động: He turns Kiên's photo over and studies a building entrance sketch.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Photo back beside the real tower across the street.

- Ý đồ: Connect the assigned target to a real place before the audience sees the woman living there.
- Bố cục: Use the slow push to keep Sơn, the turning photo and tower in a single spatial relationship.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He turns the photograph once to its sketch side, aligning it with the actual entrance across the street.
- Diễn xuất: His attention is professional and narrow; no anticipatory romantic expression belongs here yet.
- Điểm nét: Favor his reading distance and the sketch edge, while leaving the tower recognizable beyond.
- Chất liệu: Rain speckles the existing photograph sleeve and darkens the alley wall without obscuring the sketch.
- Âm nổi: City rain replaces shop softness; avoid an invented phone, engine arrival or future flower-shop bell.
- Nhịp diễn: The turn is brief, the comparison takes longer, and the final frame becomes a purposeful eyeline.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Match the rectangle of the building sketch to the stall mirror edge. Replace city rain with present-day coastal morning sound.

### CẢNH 03: Khách Không Nhìn Biển
*Thời gian truyện: D1 morning*
Mây chú ý cách Sơn chọn chỗ và quan sát đường.

**Câu hỏi:** Mây có nhận ra người lạ đang quan sát mình không?
**Móc vào:** Khách quay lưng ra biển và đọc đường qua gương.
**Điểm bẻ:** Cuộc hỏi tên bình thường thành cuộc quan sát hai chiều.
**Gieo / trả:** Tư thế nhìn đường được đổi nghĩa ở 35; Mây dùng xác minh chứ chưa quy kết.

**Clip 1 · MATCH · STALL · W**
- Đầu: Rectangular stall mirror under the green tarp, Sơn at the sea-facing table edge.
- Hành động: He sits with his back to the sea and watches the road reflection.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Biển phía sau chú kìa.
- Cuối: Sơn facing the street, Bảy placing coffee on his right.

- Ý đồ: Make Sơn's seating choice a small behavioral mystery that Bảy notices before Mây does.
- Bố cục: Keep the mirror rectangle related to the preceding sketch shape, with the sea behind Sơn.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He takes the seat once, turning his body toward the street instead of toward the water.
- Diễn xuất: His road attention is habitual rather than frantic; Bảy's comment carries amused curiosity.
- Điểm nét: Keep the reflected road and Sơn's posture understandable without making the mirror an impossible close-up.
- Chất liệu: The green tarp casts ordinary shade; bright sea light remains a pressure beyond the shelter.
- Âm nổi: Glass meets table on his right; Bảy's remark rides over continuous morning stall ambience.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow the audience to read the wrong-way seat before the comment names the oddity.

**Clip 2 · J · STREET · T**
- Đầu: Thành riding slowly, Mây behind him wearing a helmet.
- Hành động: Mây notices a deliberate glance from the coffee stall off screen and signals Thành to stop.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Dừng chỗ này.
- Cuối: Motorcycle stopped beyond the stall, both helmets still on.

- Ý đồ: Introduce Mây through a precise observation rather than an instant supernatural diagnosis.
- Bố cục: Track the motorcycle smoothly at its established low street speed, both helmeted riders readable.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây signals a stop before the motorcycle settles beyond the stall, staying seated behind Thành.
- Diễn xuất: Her response is interested and economical; Thành reacts with routine familiarity rather than comic surprise.
- Điểm nét: Keep the riders' faces and hand signal readable; the watched customer remains off screen.
- Chất liệu: Road glare slips across helmet rims and the worn motorcycle, retaining the town's modest scale.
- Âm nổi: The engine decelerates naturally under her short instruction; do not exaggerate tire friction.
- Nhịp diễn: Notice, signal and stop form one progression, ending with both helmets still in place.

**Clip 3 · CUT · STALL · M**
- Đầu: Mây seated facing Sơn, notebook still closed.
- Hành động: She asks his name while he moves his bag away from the walkway.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Anh tên gì?
- Cuối: Bag under Sơn's chair, Mây looking at his bare hands.

- Ý đồ: Let a routine question reveal Sơn's need to control space without making him rude.
- Bố cục: Use the medium two-person setup so Mây's closed notebook and Sơn's bag share the lower frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He moves the bag clear of the walkway once as she asks his name.
- Diễn xuất: Mây is courteous but attentive; Sơn's tidiness should read as reflex, not a staged concealment.
- Điểm nét: Prioritize faces and bare hands, keeping the notebook visible but not yet an investigative flourish.
- Chất liệu: Weathered plastic and coffee condensation ground the exchange in an ordinary public place.
- Âm nổi: The bag settles beneath the chair with a modest cloth scrape; ambience does not stop for the question.
- Nhịp diễn: Give his hands a useful task during the question, then leave time for the delayed eye contact.

**Clip 4 · CONTINUE · STALL · M**
- Đầu: Bag under Sơn's chair, Mây looking at his bare hands.
- Hành động: Sơn answers and finally meets her eyes.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Sơn. Tôi thuê nhà trên đồi.
- Cuối: Mây opens her notebook, Sơn's cup remains untouched.

- Ý đồ: Let the offered first name be a concession while keeping the man behind it unreadable.
- Bố cục: Retain the exact medium composition, same table positions and untouched cup from the preceding frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn finally raises his eyes; Mây opens her notebook only after the answer reaches her.
- Diễn xuất: He states a fact without friendliness or aggression; she accepts it without pretending belief is established.
- Điểm nét: Keep both eyes readable at the existing depth, with no cut to a notebook insert.
- Chất liệu: The still coffee surface contrasts with small tarp shadows moving across the table.
- Âm nổi: His name should be clean and unhurried, followed by a single notebook rustle.
- Nhịp diễn: The gaze arrives before the full answer ends; no hand returns to replay the bag movement.

**Clip 5 · L · STREET · O**
- Đầu: Mây beside the parked motorcycle, Thành ready to leave.
- Hành động: She gives Thành the name and asks him to check the rental report.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Kiểm tra giúp chị. Kín thôi.
- Cuối: Thành noting the name, Mây glancing toward the hill.

- Ý đồ: Show Mây converting curiosity into a discreet check while keeping the encounter socially ordinary.
- Bố cục: Frame over Thành toward Mây's visible speaking face, motorcycle between them and the stall beyond.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She gives him the name without pointing openly toward Sơn, then glances toward the hill.
- Diễn xuất: Her voice lowers only enough for privacy; Thành's attentive note is work, not a nervous gag.
- Điểm nét: Hold Mây's mouth and Thành's noting hand in usable depth without cutting to the page.
- Chất liệu: Helmet edges, notebook paper and worn handlebar surfaces remain practical, unglamorized details.
- Âm nổi: Keep distant traffic and stall glasses under her sentence; the parked engine should not roar again.
- Nhịp diễn: One instruction, one acknowledgement through posture, then let the hill glance motivate the next concern.

**Clip 6 · CUT · STALL · I**
- Đầu: Sơn's right hand beside the coffee cup.
- Hành động: He stops touching the closed watch and turns the cup handle outward.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Cup handle facing the road, watch still closed at his belt.

- Ý đồ: Leave a trace of controlled unease after Mây departs without adding a new clue.
- Bố cục: Hold the detail frame on Sơn's right hand, existing cup handle and closed belt watch.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He ends the unconscious watch touch and turns the cup handle outward once.
- Diễn xuất: The movement is precise but not fussy; tension lives in his delayed release of the handle.
- Điểm nét: Keep the cup rim and hand plane clear, watch lid recognizable but never opened.
- Chất liệu: Condensation slides on the glass while the silver lid stays dull in the awning shade.
- Âm nổi: A faint ceramic or glass movement replaces dialogue; the stopped watch contributes no ticking.
- Nhịp diễn: Start after the questioning has ended, complete the small correction and leave natural motion at the edge.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Introduce the flower-shop bell over the final coffee-cup detail, then cut into the first visit. No repeated cup movement.

### CẢNH 04: Quá Khứ: Ba Lần Ghé Tiệm
*Thời gian truyện: PAST one month earlier afternoon*
Tình cảm nảy từ hành vi cụ thể qua ba lần gặp.

**Câu hỏi:** Một người chỉ nhìn mục tiêu có chịu nhìn Hạ không?
**Móc vào:** Chuông tiệm vang nhưng Sơn nhìn qua vai người bán hoa.
**Điểm bẻ:** Ba lần ghé thay từ theo dõi tòa nhà sang tự nguyện ở lại.
**Gieo / trả:** Việc sửa xô và chọn nán lại làm tình cảm có hành vi, tránh chỉ dựa vào lời tán tỉnh.

**Clip 1 · J · FLOWER · W**
- Đầu: Hạ cutting lilies as the entrance bell rings.
- Hành động: Sơn enters, sees Kiên's lobby across the road, chooses the window chair.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Anh mua hoa cho ai?
- Cuối: Sơn at the window, Hạ behind the flower counter.

- Ý đồ: Show the professional reason for entering before Hạ makes the encounter personal.
- Bố cục: The wide frame links counter, window chair and the tower lobby across the road without changing location.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn crosses toward the window chair while Hạ continues cutting existing lilies behind the counter.
- Diễn xuất: Hạ's greeting is easy and direct; Sơn answers her presence first with a glance past her.
- Điểm nét: Use depth to keep lobby direction understandable while reserving visual warmth for the foreground shop.
- Chất liệu: Fresh cut stems, damp bucket rims and warm window reflections suggest a shop being worked in.
- Âm nổi: Let the bell finish before her question becomes clear; keep scissors and water modest beneath it.
- Nhịp diễn: Give his route to the chosen chair an unbroken purpose, avoiding an ornamental walk around the flowers.

**Clip 2 · CUT · FLOWER · M**
- Đầu: A single lily between Hạ and Sơn.
- Hành động: A delivery boy bumps a bucket outside, Sơn steadies Hạ's falling vase.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Cho người không biết nhận hoa.
- Cuối: Vase safe between their hands, Sơn releases it first.

- Ý đồ: Let the first connection grow from competent care rather than instant flirtation.
- Bố cục: Frame the lily and existing vase between their hands, keeping both upper bodies in the medium shot.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn steadies the falling vase within reach, then releases it to Hạ without holding her fingers.
- Diễn xuất: His reply can be faintly self-mocking; Hạ registers the difference between the guarded man and helpful hands.
- Điểm nét: Keep the vase lip and both hands readable, without a separate impact or reaction angle.
- Chất liệu: A trace of water rocks inside the saved vase; paper wrapping remains slightly damp at its edge.
- Âm nổi: The disturbance outside is brief, then a small water slosh and his understated sentence fill the beat.
- Nhịp diễn: Catch quickly, release sooner than romance would demand, let her reaction carry the extra time.

**Clip 3 · MATCH · FLOWER · M**
- Đầu: Same window chair on the following afternoon, fresh flowers changed.
- Hành động: Hạ places a second lily on the counter before Sơn can ask.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Hôm nay vẫn người đó hả?
- Cuối: Two lilies in separate paper sleeves, Hạ suppressing a smile.

- Ý đồ: Make the second visit feel like Hạ recognizing a pattern while giving Sơn room to deny it.
- Bố cục: Match the prior table geometry with changed flowers and two separate wrapped lilies in view.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ places the second lily before he asks, withdrawing her hand rather than pushing it at him.
- Diễn xuất: Her smile is suppressed, not coyly performed; his pause should reveal surprise at being remembered.
- Điểm nét: Keep her face and the separate sleeves at shared readable depth, no readable date cue required.
- Chất liệu: Fresh stems and different wrapping folds distinguish the afternoon without inventing a calendar insert.
- Âm nổi: A bell tail and paper sleeve settle under her line, with ordinary outside traffic continuing.
- Nhịp diễn: Let recognition precede the teasing question, ending before Sơn supplies an unnecessary extra answer.

**Clip 4 · CUT · FLOWER · O**
- Đầu: Hạ facing the window, Sơn beside the second lily.
- Hành động: She follows his eyeline to Kiên's lobby and then looks back at him.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Hạ waiting for an answer, Sơn no longer watching the lobby.

- Ý đồ: Allow Hạ to notice the surveillance without turning the scene into an accusation.
- Bố cục: Use the existing shoulder framing to follow her eyeline from Sơn toward the window and back.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She looks once toward the lobby, returns to his face and simply waits.
- Diễn xuất: Curiosity stays warmer than suspicion; Sơn's attention leaves the lobby reluctantly but completely for this beat.
- Điểm nét: Keep her eyes as the focus destination while the lobby remains only a contextual background shape.
- Chất liệu: Window reflections move softly across flower buckets; neither person touches a new object.
- Âm nổi: No line is needed; let distant street noise expose the silence between their shared looks.
- Nhịp diễn: Make the outward glance short and the return longer, so the human connection wins the duration.

**Clip 5 · MATCH · FLOWER · M**
- Đầu: Third afternoon, Sơn holding a repaired bucket handle.
- Hành động: He returns the bucket he repaired without being asked.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Anh nhớ cái xô hơn nhớ tiền.
- Cuối: Hạ holding the repaired bucket, Sơn with empty hands.

- Ý đồ: Make the third return an offered kindness that Sơn cannot explain as surveillance work.
- Bố cục: Keep the repaired handle and Hạ's receiving hands inside the existing medium composition.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He returns the repaired bucket, letting her take its weight before his hands leave it.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn avoids pride; Hạ's joke should be affectionate surprise at practical attention, not a laugh track setup.
- Điểm nét: Keep the repaired area visible without pretending its mechanism needs an instructional close-up.
- Chất liệu: Scuffed plastic remains scuffed; the repair is modest and used, not magically replaced by a new bucket.
- Âm nổi: A light handle creak and her line carry the moment, with no reward flourish in the music.
- Nhịp diễn: Hold the awkward emptiness of his hands after the handover before she finishes noticing the gesture.

**Clip 6 · CUT · FLOWER · P**
- Đầu: Sơn standing by the open shop door.
- Hành động: He starts to leave, then turns back to hear Hạ finish laughing.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn staying in the doorway, Hạ smiling behind the counter.

- Ý đồ: Finish the meeting on Sơn choosing to stay, a small change that will later cost him.
- Bố cục: The slow push holds him at the open doorway, with Hạ's smile in the same shop depth.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He starts to leave but arrests the departure within the doorway, turning only once toward her.
- Diễn xuất: His face softens without an instant grin; her laugh subsides naturally when she realizes he listened.
- Điểm nét: Favor his reaction while retaining enough of Hạ to show who caused it, no new reverse shot.
- Chất liệu: Door glass and flower wrapping carry late light; no new flower is exchanged here.
- Âm nổi: Let her existing laughter trail into normal shop sound; avoid adding dialogue to fill the silence.
- Nhịp diễn: His halted exit is the event, so spend the remaining seconds on the decision rather than another step.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Carry the shop-door bell resonance briefly into the quiet cabin, cut to the already-open bag and new evidence action.

### CẢNH 05: Sổ Đen Và Hạn Chót
*Thời gian truyện: D1 noon*
Giới thiệu bằng chứng bị lấy và lịch giao hàng trong bảy ngày.

**Câu hỏi:** Trong túi Sơn có gì khiến người khác phải tìm tới?
**Móc vào:** Túi mở ra nhưng người mở lại giấu thứ nguy hiểm lên cao.
**Điểm bẻ:** Vật chứng đưa ra một thời hạn bên ngoài và nguy cơ kéo tới nhà.
**Gieo / trả:** Sổ, USB, lịch tàu và tin niêm phong trở thành nguồn đối chiếu, không là đồ trang trí.

**Clip 1 · L · CABIN · M**
- Đầu: Canvas bag open on the cabin table.
- Hành động: Sơn lays out the black ledger, a sealed tin and a concealed handgun case.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Tôi mang theo thứ họ cần bịt lại.
- Cuối: Three separate objects on the table, bag empty beside them.

- Ý đồ: Replace the quiet romantic memory with objects that reveal why Sơn cannot simply rest.
- Bố cục: Use the medium frame to read the open bag and three separate items without a montage.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He lays out ledger, sealed tin and closed case in distinct spaces, never stacking their identities.
- Diễn xuất: His orderly hands contrast with fatigue in his shoulders; the narration should sound factual, not boastful.
- Điểm nét: Keep the tabletop and his lowered face at practical depth so the inventory remains one action.
- Chất liệu: Canvas creases, black leather and dented tin each have a different surface response to daylight.
- Âm nổi: Let three modest set-down sounds define the objects, with wind continuing through the cabin gaps.
- Nhịp diễn: Do not give each item an artificial reveal pause; the meaningful pause comes after the bag empties.

**Clip 2 · CUT · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: Black ledger open, USB taped inside the back cover.
- Hành động: He removes the USB and finds the matching dispatch receipt underneath.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: USB and receipt placed side by side, ledger still open.

- Ý đồ: Reveal a second layer of evidence through a believable physical discovery, not a screen animation.
- Bố cục: The detail shot holds the back cover, taped USB and receipt in a single usable plane.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He peels the existing tape, removes the USB and exposes the paper beneath without hiding either.
- Diễn xuất: Fingers slow briefly when he recognizes the receipt, suggesting significance before the next line explains it.
- Điểm nét: Hold the object plane steady; readable layout matters more than legible handwritten words or digits.
- Chất liệu: Worn adhesive stretches slightly, and the receipt retains the crease from being stored under the drive.
- Âm nổi: Use tape lift and paper separation as small clear sounds; no electronic chime belongs to an unplugged USB.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the receipt appear because the USB moves, then settle both side by side for the continuation.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: USB and receipt placed side by side, ledger still open.
- Hành động: Sơn traces seven dated boxes on the receipt and stops at a ship stamp.
- Thoại: Sơn, ngoài khung hình: Bảy ngày nữa, chuyến hàng rời bến.
- Cuối: His fingertip on the last box beside the ship stamp.

- Ý đồ: Turn the receipt into an external deadline while keeping its printed content visually unobtrusive.
- Bố cục: Keep the exact preceding detail setup, USB and receipt still sharing the same table orientation.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn follows the existing sequence of boxes once, stopping at the ship stamp rather than tapping repeatedly.
- Diễn xuất: The fingertip becomes firmer at the last box; his off-screen voice remains controlled rather than prophetic.
- Điểm nét: Retain the same focus plane as the receipt reveal, with the final box and stamp readable as shapes.
- Chất liệu: A curled paper corner shifts in the cabin breeze while his finger keeps the relevant area flat.
- Âm nổi: One fingertip drag under the sentence, no added ticking or departing ship sound to overstate the clue.
- Nhịp diễn: The sentence earns the final stop; let the fingertip remain with slight live movement after the words.

**Clip 4 · CUT · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: Tin open, three sealed fictional Tê vials in fitted slots.
- Hành động: He counts the intact seals, closes the lid without using anything.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sealed tin closed, three vials accounted for inside.

- Ý đồ: Introduce the sealed substance as evidence of a larger system, without fetishizing its use.
- Bố cục: Use the detail composition to show all three fitted slots and the intact vial caps together.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He checks the three seals in place and closes the tin lid once without removing a vial.
- Diễn xuất: His hands show familiarity with the objects but no pleasure or curiosity about using them.
- Điểm nét: Keep seals and tin edge crisp, avoiding a macro liquid view that suggests an instructional demonstration.
- Chất liệu: The dented lid and fitted lining look handled, while the sealed glass stays clean and ordinary.
- Âm nổi: Small glass restraint and a single lid click should be softer than the surrounding wind.
- Nhịp diễn: Count by a short eye or hand progression, then close with finality instead of another suspenseful reopening.

**Clip 5 · CUT · CABIN · M**
- Đầu: Sơn below the cabin's exposed roof beam.
- Hành động: He secures the closed handgun case above the beam, away from reach.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Case hidden above the beam, Sơn's empty hands below.

- Ý đồ: Show Sơn separating himself from violence while not pretending the past has vanished.
- Bố cục: The medium setup keeps his empty hands and the beam recess within the same physical frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He secures the complete closed case above the beam, then lowers both hands where they can be seen.
- Diễn xuất: Avoid a triumphant resolve; he checks placement from habit and seems heavier once the hands return.
- Điểm nét: Keep enough depth to understand the recess, without exposing hidden contents or a new weapons insert.
- Chất liệu: Old timber, worn case leather and pale dust belong to the existing cabin, not an armory display.
- Âm nổi: A restrained case-to-wood contact and his exhale are sufficient; never add a weapon-cocking sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete the placement once and end below the beam, allowing the absence from his hands to register.

**Clip 6 · J · CABIN · M**
- Đầu: Bảy at the porch threshold carrying a meal.
- Hành động: Sơn covers the ledger, Bảy leaves the meal without crossing the room.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Ăn đi. Sáng nay chú đỡ tui cái thùng.
- Cuối: Meal by the door, covered ledger farther inside.

- Ý đồ: Make hospitality interrupt concealment, binding Bảy's later help to an earned ordinary kindness.
- Bố cục: Frame the threshold and covered ledger with clear distance, Bảy outside the private room's deeper space.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn covers the ledger once while Bảy lowers the meal near the door and stops there.
- Diễn xuất: Bảy's gratitude stays practical; Sơn's relief is mixed with discomfort at being cared for.
- Điểm nét: Keep the two people readable without shifting focus into the concealed book or forcing Bảy to notice it.
- Chất liệu: Meal-container steam and the existing cloth cover bring warmth into the otherwise sparse room.
- Âm nổi: A soft container landing and Bảy's unforced voice replace the tin's hard click, with no new musical comfort cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Let his boundary-respecting stop matter as much as the meal; do not send him walking through the room.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: The meal container settling at the cabin door bridges to the flower-shop cash drawer. Do not replay the tin being closed.

### CẢNH 06: Quá Khứ: Lời Mời Về Biển
*Thời gian truyện: PAST one month earlier night*
Hạ đặt giới hạn rồi tặng đồng hồ như một lời hẹn.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn có thể nói thật mà không mất người anh muốn giữ không?
**Móc vào:** Chuyển động rót tiền dừng khi bao súng lộ ra.
**Điểm bẻ:** Hạ lùi lại, rồi trao lời hẹn khi anh dám thừa nhận chưa biết cách dừng.
**Gieo / trả:** Đồng hồ không mở; một bàn tay trần gieo lựa chọn tay không và lần mở ở 52.

**Clip 1 · L · FLOWER_BACK · M**
- Đầu: Hạ putting the day's cash away, Sơn near the rear door.
- Hành động: His jacket shifts, showing the closed holster, Hạ stops moving.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Anh làm việc gì vậy?
- Cuối: Hạ's hand resting on the cash drawer, distance between them.

- Ý đồ: Make Hạ's discovery interrupt an ordinary task before she has language for what she has seen.
- Bố cục: Keep both upper bodies and the cash drawer inside the locked medium composition, door behind Sơn.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: His jacket shifts in existing movement, revealing the closed holster; her working hand stops where it is.
- Diễn xuất: Her question begins with contained alarm, not immediate acceptance; Sơn notices the stopped hand before answering.
- Điểm nét: Hold the same practical plane across her face and his jacket edge, not a separate glamorous weapon reveal.
- Chất liệu: Paper money and the drawer's worn edge remain domestic details beside the dark closed holster.
- Âm nổi: Let the cash rustle stop audibly while kettle and street bed continue underneath her question.
- Nhịp diễn: The interruption creates the tension; leave enough time for Sơn to recognize that she has seen it.

**Clip 2 · CONTINUE · FLOWER_BACK · M**
- Đầu: Hạ's hand resting on the cash drawer, distance between them.
- Hành động: Sơn cannot answer, Hạ closes the drawer and steps back.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Em không muốn sống trong lời giấu.
- Cuối: Sơn remaining by the door, Hạ beyond the table.

- Ý đồ: Let Hạ establish a boundary that makes the later gift a choice rather than blind forgiveness.
- Bố cục: Preserve the preceding medium setup and shared depth; the table stays between them as she steps back.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She closes the drawer once and takes a measured step beyond the table while he stays by the door.
- Diễn xuất: Her hurt is clear but not pleading; Sơn's inability to answer should feel more exposed than a confession.
- Điểm nét: Keep their established eyelines, allowing physical distance to carry the shift without a rack or reframe.
- Chất liệu: The closed drawer and unoccupied table space create a practical gap, not a symbolic lighting transformation.
- Âm nổi: The drawer's modest closure lands before her sentence, then ordinary room sound returns without a dramatic drop.
- Nhịp diễn: Give her retreat its full time, and let the sentence end before either person repairs the distance.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · FLOWER_BACK · M**
- Đầu: Sơn remaining by the door, Hạ beyond the table.
- Hành động: Sơn removes his right glove and lays it on the table.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Anh muốn dừng. Chưa biết cách.
- Cuối: Bare right hand beside one black glove, Hạ watching.

- Ý đồ: Make the first bare hand an attempt at honesty whose inadequacy Sơn already understands.
- Bố cục: Keep the exact prior medium composition so the removed glove does not become a magic costume reset.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn removes only the right glove and lays it on the table, left glove remaining on.
- Diễn xuất: His line should carry uncertainty rather than a promise of instant reform; Hạ listens without rewarding him immediately.
- Điểm nét: Keep the existing face-and-table plane stable, with the single loose glove legible below their eyelines.
- Chất liệu: The bare skin against matte black leather gives a quiet material contrast under the same warm light.
- Âm nổi: Leather pulling free and a small tabletop landing accompany his low line, with the kettle still present.
- Nhịp diễn: The glove comes off in one measured action; his admission follows instead of being stretched over every finger.

**Clip 4 · CUT · FLOWER_BACK · I**
- Đầu: Hạ holding a closed silver pocket watch by its chain.
- Hành động: She winds it with the lid still shut, the ticking becomes audible.
- Thoại: Hạ, ngoài khung hình: Của ba em. Nó còn chạy.
- Cuối: Closed watch resting on Hạ's open palm.

- Ý đồ: Give Hạ's family watch an ordinary working life before it acquires emotional meaning.
- Bố cục: Use the detail view of her palm, crown and closed lid, keeping the chain connected and recognizable.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She winds the existing crown gently and lets the case settle in her open palm without pressing the clasp.
- Diễn xuất: Her fingers are familiar and unceremonious, suggesting a family object rather than a mysterious talisman.
- Điểm nét: Hold the closed case and fingertips at one stable depth; never reveal the inner photograph here.
- Chất liệu: Worn silver and the small flower engraving catch warm light without an exaggerated glitter effect.
- Âm nổi: One subtle winding sound gives way to a soft real tick, distinct from the kettle and distant street.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the tick become audible after the hand stops, then preserve the closed object for the handover.

**Clip 5 · CUT · FLOWER_BACK · M**
- Đầu: Hạ extending the watch toward Sơn's bare right hand.
- Hành động: She sets it in his palm, lets him decide to close his fingers.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Hết việc, mở ra. Rồi về biển.
- Cuối: Sơn holding the closed watch, Hạ's hand withdrawn.

- Ý đồ: Make accepting the watch require Sơn's voluntary response, preserving Hạ's agency in the offer.
- Bố cục: Return to the medium setup with both faces and the exchange visible, table still between them.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ sets the case in his bare right palm, withdraws her hand and waits for him to close his fingers.
- Diễn xuất: Her invitation is hopeful but conditional; he receives it with hesitation, not a romantic grin on cue.
- Điểm nét: Keep their faces and joined object area readable without cutting to an insert or changing the setup.
- Chất liệu: The thin chain moves naturally against his palm, while the single loose glove remains where it was left.
- Âm nổi: Her sentence should be intimate and clear above the quiet tick, with no swelling score sealing the promise.
- Nhịp diễn: Leave the space after her hand withdraws; his fingers closing should be the answer before he speaks again.

**Clip 6 · CONTINUE · FLOWER_BACK · M**
- Đầu: Sơn holding the closed watch, Hạ's hand withdrawn.
- Hành động: Sơn pockets the watch, leaves the glove on the table for a moment.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Anh sẽ về nói thật với em.
- Cuối: His bare hand returns for the glove, Hạ turns off the kettle.

- Ý đồ: Finish on an imperfect intention rather than treating the gift as instant transformation.
- Bố cục: Maintain the prior medium frame and focus; the watch travels only from his palm to his pocket.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He pockets the still-closed watch, hesitates over the loose right glove and reaches back for it once.
- Diễn xuất: His promise should contain shame at still needing the glove; Hạ turns off the kettle without a reproachful display.
- Điểm nét: Keep the same face-and-table readability, allowing the hand's return to be seen without a sudden close-up.
- Chất liệu: The pocket takes the watch's weight, leather stays matte, and the kettle steam begins thinning naturally.
- Âm nổi: The kettle switch ends the hiss after his line; do not use the watch as an unnaturally loud metronome.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow a short gap before the hand returns for the glove, ending on contradiction rather than a second farewell.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Let the kettle hiss finish. Cut to the next morning doorframe and its second tally, daylight replacing the warm room.

### CẢNH 07: Mây Gõ Cửa
*Thời gian truyện: D2 morning*
Mây tìm thấy giấy giả và chọn theo dõi người truy tìm Sơn.

**Câu hỏi:** Giấy tờ giả sẽ khiến Mây bắt ngay hay theo dấu người truy tìm?
**Móc vào:** Vạch thứ hai bị tiếng xe cắt ngang.
**Điểm bẻ:** Lời ở hết tuần được kiểm chứng và trở thành hồ sơ.
**Gieo / trả:** Mây giữ luật bằng bước kiểm tra rõ; thời hạn Sơn né tránh được soi ở 14, 17.

**Clip 1 · OPEN · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: One mark on the doorframe, tool below it.
- Hành động: Sơn carves the second mark, a motorcycle stops outside.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Two marks, tool put on the shelf.

- Ý đồ: Make the daily count vulnerable to an interruption from outside Sơn's control.
- Bố cục: Keep the detail angle on one existing tally and the tool beginning a second, with wood orientation consistent.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He finishes only the second mark as the approaching motorcycle settles outside, then puts the tool on the shelf.
- Diễn xuất: The hand remains steady, but its brief pause at the engine sound reveals attention beyond the carving.
- Điểm nét: Keep the wood surface and exact mark count clear; do not reveal the rider through a new angle.
- Chất liệu: Two pale cuts in old wood distinguish new days without generated numerals, labels or extra scratches.
- Âm nổi: The scrape is interrupted by a believable engine stop, followed by quieter pine wind rather than a sting.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete the new cut before withdrawing the hand, so the interruption does not create an unfinished duplicate mark.

**Clip 2 · J · PORCH · M**
- Đầu: Mây outside the threshold, Sơn holding the door open.
- Hành động: She waits for permission before asking for his papers.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Tôi cần xác nhận người thuê nhà.
- Cuối: Sơn passing a card across the threshold.

- Ý đồ: Show Mây's lawful courtesy making evasion harder than a forceful entrance would.
- Bố cục: The medium porch setup keeps the open threshold as a visible boundary between visitor and renter.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She waits outside until acknowledged; Sơn passes the card across instead of inviting her deeper immediately.
- Diễn xuất: Mây's tone is neutral and patient, while Sơn overcontrols the simple handover to hide discomfort.
- Điểm nét: Hold faces and the passing card in practical shared depth, with no readable official details required.
- Chất liệu: Doorframe weathering and the card's ordinary plastic surface keep the encounter mundane and credible.
- Âm nổi: A light knock or threshold step already motivated by arrival may sit under her line; no threatening percussion.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the respectful waiting occupy a beat before the request, then make the card exchange clean and singular.

**Clip 3 · CUT · CABIN · O**
- Đầu: Mây at the doorway with the card, Sơn beside the table.
- Hành động: She studies the card and his face without entering deeper.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Anh định ở tới khi nào?
- Cuối: Card returned to Sơn, notebook still open.

- Ý đồ: Make Mây compare a person to a document without performing instant guilt detection.
- Bố cục: Use the specified shoulder view to retain Sơn by the table and Mây at the doorway with the card.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She studies the photograph shape, returns her eyes to his face and hands the card back once.
- Diễn xuất: Her question is open rather than accusatory; his stillness should invite scrutiny without announcing a villain.
- Điểm nét: Keep Mây's speaking face readable past Sơn's near edge, with card detail secondary rather than legible text.
- Chất liệu: Window light grazes the card and notebook paper, while the interior beyond remains modest and spare.
- Âm nổi: Her paper and card handling stays soft below the question, with the outside motorcycle now quiet.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow a practical comparison interval, then return the card before the next evasive answer begins.

**Clip 4 · CUT · CABIN · M**
- Đầu: Sơn beside the two fresh marks.
- Hành động: He looks at the marks instead of answering, Mây notices the evasion.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Hết tuần này.
- Cuối: Mây's eyeline fixed on the doorframe.

- Ý đồ: Tie Sơn's evasive answer to the visible count so Mây gains a behavioral fact.
- Bố cục: The medium composition holds Sơn beside the two fresh marks and Mây's reaction in the same space.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He looks toward the marks instead of directly at her, then gives the limited answer without moving away.
- Diễn xuất: He should not smirk at withholding information; Mây registers the relationship and lets it remain unanswered.
- Điểm nét: Keep his glance and her receiving eyeline clear, avoiding a new insert that would repeat the carving shot.
- Chất liệu: Fresh wood remains pale against the old frame, with daylight holding the same direction as the visit.
- Âm nổi: A brief pause before his short answer should be heard as reluctance, not filled by a ticking cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Use the silence to let Mây follow his glance, then end with her attention on the count.

**Clip 5 · L · POST · I**
- Đầu: Thành at the old computer, Mây standing behind him.
- Hành động: A record check returns a mismatched identity, Thành prints the result.
- Thoại: Thành, ngoài khung hình: Giấy không khớp hồ sơ, chị.
- Cuối: Printer feeding one page, Mây taking it.

- Ý đồ: Let verification produce the first clear discrepancy without a melodramatic computer reveal.
- Bố cục: Use the detail setup on printer output, keyboard edge and the hands receiving the page.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Thành completes the existing check, the printer feeds one result, and Mây takes it by its edge.
- Diễn xuất: Their hands become more deliberate at the mismatch; no visible actor needs an exaggerated shocked face.
- Điểm nét: Keep the result as layout and photograph shapes, not readable personal data or a glowing error graphic.
- Chất liệu: Old plastic keys and lightly curled paper make the modest station tools feel used and practical.
- Âm nổi: Printer rollers and a single page feed support Thành's off-screen finding; no digital alarm is necessary.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the paper emerge at ordinary speed, then give the fact room to land before filing it.

**Clip 6 · CUT · POST · M**
- Đầu: Mây laying the result in a new investigation folder.
- Hành động: She assigns one discreet observer and asks for outside vehicle reports.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Theo người tìm anh ta nữa.
- Cuối: Folder open with Sơn's rental note, Thành calling for support.

- Ý đồ: Show Mây choosing a method of investigation, keeping suspicion separate from proof.
- Bố cục: Frame both officers with folder in the lower medium space and support radio already available.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She files the mismatch and assigns discreet observation while Thành begins the support request.
- Diễn xuất: Mây is purposeful, not conspiratorial; Thành's readiness makes this feel like coordinated work rather than a secret favor.
- Điểm nét: Keep her face and the open folder readable, with the radio work secondary in the same setup.
- Chất liệu: Plain paper and the rental note give the inquiry physical continuity instead of another screen display.
- Âm nổi: The support channel opens quietly beneath her instruction, maintaining a working-station bed after the paper reveal.
- Nhịp diễn: One clear assignment closes the scene; avoid an extra pointing gesture or a second request to repeat the instruction.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Introduce garage ventilation hum before the flashback cut away from the station radio and folder.

### CẢNH 08: Quá Khứ: Đêm Bị Gài
*Thời gian truyện: PAST one month earlier night*
Sơn bị gài với bằng chứng giả và đồng hồ hỏng khi thoát.

**Câu hỏi:** Ai đáng ra ra khỏi thang máy, và tại sao Dũng thay người đó?
**Móc vào:** Xe trắng chờ, nhưng người ra là Dũng.
**Điểm bẻ:** Lối nhận việc bị biến thành điểm quy kết, Sơn mất cả nhịp an toàn lẫn tiếng tích tắc.
**Gieo / trả:** Đồng hồ dừng gieo cho 52; quần áo và găng vẫn theo Sơn tới tiệm hoa.

**Clip 1 · J · GARAGE · W**
- Đầu: Sơn behind a pillar, a white car at the far lift.
- Hành động: He checks the lift reflection without drawing his handgun.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn concealed behind the pillar, lift doors opening.

- Ý đồ: Create anticipation by letting the expected lift and white car occupy space before the wrong man appears.
- Bố cục: Use the wide garage view to connect Sơn's pillar, reflective surface, lift and waiting white car.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn checks the lift through reflection without drawing or changing pillars before the doors begin to open.
- Diễn xuất: His professional patience carries unease only when the lift opens, not from a constant frightened expression.
- Điểm nét: Retain readable route depth; the reflection should be plausible at this angle, not a magical close-up inside a mirror.
- Chất liệu: Concrete bands and restrained yellow practicals organize the garage, with a faint wet sheen near the ramp.
- Âm nổi: Ventilation hum, distant tire resonance and the actual lift movement build the pause without a score ramp.
- Nhịp diễn: Spend time on expectation, then let the doors start opening near the end rather than revealing Dũng early.

**Clip 2 · CUT · GARAGE · M**
- Đầu: Dũng emerging alone from the lift.
- Hành động: He slides a document envelope toward Sơn with a shoe, blocks the lift.
- Thoại: Dũng (@Dũng), nói trong hình: Má đổi chỗ giao. Nhặt đi.
- Cuối: Envelope on the floor between them, Dũng in front of the lift.

- Ý đồ: Make Dũng's familiar presence feel wrong through his handling of the envelope and blocked exit.
- Bố cục: The medium setup keeps Dũng's speaking face, shoe and floor envelope in a readable vertical relationship.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He slides the envelope once with his shoe and stays between Sơn and the lift opening.
- Diễn xuất: His casual instruction should sound rehearsed; Sơn's body stays contained rather than immediately attacking.
- Điểm nét: Hold Dũng's mouth and the envelope landing area readable, without an extra low-angle insert.
- Chất liệu: Scuffed shoe leather drags over concrete, while the envelope looks ordinary enough to be plausible work.
- Âm nổi: A dry paper scrape ends before the line's final word, lift ambience continuing behind his voice.
- Nhịp diễn: The slide is quick and dismissive; the wait afterward gives Sơn time to notice what is wrong.

**Clip 3 · CUT · GARAGE · I**
- Đầu: Sơn's gloved hand above the open envelope.
- Hành động: He sees his own staged photograph and a stolen seal inside.
- Thoại: Sơn, ngoài khung hình: Mày muốn tao nhận cái này.
- Cuối: His hand withdrawing from the planted evidence.

- Ý đồ: Let recognition of the planted evidence change the meaning of the meeting before the chase begins.
- Bố cục: Use the detail frame on the open envelope, gloved hand and two incriminating items together.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: The gloved hand approaches but withdraws when Sơn recognizes the staged photograph and stolen seal.
- Diễn xuất: Recognition should appear through arrested fingers and a controlled retreat, not an invented face reaction within the insert.
- Điểm nét: Keep both items recognizable as shapes, with no legible legal text or inexplicably sharp hidden document.
- Chất liệu: Worn photograph edges and the seal's hard outline contrast with the soft envelope flap and black glove.
- Âm nổi: Only paper movement and his low off-screen sentence break the garage hum; do not add a camera shutter effect.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the viewer a fraction to read the trap before his hand retreats and the accusation lands.

**Clip 4 · J · GARAGE · H**
- Đầu: Light beams sweeping toward Sơn's pillar.
- Hành động: He ducks behind a parked car, the closed watch strikes concrete.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn behind the car with the watch chain taut, alarm sounding.

- Ý đồ: Turn the trap into immediate physical pressure and damage the watch without making it an action trophy.
- Bố cục: Keep the handheld medium-wide on the pillar, nearby parked car and Sơn's crouching path from one side.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He ducks behind the car in one defensive movement; the pocketed watch hits concrete as his body folds.
- Diễn xuất: Fear shows as a sudden narrowed breath, then he regains control instead of posing through danger.
- Điểm nét: Keep his whole defensive movement readable, not a separate slow-motion watch impact or disorienting lens change.
- Chất liệu: Search beams move across existing concrete and car paint, leaving the concealed space readable at the end.
- Âm nổi: One dull metal knock is swallowed by alarm and footsteps; no firearm discharge or impact on a body is added.
- Nhịp diễn: The duck is fast, the concealed breathing interval longer, leaving the chain tension as a consequence.

**Clip 5 · CUT · GARAGE_EXIT · T**
- Đầu: Sơn at a low maintenance door, jacket still on.
- Hành động: He slips through the door and pulls it shut without dropping his clothes.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn outside the closed maintenance door, black gloves still on.

- Ý đồ: Make escape depend on taking a reachable maintenance exit, preserving the clothes and object continuity.
- Bố cục: Track laterally with Sơn through the existing low door and into the sheltered lane, retaining the established direction.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He slips through once, pulls the door shut and stays outside it with both black gloves still worn.
- Diễn xuất: He is economical but physically taxed; a delayed inhale after the closure keeps him human.
- Điểm nét: Keep his jacket, door edge and exiting body readable, without changing to an impossible interior view mid-shot.
- Chất liệu: Rain-darkened door paint and his damp suit carry the garage into the lane without adding a costume reset.
- Âm nổi: Door latch and receding garage alarm connect the spaces, his footfalls changing surface naturally.
- Nhịp diễn: The door closes only after his body clears it; allow the first safe breath to finish the movement.

**Clip 6 · L · ALLEY · P**
- Đầu: Sơn under the rear awning, closed watch in his hand.
- Hành động: He listens at the shut lid, hears no ticking, then sees the flower-shop lights.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Đêm đó, thứ đầu tiên ngừng là đồng hồ.
- Cuối: His face turning toward the distant flower shop.

- Ý đồ: Let the silent watch create a private loss before Sơn discovers a greater one at the flower shop.
- Bố cục: The restrained push holds his closed watch near his face and the distant shop-light direction beyond.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He listens at the lid without opening it, then turns toward the lights once the silence registers.
- Diễn xuất: His voice-over stays retrospective and dry; present expression is concentration giving way to alarm, not performed grief yet.
- Điểm nét: Favor his face and closed case at a sensible reading distance, leaving the shop as a distant light cue.
- Chất liệu: Wet lapel edges and muted silver retain the night's wear, with no inner watch face exposed.
- Âm nổi: Suppress any ticking completely; rain drips and his breathing remain under the narration and distant city bed.
- Nhịp diễn: Leave a small listening interval before the turn, so absence of sound motivates where his attention goes.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: City rain falls away, the arriving black car establishes present-day afternoon. Do not replay the garage escape.

### CẢNH 09: Hai Người Lạ
*Thời gian truyện: D2 afternoon*
Bảy và Thành tạo mạng quan sát, kẻ truy tìm để lại dấu.

**Câu hỏi:** Bảy che được Sơn bao lâu trước khi người lạ hiểu thị trấn này?
**Móc vào:** Hai người tìm cùng một sẹo, Bảy vẫn giữ nhịp lau quán.
**Điểm bẻ:** Lời hỏi người trở thành manh mối về tuyến vận chuyển.
**Gieo / trả:** Vòng tay công ty, biển số và danh thiếp hợp thành ba nguồn đối chiếu thay vì đoán.

**Clip 1 · OPEN · STREET · W**
- Đầu: A black car parking outside Bảy's stall.
- Hành động: Two unnamed men step out and display Sơn's surveillance photograph.
- Thoại: Người trong cảnh, nói trong hình: Chú thấy người này không?
- Cuối: Photograph extended toward Bảy, car door left open.

- Ý đồ: Make the same ordinary stall feel exposed when strangers use a photograph as a demand.
- Bố cục: Use the wide street setup to keep the black car, open door and Bảy's counter position related.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: The unnamed men leave their car and extend the existing photograph without crowding into a new interior angle.
- Diễn xuất: Their manner is businesslike entitlement, not a theatrical thug performance; Bảy studies them before answering.
- Điểm nét: Keep the offered photograph as a printed face, never placing Sơn physically beside the visitors.
- Chất liệu: Dust on the car and the stall's everyday clutter distinguish outside money from a working seafront.
- Âm nổi: The arriving engine settles to idle, one door remains open, and the short question cuts through normal stall noise.
- Nhịp diễn: Let their arrival occupy space before the question, then stay on the photograph held out for a response.

**Clip 2 · CUT · STALL · M**
- Đầu: Bảy behind the counter studying the offered photograph.
- Hành động: He gives a vague answer while sliding his phone below the counter.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Ở đây người giống người nhiều lắm.
- Cuối: Phone hidden beneath the counter, Bảy still holding the photo.

- Ý đồ: Show Bảy deciding to protect a person through a small lie he must manage in real time.
- Bố cục: The medium view favors his face and counter edge, with the offered photograph still in his hand.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He answers vaguely while lowering the existing phone beneath the counter with his free hand.
- Diễn xuất: His eyes stay on the visitors, and the line is casual enough to hide the choice rather than celebrate cleverness.
- Điểm nét: Hold his face and photograph at useful depth, allowing the phone to disappear without a separate secretive insert.
- Chất liệu: His moustache, worn counter and ordinary glass reflections should stay grounded, with no sudden stylized shadow shift.
- Âm nổi: Normal street sound continues; any phone handling stays quieter than his deliberately unremarkable sentence.
- Nhịp diễn: Keep the hand movement small and subordinate, leaving the audience to notice it rather than announcing a trick.

**Clip 3 · L · MARKET · O**
- Đầu: Sơn behind a fish stall with a clear view of the black car.
- Hành động: He notices one man's dock-worker wristband and stays out of sight.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Wristband visible through a gap, Sơn concealed behind a crate.

- Ý đồ: Give Sơn a usable observation while showing that remaining hidden costs him access to the exchange.
- Bố cục: The shoulder view from his shelter retains the crate gap and distant black-car side in plausible alignment.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He stays behind the fish-stall cover and follows only the wristband visible through the existing gap.
- Diễn xuất: He does not lean into danger for a perfect view; his stillness should show disciplined impatience.
- Điểm nét: Use enough depth for the wristband shape and cover relationship, without an impossible readable emblem close-up.
- Chất liệu: Wet crate edges and muted market shade partially interrupt the view, preserving a believable obstructed sightline.
- Âm nổi: Nearby water and market work soften the distant voices, so the clue comes visually rather than from perfect eavesdropping.
- Nhịp diễn: A brief clear glimpse is enough; resist repeating the wrist reveal or making the man pose for identification.

**Clip 4 · CUT · STREET · I**
- Đầu: Thành beside his parked motorcycle, notebook partly hidden.
- Hành động: He records the plate and the same dock-company emblem.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Plate sketch and company emblem on separate notebook lines.

- Ý đồ: Let Thành's quiet competence contribute a second independent clue rather than another heroic confrontation.
- Bố cục: Use the fixed detail angle on his partly hidden notebook and writing hand beside the parked motorcycle.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He records what he sees as simple plate and emblem shapes on separate lines, without presenting the page to camera.
- Diễn xuất: His fingers move carefully because concealment matters, but the pen should not tremble for comic effect.
- Điểm nét: Keep paper and hand clear, with the observed vehicle remaining contextual rather than forcing two sharp distant planes.
- Chất liệu: Notebook creases, pencil pressure and the scratched motorcycle surface reinforce practical field work.
- Âm nổi: Use faint writing and small page friction beneath the continuing road bed, not a fake camera shutter.
- Nhịp diễn: Let him finish each distinct notation once, then end before another unnecessary glance rewrites the same information.

**Clip 5 · CUT · STALL · W**
- Đầu: The two men returning to their black car.
- Hành động: They leave a contact card, Bảy waits until the car drives off.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Contact card under Bảy's glass, road clear.

- Ý đồ: Let the visitors leave a future route into the investigation while Bảy waits until immediate danger passes.
- Bố cục: Keep counter, departing men and black car inside the established wide stall-to-road relationship.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They leave one contact card, return to the car and drive away; Bảy acts only once the road is clear.
- Diễn xuất: His composure should ease only after they recede, without a visible victorious grin at deceiving them.
- Điểm nét: Maintain readable positions and the card's final place under his glass, not a legible phone-number insert.
- Chất liệu: The small card lies flat beneath an ordinary glass, its edges carrying more significance than printed text.
- Âm nổi: Footsteps and engine departure recede naturally, leaving the awning and sea audible again.
- Nhịp diễn: Hold the wait for the car to clear before resolving on the card, without replaying its placement.

**Clip 6 · J · POST · M**
- Đầu: Thành placing his notes beside Bảy's contact card.
- Hành động: Mây connects the emblem to the dispatch company and requests a check.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Họ tìm người. Ta tìm đường đi.
- Cuối: Mây marking the company entry, Thành preparing the request.

- Ý đồ: Convert scattered observations into a direction of inquiry, giving Mây a discovery instead of instant certainty.
- Bố cục: Frame the two officers and table evidence in the medium setup, each source visibly separate.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Thành places his notes beside the card; Mây aligns only the company emblem relationship and requests verification.
- Diễn xuất: Mây's confidence comes from connecting sources, while Thành watches to understand the next task rather than admiring her intuition.
- Điểm nét: Keep faces and the small comparison zone readable without turning company marks into legible generated lettering.
- Chất liệu: Paper from street notes and the cleaner contact card retain different textures and origins.
- Âm nổi: A modest paper slide and quiet support-channel activity sit beneath Mây's concise thought.
- Nhịp diễn: Pause on the match before the instruction; avoid reading every plate, emblem and company detail aloud again.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Introduce wind through the damaged shop door before cutting from the company inquiry to the torn scarf trace.

### CẢNH 10: Quá Khứ: Dấu Vết Còn Lại
*Thời gian truyện: PAST one month earlier dawn*
Sơn nhặt một mảnh khăn và lấy sổ từ kho mình biết.

**Câu hỏi:** Hạ để lại dấu hay chỉ để lại thứ Sơn muốn tin?
**Móc vào:** Cả tiệm hỏng nhưng một mảnh xanh mắc đúng vị trí.
**Điểm bẻ:** Một mảnh khăn cho thấy bị đưa đi; Sơn chọn lấy chứng cứ thay vì trở về nhận lệnh.
**Gieo / trả:** Mảnh ngắn chưa là khăn đầy đủ; nguồn sổ và tin được trả lời để không xuất hiện thần kỳ.

**Clip 1 · J · FLOWER · W**
- Đầu: Broken shop glass, a pale-blue strip caught on the latch.
- Hành động: Sơn enters carefully and stops at the torn strip.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn facing the latch, torn strip still attached.

- Ý đồ: Make absence tangible through a small deliberate trace within a damaged familiar space.
- Bố cục: Use the wide shop view to retain broken glass, displaced flowers and the pale-blue strip at the latch together.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn enters carefully along a clear footpath and stops before touching the strip.
- Diễn xuất: His body should slow as recognition arrives, keeping shock inward rather than dropping immediately to his knees.
- Điểm nét: Allow the strip to be findable within the room, without an artificial spotlight or a sudden detail cut.
- Chất liệu: Damaged paper sleeves and damp flower stems echo the living shop without adding evidence of unseen graphic harm.
- Âm nổi: Muted wind through the broken door and a cautious footstep replace the former welcoming bell.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the audience search the space with him, then stop on the found strip before recovery begins next shot.

**Clip 2 · CUT · FLOWER · I**
- Đầu: Sơn's right glove beside the broken latch.
- Hành động: He removes the glove once and frees only the small torn strip.
- Thoại: Sơn, ngoài khung hình: Hạ để lại cái này.
- Cuối: Bare right hand holding the short scarf fragment.

- Ý đồ: Distinguish the recovered short fragment from the larger scarf so the later threat has physical meaning.
- Bố cục: Hold the detail frame on latch, right glove and the small attached strip, preserving their scale.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He removes only the right glove, stores it, and frees the short torn end without taking a full scarf.
- Diễn xuất: Bare fingertips become more tentative than the gloved approach; his spoken recognition should be almost involuntary.
- Điểm nét: Keep the torn edge and hand sharp, not an insert of Hạ's face or the watch interior.
- Chất liệu: Blue fibers catch on the already damaged latch; slight fraying matters more than a dramatic tear effect.
- Âm nổi: A soft fabric release and his off-screen name for the trace carry the beat, with room wind uninterrupted.
- Nhịp diễn: Take enough time to show what comes free, then leave the small piece clearly resting in his palm.

**Clip 3 · J · FLOWER · M**
- Đầu: Sơn near the broken doorway, a neighbor outside the frame.
- Hành động: He listens to the neighbor describe a black car and three men.
- Thoại: Người trong cảnh, ngoài khung hình: Cổ bị đưa đi. Còn quay lại nhìn cửa.
- Cuối: Sơn looking toward the tire tracks outside.

- Ý đồ: Let the witness offer facts while Sơn supplies the fear, preserving uncertainty about Hạ's fate.
- Bố cục: The medium setup stays on Sơn at the damaged doorway, neighbor speaking from the established off-screen side.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He listens toward the neighbor, then follows the described departure toward tire tracks outside with his gaze.
- Diễn xuất: His face should sharpen at the direction of the car, avoiding relief or certainty the testimony cannot support.
- Điểm nét: Hold his listening face and doorway relationship; do not invent a new neighbor portrait or readable tire-code close-up.
- Chất liệu: Moist pavement beyond the door provides a plausible trace direction while flower debris remains near his feet.
- Âm nổi: Keep the witness voice slightly outside and local, with no processed threatening effect or reconstructed abduction screams.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the factual sentence finish before his eyes move, so the direction feels learned rather than clairvoyant.

**Clip 4 · CUT · FLOWER · C**
- Đầu: Sơn's phone vibrating against his jacket.
- Hành động: He answers, hears Bà Hai claim Hạ has left him, ends the call.
- Thoại: Bà Hai, qua thiết bị: Về tiệm, má còn giữ chỗ cho con.
- Cuối: Phone lowered, Sơn's bare right hand steady again.

- Ý đồ: Make Bà Hai's familiar reassurance sound like a trap when Sơn can no longer accept it.
- Bố cục: Use the close-up to hold his face and phone edge without displaying the caller or an animated screen portrait.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He answers once, hears the invitation and lowers the phone after ending the call himself.
- Diễn xuất: His anger is controlled into a steadier bare right hand, not a shouted reply or a visible rage tremor.
- Điểm nét: Favor his eyes and the phone at a natural distance; keep any screen content unreadable and secondary.
- Chất liệu: Rain still clings to his suit edge, contrasting with the dry composure returning to his face.
- Âm nổi: The filtered familiar voice stays soft, then cuts cleanly to the damaged shop's real ambience.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow her sweetness to finish before he terminates the call, leaving the refusal as an action rather than extra dialogue.

**Clip 5 · L · SILK_STORE · T**
- Đầu: Rear stockroom door of the silk shop closed before opening hours.
- Hành động: Sơn uses his old key and reaches the shelf he once guarded.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn beside the stock shelf, black ledger and tin visible.

- Ý đồ: Make taking evidence a conscious alternative to obeying Bà Hai's invitation, using a place Sơn knows.
- Bố cục: Track along the rear stockroom entry toward the familiar shelf, keeping the route in one direction.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: His existing key opens the same rear access and remains with him as he reaches the shelf.
- Diễn xuất: His manner is purposeful but not excited; familiarity should reduce searching, making the choice feel morally deliberate.
- Điểm nét: Keep Sơn and the target shelf readable as he enters, rather than cutting to an unexplained object location.
- Chất liệu: Old latch wear and dust patterns indicate routine access, without adding a new hidden compartment or password cue.
- Âm nổi: One restrained latch sound gives way to morning city noise outside and his quiet steps inside.
- Nhịp diễn: Move directly to the known shelf; do not spend the full clip on a suspenseful unlock that solves no new problem.

**Clip 6 · CUT · SILK_STORE · M**
- Đầu: Ledger and sealed tin on the stockroom shelf.
- Hành động: Sơn takes the ledger, attached receipt and sealed tin into his bag.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Tôi lấy sổ của họ. Không lấy lại được cô ấy.
- Cuối: Shelf empty at those slots, bag clasp closed.

- Ý đồ: Pay off the origin of the evidence while letting Sơn admit that it cannot replace the missing person.
- Bố cục: The medium composition keeps ledger, receipt, sealed tin and open bag together at the known shelf.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He takes those existing items in one purposeful packing sequence and closes the clasp after the shelf slots empty.
- Diễn xuất: The narration carries regret rather than pride at a successful theft; hands remain disciplined through it.
- Điểm nét: Keep the difference between emptied shelf positions and packed objects understandable without individual hero inserts.
- Chất liệu: Black ledger leather, folded receipt and dented tin retain their distinct textures inside the plain canvas bag.
- Âm nổi: Use subdued object contacts and one final clasp, with no triumph cue or unshown alarm effect.
- Nhịp diễn: Do not pause to reintroduce each object; the empty slots and final closed bag complete the consequence.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Match the closed bag clasp to the sealed tin latch in the lamplit cabin. No second theft or opening of the tin.

### CẢNH 11: Bảy Ngày Không Chạy
*Thời gian truyện: D2 night*
Mây nhìn thấy nguy cơ, Sơn chấp nhận để lại một đường liên lạc.

**Câu hỏi:** Mây đang nhìn kẻ bỏ trốn hay người cố để mình được thấy?
**Móc vào:** Bức ảnh bị úp nhưng chiếc đồng hồ vẫn đóng.
**Điểm bẻ:** Sơn bật đèn cho người quan sát thấy anh không bỏ đi.
**Gieo / trả:** Ánh đèn được dùng như hành vi chấp nhận giám sát, không giả làm tín hiệu bí mật mới.

**Clip 1 · MATCH · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: Closed tin beside the dispatch receipt under lamplight.
- Hành động: Sơn puts a pencil mark at the last shipping date, leaving the tin sealed.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Chuyến hàng đi rồi, dấu vết cũng đi.
- Cuối: Receipt weighted flat by the sealed tin.

- Ý đồ: Revisit the shipping date as a decision to act on it, not a repeated explanation of the same clue.
- Bố cục: Match the bag-clasp geometry into the existing sealed tin and receipt under the cabin lamp.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn makes one small pencil mark at the final date and weights the paper with the still-sealed tin.
- Diễn xuất: The marking pressure is firmer than the earlier tracing, indicating commitment rather than a new discovery.
- Điểm nét: Keep pencil, final box and tin edge at the same stable detail depth; handwritten words stay unreadable.
- Chất liệu: Warm lamplight catches the paper crease while the closed tin remains a dull, practical weight.
- Âm nổi: Pencil drag and tin settling replace the earlier USB tape sounds, with night pine wind underneath.
- Nhịp diễn: Make the marking brief and the resulting weighted paper clear, leaving the voice-over no reason to repeat all seven boxes.

**Clip 2 · CUT · CABIN · M**
- Đầu: Sơn seated with a loose photograph of Hạ from his wallet.
- Hành động: He places the photograph face down when a distant motorcycle stops.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Loose photograph face down, Sơn looking toward the window.

- Ý đồ: Show Sơn guarding a private attachment when he suspects observation, without revealing the watch early.
- Bố cục: The medium view holds his face, loose wallet portrait and cabin window within the same quiet arrangement.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He turns the separate photograph face down once when the distant motorcycle stops, then looks toward the window.
- Diễn xuất: The concealment is protective and ashamed rather than evidence-destroying; his face changes before the hand finishes.
- Điểm nét: Keep the loose photograph clearly outside any watch, with his eyeline toward the window readable.
- Chất liệu: The portrait's thin paper edge and worn wallet distinguish it from the heavy closed silver case.
- Âm nổi: A faint paper touch follows the distant engine settling; the silent present-day watch adds no tick.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the outside sound motivate the turn, then stay with the changed attention instead of flipping the photo again.

**Clip 3 · CUT · DUNE · O**
- Đầu: Mây behind a pine trunk overlooking the lit cabin.
- Hành động: She notices a dark case edge in the beam recess through the window and records the hiding place as a suspicion.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Binoculars lowered, Mây recording the observation.

- Ý đồ: Make Mây's observation a plausible suspicion about a hiding place, preserving uncertainty and investigative discipline.
- Bố cục: Use the shoulder perspective from behind the pine, with the cabin window occupying a small believable sightline.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She observes through binoculars, lowers them and notes the beam recess rather than seeing through solid wood.
- Diễn xuất: Her expression should acknowledge incompleteness; she records carefully instead of reacting as though the case contents are proven.
