- Điểm nét: Keep Mây and her sightline coherent, not a magical sharp zoom from distant tree to concealed interior.
- Chất liệu: Pine bark and needles interrupt the foreground; warm cabin light reaches only the parts actually exposed.
- Âm nổi: Keep near wind and the quiet notebook movement, with no distant interior sound magically audible to her.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the observation take time, but end on the note that distinguishes seeing from assuming.

**Clip 4 · J · DUNE · M**
- Đầu: Thành joining Mây below the ridge.
- Hành động: He hands her confirmation that the black car belongs to the dock company.
- Thoại: Thành (@Phó Thành), nói trong hình: Xe cùng công ty trên tờ giao hàng.
- Cuối: Company report in Mây's hand, cabin light behind them.

- Ý đồ: Use Thành's report to tighten the connection between the visitors and shipping evidence, without another revelation speech.
- Bố cục: Frame both below the ridge, keeping the cabin light in the background as a steady reference.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Thành hands the existing confirmation to Mây; she receives it without turning openly toward the cabin.
- Diễn xuất: He delivers the finding low and efficiently, while her response is a silent recalibration rather than astonishment.
- Điểm nét: Hold their faces and paper handover at shared depth, avoiding a new company-logo insert.
- Chất liệu: Paper edges catch the cabin glow; pine shadow remains irregular and natural around their shoulders.
- Âm nổi: Their restrained voices sit close under the pine wind, with no phone chime announcing the report.
- Nhịp diễn: One clear handover and a short processing pause are enough; let the light behind them retain the unresolved subject.

**Clip 5 · CUT · CABIN · C**
- Đầu: Sơn at the cabin window with the watch lid still closed.
- Hành động: He senses the observer, switches on the porch light and steps slightly back within the visible window sightline.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Tôi ở đây. Không đi đâu.
- Cuối: Porch lit, Sơn visible inside but no longer at the window.

- Ý đồ: Make Sơn's response to being watched an offer to be seen rather than another attempt to hide.
- Bố cục: Use the close-up from the established window side, keeping the closed watch and his face related.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He switches on the porch light, then moves back far enough to be visible inside without standing in the window.
- Diễn xuất: His quiet statement sounds addressed to whoever is outside, not a boast of superior awareness.
- Điểm nét: Favor his face and closed lid, allowing the new porch glow to change the background rather than the lens.
- Chất liệu: Warm light finds the weathered frame and jacket shoulder while the watch interior remains concealed.
- Âm nổi: A small switch click and ordinary porch-fixture sound accompany his quietly visible sentence, then pine wind persists.
- Nhịp diễn: Switch, step back and wait form one answer; do not repeatedly scan the tree line or approach the window again.

**Clip 6 · L · DUNE · W**
- Đầu: Mây and Thành below the newly lit porch.
- Hành động: Mây folds her notebook and orders the suspect approached in daylight.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Sáng mai hỏi thẳng. Đừng để dân tới gần.
- Cuối: Both moving down the dune path away from the cabin.

- Ý đồ: Let Mây respond to the offered visibility by choosing timing and public safety, not an impulsive raid.
- Bố cục: Use the wide view below the porch to hold both officers and the now-lit cabin in a single geography.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She folds the notebook once, gives the instruction and leads Thành down the existing path.
- Diễn xuất: Her restraint is an active choice; Thành follows promptly instead of asking a second explanatory question.
- Điểm nét: Keep moving figures and porch light readable without an extra long-lens view through the cabin window.
- Chất liệu: Night sand and pine shadows make their safe descent tangible, with the cabin remaining a steady fixed point.
- Âm nổi: Her line stays low but clear over footsteps shifting on sand; no tactical radio burst is invented.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the instruction finish before the descent carries them away, preserving the porch light as an unresolved last presence.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Carry pine wind across the night-to-morning cut, with a clear daylight change. The third mark is a new action.

### CẢNH 12: Rừng Dương
*Thời gian truyện: D3 morning*
Sơn chống phục kích, cứu người sau khi thuốc gây bất động.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn sẽ cứu chính người vừa đuổi bắt mình không?
**Móc vào:** Dấu chân mới còn dẫn ra cửa sau.
**Điểm bẻ:** Một pha tự vệ trở thành lựa chọn gọi cứu người và đứng lại.
**Gieo / trả:** Phản ứng Tê buộc có bác sĩ, đồng thời chứng minh Sơn có thể ngừng ra tay thêm.

**Clip 1 · OPEN · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: Two marks on the frame, sunlight just reaching the wood.
- Hành động: Sơn makes the third mark, hears footsteps and leaves by the rear door.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Three marks, rear door moving closed behind him.

- Ý đồ: Turn the familiar morning tally into an alarmed departure without replaying earlier carving beats.
- Bố cục: The detail setup holds two existing marks and the fresh third, with rear-door direction at the frame edge.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn finishes the third tally, registers approaching footsteps and leaves by the existing rear exit.
- Diễn xuất: The tool hand becomes economical, not panicked; the choice of exit should show practiced awareness.
- Điểm nét: Keep the count exact and readable as cuts in wood, without showing pursuers in a second angle.
- Chất liệu: Fresh shavings catch raking light while the rear door moves naturally in the pine breeze.
- Âm nổi: Footsteps arrive over the last short scrape, then the rear latch and retreating step change the sound direction.
- Nhịp diễn: Do not prolong the carving after the threat is heard; the completed mark stays as proof he just left.

**Clip 2 · CUT · CABIN · W**
- Đầu: Empty cabin interior, front door pushed inward by two unnamed men.
- Hành động: They search the empty bed and see fresh footprints leading out back.
- Thoại: Người trong cảnh, nói trong hình: Nó vừa ra sau.
- Cuối: Both intruders turning toward the rear door, cabin otherwise empty.

- Ý đồ: Let the intruders arrive a beat too late, making the empty cabin itself the suspenseful subject.
- Bố cục: Use the wide interior setup with front entrance, bed and rear exit all spatially readable.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: The two unnamed men search the bed, discover fresh footprints and turn together toward the back.
- Diễn xuất: They should be wary and impatient rather than inept; no named lead secretly lingers in the room.
- Điểm nét: Keep the room geography clear, with only enough footprint detail to explain their turn.
- Chất liệu: Rumpled empty bedding and disturbed sand are existing traces, not added weapons, photographs or hidden traps.
- Âm nổi: Front door movement, bed fabric and their short line sit above the wind through the open exit.
- Nhịp diễn: Search briefly, find one direction, then turn; avoid a complete repetitive ransacking montage inside a single clip.

**Clip 3 · CUT · DUNE · H**
- Đầu: One pursuer stepping around a pine, Sơn behind the trunk.
- Hành động: Sơn takes his balance and pins his weapon hand without striking his head.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Bỏ xuống.
- Cuối: Pursuer seated against the trunk, Sơn holding the wrist away.

- Ý đồ: Show Sơn choosing control over injury, while retaining the physical difficulty of stopping a pursuer.
- Bố cục: The restrained handheld medium-wide keeps pine trunk, both bodies and the weapon hand readable together.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He uses the trunk-side position to take balance and hold the reaching wrist away from both faces.
- Diễn xuất: His breath tightens with effort; the pursuer resists instead of instantly becoming a limp stunt prop.
- Điểm nét: Hold the full defensive relation from one side, with no close-up describing a damaging joint technique.
- Chất liệu: Sand shifts under their feet and rough bark catches clothing, grounding weight without graphic injury.
- Âm nổi: Short breaths, fabric tension and sand displacement replace exaggerated impact booms under his instruction.
- Nhịp diễn: Make contact and controlled descent one short struggle, then keep the restrained posture alive with visible effort.

**Clip 4 · CONTINUE · DUNE · H**
- Đầu: Pursuer seated against the trunk, Sơn holding the wrist away.
- Hành động: The second pursuer lunges with a fictional Tê applicator, Sơn deflects the arm.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Second man stumbling into sand, applicator out of his grasp.

- Ý đồ: Escalate from a controlled restraint to an unpredictable second threat, not a display of effortless mastery.
- Bố cục: Preserve the same handheld setup, axis and existing body positions at the seam as the second man enters.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn reacts to the new reaching arm while staying aware of the first pursuer, ending with the device out of grasp.
- Diễn xuất: Show surprise in his breath and shoulders; the second man overcommits rather than politely waiting for a move.
- Điểm nét: Retain the preceding useful depth and whole-arm readability, without a new device-application close-up or rack.
- Chất liệu: Loose sand and suitless field-jacket fabric carry the abrupt loss of balance through ordinary materials.
- Âm nổi: A scuff, strained exhale and small object landing should dominate; no supernatural pulse announces Tê.
- Nhịp diễn: The new attack interrupts the held control, then the stumble ends the beat without repeating the previous takedown.

**Clip 5 · CUT · DUNE · M**
- Đầu: Second man motionless but visibly breathing, first man still conscious.
- Hành động: Sơn checks the fallen man's breathing and calls toward the road for help.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Có người cần bác sĩ. Đồi dương.
- Cuối: Sơn kneeling beside the breathing man, phone at his ear.

- Ý đồ: Make the decisive moral action a call for help after the opponent has become vulnerable.
- Bố cục: The medium view includes Sơn kneeling, the fallen man's breathing torso and the conscious man kept separate.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn checks for visible breathing, keeps his distance from the dropped device and calls toward the known road.
- Diễn xuất: His urgency is practical concern rather than guilt performed at camera; he does not admire the result.
- Điểm nét: Keep Sơn's mouth and the breathing patient readable, without graphic medical inspection or instructional detail.
- Chất liệu: Sand clings to clothing and the pine shadows continue moving, preventing the fallen figure from reading as dead.
- Âm nổi: His clear request is close above breath and wind; no pulse monitor or clinic equipment is heard here.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the breathing observation a beat before the call, so care motivates the words rather than following them decoratively.

**Clip 6 · J · DUNE · W**
- Đầu: Mây and Thành arriving with a stretcher at the tree line.
- Hành động: Sơn steps away with empty hands, Mây takes control of the two men.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Anh ở lại. Tôi cần nghe hết.
- Cuối: Mây by the stretcher, Sơn standing where she can see him.

- Ý đồ: End the action on Sơn accepting scrutiny, making cooperation a cost he consciously pays.
- Bố cục: Use the wide tree-line view to connect stretcher arrival, the two attackers and Sơn's empty hands.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He steps back once to give the stretcher room and stays where Mây can supervise him.
- Diễn xuất: Mây's command is firm but not triumphant; Sơn obeys without a defiant stare or another attempted escape.
- Điểm nét: Hold the whole rescue arrangement clear, with no cut to a face that loses the patient positions.
- Chất liệu: Stretcher fabric, sand and field clothing stay practical; the morning sun does not become emergency-colored lighting.
- Âm nổi: Approaching steps and stretcher handling merge with Mây's line, with the breathing thread ready for the clinic cut.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the rescue movement continue while Sơn becomes still by choice, avoiding a victory pose or frozen group tableau.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Carry the fallen man breathing under the cut to the clinic. No replay of the fall or stretcher arrival.

### CẢNH 13: Phòng Khám
*Thời gian truyện: D3 noon*
Bác sĩ xác định dấu hiệu Tê, Mây có vật chứng thay vì đoán bằng mặt.

**Câu hỏi:** Mây có thể tin điều nào giữa lời đồn Bóng Ma và người còn thở?
**Móc vào:** Chưa hỏi cung vội khi một bệnh nhân vẫn bất động.
**Điểm bẻ:** Một nghi ngờ về thuốc thành vật chứng, một biệt danh thành người giữ sổ.
**Gieo / trả:** Linh loại bỏ giờ tỉnh chính xác, Mây tách quan sát khỏi kết luận và giữ chứng cứ.

**Clip 1 · L · CLINIC · W**
- Đầu: Two attackers on separate beds, one sitting up under supervision.
- Hành động: Linh checks the immobile man's breathing while Mây records the time.
- Thoại: Bác sĩ Linh (@Bác sĩ Linh), nói trong hình: Còn thở. Chưa được rời mắt.
- Cuối: Linh at bedside, Thành watching the conscious attacker.

- Ý đồ: Make care precede interrogation so the clinic scene changes the meaning of the preceding violence.
- Bố cục: The wide view distinguishes two beds, Linh at the immobile man and Thành watching the conscious one.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Linh stays beside visible breathing while Mây records the observation, keeping access around the bed clear.
- Diễn xuất: His line is calm practical attention; Mây absorbs the need for patience rather than demanding instant answers.
- Điểm nét: Keep both patient positions and the observing people legible, without an invented monitor display or intrusive medical close-up.
- Chất liệu: Green curtain folds and worn steel beds give the room lived-in restraint, not a glossy emergency ward.
- Âm nổi: Continue the breathing thread from the dune into quiet ventilation and Linh's measured voice.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow a short observation before the line, then hold ongoing care rather than a dramatic pronouncement of survival.

**Clip 2 · CUT · CLINIC · I**
- Đầu: Seized applicator sealed on a tray, Linh beside it.
- Hành động: He compares its unusual cap to a reference case photograph he kept.
- Thoại: Bác sĩ Linh, ngoài khung hình: Tôi từng gặp loại Tê này.
- Cuối: Two matching cap shapes, both separate from the patient.

- Ý đồ: Make recognition of Tê depend on a remembered physical match, not instant laboratory omniscience.
- Bố cục: Use the locked detail setup to show sealed device, reference photograph and comparing hand together.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Linh positions the existing photograph beside the sealed object without opening, testing or demonstrating it.
- Diễn xuất: His fingers pause at the cap shape with cautious recognition, not certainty about every medical consequence.
- Điểm nét: Keep the two relevant shapes in one clear plane, the patient outside this object-comparison frame.
- Chất liệu: Transparent evidence wrapping slightly dulls the device surface, distinguishing a secured exhibit from something ready to use.
- Âm nổi: A tray touch and photograph scrape sit under the off-screen line; no microscope, scanner or chemical reaction is introduced.
- Nhịp diễn: Compare once, then let the uncertainty remain in the still unopened object for the following explanation.

**Clip 3 · CUT · CLINIC · M**
- Đầu: Mây facing Linh beside the closed treatment curtain.
- Hành động: He explains that recovery varies and requires monitoring, not an exact timer.
- Thoại: Bác sĩ Linh (@Bác sĩ Linh), nói trong hình: Đừng tin ai hẹn đúng phút tỉnh.
- Cuối: Mây crossing out her tentative time estimate.

- Ý đồ: Protect future suspense by refusing an exact recovery clock while keeping the doctor useful.
- Bố cục: Frame Mây and Linh beside the closed curtain, with her note-taking hand visible in the medium shot.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Linh answers without a demonstration; Mây crosses out only her tentative estimate and listens through the rest.
- Diễn xuất: His firmness should be weary experience rather than a lecture, and her revision an acceptance of limits.
- Điểm nét: Keep their faces at the same readable depth; the crossed-out note remains a shape rather than an exposed medical number.
- Chất liệu: The green curtain shifts gently beside them while steady practical light keeps skin tones neutral.
- Âm nổi: His measured voice sits over the ordinary clinic bed; do not add a countdown or rising pulse effect.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the warning enough room to be understood, then end on Mây changing the note instead of repeating the rule.

**Clip 4 · CUT · CLINIC · M**
- Đầu: Thành beside the conscious attacker, Mây holding the evidence bag.
- Hành động: The attacker recognizes the shipping emblem and stops answering.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Ai đưa các anh tới đây?
- Cuối: Attacker looking away from the bag, Thành noting the reaction.

- Ý đồ: Let a suspect's silence become evidence of recognition without pretending it is a full confession.
- Bố cục: Use the medium framing to retain Mây's speaking face, Thành and the conscious attacker within supervised space.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây shows the existing bag at the man's eyeline; Thành watches him turn away rather than pushing him upright.
- Diễn xuất: Mây asks plainly; the attacker stops answering through a tightened mouth, not a villainous laugh.
- Điểm nét: Keep the questioner and the turning face readable, with the emblem secondary and never legibly lettered.
- Chất liệu: The sealed bag catches plain window light; sheets remain disturbed from treatment, not staged like an interrogation set.
- Âm nổi: A slight wrapping rustle leads into the question, after which the man's withheld answer is allowed to be silent.
- Nhịp diễn: Avoid another forced question in the spare seconds; let Thành's quiet noting of the response close the beat.

**Clip 5 · CUT · CLINIC · C**
- Đầu: The conscious attacker off screen, Mây listening without interrupting.
- Hành động: He lets slip Sơn's nickname, Mây closes the evidence folder.
- Thoại: Người trong cảnh, ngoài khung hình: Bóng Ma giữ sổ. Dũng cần sổ.
- Cuối: Mây's closed folder resting against her chest.

- Ý đồ: Let an off-screen slip of the nickname redirect Mây toward the person with the ledger.
- Bố cục: The close-up stays on Mây listening, folder held low enough that its closure is visible.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She hears the slip without interrupting and closes the existing folder only after the important words.
- Diễn xuất: Her face narrows into a decision, not a sudden gasp; she does not mouth the off-screen man's sentence.
- Điểm nét: Hold the existing close-up plane on her eyes and speaking-rest mouth, with no reaction insert of the suspect.
- Chất liệu: Plain folder paper and her sun-lined face remain unadorned under the clinic's ordinary light.
- Âm nổi: The suspect's off-screen voice is weak but intelligible, then a single folder closure settles the information.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the voice finish first; her delayed closure makes the finding feel processed rather than announced by a cut.

**Clip 6 · L · POST · M**
- Đầu: Mây returning to the station, Thành at the support radio.
- Hành động: She requests a district team to protect the town and heads up the hill.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Giữ người và vật chứng. Tôi gặp Sơn.
- Cuối: Thành confirming the support request, Mây leaving with the folder.

- Ý đồ: Show that seeking Sơn does not mean abandoning the people and evidence already in custody.
- Bố cục: Frame Mây, Thành, support radio and the departing route in the same medium station setup.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She assigns custody and town protection before taking the folder and leaving toward the hill.
- Diễn xuất: Her authority is clear and workmanlike; Thành's acknowledgement should be competent rather than needlessly anxious.
- Điểm nét: Keep her mouth and Thành's radio hand readable, without cutting to an unseen superior or adding another officer portrait.
- Chất liệu: Daylight across the desk and used folder edges retain continuity with this modest station's earlier checks.
- Âm nổi: A quiet radio confirmation follows her assignment, never competing as a second full spoken exchange.
- Nhịp diễn: Finish the practical instruction before the departure, so the scene feels like a completed delegation rather than a cliffhanger trick.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Station radio fades into porch wind. Cut to Mây joining the waiting Sơn and placing her cuffs once.

### CẢNH 14: Còng Và Bằng Chứng
*Thời gian truyện: D3 afternoon*
Sơn đưa bằng chứng kiểm chứng được, Mây nhận ra Hạ qua ảnh rời.

**Câu hỏi:** Mây có thể hợp tác với một người biết chuyện Hạ mà không bỏ luật?
**Móc vào:** Hai vật có thể còng người và chứng minh lời nói nằm giữa hai ly trà.
**Điểm bẻ:** Chứng cứ tạo hợp tác, ảnh rời biến vụ án thành chuyện của chính Mây.
**Gieo / trả:** Không mở đồng hồ để gây nhận diện; cú thấy ảnh cần im đủ lâu trước câu hỏi tiếp.

**Clip 1 · CUT · PORCH · M**
- Đầu: Two tea glasses on the porch table, Sơn already seated.
- Hành động: Mây sits opposite and places her cuffs beside her notebook.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Anh là người họ gọi Bóng Ma?
- Cuối: Cuffs between the glasses, neither person touching them.

- Ý đồ: Make the negotiation's moral boundary visible before either person asks for trust.
- Bố cục: The medium table setup holds two tea glasses and space where Mây's cuffs enter between them.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She sits opposite Sơn and places the cuffs once beside her notebook, leaving both untouched afterward.
- Diễn xuất: Her question is direct without provocation; Sơn acknowledges the name through attention, not a smug pose.
- Điểm nét: Keep their faces and the object boundary at readable shared depth, with no need for a separate symbolic insert.
- Chất liệu: The cuffs' dull metal and tea-glass warmth coexist on weathered porch wood under the same late sun.
- Âm nổi: The modest cuff contact should be clear but not amplified like a weapon being cocked.
- Nhịp diễn: Place, ask and wait are distinct beats; neither reaches for the cups to manufacture activity during the answer space.

**Clip 2 · CUT · PORCH · I**
- Đầu: Sơn's closed bag on the table, Mây's evidence folder beside it.
- Hành động: He removes the black ledger and USB, places both within her reach.
- Thoại: Sơn, ngoài khung hình: Đối chiếu công ty đó với sổ này.
- Cuối: Ledger and USB beside Mây's folder, Sơn's hands withdrawn.

- Ý đồ: Let Sơn offer something independently checkable, shifting from evasion toward responsibility.
- Bố cục: The detail shot keeps his bag, Mây's folder and the arriving ledger and USB physically separate.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He takes both items out, places them within her reach and withdraws his hands instead of guiding hers.
- Diễn xuất: Express the risk in the deliberate withdrawal; his off-screen sentence is an invitation to verify, not a demand for belief.
- Điểm nét: Keep the black cover and drive identifiable at one stable plane, without opening an extra screen or revealing readable accounts.
- Chất liệu: Canvas, worn leather and evidence-folder paper have distinct surfaces that preserve each object's origin.
- Âm nổi: Two restrained set-down sounds precede his low line; porch wind continues without a revelation sting.
- Nhịp diễn: Make the offer clean and singular, ending with empty hands outside the evidence area rather than another explanatory tap.

**Clip 3 · CUT · PORCH · M**
- Đầu: Mây checking an entry against the vehicle report.
- Hành động: She finds the matching dispatch company, takes a photograph of the page.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Chuyện này tôi sẽ báo lên.
- Cuối: Mây's phone above the matching entry, Sơn waiting.

- Ý đồ: Give Mây a small verified success before the personal shock arrives, preserving her professional basis for cooperation.
- Bố cục: Use the medium composition to hold her face, existing vehicle report and the open ledger comparison.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She matches one company entry, photographs that page once and keeps her attention on the evidence.
- Diễn xuất: Her line conveys procedure, not a promise of protection; Sơn waits without trying to charm or hurry her.
- Điểm nét: Keep face and page area readable, no legible company name required, and keep the phone screen secondary.
- Chất liệu: Late light across paper grain and the black cover contrasts with the cooler shade under her arm.
- Âm nổi: A soft phone shutter may follow the actual photograph, while her voice remains the clearest sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Let verification precede the sentence so it answers a real discovery rather than simply narrating the hand movement.

**Clip 4 · CONTINUE · PORCH · M**
- Đầu: Mây's phone above the matching entry, Sơn waiting.
- Hành động: Sơn agrees to remain available and names the final shipment.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Tôi khai. Nhưng còn người bị giữ.
- Cuối: Mây writing the deadline, Sơn no longer hiding his bag.

- Ý đồ: Let Sơn accept cooperation but expose the unresolved human stake behind the financial evidence.
- Bố cục: Preserve the same medium composition, table orientation and focus depth from Mây's verification.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn leaves the bag open and agrees to stay available while Mây notes the final departure.
- Diễn xuất: He should sound more exposed when mentioning the held person than when admitting the ledger is his.
- Điểm nét: Keep both eyelines and note-taking hand within the prior useful depth, without a new document insert.
- Chất liệu: The open bag no longer shields the book, creating a subtle material difference from earlier concealment.
- Âm nổi: His line has room above porch wind; Mây's writing begins after the key admission instead of scratching through it.
- Nhịp diễn: End on her recording the deadline and his cessation of concealment, not on another attempt to explain the same shipping schedule.

**Clip 5 · CUT · PORCH · I**
- Đầu: Sơn sliding Hạ's loose photograph across the table.
- Hành động: Mây recognizes the face and holds the photograph by its white edge.
- Thoại: Mây, ngoài khung hình: Hạ. Bạn hồi nhỏ của tôi.
- Cuối: Hạ's loose portrait in Mây's hand, watch nowhere opened.

- Ý đồ: Make Mây's recognition land through touch and hesitation while keeping the watch's future reveal intact.
- Bố cục: Use the detail frame on the loose portrait crossing wood into Mây's hand, separate from any silver case.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She catches only the white photograph edge, stopping the slide without covering Hạ's face.
- Diễn xuất: Her fingers momentarily firm at the edge; the off-screen recognition is quieter than a shouted shock.
- Điểm nét: Keep the printed face and holding fingertips at one plane, never spawning Hạ physically beside the table.
- Chất liệu: The portrait's worn white border and matte surface distinguish it from a glowing phone or watch interior.
- Âm nổi: Paper drag stops abruptly enough to register, then the short name lands against continuous sea wind.
- Nhịp diễn: Let her hand stop the photograph before the name comes; avoid a second photograph pass or an added flashback inside this clip.

**Clip 6 · CUT · PORCH · C**
- Đầu: Mây looking up from the photograph at Sơn.
- Hành động: She asks where Hạ is, Sơn has to hold her gaze.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Anh kể từ đầu.
- Cuối: Mây waiting, Sơn unable to lower his eyes.

- Ý đồ: Turn professional cooperation into personal accountability without letting either person escape the moment.
- Bố cục: The close-up favors Mây's visible speaking face, with Sơn only as the established near edge or receiving profile.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She lifts her gaze from the loose photograph and waits for Sơn to meet it.
- Diễn xuất: The question carries restrained hurt rather than immediate tears; his refusal to look away is the answer before words.
- Điểm nét: Keep Mây's eyes and mouth clear, with no sudden rack to the portrait or a second close-up of Sơn.
- Chất liệu: Late porch light remains unchanged, refusing to turn the discovery into an artificial lighting event.
- Âm nổi: Her concise instruction is followed by unfilled wind and breath, no music instructing the audience to grieve.
- Nhịp diễn: Stay longer on the held gaze after the line than on the line itself, then let the past answer through the next cut.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: A ferry horn enters while Mây waits for an answer. Cut to Hạ alive at the city pier, not to a second photo reveal.

### CẢNH 15: Quá Khứ: Đường Về
*Thời gian truyện: PAST one month earlier evening*
Hạ gieo kiến thức hải đăng và mục tiêu mở sạp hoa.

**Câu hỏi:** Hạ thật sự muốn cùng Sơn đi đâu nếu anh không còn làm nghề?
**Móc vào:** Ở bến xa quê, cô tả cửa về chứ không kể định mệnh.
**Điểm bẻ:** Cô nói một đời sống cụ thể và chủ động kéo anh khỏi hướng chuyến tàu.
**Gieo / trả:** Cửa kỹ thuật và đèn dự phòng gieo hữu cơ qua nghề của cha, trả ở 45–51.

**Clip 1 · J · CITY_PIER · W**
- Đầu: Hạ walking along the city pier, Sơn beside her.
- Hành động: She stops at a ferry's distant light and names her home town.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Hòn Sóng. Nhà em ở đó.
- Cuối: Both facing the water, ferry light far beyond.

- Ý đồ: Let Hạ introduce her own home as a desired future, not simply information Sơn will later use.
- Bố cục: The wide pier view gives both walkers and the distant ferry one shared direction across open water.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She stops at the rail to name the town; he stops because she does, not because of a target.
- Diễn xuất: Her tone warms at the name, while his attention turns from the ferry toward her face.
- Điểm nét: Keep the two figures and ferry light readable at wide depth without a separate scenic insert.
- Chất liệu: Warm pier lamps meet the last daylight, leaving ordinary rail wear and water movement visible.
- Âm nổi: A low ferry horn stays distant enough to support, not overwhelm, her simple naming of home.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow walking to settle before the name, letting the pause feel like memory rather than a forced plot clue.

**Clip 2 · CUT · CITY_PIER · M**
- Đầu: Hạ drawing a round tower in condensation on a railing panel.
- Hành động: She sketches a small maintenance hatch below the gallery.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Ba em giữ đèn. Em thuộc từng cửa.
- Cuối: Her fingertip at the hatch sketch, Sơn watching.

- Ý đồ: Make knowledge of the lighthouse arise from a child's familiarity with a parent's work.
- Bố cục: The medium composition keeps Hạ's face and the existing misted glass panel within the same reach.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She draws the rough tower and hatch from habit while Sơn follows her finger, no blueprint performance.
- Diễn xuất: Her ease should be affectionate and slightly amused at the specificity; he listens without treating it as tactical briefing.
- Điểm nét: Keep the finger sketch and faces within useful medium depth, never changing to a revealing macro map.
- Chất liệu: Moisture smears under her fingertip, making the rough little hatch imperfect and temporary rather than diagrammatically precise.
- Âm nổi: A soft fingertip drag and open-air water bed support her voice, with no ominous clue emphasis.
- Nhịp diễn: Let her drawing accompany the sentence, then stop before adding unnecessary arrows, routes or an invented floor plan.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · CITY_PIER · M**
- Đầu: Her fingertip at the hatch sketch, Sơn watching.
- Hành động: Sơn asks about the sea, she points to the town in an old photo.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Mất điện vẫn có đèn dự phòng.
- Cuối: Old lighthouse photo held between them, maintenance hatch visible.

- Ý đồ: Give the memory a real family object while keeping the future rescue knowledge secondary to intimacy.
- Bố cục: Hold the exact medium setup and established glass-panel relation as the existing photograph enters the shared plane.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ brings the old lighthouse photo between them near the earlier sketch, Sơn remaining beside her.
- Diễn xuất: She speaks from familiarity, not as if foreshadowing danger; his quiet attention shows respect for her ordinary history.
- Điểm nét: Retain the prior depth and face readability, letting the photo stay recognizable without a new sharp foreground rack.
- Chất liệu: The small photograph has worn edges and muted color, unlike the wet temporary finger sketch behind it.
- Âm nổi: Paper handling is slight beneath her line; the ferry and water remain at the same distance as before.
- Nhịp diễn: The new photo completes the thought once, then leaves room for her actual wish rather than dwelling on the hatch.

**Clip 4 · CONTINUE · CITY_PIER · M**
- Đầu: Old lighthouse photo held between them, maintenance hatch visible.
- Hành động: Hạ puts the photograph away and names the life she wants.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Em muốn mở sạp hoa cạnh chợ cá.
- Cuối: Her empty hands resting on the rail, Sơn beside her.

- Ý đồ: Let Hạ name a life she wants for herself, giving her more than a role as Sơn's escape fantasy.
- Bố cục: Keep the same medium composition and focus plane while the photograph is put away within existing reach.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She pockets the photograph and rests both empty hands on the rail, facing the town's imagined direction.
- Diễn xuất: Her wish sounds modest and practical, with a small trace of pleasure at describing it aloud.
- Điểm nét: Maintain the same readable faces and hands, without a scenic cutaway illustrating a shop that does not yet exist.
- Chất liệu: Her pale scarf and worn rail catch soft light; the open hands replace the earlier information-bearing photo.
- Âm nổi: Her sentence stays close against ordinary harbour sound, avoiding a sentimental swell that turns the wish into a prophecy.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the practical words finish before she looks at Sơn, giving him a chance to meet the life she has described.

**Clip 5 · CUT · CITY_PIER · C**
- Đầu: Hạ with her pale-blue scarf loose at her neck.
- Hành động: Wind lifts one end, Sơn catches it before it touches dirty water.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Scarf back in Hạ's hand, his gloved hand released.

- Ý đồ: Make a small protective reflex intimate without confusing it with ownership of the scarf.
- Bố cục: The close-up holds Hạ's face, the loose scarf end and Sơn's gloved hand within one plausible reach.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Wind lifts the existing scarf; Sơn catches its end and returns the cloth to her hand immediately.
- Diễn xuất: He is gentle without lingering possession, and her brief look acknowledges care rather than helplessness.
- Điểm nét: Keep the caught fabric and faces at useful close-up depth, never making the water an impossible sharp insert.
- Chất liệu: Blue silk moves lightly against black glove, its intact edge clear before the later torn-fragment scenes.
- Âm nổi: Use fabric movement over the steady pier wind, not a splash or dramatic catch sound that did not occur.
- Nhịp diễn: The catch is quick and the release unforced; let the moment after he lets go carry the intimacy.

**Clip 6 · CUT · CITY_PIER · W**
- Đầu: The two standing at the pier rail, a ship horn beginning.
- Hành động: Hạ starts walking back, Sơn chooses to follow her instead of the ferry.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Bạn em còn giữ chỗ đó không?
- Cuối: Both leaving left to right, open water behind.

- Ý đồ: End the memory on Sơn following a person's chosen direction instead of the working route in front of him.
- Bố cục: The wide pier setup keeps the rail, ferry direction and their left-to-right walking path easy to read.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ leaves first along the existing walkway; Sơn chooses to follow after one brief hesitation.
- Diễn xuất: His question should carry tentative interest in belonging, not a polished romantic line; she need not add a reply.
- Điểm nét: Keep both moving figures in the established wide depth, preserving departure direction without an extra face insert.
- Chất liệu: Water remains open behind them as their bodies leave the rail, warm pier lamps staying consistent.
- Âm nổi: The ferry horn begins at its real distance and can carry the edit, while footsteps remain light and ordinary.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the hesitation less time than the choice, ending on shared forward movement rather than another farewell pose.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Retain the city ferry horn briefly over the coastal lighthouse entrance, then change to local wind.

### CẢNH 16: Hải Đăng Và Một Câu Hỏi
*Thời gian truyện: D3 late afternoon*
Mây tìm chứng cứ sống và kiểm tra lối kỹ thuật trước cao trào.

**Câu hỏi:** Một giả định Hạ đã chết có chịu được câu hỏi của Mây không?
**Móc vào:** Chính nơi Hạ nhớ trở thành chỗ hai người phải đối diện điều chưa biết.
**Điểm bẻ:** Mây thay tuyệt vọng bằng việc tìm người sống và một bước kiểm tra hiện trường.
**Gieo / trả:** Lối phụ và thử đèn chỉ làm một lần, không nhấn như quảng cáo bí kíp cứu người.

**Clip 1 · L · LIGHTHOUSE_BASE · W**
- Đầu: Mây unlocking the lighthouse's lower iron door, Sơn behind her.
- Hành động: She shows him the old service route and starts up the stairs.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Hạ lớn lên ở đây.
- Cuối: Main door open, service hatch visible beside the stair base.

- Ý đồ: Make returning to Hạ's real place shift the investigation from stories to physical geography.
- Bố cục: The wide entrance view connects main door, first stair section and visible service route in one understandable arrangement.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây opens the lower door with the existing key, steps through and lets Sơn follow toward the stairs.
- Diễn xuất: She moves with local familiarity rather than performing a tour; his pace is attentive, not nostalgic ownership.
- Điểm nét: Keep both bodies and door relationships legible, without compressing the route into disconnected architectural inserts.
- Chất liệu: Rusted paint, worn stair edges and sea-blown grass mark a used public building, not a mystical ruin.
- Âm nổi: Key and iron latch introduce the local wind; the sentence stays practical above the threshold sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Finish entry and direction before the next gallery shot, leaving the door open where the endpoint requires it.

**Clip 2 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Both on the gallery, facing the houses below.
- Hành động: Mây identifies Hạ's family home without romanticizing Sơn's account.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Tôi chưa thấy nó về gần tháng.
- Cuối: Mây pointing down to the house, Sơn facing her.

- Ý đồ: Let Mây place Hạ's absence inside a real home and a real friendship, not just Sơn's grief.
- Bố cục: Use the medium gallery view to include both faces and her pointing direction toward the harbour houses.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây identifies the house once while staying on her side of the rail, then looks back toward Sơn.
- Diễn xuất: Her tone is matter-of-fact with held concern; his reaction should not claim he knows Hạ's home better than she does.
- Điểm nét: Keep faces primary and the house direction contextual, avoiding an impossible readable close-up of a distant address.
- Chất liệu: Salt-worn rail and low evening light hold the same coastal world, with no new memorial objects.
- Âm nổi: Wind remains present but controlled under her short factual line, with distant harbour sound barely defined.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the pointing gesture establish the fact, then spend the remainder on what the unanswered absence means between them.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Mây pointing down to the house, Sơn facing her.
- Hành động: Sơn admits he has only a threat and a torn piece of cloth.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Tôi tưởng họ đã giết cô ấy.
- Cuối: Mây looking directly at him, Sơn's hands empty on the rail.

- Ý đồ: Let Sơn state an assumption as his own fear rather than establishing Hạ's death as objective truth.
- Bố cục: Preserve the preceding medium setup, gallery positions and focus while the pointing hand lowers naturally.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He admits what he believed, hands empty on the rail, leaving Mây room to test it.
- Diễn xuất: His voice should be reluctant and ashamed, not a definitive narrator's verdict; she listens without accepting the premise.
- Điểm nét: Hold the same face-and-rail plane, keeping his unarmed hands readable without introducing a separate symbolic insert.
- Chất liệu: The rough rail presses into bare palms while wind moves the jacket, maintaining ordinary physical presence.
- Âm nổi: Leave a breath before the admission and no graveyard-like musical punctuation after the word describing his fear.
- Nhịp diễn: The admission slows the scene briefly, but its incompleteness should invite Mây's next practical question immediately.

**Clip 4 · CONTINUE · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Mây looking directly at him, Sơn's hands empty on the rail.
- Hành động: Mây refuses the assumption and asks for verifiable contact details.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Chưa có chứng cứ. Ta tìm người sống.
- Cuối: Sơn offering the phone, Mây taking it.

- Ý đồ: Make Mây's refusal of an unsupported conclusion feel like an act of care grounded in evidence.
- Bố cục: Retain the same medium camera, eyelines and depth at the continuation seam; do not reframe for an inspirational speech.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She asks for contact evidence, and Sơn offers the existing phone into her hand without resistance.
- Diễn xuất: Her line is firm and steady, carrying hope through a practical verb; his release of the phone accepts her method.
- Điểm nét: Keep both faces and the modest handover inside the established plane, without reading the phone screen.
- Chất liệu: The ordinary phone sits briefly against weathered rail and khaki sleeve, not a glowing new clue display.
- Âm nổi: Her short sentence rises clearly above wind; a soft phone-to-palm contact closes the beat.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the refusal come before the handover, so the phone feels like the consequence of changing his assumption.

**Clip 5 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE_SERVICE · I**
- Đầu: Mây at the labeled-in-shapes backup-light lever, no readable text.
- Hành động: She tests the relay once and confirms the lamp works, then switches it off.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Backup lamp dark again, lever pointing down.

- Ý đồ: Plant a working public fixture quietly, so its later use feels earned rather than magically discovered.
- Bố cục: Use the detail setup to show the existing lever, its shape cues and the relay housing together.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây tests the relay once, verifies operation through its real response, then returns the lever to the down position.
- Diễn xuất: Her hand is familiar and routine, not secretive; no face reaction should announce a future rescue device.
- Điểm nét: Keep lever and moving hand at one steady plane, with no cut to a new exterior lamp angle.
- Chất liệu: Old paint and slight metal wear make the mechanism look maintained enough to function without looking newly installed.
- Âm nổi: A modest relay click and brief fixture response are enough; do not add a heroic electrical surge.
- Nhịp diễn: On, confirmation, off form one simple test, ending clearly dark so the later switch has an unambiguous starting state.

**Clip 6 · L · LIGHTHOUSE_BASE · W**
- Đầu: Both already at the open lower doorway after the descent.
- Hành động: Mây locks the public entrance and keeps the service key accessible.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Đem USB tới đồn. Ngay tối nay.
- Cuối: Key clipped to Mây's belt, Sơn heading toward the station.

- Ý đồ: Close the visit with a concrete next investigative step, keeping the geography and key custody clear.
- Bố cục: The wide lower-door view shows both already descended, main path toward town and key at Mây's belt.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây locks the public entrance once, clips the key where it remains accessible and starts toward the station with Sơn.
- Diễn xuất: Her urgency is procedural rather than panic; he follows her direction without another speech about loss.
- Điểm nét: Hold door, figures and path at readable depth, avoiding a second climb or another gallery establishing shot.
- Chất liệu: The key's small metal shape contrasts with the old iron lock while daylight continues fading naturally.
- Âm nổi: A final lock contact and footsteps can yield to the incoming station fan, with her instruction clean above them.
- Nhịp diễn: Finish the lock before movement away, letting the spoken destination propel the next scene instead of repeating the visit's discoveries.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: The clipped service key settles, incoming station fan introduces the USB desk. Do not replay the backup-lamp test.

### CẢNH 17: Phần Dữ Liệu Bị Khóa
*Thời gian truyện: D3 night*
Bằng chứng có đường xác minh và hạn chuyển người cụ thể.

**Câu hỏi:** Dữ liệu đủ dẫn tới Hạ hay chỉ đủ dẫn tới một vụ khác?
**Móc vào:** USB gốc được cất trước khi mở bản làm việc.
**Điểm bẻ:** Một thư mục khóa biến hạn bảy ngày thành lịch chuyển người cụ thể.
**Gieo / trả:** Chữ ký, tuyến hàng, khóa token có nhiệm vụ khác nhau; kế vào thành phố xuất phát từ thiếu hụt cụ thể.

**Clip 1 · J · POST · W**
- Đầu: Three people around the station computer, USB on the desk.
- Hành động: Thành makes a working copy before opening the accounts.
- Thoại: Thành (@Phó Thành), nói trong hình: Giữ bản gốc. Em mở bản sao.
- Cuối: Original USB in evidence bag, copy connected to the computer.

- Ý đồ: Make the investigation's first digital step preserve trust in the evidence instead of treating a USB as magic.
- Bố cục: The wide desk setup gives each of the three a distinct place, with original and working drive separated.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Thành copies first and stores the original before proceeding, Mây watching custody and Sơn watching the screen.
- Diễn xuất: Thành's confidence comes from knowing his task; Sơn waits rather than taking over someone else's expertise.
- Điểm nét: Keep the small handover zone and three reactions legible without requiring readable filenames or desktop text.
- Chất liệu: An old fan and modest computer case keep the station practical, with evidence sleeve and drive visually distinct.
- Âm nổi: Keyboard, sleeve movement and fan are enough; the first line should be ordinary work rather than a hacking triumph.
- Nhịp diễn: Let preserving the original precede opening the accounts, establishing a procedure that the later discoveries can lean on.

**Clip 2 · CUT · POST · I**
- Đầu: Account folders open without readable text.
- Hành động: Thành matches the ledger dates to paid shipments at the river factory.
- Thoại: Thành, ngoài khung hình: Cùng xưởng. Cùng ngày chuyển tiền.
- Cuối: A matching sequence highlighted, ledger open beside the keyboard.

- Ý đồ: Let one matching sequence connect money to the factory, avoiding a stream of unfilmable numbers.
- Bố cục: Use the detail composition across ledger edge, keyboard and broad account layout without demanding legible text.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Thành aligns the existing dates and paid-shipment pattern, stopping on one correspondence rather than scrolling endlessly.
- Diễn xuất: His hands briefly slow at the match; the off-screen finding is restrained because more remains locked.
- Điểm nét: Hold the paper and screen relationship at usable depth, avoiding a fantastical sharp reflection of both in eyes.
- Chất liệu: Screen light remains modest on paper grain, with highlighted shapes distinct from the original handwriting texture.
- Âm nổi: A few real keystrokes stop before his sentence; do not add fabricated upload or access-granted alarms.
- Nhịp diễn: Reveal the correspondence once, then give the still-open ledger time to make the connection feel verified.

**Clip 3 · CUT · POST · M**
- Đầu: Mây looking from the matched shipments to Sơn.
- Hành động: He identifies Kiên as the payer named in the open accounts.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Kiên trả tiền. Bà Hai giữ đường hàng.
- Cuối: Mây separating Kiên's file from Bà Hai's file.

- Ý đồ: Separate Kiên's money from Bà Hai's logistics so the later conflict has identifiable interests.
- Bố cục: The medium view retains Mây's questioning eyeline toward Sơn and Thành close enough to hear.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn identifies the payer while Mây separates the existing file groups, making their relationship physical on the desk.
- Diễn xuất: He speaks as someone admitting insider knowledge, not delivering a detective's victorious explanation.
- Điểm nét: Keep the speaking face and two file positions readable without a new portrait or unreadable financial zoom.
- Chất liệu: Paper group edges and the black ledger maintain a concrete distinction between actors in the scheme.
- Âm nổi: His low line is clear over the same fan bed; no new villain theme begins for a name already heard.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the statement be brief and the file separation complete it, avoiding a second sentence restating the same money trail.

**Clip 4 · CUT · POST · I**
- Đầu: A locked dispatch folder beside an open weekly schedule.
- Hành động: They establish the final ship departure is day seven at night, while the passenger manifest remains locked.
- Thoại: Mây, ngoài khung hình: Ngày bảy. Phải mở được phần khóa trước đó.
- Cuối: Mây circling the last departure on her paper copy.

- Ý đồ: Make the locked dispatch detail a remaining obstacle, preserving the later proof of Hạ's transfer.
- Bố cục: The detail setup holds the locked-folder shape beside the open weekly schedule and Mây's paper copy.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They identify the final ship departure, and Mây circles that box without pretending the sealed manifest is open.
- Diễn xuất: Her hand pauses at the locked portion, showing the limit of what they know rather than certainty about Hạ's name.
- Điểm nét: Keep only the broad schedule and lock relationship clear; no readable passenger list or premature Hạ portrait belongs here.
- Chất liệu: A single pencil circle contrasts with the screen's inaccessible folder shape, grounding the unresolved information.
- Âm nổi: Paper and one key press sit beneath the off-screen line, with no countdown sound or false decryption chime.
- Nhịp diễn: The deadline should tighten the problem, then end on what still cannot be read rather than a complete solution.

**Clip 5 · CUT · POST · M**
- Đầu: Sơn recognizing a small token icon in the locked folder.
- Hành động: He describes the hardware key kept beside the red original ledger.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Khóa trong két. Cạnh sổ đỏ.
- Cuối: Thành drawing the token shape in his notebook.

- Ý đồ: Turn the obstruction into a specific risky next step that depends on Sơn's past access.
- Bố cục: Frame Sơn speaking with Mây and Thành in the same medium desk setup, screen remaining contextual.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn names the hardware token's location, and Thành sketches its existing shape once in the notebook.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn's recognition should carry reluctance at returning to Bà Hai, while Thành focuses on retaining a practical description.
- Điểm nét: Keep Sơn's mouth and the drawing hand readable, without adding a large readable token label or a fantasy map.
- Chất liệu: Plain pencil and paper make the small security object concrete before it is physically retrieved later.
- Âm nổi: The line lands over restrained fan and pencil movement; no vault-opening sound should foreshadow success.
- Nhịp diễn: Leave a beat before naming the safe, allowing the cost of the location to register before the sketch finishes.

**Clip 6 · CUT · POST · W**
- Đầu: Mây on the station phone, Thành with the copied evidence.
- Hành động: She briefs district support and assigns town protection before going to the city.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Tôi theo dấu Hạ. Không rút người giữ thị trấn.
- Cuối: Protection roster on the desk, Thành acknowledging his post.

- Ý đồ: End the investigation scene on an accountable choice to leave town with protection arranged, not on more exposition.
- Bố cục: The wide setup includes station phone, copied evidence and roster with all three people in their working positions.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây briefs support, assigns Thành's town role and leaves the roster before preparing to travel.
- Diễn xuất: Her concern for Hạ stays under professional control; Thành accepts responsibility without another comic objection.
- Điểm nét: Keep figures and document handover legible, with no extra remote superior face or screen call added.
- Chất liệu: The roster is plain work paper, not a dramatic operations board suddenly appearing for the trip.
- Âm nổi: Use a low confirmation tone from the established channel beneath the instruction, without a second full conversation.
- Nhịp diễn: The delegation is the turn; finish with Thành acknowledging and let incoming morning engines provide the next pressure.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Close the night station sound cleanly. New morning engines establish Dũng arriving with changed stakes, not a repeated visitor search.

### CẢNH 18: Dũng Tới Biển
*Thời gian truyện: D4 morning*
Dũng đe dọa bằng phần khăn còn lại, để lộ lệnh về thành phố.

**Câu hỏi:** Dũng có quyền bước vào mọi chỗ hắn muốn không?
**Móc vào:** Cái ghế trống bị hất đổ, Bảy chưa bỏ chỗ đứng.
**Điểm bẻ:** Mây giữ ranh giới trước đe dọa, chiếc khăn làm lời đe dọa thật hơn.
**Gieo / trả:** Lệnh gọi về thành phố giải thích Dũng ở 24; sự nhượng bộ của hắn không được diễn như hết nguy hiểm.

**Clip 1 · OPEN · STREET · W**
- Đầu: Two black cars stopping outside the coffee stall.
- Hành động: Dũng steps out, knocks one empty chair aside, asks after his men.
- Thoại: Dũng (@Dũng), nói trong hình: Hai người tao gửi đâu?
- Cuối: Chair on its side, Bảy behind the counter.

- Ý đồ: Let Dũng's entitlement disturb the town's ordinary order through one clearly visible trespass.
- Bố cục: Use the wide street composition to keep both black cars, empty chair and Bảy behind his counter related.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng steps out and knocks only the empty chair aside, his men staying near their cars.
- Diễn xuất: He expects the place to yield before he speaks; Bảy remains in place without exaggerated fearless posturing.
- Điểm nét: Keep the chair's path and standing bodies readable, avoiding a low-angle glorification of his size.
- Chất liệu: Dusty tires, scuffed chair legs and ordinary seafront light preserve the modest scale of the intrusion.
- Âm nổi: A dry chair scrape briefly interrupts traffic and awning sounds, then his question takes the foreground.
- Nhịp diễn: The chair disturbance is quick and singular; let its awkward resting position persist as a problem someone must answer.

**Clip 2 · CUT · STALL · M**
- Đầu: Bảy setting his glass down before answering.
- Hành động: He points toward the clinic, refusing to let Dũng pass behind his counter.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Phòng khám. Muốn gặp thì hỏi công an.
- Cuối: Bảy blocking the counter entrance, Dũng in the public aisle.

- Ý đồ: Give Bảy a boundary he can hold without turning an elderly civilian into an action hero.
- Bố cục: Frame the counter opening between Bảy's speaking face and Dũng's looming position in the public aisle.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Bảy sets his existing glass down, points toward the clinic and stays across the staff entrance.
- Diễn xuất: He is wary but firm, refusing access by ordinary ownership of his space rather than a threatening gesture.
- Điểm nét: Keep his face and the counter boundary in shared medium depth, leaving Dũng's size contextual rather than dominant.
- Chất liệu: The used counter edge and plain glass reinforce what Bảy is actually protecting, a working place.
- Âm nổi: The glass landing precedes the line, then a short ordinary room pause keeps the threat human rather than musical.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the boundary hold after the sentence; do not make Dũng test it with an extra shove or repeat the question.

**Clip 3 · J · STREET · W**
- Đầu: Mây and Thành approaching on foot, a support vehicle farther back.
- Hành động: Mây makes Dũng put the chair back before continuing.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Dựng lại. Rồi nói chuyện.
- Cuối: Chair upright, Dũng's men waiting by their cars.

- Ý đồ: Let Mây restore a small public rule before discussing a larger threat, making authority visible in action.
- Bố cục: The wide view retains the fallen chair, Mây and Thành approaching, and the support vehicle farther back.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây stops within speaking distance and waits for Dũng to restore the chair before anyone advances.
- Diễn xuất: Her command is calm and non-negotiable; Dũng complies with visible resentment, retaining menace rather than becoming a clown.
- Điểm nét: Keep the chair and all three body positions legible, with no need for a new handcuff close-up.
- Chất liệu: Coastal daylight keeps the restored chair as ordinary furniture, not a newly heroic prop lit for a reveal.
- Âm nổi: A second, different chair sound belongs only to its restoration, followed by her concise instruction.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the waiting a little duration so compliance matters; finish upright before turning toward the scarf threat.

**Clip 4 · CUT · STALL · I**
- Đầu: Dũng's hand above the counter holding a long torn blue scarf.
- Hành động: He lays the remaining scarf down without letting Mây approach him.
- Thoại: Dũng, ngoài khung hình: Đưa cho Sơn. Nó biết của ai.
- Cuối: Long scarf flat on the counter, one end torn.

- Ý đồ: Make the remaining scarf an intimate threat through its material match, without claiming it proves Hạ alive.
- Bố cục: Use the detail shot on Dũng's hand, the long scarf and Mây's held distance at the counter edge.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He lays the long remainder down once and withdraws just enough to prevent a casual snatch.
- Diễn xuất: His off-screen line should be dismissive confidence; Mây's still hand shows attention rather than immediate grabbing.
- Điểm nét: Keep the torn end clear at one scale, preserving that this is the long remainder, not Sơn's short fragment.
- Chất liệu: Frayed blue silk against rough counter wood echoes the latch trace without manufacturing a second scarf.
- Âm nổi: The soft cloth slide contrasts with Dũng's rough voice; no heartbeat effect announces the connection.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the torn edge settle before the name is spoken, then leave Mây the decision to collect it later.

**Clip 5 · CUT · STREET · C**
- Đầu: Dũng beside his open car door, phone vibrating.
- Hành động: He hears a recall to the silk shop and ends the call angrily.
- Thoại: Bà Hai, qua thiết bị: Về tiệm. Đêm nay có khách.
- Cuối: Dũng getting into the car, no longer waiting at the stall.

- Ý đồ: Explain Dũng's later city presence while showing he also lives under someone else's control.
- Bố cục: The close-up holds Dũng beside the open car door with the vibrating phone at a practical height.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He answers the existing recall, ends the call sharply and turns into the car instead of lingering.
- Diễn xuất: His irritation should be directed at being summoned, not at a caller body the shot never shows.
- Điểm nét: Keep his eyes and phone edge clear, screen unreadable and Bà Hai only a filtered voice.
- Chất liệu: A small earring glint and dusty door paint retain the same arrival world without a new costume or luxury insert.
- Âm nổi: Phone vibration stops on answering; her familiar softness through compression precedes the car movement.
- Nhịp diễn: The change is from controlling the stall to obeying a recall; avoid a second threat line before he leaves.

**Clip 6 · L · STALL · M**
- Đầu: Mây collecting the scarf after the cars leave.
- Hành động: She asks Thành to keep patrols on the clinic and the harbour road.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Hắn đi rồi. Vẫn giữ cửa ngõ.
- Cuối: Thành heading toward the patrol vehicle, scarf in Mây's bag.

- Ý đồ: Show Mây treating the threat as evidence and a continuing risk, rather than celebrating the men's departure.
- Bố cục: Use the medium stall view with the cleared road beyond, long scarf and her existing case bag close together.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She collects the scarf after the cars leave and assigns Thành to keep the two relevant routes watched.
- Diễn xuất: Her relief is limited; Thành moves promptly toward the support vehicle without turning the order into reassurance comedy.
- Điểm nét: Hold the evidence collection and speaking face readable, keeping the departing road secondary and not replayed.
- Chất liệu: The blue cloth entering the practical bag contrasts with the returned everyday counter, not a shrine-like display.
- Âm nổi: Let engine sound recede beneath her instruction, then restore awning and sea without a victory cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Collection follows departure once; the final movement is Thành taking his duty, not another look at the torn end.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: The evidence-bag rustle bridges to two separated scarf pieces on the cabin table. Do not repeat Dũng laying it down.

### CẢNH 19: Hai Mảnh Khăn
*Thời gian truyện: D4 noon*
Hai mảnh cùng một chiếc khăn khớp nhau và thúc đẩy chuyến đi.

**Câu hỏi:** Hai mảnh khớp có đủ chứng minh Hạ còn sống không?
**Móc vào:** Mép rách gần chạm nhưng vẫn còn khe hở.
**Điểm bẻ:** Chúng chỉ chứng minh cùng nguồn; Mây và Sơn vẫn chọn điều tra thay vì tin ngay.
**Gieo / trả:** Không sửa khăn thành lành, không có vạch thứ tư bị khắc hai lần; điện thoại mở sang đòi bằng chứng sống.

**Clip 1 · MATCH · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: Two separate pieces of pale-blue cloth on the table.
- Hành động: Sơn aligns the torn edges without pretending they are repaired.
- Thoại: Sơn, ngoài khung hình: Một chiếc. Hạ xé đầu này.
- Cuối: Matched torn edges separated by a narrow gap.

- Ý đồ: Let matching torn edges create stronger evidence while preserving what the cloth cannot tell them.
- Bố cục: Use the locked detail setup to compare two pieces at the same scale, edges separated by a visible narrow gap.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn aligns the short fragment beside the long remainder and stops before pretending to mend them.
- Diễn xuất: His fingers hesitate at near contact, carrying longing that the factual line alone should not overstate.
- Điểm nét: Keep the matching fibers and gap crisp, with no inserted image of the abduction or inner watch face.
- Chất liệu: Different folds show how each piece was carried, while matching pale-blue weave ties them to one object.
- Âm nổi: Only cloth sliding over wood and his low off-screen sentence interrupt cabin wind.
- Nhịp diễn: Give recognition a quiet beat, then preserve the gap so emotional completion does not masquerade as physical repair.

**Clip 2 · CUT · CABIN · M**
- Đầu: Three marks on the frame, Sơn beside the shelf.
- Hành động: He makes only the fourth mark and puts the tool away.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Exactly four marks, tool on shelf.

- Ý đồ: Let the fourth tally register a new day of pressure without turning counting into a repeated ritual montage.
- Bố cục: The medium view keeps Sơn, three existing marks and the shelf where the tool returns in one setup.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He makes exactly one new mark, then places the tool away without re-cutting earlier lines.
- Diễn xuất: His movement is more decisive than the first day's, but not angry enough to damage the whole frame.
- Điểm nét: Keep the total count readable alongside his body, avoiding another identical isolated carving close-up.
- Chất liệu: Fresh wood beside older pale cuts gives time a physical trace while noon light stays harsh and natural.
- Âm nổi: One short scrape and shelf contact carry the action, with no recycled seven-day voice-over.
- Nhịp diễn: Do the work briskly and leave the completed four visible, using the remaining breath as resolve rather than padding.

**Clip 3 · CUT · CABIN · M**
- Đầu: Sơn placing the small piece in an envelope.
- Hành động: Mây takes custody of the long remainder in her evidence bag.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Tôi giữ phần này làm chứng.
- Cuối: Small fragment with Sơn, long remainder sealed with Mây.

- Ý đồ: Make evidence custody part of growing trust, while leaving Sơn a small private piece to carry.
- Bố cục: Frame both at the table, distinct envelope and evidence bag in the lower medium space.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn stores his short fragment while Mây seals only the long remainder into her existing bag.
- Diễn xuất: Neither should handle the cloth sentimentally for too long; the care comes through precision and mutual permission.
- Điểm nét: Keep the two destinations readable at the established table depth, so they cannot seem to merge into one new package.
- Chất liệu: Plain envelope paper and clear protective sleeve retain different functions and ownership.
- Âm nổi: A modest envelope fold and sleeve closure sit beneath Mây's line, with no ceremonial sealing flourish.
- Nhịp diễn: Let one piece be put away before the other, then end with both owners' hands clear of the table center.

**Clip 4 · CONTINUE · CABIN · M**
- Đầu: Small fragment with Sơn, long remainder sealed with Mây.
- Hành động: Mây shows the confirmed town-protection roster and lays out her denim jacket for the trip.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Thành có người hỗ trợ. Ta đi.
- Cuối: Denim jacket ready beside Mây's bag, both still by the table.

- Ý đồ: Show Mây joining the risk by preparation, not by abandoning her responsibility or instantly changing clothes.
- Bố cục: Maintain the exact preceding medium setup, focus and table positions through the continuation seam.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She displays the confirmed protection roster and lays her denim jacket ready beside the existing travel bag.
- Diễn xuất: Her resolve is practical; Sơn's attention shifts from the cloth to the preparation without another warning gesture.
- Điểm nét: Keep the same table-and-face plane, with the roster readable as confirmed work rather than legible official text.
- Chất liệu: Denim joins khaki and plain paper as a planned change, not an unexplained costume transformation during the shot.
- Âm nổi: A soft jacket fold and paper movement support her brief statement, leaving wind and room tone intact.
- Nhịp diễn: One preparation sequence completes the decision, then allow the next cut to contain the actual change of clothing.

**Clip 5 · CUT · PORCH · O**
- Đầu: Sơn and Mây facing the path down from the porch.
- Hành động: Sơn warns her about Bà Hai, Mây names her own reason for going.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Tôi đi tìm Hạ. Không đi trốn với anh.
- Cuối: Both committed to the same path, bags ready.

- Ý đồ: Let Mây define her own reason for going, preventing the trip from becoming Sơn's possessive rescue narrative.
- Bố cục: Use the shoulder view to favor her visible speaking face, both bodies oriented toward the existing descent path.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn holds back a fraction at the porch edge while she sets the direction toward the bags and path.
- Diễn xuất: Her correction is steady rather than combative; his response should be acceptance of her agency, not offended pride.
- Điểm nét: Hold her eyes and mouth clearly past his near shoulder, with the route still an understandable background direction.
- Chất liệu: Denim replaces khaki after the stated ellipsis, while his olive jacket and bag remain unchanged.
- Âm nổi: Her line is clear over porch wind, with no invented warning speech from Sơn needed inside this clip.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the correction space to stand, then let their shared orientation conclude it rather than adding a nod for every clause.

**Clip 6 · J · PORCH · I**
- Đầu: Old phone lying on the porch table beside Sơn's bag.
- Hành động: It vibrates from an unknown caller, Sơn waits for Mây's nod.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn's finger answering, Mây beside him.

- Ý đồ: Let an unanswered phone interrupt a completed choice, not manufacture the reason they suddenly travel.
- Bố cục: The detail frame holds the old phone on the table and the edge of the already-packed canvas bag.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: It vibrates once as a call event; Sơn waits for Mây's silent assent before answering.
- Diễn xuất: His finger should hover briefly from caution, not because he is performing a gadget reveal.
- Điểm nét: Keep phone and answering hand at one plane, any caller information unreadable and Mây present only as established beside him.
- Chất liệu: Worn phone casing and canvas weave keep the interruption unglamorous, with no new device or screen graphic.
- Âm nổi: Use the phone's real modest vibration against wood, cutting it off cleanly when the call is answered.
- Nhịp diễn: The wait is short but meaningful; end at the answer so the next shot begins speaker mode without a second ring.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: The phone vibration stops when answered. Cut wider for speaker mode, without a second ring or second answering gesture.

### CẢNH 20: Lời Mời Không Tên
*Thời gian truyện: D4 afternoon*
Người môi giới chứng minh biết Hạ và muốn Sơn phục vụ kế hoạch riêng.

**Câu hỏi:** Người gọi biết Hạ hay chỉ biết cách khiến Sơn làm theo?
**Móc vào:** Một chi tiết riêng tư làm thay đổi hơi thở người nghe, không lộ mặt người gọi.
**Điểm bẻ:** Sơn đòi bằng chứng sống và tự ngắt cuộc mặc cả đầu tiên.
**Gieo / trả:** Video 27 sẽ trả điều kiện này; hai điện thoại giữ chức năng nghe và ghi khác nhau.

**Clip 1 · CUT · PORCH · M**
- Đầu: Phone answered in Sơn's hand, Mây beside him at the table.
- Hành động: Sơn puts the caller on speaker, Mây starts a recording on her phone.
- Thoại: Người gọi giấu tên, qua thiết bị: Hạ vẫn giữ cái kẹp tóc màu bạc.
- Cuối: Two phones apart on the table, both listening.

- Ý đồ: Make the caller's access to a personal detail unsettle Sơn while Mây preserves room for doubt.
- Bố cục: The medium setup separates the listening phone from Mây's recording phone on the same porch table.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn switches the already-answered call to speaker as Mây begins recording, neither device changing owner.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn's reaction is contained; Mây watches him as much as the phones, not accepting the claim as proof.
- Điểm nét: Keep faces and the two device positions readable without a fabricated caller image or legible recording waveform.
- Chất liệu: Ordinary worn phone surfaces and porch wood contrast with the emotional weight of the voice.
- Âm nổi: The filtered caller sounds human and controlled, not electronically monstrous; wind stays low beneath the private detail.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the detail arrive before either person reacts, then hold the separate phones to preserve their different functions.

**Clip 2 · CUT · PORCH · C**
- Đầu: Sơn listening without touching either phone.
- Hành động: The private detail makes him lean forward despite himself.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Anh gặp cô ấy ở đâu?
- Cuối: Sơn's expression changed, Mây noticing the change.

- Ý đồ: Expose the hope Sơn cannot suppress without letting it become certainty that Hạ is alive.
- Bố cục: Use the close-up on his listening face, with Mây still in the established surrounding space.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He leans forward only slightly and leaves both phones untouched while asking where the caller met her.
- Diễn xuất: His eyes sharpen before the voice changes; the question should reveal need that he would prefer to hide.
- Điểm nét: Hold the current facial plane, avoiding a rack to Mây or an invented inset of the hair clip.
- Chất liệu: Wind moves a lapel edge while his hands stay outside the device area, keeping vulnerability in the face.
- Âm nổi: A short intake of breath precedes his line; the caller's channel remains present but does not overlap as a second sentence.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the personal reaction a fraction before the question, then leave the unanswered space audible.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · PORCH · C**
- Đầu: Sơn's expression changed, Mây noticing the change.
- Hành động: The caller refuses a location and names the red ledger in Bà Hai's safe.
- Thoại: Người gọi giấu tên, qua thiết bị: Lấy sổ đỏ. Tôi cho anh thấy cô ấy.
- Cuối: Sơn looking toward his bag, recorder still running.

- Ý đồ: Let the caller convert that hope into an assignment, making manipulation legible in the same held reaction.
- Bố cục: Keep the exact close-up, camera position and focus from Sơn's question at the seam.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn listens to the refused location and instruction, then lets his eyes move toward his existing bag.
- Diễn xuất: His expression should close slightly as he recognizes the steering; do not add a new shocked response.
- Điểm nét: Retain the previous face plane and peripheral device relationship, without a new safe image or ledger flashback.
- Chất liệu: The same porch light and jacket texture remain unchanged, so the power shift comes from his listening.
- Âm nổi: The compressed voice stays smooth and unhurried; the recording phone must not emit a distracting notification.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow the instruction to finish before the bag glance, making that movement a response rather than a repeated search.

**Clip 4 · CUT · PORCH · M**
- Đầu: Mây holding the recorder steady beside Sơn.
- Hành động: She signals that the caller is steering them toward a conflict.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Đừng nhận thêm điều kiện.
- Cuối: Sơn's hand hovering over the end-call button.

- Ý đồ: Give Mây a visible intervention before Sơn can be drawn into a new bargain.
- Bố cục: Return to the medium two-person setup with recorder steady and the listening phone separate.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She keeps recording and gives the short warning, Sơn's hand moving only toward the end-call control.
- Diễn xuất: Her tone is low and firm rather than an order shouted over him; he hears the practical boundary.
- Điểm nét: Keep her mouth and his hovering hand readable at one depth, without a new phone-screen close-up.
- Chất liệu: Two ordinary devices and the bag retain their fixed positions, preventing props from becoming a visual shell game.
- Âm nổi: Her words sit cleanly above the line's quiet room noise; no suspense pulse needs to announce good judgment.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the warning complete before his hand reaches the control, leaving the actual decision to the next continuous beat.

**Clip 5 · CONTINUE · PORCH · M**
- Đầu: Sơn's hand hovering over the end-call button.
- Hành động: Sơn demands proof of life before discussing any other job.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Cho tôi thấy Hạ còn sống trước.
- Cuối: His hand ends the call rather than waiting for permission.

- Ý đồ: Let Sơn reclaim the terms by demanding evidence and ending contact himself.
- Bố cục: Preserve the same medium setup and existing focus, phone positions and hand location from Mây's intervention.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He states the condition, ends the call once and leaves the listening phone where it is.
- Diễn xuất: The firmness should cost him something; he still wants the caller to be right, but will not perform another job blindly.
- Điểm nét: Keep his mouth and final button action readable without a change of lens or a new speakerphone graphic.
- Chất liệu: The phone remains an ordinary object after the line ends, not a glowing symbol under changed light.
- Âm nổi: The channel cuts cleanly after his demand, restoring exposed porch wind rather than an electronic menace tone.
- Nhịp diễn: Hold a short living silence after disconnection, so the choice lands without repeating the warning or bargaining again.

**Clip 6 · L · STREET · W**
- Đầu: Night coach approaching the town stop, both travelers waiting.
- Hành động: They board separately with their bags, watched by Thành from across the road.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Coach door closing, Thành remaining in town.

- Ý đồ: Close the act on an already-chosen journey, preserving the cost of leaving Thành responsible for home.
- Bố cục: Use the wide stop view to relate travelers, coach door and Thành across the road without shifting into the vehicle.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn and Mây board once with their own bags while Thành stays outside on the town side.
- Diễn xuất: Their departures are purposeful rather than romantic; Thành watches as someone receiving responsibility, not waving at a holiday trip.
- Điểm nét: Keep all positions readable at wide depth, with no new close-up farewell or invented passengers demanding attention.
- Chất liệu: Coach door wear, packed bags and ordinary road dust connect the departure to the opening arrival without copying it.
- Âm nổi: Engine and door sounds come from the same coach, ready to continue into the on-board scene without restarting.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the two board in succession, then stay on the closed door and the person remaining rather than adding another goodbye.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep the same departing coach engine into the on-board view. No second boarding or repeat door close.

### CẢNH 21: Xe Đò Đêm
*Thời gian truyện: D4 night*
Hai người vào thành phố với trạng thái trang phục rõ.

**Câu hỏi:** Anh có thể mặc lại bộ đồ cũ mà không quay về con người cũ không?
**Móc vào:** Bộ vest lộ ra ngay sau tin tuần tra, đặt việc riêng cạnh trách nhiệm chung.
**Điểm bẻ:** Sơn mang vest nhưng cất găng, Mây vẫn giữ liên lạc với thị trấn.
**Gieo / trả:** Gieo tay trần và chiếc túi sẽ được để lại ở cảnh35.

**Clip 1 · J · BUS · W**
- Đầu: Sơn seated by the coach window, Mây across the narrow aisle.
- Hành động: Mây checks the patrol confirmation while Sơn opens his travel bag.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Patrol message acknowledged, folded black suit visible in the bag.

- Ý đồ: Place private dread beside Mây's still-active responsibility for the town.
- Bố cục: Use the aisle as a narrow divider, bag low and her confirmation phone higher.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Both remain seated through the coach sway; Sơn opens the bag without crossing toward her.
- Diễn xuất: Mây reads efficiently then checks him, while Sơn avoids seeking permission for the suit.
- Điểm nét: Keep the suit reveal secondary until her patrol acknowledgement has registered.
- Chất liệu: Rain tracks outside, worn canvas and damp denim make this practical travel.
- Âm nổi: Maintain steady engine, soft rain and one small bag-clasp sound, without a suspense sting.
- Nhịp diễn: Confirmation first, folded suit last; let that new object change the silence.

**Clip 2 · CUT · BUS · M**
- Đầu: Black suit folded across Sơn's knees, gloves tucked inside.
- Hành động: He touches the gloves, Mây keeps her attention on his face.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Anh vẫn cần bộ đó sao?
- Cuối: Gloves still folded, Sơn's hand withdrawn.

- Ý đồ: Make the gloves an invitation he is afraid to accept again.
- Bố cục: Frame the folded suit across his knees with both faces readable above it.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: His fingertips touch only the glove edge, then withdraw to the same lap position.
- Diễn xuất: Mây asks without accusation; Sơn's response is a controlled swallow rather than a dramatic flinch.
- Điểm nét: Keep both people and gloves readable in the existing medium shot, no rack to another angle.
- Chất liệu: The supple dark leather contrasts with her rain-marked denim and his rough canvas bag.
- Âm nổi: His line must not compete with cloth movement or an artificially amplified heartbeat.
- Nhịp diễn: Leave the question hanging after his hand leaves, allowing recognition rather than explanation.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · BUS · M**
- Đầu: Gloves still folded, Sơn's hand withdrawn.
- Hành động: Sơn returns the folded suit and gloves from his knees into the open bag, closes it and speaks about his first employer without a full flashback.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Bà cho tôi chỗ ở. Rồi giữ luôn lối ra.
- Cuối: Bag closed between his feet, Mây listening.

- Ý đồ: Show that admitting dependency costs Sơn more than touching the old uniform.
- Bố cục: Retain exactly the preceding medium composition, aisle width and seated eyelines.
