- Vị trí / chuyển động: He returns the folded suit and gloves to the open bag, closes it, then lowers it between his feet.
- Diễn xuất: Say the sentence as an unwelcome fact he has only just permitted himself to name.
- Điểm nét: Keep attention on his face while her listening remains visible at the same depth.
- Chất liệu: The bag's worn corners settle against his shoes, underlining years of carrying this life.
- Âm nổi: Let the bag close sound finish before the quieter final clause is heard.
- Nhịp diễn: The first clause acknowledges shelter; the second arrives after a small embarrassed breath.

**Clip 4 · L · REST_STOP · W**
- Đầu: Coach stopped under a rest-stop awning, travelers already outside.
- Hành động: Sơn walks toward the changing room, Mây waits beside the coach.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Mây beside the coach door, Sơn beyond the changing-room entrance.

- Ý đồ: Separate the decision to change clothes from a glamorous transformation montage.
- Bố cục: Keep the coach door, waiting Mây and changing-room entrance together in one wide frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn walks directly under the awning; she holds her station near the coach door.
- Diễn xuất: She watches him go as a partner assessing risk, not as an admirer of his profession.
- Điểm nét: Hold the whole route legible; do not follow him into the changing room inside this take.
- Chất liệu: Wet paving reflects ordinary rest-stop lamps, with rain falling beyond the sheltered path.
- Âm nổi: Idling engine continues, footsteps briefly sharpen under the roof, no heroic music rise.
- Nhịp diễn: Let his exit from her immediate reach create the small uncertainty before the cut.

**Clip 5 · CUT · REST_ROOM · M**
- Đầu: Sơn before the mirror, suit already on and gloves in his hand.
- Hành động: He pockets the gloves unused, leaving both hands bare.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Tôi mặc lại vest. Không mang lại đôi tay cũ.
- Cuối: Sơn in black suit with bare hands, olive jacket folded in his bag.

- Ý đồ: Define his change through a missing glove rather than the attractiveness of the suit.
- Bố cục: Use the existing mirror to include his direct shoulder and one reflected face, not twin bodies.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He pockets the gloves once; both bare hands finish visible below the jacket edge.
- Diễn xuất: He inspects himself with guarded fatigue, avoiding a triumphant straightening of his shoulders.
- Điểm nét: Keep the reflected eyes sharp enough to read while the folded olive jacket remains identifiable.
- Chất liệu: Cold rest-room tile and a plain mirror deny the costume change any luxury.
- Âm nổi: Narration sits over muffled engine and a slight leather rustle, with no added ticking.
- Nhịp diễn: Pause only long enough to register the bare hands after the gloves disappear.

**Clip 6 · L · REST_STOP · W**
- Đầu: Mây holding the coach door open, Sơn returning with his bag.
- Hành động: They reboard, Sơn puts the bag above his seat.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Both back on the coach, olive jacket stored inside the bag.

- Ý đồ: Return the changed man to the same ordinary journey without repeating his arrival.
- Bố cục: Anchor the coach doorway and Mây outside it, preserving his approach direction from the room.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He comes back carrying the bag, boards after her, and stows it once overhead.
- Diễn xuất: Mây recognizes the change with a restrained look, then makes room rather than commenting.
- Điểm nét: Keep his hands and the overhead bag placement readable within the existing wide setup.
- Chất liệu: The olive jacket stays contained in canvas, while rain beads on the bus exterior.
- Âm nổi: Keep the same engine character and coach-door mechanisms, not a fresh engine start.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete reboarding economically; the stowed bag is the quiet confirmation that the change holds.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Coach-window rain becomes rain on the silk-shop rear awning. The city arrival is a time ellipsis, not another travel montage.

### CẢNH 22: Két Sau Bình Phong
*Thời gian truyện: D4 late night*
Lấy khóa số và sổ đỏ, Bà Hai chủ động giữ họ lại.

**Câu hỏi:** Bà Hai thực sự bị đột nhập hay đang dẫn họ vào cuộc trao đổi?
**Móc vào:** Chìa vẫn mở được, khiến căn nhà cũ đáng ngờ hơn một ổ khóa khó.
**Điểm bẻ:** Bà để lấy sổ vì cần Sơn đưa Kiên tới, quyền mặc cả đổi từ tài liệu sang con người.
**Gieo / trả:** Sổ đỏ và token có đường ra rõ, còn Bà Hai chủ động khóa đường tâm lý.

**Clip 1 · J · SILK_REAR · W**
- Đầu: Sơn and Mây at the closed rear door in the rain.
- Hành động: He uses the old shop key while Mây watches the alley.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Chìa cửa cũ. Tôi giữ hai mươi lăm năm.
- Cuối: Rear door opening inward, both travelers at the threshold.

- Ý đồ: Make the still-working key feel like permission whose price has not been disclosed.
- Bố cục: Keep the closed rear door central and enough alley behind Mây to justify her lookout.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn uses his right hand on the lock, Mây staying clear of the inward door swing.
- Diễn xuất: He knows the mechanism by touch; the lack of resistance unsettles rather than reassures him.
- Điểm nét: The doorway and their faces share useful depth; no insert or extra close-up is needed.
- Chất liệu: Rain darkens old wood beneath the shallow awning, with no newly invented sign or lock.
- Âm nổi: Give the old key turn a modest audible click beneath his short explanation.
- Nhịp diễn: Do not stretch the unlocking; the easy opening should create the more troubling question.

**Clip 2 · CUT · SILK · T**
- Đầu: Both inside the dim shop, rear door behind screen left.
- Hành động: Sơn leads along the silk racks to the screen covering the safe.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Screen pulled aside, safe in front of Sơn, Mây by the exit.

- Ý đồ: Show intimate knowledge of the shop turning into vulnerability for both travelers.
- Bố cục: Track along their established aisle, silk racks framing the screened safe at the far end.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn leads without searching; Mây keeps a clear route back to the rear exit.
- Diễn xuất: His certainty is habitual, while her small checks reveal she refuses to inherit that certainty.
- Điểm nét: Keep the safe screen readable ahead of him, not a sudden focus reveal behind their backs.
- Chất liệu: Silk brushes close to the frame, narrowing the path without becoming dreamlike curtains.
- Âm nổi: Soft shoe steps, rain outside and a single cloth brush carry the intrusion.
- Nhịp diễn: Let familiarity make the approach efficient; the waiting safe is the final image of confidence.

**Clip 3 · CUT · SILK · I**
- Đầu: Safe open, red ledger and USB-shaped hardware token inside.
- Hành động: Sơn removes only the red ledger and hardware token.
- Thoại: Sơn, ngoài khung hình: Khóa số đây. Không phải chìa cửa.
- Cuối: Red ledger under his left arm, token in right hand.

- Ý đồ: Turn the search into a concrete gain before Bà Hai takes back the encounter.
- Bố cục: Use one stable detail view that distinguishes red cloth ledger from the small metal token.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: His right hand takes the token, left arm receives the book, no rummaging through unrelated valuables.
- Diễn xuất: Hands remain businesslike, stopping briefly at the token to verify it is the needed object.
- Điểm nét: Keep both acquired objects identifiable within the same focal plane and unchanged detail setup.
- Chất liệu: Steel safe edge, red cloth wear and token shape provide specific visual separation.
- Âm nổi: An ordinary binding scrape and small token tap are enough; do not add a digital success chime.
- Nhịp diễn: Extraction happens once and ends with both objects secure, leaving no reset before the interruption.

**Clip 4 · J · SILK · W**
- Đầu: Desk lamp switching on behind the open safe.
- Hành động: Bà Hai is revealed at the desk as both visitors turn toward her.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Má để cửa cho con.
- Cuối: All three visible, safe open behind Sơn.

- Ý đồ: Reveal that their apparent intrusion has been an invitation controlled by Bà Hai.
- Bố cục: Hold all three positions in the wide shot, safe behind Sơn and lit desk behind the turn.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Both travelers turn toward the desk without advancing, leaving Mây aligned with the rear exit.
- Diễn xuất: Bà Hai is already settled, greeting them softly; their delayed recognition supplies the shock.
- Điểm nét: The lamp and her face become readable together without an internal cut or artificial zoom.
- Chất liệu: Warm desk light lands on violet silk, while cool alley spill still outlines the escape route.
- Âm nổi: Lamp switch first, then her voice; do not accompany the reveal with a generic sting.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow one beat to identify her before the greeting, then hold the unequal stillness.

**Clip 5 · CUT · SILK · M**
- Đầu: Bà Hai facing Sơn with both palms on the desk.
- Hành động: She permits him to keep the ledger while asking for Kiên's return.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Giữ sổ. Đổi lại, đưa Kiên về đây.
- Cuối: Sơn retaining the ledger, Mây beside the rear exit.

- Ý đồ: Expose why she lets the ledger leave her hand: she wants a person delivered instead.
- Bố cục: Frame Bà Hai's open palms with Sơn retaining the red ledger, Mây still near the exit.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She does not reach for either object; Sơn does not accept a seat or close the distance.
- Diễn xuất: Her offer should sound maternal on the surface and commercially exact underneath.
- Điểm nét: Keep ledger and her eyes sufficiently readable to show where her interest has moved.
- Chất liệu: The polished desk separates her domestic hospitality from the safe standing open behind them.
- Âm nổi: Let her low offer remain dry against the room tone, without swelling threat music.
- Nhịp diễn: The permission to keep the book should land before the demanded return of Kiên.

**Clip 6 · CONTINUE · SILK · M**
- Đầu: Sơn retaining the ledger, Mây beside the rear exit.
- Hành động: Mây stays standing as Sơn draws a chair away from the desk.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Hạ ở đâu?
- Cuối: Chair angled toward the exit, three separate tea cups untouched.

- Ý đồ: Make Sơn refuse her comfortable family arrangement through the chair he does not occupy.
- Bố cục: Preserve the preceding medium lens, desk relation and three-person depth exactly.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He draws the chair away at an angle toward escape; Mây never takes the spare place.
- Diễn xuất: His question strips courtesy away, while Bà Hai lets the empty seat remain a rebuke.
- Điểm nét: Keep his face and the unused cup visible; do not cut to isolated furniture.
- Chất liệu: Chair feet catch lightly on the shop floor, three tea surfaces remain undisturbed.
- Âm nổi: Allow a modest chair scrape before the question, with no duplicate cup-setting sounds.
- Nhịp diễn: The chair movement creates expectation of sitting; the unanswered question defeats it instead.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep the same desk room tone, match the three untouched cups and angled chair before Bà Hai offers tea.

### CẢNH 23: Giá Của Một Người
*Thời gian truyện: D4 late night*
Bà Hai bán Hạ, Mây chọn bảo vệ bằng chứng thay vì nghe thêm.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn có còn gọi bà là má sau khi biết Hạ đã bị bán?
**Móc vào:** Chén trà không ai nhận khiến sự tiếp đãi trở thành áp lực.
**Điểm bẻ:** Một câu thú nhận làm quan hệ gia đình thành giao dịch, Mây kéo cuộc gặp trở lại chứng cứ.
**Gieo / trả:** Bà gọi Dũng thay vì giằng sổ, trả giá cho việc để họ rời tiệm.

**Clip 1 · MATCH · SILK · M**
- Đầu: Three untouched cups, Sơn's chair angled toward the rear exit.
- Hành động: Bà Hai offers tea, neither visitor reaches for it.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Kiên trả tiền để con im miệng.
- Cuối: Cup offered but untouched between Bà Hai and Sơn.

- Ý đồ: Make an offered comfort reveal a coercive history rather than warm hospitality.
- Bố cục: Match the three untouched cups and angled chair, keeping Bà Hai dominant without looming theatrics.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She offers one cup across the same distance; neither traveler completes the handover.
- Diễn xuất: Her patience suggests she expects obedience, while Sơn's refusal is almost entirely stillness.
- Điểm nét: Keep the offered cup and his unmoving hand readable in the same medium setup.
- Chất liệu: Thin steam, smooth porcelain and the worn table edge distinguish domestic ritual from negotiation.
- Âm nổi: Small porcelain contact should be real and restrained, with her voice staying clear.
- Nhịp diễn: Do not retract the cup too soon; the unaccepted offer becomes the uncomfortable end point.

**Clip 2 · CUT · SILK · C**
- Đầu: Sơn standing beside the angled chair.
- Hành động: He repeats the question without accepting her bargaining language.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Con hỏi Hạ.
- Cuối: Sơn still standing, Bà Hai lowering the cup.

- Ý đồ: Show Sơn protecting one question from all the explanations designed to exhaust him.
- Bố cục: Stay close to his face with the angled chair edge enough to locate him standing.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He remains beside the chair, not leaning over the desk or touching Bà Hai.
- Diễn xuất: The word naming Hạ carries effort; he avoids anger performed as shouting or clenched teeth.
- Điểm nét: Keep his eyes sharp, with Bà Hai's lowered cup a contextual softness rather than a second target.
- Chất liệu: Rain-dark suit cloth and bare knuckles carry his vulnerability without sweat added for effect.
- Âm nổi: Let the simple question interrupt the tea ritual, leaving the room immediately quiet afterward.
- Nhịp diễn: A short held look before speech, then enough space for the refusal of her terms.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · SILK · C**
- Đầu: Sơn still standing, Bà Hai lowering the cup.
- Hành động: Bà Hai admits transferring Hạ to Kiên but cannot prove her present condition.
- Thoại: Bà Hai, ngoài khung hình: Má không giết. Má bán.
- Cuối: Cup touching the saucer, Bà Hai's bracelet fixed in frame.

- Ý đồ: Let the sale admission wound him while revealing that she still considers herself reasonable.
- Bố cục: Keep the exact close composition from the preceding shot, using her voice without forcing another angle.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn receives the admission; the cup settles within the existing scene rather than a separate insert.
- Diễn xuất: He barely changes at first, then loses a fraction of his practiced eye contact.
- Điểm nét: Attention remains on the listener's face; her fixed bracelet stays only where the setup already permits.
- Chất liệu: Porcelain touches saucer once, a small domestic sound against the cruelty of the sentence.
- Âm nổi: Her voice is level and intimate, never monstrous; no sound effect should tell the audience how to feel.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the admission finish cleanly, followed by a breath in which its meaning arrives.

**Clip 4 · CUT · SILK · M**
- Đầu: Mây between the rear door and the desk, Sơn holding the red ledger.
- Hành động: She photographs Bà Hai beside the open safe, preparing to leave.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Đủ rồi. Chúng tôi giữ lời bà vừa nói.
- Cuối: Phone lowered, evidence bag ready in Mây's hand.

- Ý đồ: Make Mây give the encounter a practical exit when Sơn is at risk of becoming trapped emotionally.
- Bố cục: Frame her between door and desk, keeping Sơn's ledger grip inside the same medium composition.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She documents Bà Hai beside the safe, lowers the phone and prepares the evidence bag.
- Diễn xuất: She stays firm without triumph, aware this record matters more than winning an argument.
- Điểm nét: Keep phone orientation, Bà Hai's seated place and open safe understandable without reading a screen.
- Chất liệu: Plain denim and a practical evidence sleeve oppose the shop's polished rituals.
- Âm nổi: The photograph uses no exaggerated shutter effect; her line is the clean interruption.
- Nhịp diễn: One decisive documenting action, then withdrawal preparations that create movement toward the blocked exit.

**Clip 5 · CUT · SILK · H**
- Đầu: Two unnamed guards blocking the rear exit.
- Hành động: Sơn rolls one silk bolt into their path, Mây restrains the nearer guard with one pair of cuffs.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: One guard cuffed to the desk leg, exit temporarily clear.

- Ý đồ: Turn accumulated restraint into one readable opening for escape, not an exhibition fight.
- Bố cục: Keep guards, rolling silk and desk leg within the same side of the action axis.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn rolls one bolt across their path; Mây secures the nearer guard while the route opens.
- Diễn xuất: Both act to leave, without finishing blows, extra taunts or admiring their own efficiency.
- Điểm nét: Depth must show the obstruction and cuff anchor together; never hide the result in camera shake.
- Chất liệu: Heavy silk deforms against the floor, metal cuff catches the practical lamp briefly.
- Âm nổi: Use the bolt thud, cuff click and short breath, no repeated body-impact library sounds.
- Nhịp diễn: The action accelerates once, then lands clearly on the temporary opening they have earned.

**Clip 6 · L · SILK_REAR · W**
- Đầu: Sơn and Mây moving through the rear doorway, Bà Hai inside.
- Hành động: Bà Hai signals the other guard to follow instead of risking the ledger in a struggle.
- Thoại: Bà Hai, ngoài khung hình: Dũng. Cửa sau.
- Cuối: Travelers outside, guard lifting a phone, Bà Hai staying by the desk.

- Ý đồ: Show Bà Hai accepting a limited loss while choosing the next pressure point.
- Bố cục: Keep rear threshold framing the escaping pair, Bà Hai still visible within the warmer shop.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They clear the doorway; the remaining guard takes her signal and lifts his existing phone.
- Diễn xuất: She does not rush after them; irritation is a smaller pause before naming Dũng.
- Điểm nét: Keep the raised phone distinct from the ledger leaving frame, avoiding an unclear object exchange.
- Chất liệu: Cool wet alley contrasts with the steady warm desk behind the door.
- Âm nổi: Her off-screen command can pass through the departure, then footsteps take over naturally.
- Nhịp diễn: Do not replay the escape obstacle; finish on the command becoming pursuit through the guard.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Accelerating footsteps give way to garage echo before the ramp picture. Do not replay the silk-bolt obstruction.

### CẢNH 24: Bãi Xe: Một Người Đồng Hành
*Thời gian truyện: D4 late night*
Dũng bị chặn, Mây dùng chiếc còng thứ hai và rút lui.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn và Mây có thoát cùng bằng chứng khi Dũng chặn lối không?
**Móc vào:** Lối ra hiện rõ rồi Dũng bước vào đúng khoảng trống đó.
**Điểm bẻ:** Mây cứu Sơn bằng chiếc còng thứ hai, nhưng cổ tay anh bị tổn thương.
**Gieo / trả:** Vết thương bên trái, hai còng đã dùng và cách Dũng thoát đều được nhìn thấy.

**Clip 1 · J · GARAGE · W**
- Đầu: Sơn and Mây already entering the garage ramp from the alley.
- Hành động: They move along the same car row, rear exit visible beyond.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Both behind a parked van, exit two pillars ahead.

- Ý đồ: Give the audience a possible escape route before the pursuer takes it away.
- Bố cục: Use the wide setup to show ramp, car row and the exit two pillars beyond.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn and Mây stay together behind the van, moving in the same screen direction.
- Diễn xuất: Their pace is urgent but controlled, checking the open space rather than signaling panic to camera.
- Điểm nét: Keep the exit readable throughout; it is the meaningful background, not anonymous parked cars.
- Chất liệu: Damp concrete and regular pillar spacing give the movement a measurable distance.
- Âm nổi: Garage ventilation and footsteps replace alley rain without a sudden musical chase reset.
- Nhịp diễn: Establish the reachable exit early, then end with both still separated from it by two pillars.

**Clip 2 · CUT · GARAGE · H**
- Đầu: One guard reaching around the van toward Mây.
- Hành động: Sơn closes the car door between guard and Mây, keeping their path clear.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Lối bên trái.
- Cuối: Guard stopped by the door, Mây moving past the next pillar.

- Ý đồ: Show cooperation through Mây continuing the route while Sơn buys a brief gap.
- Bố cục: Frame the van corner, approaching guard and next pillar in one clear handheld medium-wide.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn closes the door into the path as a barrier, Mây taking the space it creates.
- Diễn xuất: Her direction is short and useful, his attention on the guard's approach rather than a punishing strike.
- Điểm nét: Keep the moving door and her exit side readable, never hide them behind a whip pan.
- Chất liệu: Car paint catches the garage light; door rubber and concrete remain ordinary materials.
- Âm nổi: One door stop and short footwork, with her line above the garage echo.
- Nhịp diễn: The barrier takes a moment, not an extended exchange, and Mây has advanced when it ends.

**Clip 3 · CUT · GARAGE · W**
- Đầu: Dũng coming down from the ramp in response to Bà Hai's call.
- Hành động: He cuts off the direct exit, forcing Sơn back toward a railing.
- Thoại: Dũng (@Dũng), nói trong hình: Má còn chưa nói xong.
- Cuối: Dũng between the travelers and exit, railing behind Sơn.

- Ý đồ: Let Dũng take the visible exit away with confidence grounded in knowing this family dispute.
- Bố cục: Use the wide frame to locate his descent from the ramp and the rail behind Sơn.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng occupies the direct path rather than sprinting at them, forcing the travelers laterally.
- Diễn xuất: He delivers the message as if returning runaway property, not initiating a sporting duel.
- Điểm nét: Keep both the obstruction he creates and the remaining railing route legible.
- Chất liệu: Yellow practicals repeat along the ramp, making his slow approach feel unavoidable.
- Âm nổi: His boots echo before the line; avoid an arrival sting or extra engine sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Give him time to claim the space, leaving the travelers visibly short of the exit.

**Clip 4 · CUT · GARAGE · H**
- Đầu: Dũng reaching for the ledger, Sơn protecting it under his left arm.
- Hành động: Dũng drives Sơn's left wrist into the pillar, Mây circles behind him.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn down on one knee, ledger secure, Mây at Dũng's right side.

- Ý đồ: Make the injury the cost of keeping evidence, not incidental decoration after a fight.
- Bố cục: Keep the ledger under Sơn's left arm, pillar and Mây's circling route in the same frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng reaches for the book, pressing the left wrist into the pillar as Sơn protects it.
- Diễn xuất: Pain first interrupts Sơn's breath, then balance; Mây responds to the opening without waiting for a look.
- Điểm nét: The left side must remain readable so later bandaging does not reverse the injury.
- Chất liệu: Rough pillar surface and strained black sleeve tell the contact without an open wound.
- Âm nổi: Use a dry contact sound and involuntary breath rather than a graphic impact exaggeration.
- Nhịp diễn: One collision takes him to a knee, ending with Mây at the side she needs.

**Clip 5 · CONTINUE · GARAGE · H**
- Đầu: Sơn down on one knee, ledger secure, Mây at Dũng's right side.
- Hành động: Mây cuffs Dũng's right wrist to the solid ramp rail using her second pair.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Đủ hai chiếc rồi.
- Cuối: Dũng restrained to the intact rail, Mây helping Sơn up.

- Ý đồ: Pay off Mây's second cuff with a rescue decision instead of a victory flourish.
- Bố cục: Retain the exact handheld setup, axis and figure positions established by the preceding struggle.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She closes the cuff on his right wrist and solid rail, then immediately helps Sơn.
- Diễn xuất: The line is breathless accounting, not a punchline; Dũng understands the restraint before anger rises.
- Điểm nét: Keep wrist, anchored rail and Mây's helping hand visible in one readable depth.
- Chất liệu: Intact steel rail should not flex theatrically; the cuff chain becomes a precise boundary.
- Âm nổi: One cuff click, chain tension and their short recovery breaths carry the result.
- Nhịp diễn: Finish the restraint efficiently and spend the remaining beat on helping the injured companion away.

**Clip 6 · L · GARAGE_EXIT · W**
- Đầu: The two travelers crossing the exit, Dũng behind them at the rail.
- Hành động: An unnamed guard arrives with bolt cutters after they clear the doorway.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Travelers gone, cutters beside the cuff chain, Dũng still restrained.

- Ý đồ: Plant Dũng's possible return as an observable practical consequence without undoing the escape.
- Bố cục: Keep the doorway framing the pair leaving and Dũng still attached behind them.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: The guard arrives with cutters only after the travelers clear the exit; no cutting yet.
- Diễn xuất: Dũng's humiliation is held in the way he waits for help rather than more shouted threats.
- Điểm nét: Show the cutter jaws near the intact chain, not a duplicate cuff opening or instant break.
- Chất liệu: Metal tools and rail share the garage practical reflections, with the exit cooler beyond.
- Âm nổi: Footsteps leave first, then the small tool clink predicts continued pursuit.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the travelers disappear before the tool arrives, making their escape real but temporary.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Garage echo and the cutter sound fall away as the motel fan introduces dawn. Show no second capture of Dũng.

### CẢNH 25: Chữ Ký Và Giờ Rời Bến
*Thời gian truyện: D5 dawn*
Dữ liệu khóa được mở, xác định giờ chuyển Hạ và nơi cần xác minh.

**Câu hỏi:** Sổ đỏ mở thêm điều gì đáng đánh đổi chuyến về thành phố?
**Móc vào:** Một cổ tay sưng buộc Sơn nhận giúp đỡ trước lúc tìm tiếp.
**Điểm bẻ:** Token biến nghi ngờ thành lịch chuyển Hạ đêm ngày bảy, dữ liệu tới người nhận trước khi họ đi.
**Gieo / trả:** Bằng chứng số có bản sao, hiện trường xưởng trở thành bước xác minh cuối.

**Clip 1 · J · MOTEL · M**
- Đầu: Sơn seated on the bed, jacket off, left wrist visibly swollen without a wound.
- Hành động: Mây wraps the left wrist and leaves his right hand free.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Đừng giấu đau nữa.
- Cuối: Left wrist bandaged, black jacket on the bed beside him.

- Ý đồ: Allow the price of the escape to interrupt Sơn's habit of refusing care.
- Bố cục: Use the medium bedside composition with jacket clearly off and left wrist exposed.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây wraps only the left wrist while Sơn stays seated, his sound right hand resting freely.
- Diễn xuất: He tries to minimize pain until the wrapping makes concealment pointless; she treats him without sentimentality.
- Điểm nét: Keep hand and faces readable together, avoiding an unneeded clinical detail insert.
- Chất liệu: Plain cotton wrap, tired shirt folds and the jacket on the bed make this an exhausted pause.
- Âm nổi: Fan hum and restrained cloth handling stay beneath her quiet instruction.
- Nhịp diễn: Let him accept her help before moving to evidence, giving the relationship a genuine change.

**Clip 2 · CUT · MOTEL · I**
- Đầu: Borrowed laptop on the table, working USB copy already connected.
- Hành động: Thành guides Mây by phone as she inserts the hardware token.
- Thoại: Thành, qua thiết bị: Đúng khóa. Mở được rồi.
- Cuối: Locked folder opened, original evidence kept separately.

- Ý đồ: Convert a difficult trip into an earned technical opening, without magical screen spectacle.
- Bố cục: Keep laptop connections, working USB and distinct hardware token visible in one stable detail frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây inserts the token once while Sơn leaves his injured hand out of the operation.
- Diễn xuất: Her hands wait for Thành's confirmation rather than acting as though she already knows the result.
- Điểm nét: Use a readable folder-state change through simple shapes; no legible passwords or elaborate interface animation.
- Chất liệu: Worn laptop casing and practical cables keep the process modest and physical.
- Âm nổi: Thành's compressed voice arrives from the established phone, followed by a small ordinary confirmation tone.
- Nhịp diễn: Connection, recognition, opened state: three causal steps within one continuous detail view.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · MOTEL · I**
- Đầu: Locked folder opened, original evidence kept separately.
- Hành động: Mây matches the digital authorization to Kiên's signature in the red ledger.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Matched signature shapes, Mây saving a local copy.

- Ý đồ: Make the unlocked data matter by tying a digital authorization to the original signed book.
- Bố cục: Preserve exactly the same detail framing, connections and table layout from the token shot.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây brings the red signature page alongside the screen, comparing shapes before saving.
- Diễn xuất: Her confidence grows through checking, not a knowing smile for the camera.
- Điểm nét: Both signature shapes must share the focal area; do not rely on reflected text in eyes.
- Chất liệu: Red fabric binding remains distinct from the dark laptop and separate original evidence sleeves.
- Âm nổi: A page turn and one restrained keypress sit within the existing fan and phone bed.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the comparison complete visibly before the local copy is saved; no second unlocking flourish.

**Clip 4 · CUT · MOTEL · M**
- Đầu: Mây reading the newly opened transport schedule.
- Hành động: They learn Hạ is registered as a passenger on the day-seven night departure.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Hạ được đưa lên tàu đêm ngày bảy.
- Cuối: Sơn looking at the departure marker, left wrist held against his chest.

- Ý đồ: Turn the open folder into a deadline affecting a person, not simply another account detail.
- Bố cục: Frame Mây reading with Sơn close enough to share the information without crowding the screen.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She states the departure, he keeps his injured wrist against his chest rather than reaching impulsively.
- Diễn xuất: The name Hạ changes his attention immediately, but fear is a stopped breath rather than a speech.
- Điểm nét: Keep their reactions primary and the departure marker as a supporting shape, with no generated readable text.
- Chất liệu: Grey dawn reaches the room, exposing ordinary fatigue after the darker city pursuit.
- Âm nổi: The line is clear over the steady fan; leave the laptop quiet while they absorb it.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the deadline a short silent landing before the map becomes their next practical response.

**Clip 5 · CONTINUE · MOTEL · M**
- Đầu: Sơn looking at the departure marker, left wrist held against his chest.
- Hành động: Sơn names the river factory and points to its loading door on Mây's map with his right hand.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Xưởng này. Tôi biết cửa xuống sông.
- Cuối: Factory circled on the map, pier route beside it.

- Ý đồ: Let Sơn contribute useful knowledge while acknowledging he cannot solve this alone.
- Bố cục: Keep the preceding medium composition; the map occupies the table they already share.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He points only with his right hand, Mây securing the map rather than leaning across his bandage.
- Diễn xuất: He names the door as remembered work, carrying unease at knowing the criminal place so well.
- Điểm nét: Hold face and pointing hand together; no sudden zoom into an invented map label.
- Chất liệu: Creased paper and the drawn loading-door route are enough, with no elaborate holographic plan.
- Âm nổi: A small paper shift punctuates his direction; fan and room tone continue without a music reset.
- Nhịp diễn: The location becomes specific first, the river way out second, finishing on a usable route.

**Clip 6 · CUT · MOTEL · I**
- Đầu: Mây uploading the copied files to the assigned investigator.
- Hành động: A receipt confirms delivery before she closes the laptop.
- Thoại: Mây, ngoài khung hình: Bản sao đã tới người nhận.
- Cuối: Confirmed receipt saved, laptop shut, originals packed.

- Ý đồ: Secure the evidence beyond Sơn's body before they choose to enter danger again.
- Bố cục: Use the detail frame for the real upload state, then the laptop lid in the same setup.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây waits for delivery confirmation, saves it and closes the laptop once.
- Diễn xuất: Her pause comes before confirmation; afterward her hands are decisively ready to pack, not celebratory.
- Điểm nét: Show one clear receipt state rather than scrolling several unreadable screens.
- Chất liệu: Scuffed laptop edges and red book corner maintain object continuity while the device closes.
- Âm nổi: One modest chime is motivated by delivery, followed by the physical lid sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Do not close before delivery; leave the completed handover as the last information before the factory.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Laptop confirmation fades into distant factory pumps before the roof-vent shot. No repeat unlocking montage.

### CẢNH 26: Xưởng Bên Sông
*Thời gian truyện: D5 afternoon*
Lấy bằng chứng hiện trường và thoát bằng đường đã khảo sát.

**Câu hỏi:** Họ có lấy được chứng cứ hiện trường và rút trước khi bị chặn hết đường?
**Móc vào:** Ống thông gió cho nhìn thấy thứ trong sổ đang tồn tại ngoài đời.
**Điểm bẻ:** Mây giữ được mẫu nguyên niêm phong, đường xuống sông biến thành lối thoát thật.
**Gieo / trả:** Đường sông dẫn sang video của Hạ, tránh thêm một cuộc rượt vô nghĩa.

**Clip 1 · J · LAB_ROOF · W**
- Đầu: The pair crouched behind a roof vent overlooking the factory yard.
- Hành động: Mây films the loading emblem and the same seal shown in the accounts.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Phone aimed at the matched shipment seal, Sơn watching the route.

- Ý đồ: Make the written account acquire a real place and a risk of being seen.
- Bố cục: Keep the roof vent as foreground cover, yard seal visible through the same opening.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây films from below the vent edge, Sơn watching the route rather than leaning over her.
- Diễn xuất: They work quietly, with brief controlled checks instead of exaggerated stealth faces.
- Điểm nét: Retain enough depth to compare the existing seal shape without inventing readable labels.
- Chất liệu: Old roof grit and daylight on the loading yard replace the motel's paper evidence.
- Âm nổi: Distant pumps stay even, while a faint phone-handling rustle remains subordinate.
- Nhịp diễn: Reveal the matching mark before the route check, creating evidence and exposure in one view.

**Clip 2 · CUT · LAB · T**
- Đầu: Both inside the loading aisle, river door visible ahead.
- Hành động: Sơn takes one sealed sample carton from a documented shelf as Mây films the location.
- Thoại: Sơn, ngoài khung hình: Niêm phong nguyên. Đừng mở.
- Cuối: Single carton in Mây's evidence bag, shelf location recorded.

- Ý đồ: Show that preserving evidence now matters as much as escaping with it.
- Bố cục: Track on the established loading aisle, shelf and river door sharing the same depth.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn takes one sealed carton; Mây films its original position before bagging it.
- Diễn xuất: His care contrasts with his former role guarding the place, without a nostalgic glance.
- Điểm nét: Keep seal intact and shelf location readable, no close-up of usable chemistry instructions.
- Chất liệu: Dust on the shelf and clean packaging edges distinguish stored stock from their handled sample.
- Âm nổi: His instruction sits over pumps and one carton brush, not an action music cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Record location first, remove the single carton next, end when it is secure.

**Clip 3 · CUT · LAB · H**
- Đầu: A guard turning into the loading aisle behind them.
- Hành động: Sơn holds the guard behind a closing sliding screen while Mây reaches the river door.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Guard separated by the screen, Mây at the open river door.

- Ý đồ: Use the pursuit to narrow the escape rather than add another identical fight.
- Bố cục: Keep the turning guard, sliding screen and river door inside one defensive-action frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn holds the guard on the far side of the moving screen as Mây reaches the door.
- Diễn xuất: Both concentrate on gaining separation; neither pauses to threaten or punish him.
- Điểm nét: The closing barrier and Mây's available exit must remain readable despite modest handheld motion.
- Chất liệu: Worn screen metal vibrates naturally; industrial dust stays on existing surfaces rather than billowing theatrically.
- Âm nổi: A short screen rattle and quick breath cut through the steady machinery.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete the separation once and end with Mây able to move, no second attempted grapple.

**Clip 4 · CUT · LAB_DOCK · W**
- Đầu: Mây at the dock gate, another guard approaching from the stairs.
- Hành động: She loops a dock rope around the gate and pulls it shut between them.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Đi tiếp. Tôi giữ cửa.
- Cuối: Gate held by rope, both travelers beyond it, no new cuffs.

- Ý đồ: Give Mây her own practical choice after the two city cuffs are already spent.
- Bố cục: Use the gate and stairs in the wide composition so the approaching guard's route is obvious.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She pulls the existing dock rope across the gate and closes it between groups.
- Diễn xuất: She stays focused on the obstacle's purpose, not smiling at cleverness or replacing absent cuffs.
- Điểm nét: Keep rope, gate closure and both travelers on the outer side visually connected.
- Chất liệu: Wet dock boards, ordinary mooring rope and worn gate iron give the improvised barrier weight.
- Âm nổi: Her brief direction rises above gate movement and river sound, without new alarms.
- Nhịp diễn: The barrier earns only a short lead; she must leave as soon as it holds.

**Clip 5 · CONTINUE · LAB_DOCK · W**
- Đầu: Gate held by rope, both travelers beyond it, no new cuffs.
- Hành động: Sơn steadies a tied workboat as Mây steps aboard, then releases the mooring.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Both aboard the workboat, loose rope clear of the propeller.

- Ý đồ: Transfer danger from the blocked dock into a moving shared escape.
- Bố cục: Preserve exactly the preceding wide setup, gate position and boat relation at the seam.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn steadies the tied boat, Mây steps in once, then the mooring is released.
- Diễn xuất: Their coordination is cautious because of his left wrist, not effortless stunt timing.
- Điểm nét: Keep boarding feet and the line visible, avoiding a cut to separate hands or engine controls.
- Chất liệu: The hull shifts against the dock and rope slack behaves with the boat's movement.
- Âm nổi: Boat creak and small water slap register before the engine becomes dominant.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow the weight transfer to settle before release, ending with both safely aboard.

**Clip 6 · CUT · RIVER · W**
- Đầu: Workboat clearing the factory dock, carton secured under the bench.
- Hành động: Sơn keeps the boat on the open channel, Mây sends the recorded clip.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Giờ họ có cả sổ và xưởng.
- Cuối: Factory falling behind, phone transmission confirmed.

- Ý đồ: Let escape deliver a completed evidence transmission rather than only a prettier landscape.
- Bố cục: Use the wide river view to keep both travelers, secured carton space and shrinking factory related.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn holds the open channel, Mây checks one transmission receipt without standing in the boat.
- Diễn xuất: Relief is practical and incomplete; neither looks back as though the threat has ended.
- Điểm nét: Keep the factory legible as the source now receding, phone confirmation secondary to safe travel.
- Chất liệu: Brown river water, low evening glare and worn bench wood anchor the boat in the same world.
- Âm nổi: Engine settles into a continuous working rhythm beneath her short statement.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the factory lose scale as delivery confirms, preparing room for the intimate interruption next.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep the established boat engine as the view changes from wide channel to the two travelers. No repeat departure from the dock.

### CẢNH 27: Hạ Trong Khung Hình
*Thời gian truyện: D5 dusk*
Hạ gửi manh mối bằng hành động trong video, xác nhận còn sống.

**Câu hỏi:** Hạ còn sống, và cô đang cố chỉ cho họ thấy điều gì?
**Móc vào:** Sơn giảm tiếng máy để nghe một giọng anh đã tưởng mất.
**Điểm bẻ:** Hạ gửi dấu thay vì chỉ đọc lời của kẻ giữ, tuyến tàu được nhận diện nhưng phòng giam vẫn chưa rõ.
**Gieo / trả:** Kẹp tóc và trí quan sát của Hạ chuẩn bị cho cửa kỹ thuật ở hồi cuối.

**Clip 1 · CUT · RIVER · M**
- Đầu: Both travelers in the boat, factory now distant behind them.
- Hành động: The unknown caller sends a short video, Sơn reduces the engine noise.
- Thoại: Người gọi giấu tên, qua thiết bị: Anh đòi thấy. Đây.
- Cuối: Phone angled between both travelers, boat idling in open water.

- Ý đồ: Make receiving Hạ's image interrupt an escape that had briefly found a rhythm.
- Bố cục: Keep the phone between both travelers, boat space and receding shore still recognizable.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn reduces the engine noise before they angle the received image toward both faces.
- Diễn xuất: His urgency shows through careful handling, while Mây waits rather than grabbing the phone.
- Điểm nét: Their listening and shared device remain primary, with no floating screen or caller body.
- Chất liệu: Dusk light is softer on their travel-worn clothes, water still moving behind them.
- Âm nổi: The motor drops naturally, allowing the filtered caller to enter without a music change.
- Nhịp diễn: Reduction of noise comes first, anticipation second, received proof last.

**Clip 2 · CUT · VIDEO_ROOM · C**
- Đầu: Hạ alone in a plain bright room, camera at her eye level.
- Hành động: She says Sơn's name and deliberately turns her hair clip toward the ventilation window.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Sơn. Em còn đây.
- Cuối: Silver clip held beside a small fan with a ferry emblem beyond it.

- Ý đồ: Let Hạ use the forced recording as an opportunity to send her own information.
- Bố cục: Frame her face with the existing vent beside it, clip close enough to share attention.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She turns the silver clip toward the window deliberately, without a broad pointing performance.
- Diễn xuất: She speaks evenly for whoever holds the camera, then lets her eyes briefly indicate the clue.
- Điểm nét: Keep her eyes readable and the ferry emblem recognizable as a simple shape, not text.
- Chất liệu: Plain room surfaces and fan light make confinement banal rather than stylized torture imagery.
- Âm nổi: Her voice carries slight phone compression over the low fan hum, no thriller score.
- Nhịp diễn: Deliver the name first, then the deliberate clip gesture, holding its result long enough to notice.

**Clip 3 · CUT · RIVER · M**
- Đầu: Sơn and Mây watching the phone in the boat.
- Hành động: Mây freezes the received video locally after the meaningful gesture, not the film image.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Nó muốn mình nhìn phía sau.
- Cuối: Mây pointing at the vent detail on the phone, Sơn steadying it.

- Ý đồ: Turn proof of survival into evidence that Hạ is helping the search.
- Bố cục: Frame their faces and the actual phone, not an invented enlarged hologram between them.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây pauses only the received video locally; Sơn steadies the physical device in his sound hand.
- Diễn xuất: Mây's recognition is quiet and specific, while Sơn has to move from relief back to attention.
- Điểm nét: Keep the stopped vent detail on the phone readable as context; the film's people and water keep moving.
- Chất liệu: Screen reflection can move with the boat but must not obscure the identified fan shape.
- Âm nổi: Engine idles at its established level, one finger tap motivates the playback pause.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the meaningful gesture be noticed before her explanation, avoiding a second full video replay.

**Clip 4 · CUT · RIVER · C**
- Đầu: Sơn on the live audio line, Mây preserving the video.
- Hành động: The caller demands he remove Bà Hai before the departure deadline.
- Thoại: Người gọi giấu tên, qua thiết bị: Bà Hai ra khỏi cuộc. Hạ mới về.
- Cuối: Sơn ending the demand without agreeing to kill.

- Ý đồ: Make the caller try to reclaim control immediately after Hạ has reclaimed a little agency.
- Bố cục: Keep close on Sơn with Mây remaining in the established boat depth, recording intact.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He listens to the demand, then ends it without offering a murder promise or argument.
- Diễn xuất: His restraint hardens; the decision to end the line is small but unequivocal.
- Điểm nét: Hold his eyes as he separates concern for Hạ from obedience to the caller.
- Chất liệu: Dusk light falls across a tired face, with the closed phone casing ordinary and handled.
- Âm nổi: Filtered demand stays dry against quiet water; no villain bass effect or laughter added.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the condition finish, then a short consideration, then the single disconnection.

**Clip 5 · CUT · RIVER · M**
- Đầu: Mây comparing the ferry emblem to the dispatch paperwork.
- Hành động: She identifies the holding company's coastal route rather than an exact room.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Cùng tuyến tàu. Hạ đang gần biển.
- Cuối: Route marked toward Hòn Sóng, video saved with evidence.

- Ý đồ: Convert Hạ's clue into a useful route while preserving uncertainty about the actual room.
- Bố cục: Keep paperwork and phone between Mây and Sơn within the existing medium composition.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She compares the emblem to the dispatch route and marks the coastal direction once.
- Diễn xuất: Her confidence is limited to what matches, resisting a triumphant declaration of exact location.
- Điểm nét: Both comparison sources remain visible enough to understand, with no legible generated addresses.
- Chất liệu: Creased receipts and a scuffed phone connect the earlier paper trail to a human signal.
- Âm nổi: Paper movement and river bed continue beneath her concise conclusion.
- Nhịp diễn: Match shape, identify route, then end on the still-incomplete map rather than a complete rescue answer.

**Clip 6 · CUT · RIVER · W**
- Đầu: Boat bow facing the coastal channel, daylight fading.
- Hành động: Sơn commits to returning for a controlled exchange with police support.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Đưa cô ấy ra chỗ sáng. Rồi bắt họ.
- Cuối: Boat heading toward the coast, both watching ahead.

- Ý đồ: Show a commitment to a shared rescue plan rather than another solitary revenge vow.
- Bố cục: Use the boat's bow and coastal channel to give the decision a physical direction.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn keeps the established course, Mây remains with evidence secure rather than moving to a heroic pose.
- Diễn xuất: The line is a plan spoken to a partner, not narration addressed to the audience.
- Điểm nét: Keep both bodies and the direction ahead readable in the wide view.
- Chất liệu: Fading orange water and darkening coast carry time toward the deadline without a graphic clock.
- Âm nổi: Engine resumes a steady travel level after the call, leaving his sentence intelligible.
- Nhịp diễn: The choice lands against continuous forward travel; do not restart the boat or repeat its departure.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: River wind bridges into corridor rain in the distant memory. Do not bring back phone-video fan noise after that video has ended.

### CẢNH 28: Quá Khứ: Cánh Cửa Đầu Tiên
*Thời gian truyện: PAST twenty-five years earlier night / D6 dawn from clip 4*
Sơn chọn bỏ vũ khí hiện tại sau khi nhớ lần đầu gây hại.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn có thể chọn khác lần đầu anh đã không quay đi không?
**Móc vào:** Một âm thanh gia đình bình thường khiến cánh cửa đáng sợ hơn hình ảnh bạo lực.
**Điểm bẻ:** Ký ức khép cửa được trả lời bằng việc giao cả vũ khí và chìa cho Mây.
**Gieo / trả:** Sáu vạch đưa đồng hồ truyện tới ngày6, radio báo cái giá đang chờ dưới thị trấn.

**Clip 1 · L · OLD_CORRIDOR · W**
- Đầu: Twenty-year-old Sơn outside a closed wooden door, new gloves tucked in his pocket.
- Hành động: He hears an ordinary family sound inside and hesitates before knocking.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Lần đầu, tôi còn kịp quay đi.
- Cuối: Young Sơn's bare right hand just below the door knocker.

- Ý đồ: Give the first crime moral weight through the ordinary life beyond a closed door.
- Bố cục: Hold young Sơn, bare hand and door in the same wide corridor frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He comes close enough to knock, then leaves his hand just short of contact.
- Diễn xuất: His hesitation is real calculation interrupted by a household sound, not a haunted retrospective pose.
- Điểm nét: Keep the hand and door readable, with no glimpse of an invented victim.
- Chất liệu: Old paint, modest door hardware and rain beyond the stairs make the place lived in.
- Âm nổi: An ordinary muffled family sound stays unidentifiable beneath the older narrator, without screams.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the possibility of turning away exist before the knock is implied by the lowered hand.

**Clip 2 · CONTINUE · OLD_CORRIDOR · W**
- Đầu: Young Sơn's bare right hand just below the door knocker.
- Hành động: The door opens inward, Sơn enters, camera remains outside without showing harm.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Door closed, empty corridor, rain audible beyond the stairwell.

- Ý đồ: Leave the irreversible act outside the image while showing that he chooses to enter.
- Bố cục: Preserve the exact wide frame, corridor depth and hand position from the preceding seam.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: The door opens inward, young Sơn enters once, camera never follows him inside.
- Diễn xuất: His last visible hesitation ends in movement rather than a theatrical change of expression.
- Điểm nét: Hold the doorway as the sole focus of the action; no internal insert or shadow violence.
- Chất liệu: The same painted wood closes back into the corridor's ordinary surfaces.
- Âm nổi: Hinge, short footstep and rain remain; add no gunshot, impact or implied victim vocalization.
- Nhịp diễn: Entry is brief; the closed door and empty corridor carry the remaining uncomfortable silence.

**Clip 3 · CUT · OLD_CORRIDOR · M**
- Đầu: Young Sơn stepping back into the corridor after an unshown interval.
- Hành động: He looks at his bare hands, puts on the new gloves without relief.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Tôi đã không quay đi.
- Cuối: Both gloved hands lowered, young Sơn turning away.

- Ý đồ: Show the gloves as an unsuccessful attempt to separate himself from what he did.
- Bố cục: Use the medium frame to retain both bare hands and his young face as he exits.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He looks at his hands, puts the existing gloves on once, then turns away.
- Diễn xuất: His expression stays contained; the absence of relief matters more than visible tears.
- Điểm nét: Keep hand action and eyes sharing useful depth, without a fetishized leather insert.
- Chất liệu: New leather sits against a cheap black suit, ordinary rain light on the corridor wall.
- Âm nổi: Glove pull is small beneath the narrator; the household door remains silent.
- Nhịp diễn: Bare hands must register before he covers them, then leave no satisfying flourish after dressing.

**Clip 4 · MATCH · CABIN · M**
- Đầu: Present-day Sơn at the cabin table, Mây beside the closed handgun case.
- Hành động: He passes the complete locked case and its key to Mây.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Tôi không mang thứ này nữa.
- Cuối: Entire handgun case in Mây's custody, Sơn unarmed.

- Ý đồ: Answer the remembered failure with a present-day relinquishment that cannot be mistaken for concealment.
- Bố cục: Match the hand region into the cabin medium setup, whole locked case and key visible.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn passes both complete items to Mây, withdrawing empty hands rather than keeping a hidden component.
- Diễn xuất: He makes the decision plainly, Mây receives it without congratulating him.
- Điểm nét: Keep the full case and separate key in the same readable area, not symbolic fragments.
- Chất liệu: Worn cabin table replaces corridor paint, dawn cooling his older ungloved skin.
- Âm nổi: Case weight and key contact sit beneath the line; do not add a weapon-cocking sound.
- Nhịp diễn: The transfer finishes fully before his hands retreat, making his unarmed state the result.

**Clip 5 · CUT · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: Four marks on the frame with two small travel-date notes beside them.
- Hành động: Back from the city, Sơn carves the missing day-five and day-six marks in order.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Exactly six marks, two freshest side by side.

- Ý đồ: Restore the passage of two travel days without pretending the missing marks were already carved.
- Bố cục: Frame the existing four tallies with enough wood beside them for two fresh additions.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn adds the day-five and day-six marks in order using the established tool.
- Diễn xuất: The act is deliberate accounting, not frantic countdown performance or a new threat gesture.
- Điểm nét: Keep the changing mark count clear and the same frame surface recognizable throughout.
- Chất liệu: Fresh wood is lighter than the older grooves, with small genuine shavings.
- Âm nổi: Two brief carving scrapes and dawn pine wind replace the remembered corridor sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Let each addition be countable, then lower the tool rather than tracing the whole row again.

**Clip 6 · J · PORCH · W**
- Đầu: Both at the porch looking down on the town road.
- Hành động: They hear an abrupt radio warning, then see market shutters closing.
- Thoại: Thành, qua thiết bị: Dũng quay lại. Quán ông Bảy.
- Cuối: Mây answering her radio, Sơn looking toward Bảy's stall.

- Ý đồ: Break contemplation with a concrete consequence waiting below the hill.
- Bố cục: Use the porch wide view to join the pair's listening with the road and market sightline.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây answers the abrupt warning while Sơn turns toward Bảy's stall below.
- Diễn xuất: They become alert in different ways: she receives details, he searches for the visible problem.
- Điểm nét: Keep the town view readable enough for closing shutters, not an impossible telephoto face reveal.
- Chất liệu: Morning pine shadows and the still-ordinary porch contrast with interrupted business below.
- Âm nổi: The established radio voice cuts into wind, without a new action score resetting the film.
- Nhịp diễn: Warning first, their different reactions next, the changed town last.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Thành radio warning carries into the present-day blocked street. The town threat is the next event, not another flashback.

### CẢNH 29: Cái Giá Của Chuyến Đi
*Thời gian truyện: D6 morning*
Thành bảo vệ dân rồi bị giữ, Sơn kéo Dũng khỏi con tin.

**Câu hỏi:** Mây có cứu Thành mà không kéo dân vào thế đối đầu không?
**Móc vào:** Cảnh chắn tuần tra hỏng báo ngay rằng sự bảo vệ đã bị xuyên thủng.
**Điểm bẻ:** Sơn tự làm mồi để Mây mở đường sau, lựa chọn ở lại bắt đầu có giá.
**Gieo / trả:** Thành bảo vệ lối dân trong khi bị giữ, không chỉ chờ người cứu.

**Clip 1 · OPEN · STREET · W**
- Đầu: Mây and Sơn at the upper road, black car near the coffee stall.
- Hành động: They see a patrol barricade damaged and choose the side lane.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Both concealed behind the side-lane wall, view of the stall clear.

- Ý đồ: Make prior protection visibly breached before the rescue requires another promise of competence.
- Bố cục: Keep damaged barricade, black car and side-lane cover within the same wide street geography.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây stops short of the open road, takes the side lane with Sơn behind her.
- Diễn xuất: Her recognition of the failure is controlled and immediate, not disbelief frozen for effect.
- Điểm nét: Keep damage modest but clear, with the stall view accessible beyond their cover.
- Chất liệu: Bent barrier material and closed shop fronts carry the town's interrupted routine.
- Âm nổi: Muted stall voices and an idling car establish threat without another villain entrance sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Register the breach, choose cover, finish with a clear view of what needs rescuing.

**Clip 2 · CUT · STALL · W**
- Đầu: Thành seated with tied wrists, Bảy held behind the counter.
- Hành động: Dũng demands the ledger while tied Thành keeps his seated body angled between Dũng and the civilian rear-door gap, protecting the exit with his position.
- Thoại: Dũng (@Dũng), nói trong hình: Sổ đâu? Đừng bắt tao hỏi ông già.
- Cuối: Thành watching the rear door, Dũng facing the road.

- Ý đồ: Give Thành something active to protect even while he has lost physical freedom.
- Bố cục: Frame tied Thành, Bảy behind the counter and the rear-door zone in one wide setup.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Thành keeps his seated body oriented to conceal civilian movement, Dũng facing the public road.
- Diễn xuất: Fear shows in his breathing, but his eyes keep checking the useful exit rather than begging.
- Điểm nét: Keep the rear-door line perceptible to viewers without making Dũng turn and notice it.
- Chất liệu: Bent glasses and taut ties show the cost without adding bruises or fresh wounds.
- Âm nổi: Dũng's threat is plain and near, with quieter civilian shuffling already behind the counter.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the audience recognize Thành's protective attention before Dũng finishes demanding the book.

**Clip 3 · CUT · STREET · M**
- Đầu: Sơn and Mây behind the wall, Sơn looking at the rear stall door.
- Hành động: They divide tasks: Sơn draws Dũng away, Mây reaches Thành from behind.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Tôi kéo hắn ra. Cô đưa Thành đi.
- Cuối: Mây taking the side lane, Sơn stepping toward the road.

- Ý đồ: Make the plan a division of responsibility shaped by who is actually in danger.
- Bố cục: Use the wall edge to keep both concealed and the rear stall route in shared view.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn moves toward the road only after Mây has the side-lane path available.
- Diễn xuất: He offers himself as distraction without swagger, she accepts the useful role rather than his authority.
- Điểm nét: Keep their eyelines tied to the two separate routes, no map insert required.
- Chất liệu: Rough wall shade and bright road create a clear boundary they are about to cross.
- Âm nổi: The instruction stays low and quick against distant stall voices, no whispered exposition chorus.
- Nhịp diễn: One look at the rear door, one allocation, then movement in two different directions.

**Clip 4 · L · STREET · W**
- Đầu: Sơn visible in the road, Dũng at the stall threshold.
- Hành động: Sơn calls him and moves toward the market entrance without exposing a weapon.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Dũng. Muốn sổ thì theo tao.
- Cuối: Dũng following Sơn, stall entrance momentarily clear.

- Ý đồ: Turn Sơn's visibility into bait that opens space for someone else rather than a heroic confrontation.
- Bố cục: Keep him in the open road, Dũng at the stall threshold and market entrance ahead.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn calls once and walks toward the market without drawing anything or turning to fight.
- Diễn xuất: His certainty is deliberate performance for Dũng, while his hands stay genuinely empty.
- Điểm nét: Hold the widening gap between Dũng and stall, because that opening is the action's purpose.
- Chất liệu: Sunlit road and cool market mouth distinguish the exposed lure from the safer route.
- Âm nổi: His call should carry across the street naturally, footsteps following without a chase sting.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow Dũng to commit to following before ending on the temporarily clear entrance.

**Clip 5 · CUT · STALL_REAR · M**
- Đầu: Mây entering the stall from the rear, Bảy by Thành's chair.
- Hành động: She cuts Thành's ties while Bảy opens the civilian exit.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Đưa dân ra. Không đuổi theo hắn.
- Cuối: Thành free, Bảy ushering two unnamed civilians out back.

- Ý đồ: Pay off the lure with released people, not another round of threats.
- Bố cục: Frame rear doorway, Thành's chair and Bảy's exit path so the rescue is legible.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây frees the ties once; Bảy immediately guides the existing civilians through the back.
- Diễn xuất: Thành regains breath and attention before standing, while Mây keeps him on the evacuation task.
- Điểm nét: Keep the freed wrists and the open civilian route visible together without a separate cutting insert.
- Chất liệu: Bent glasses, chair legs and narrow threshold preserve the cramped physical setting.
- Âm nổi: Tie release, moving chairs and her short instruction replace the louder street confrontation.
- Nhịp diễn: Free Thành first, move civilians next, end with the rear route functioning rather than celebrating.

**Clip 6 · J · MARKET_ENTRY · W**
- Đầu: Sơn already at the market entrance, Dũng several paces behind.
- Hành động: Sơn ducks into a narrow aisle as a cart blocks Dũng's direct line.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn beyond the cart, Dũng forced to go around.

- Ý đồ: Let the market layout change the terms of pursuit before anyone throws another blow.
- Bố cục: Use the wide entrance view with Sơn beyond the cart and Dũng still approaching it.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn passes through the gap once, cart settling across Dũng's direct path behind him.
- Diễn xuất: He keeps moving instead of waiting for Dũng's reaction; Dũng must reassess rather than teleport around it.
- Điểm nét: Both sides of the obstruction remain readable, preserving the next aisle's screen direction.
- Chất liệu: Cart wheels, shutter edges and concrete show a practical market bottleneck.
- Âm nổi: Wheel rattle and following boots continue into the next scene's aisle sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Finish after the route is divided, giving the following shot new forward movement to perform.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep pursuing footsteps and cut past the entry cart into the aisle. Do not perform the cart obstruction twice.

### CẢNH 30: Lối Qua Chợ Cá
*Thời gian truyện: D6 morning*
Dân hỗ trợ thoát người, lực lượng hỗ trợ xử lý đám truy đuổi.

**Câu hỏi:** Chợ cá có trở thành bẫy của Sơn hay thành nơi dân bị mắc kẹt?
**Móc vào:** Một lối hẹp và xe đá làm khoảng cách quyền lực thay đổi mà không thêm đánh nhau.
**Điểm bẻ:** Đường thoát người thắng cuộc đuổi, Dũng đổi sang ép bằng hạn chuyển Hạ.
**Gieo / trả:** Lời hẹn hải đăng là lối thương lượng, Mây thừa nhận trách nhiệm với Thành.

**Clip 1 · CUT · MARKET · T**
- Đầu: Sơn just past the entry cart inside the market aisle.
- Hành động: Sơn moves deeper along the narrow aisle, keeping a stack of crates between him and pursuers.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn at the aisle junction, pursuers slowed by the crates.

- Ý đồ: Keep pursuit suspense in distance and obstruction rather than multiplying attacks.
- Bố cục: Track with Sơn in the established direction, crate stacks repeatedly separating the two lines.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He moves into the aisle junction while pursuers lose a few steps negotiating the existing crates.
- Diễn xuất: Urgency stays in pace and breath; he does not check behind with repeated dramatic head turns.
- Điểm nét: Keep the next junction readable ahead, no zoom or rack reset to a trailing face.
- Chất liệu: Damp aisle concrete and ordinary stacked containers give his route friction and scale.
- Âm nổi: Continuous footsteps and cart noise replace music-driven acceleration or invented impacts.
- Nhịp diễn: Let distance vary once as crates obstruct, ending at a new choice of aisle.

**Clip 2 · CUT · MARKET · W**
- Đầu: Bảy at a side door with two fish sellers, Sơn approaching.
- Hành động: They move a wheeled ice cart across the empty aisle after Sơn passes.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Đi lối này. Cửa sau trống.
- Cuối: Cart blocking the aisle, civilians already behind a closed door.

- Ý đồ: Make the townspeople useful by closing a route after a person escapes, then protecting themselves.
- Bố cục: Frame Bảy's side door, Sơn passing and the empty aisle the cart will block.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Bảy and the two existing sellers move the cart only after Sơn clears, then retreat inside.
- Diễn xuất: Their concentration is practical, with no cheering or desire to fight the pursuers.
- Điểm nét: Keep cart clearance and the closing civilian door in the same wide view.
- Chất liệu: Melting ice, wet wheels and door metal ground the improvised barrier in daily work.
- Âm nổi: Bảy's direction stays short above wheel scrape, then the door closes the civilian sound away.
- Nhịp diễn: The order matters: Sơn through, cart across, civilians safe, with no replay of the entrance cart.

**Clip 3 · J · MARKET_REAR · W**
- Đầu: Mây and Thành at the rear market exit, support officers arriving behind them.
- Hành động: They take two separated pursuers into custody without involving civilians.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Giữ lối thoát cho dân.
- Cuối: Pursuers held by support officers, rear exit protected.

- Ý đồ: Show support arriving where evacuation needs it rather than materializing to win a fight.
- Bố cục: Use the rear-exit wide view to separate civilian escape from restrained pursuers.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây and Thành hold the safe line while the arriving officers secure the two separated men.
- Diễn xuất: Thành stays useful despite damaged glasses; Mây checks the exit before watching an arrest.
- Điểm nét: Keep the protected lane open in frame, not filled by an unnecessary crowd.
- Chất liệu: Plain work clothing and repaired glasses retain consequences from the stall rescue.
- Âm nổi: Commands and cuff handling remain local and measured, with no crowd battle layer.
- Nhịp diễn: Arrival and custody should be economical; finish on a usable rear exit, the real gain.

**Clip 4 · CUT · MARKET · M**
- Đầu: Dũng at the far end of the obstructed aisle, Sơn beyond reach.
- Hành động: He threatens the day-seven transfer rather than another immediate fight.
- Thoại: Dũng (@Dũng), nói trong hình: Đợi thêm, Hạ lên tàu mất.
- Cuối: Dũng lowering his phone with the ship schedule visible as shapes.

- Ý đồ: Let Dũng recover pressure through time after losing immediate control of the aisle.
- Bố cục: Keep the obstruction between him and Sơn visible in the medium framing.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng displays the existing schedule on his phone, lowers it without advancing into another grapple.
- Diễn xuất: He speaks as someone willing to wait for the deadline, making stillness threatening.
- Điểm nét: The phone is evidence of leverage, not readable text requiring a screen insert.
- Chất liệu: Cold reflected light from wet crates contrasts with his exposed arms and heavy posture.
- Âm nổi: His plain threat interrupts market movement, no ominous device buzz added afterward.
- Nhịp diễn: Let Sơn understand the deadline before the phone drops, ending their physical deadlock with a different pressure.

**Clip 5 · CUT · MARKET_ENTRY · W**
- Đầu: Dũng retreating toward his car with his two remaining men.
- Hành động: He leaves a meeting place and time before withdrawing.
- Thoại: Dũng (@Dũng), nói trong hình: Bà Hai chờ ở hải đăng tối nay.
- Cuối: Black car backing away, Sơn staying inside the market entrance.

- Ý đồ: Move the story from chase to dangerous negotiation through Dũng's withdrawal.
- Bố cục: Keep the market entrance, retreating men and car relationship clear in the wide shot.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng backs toward the car with his two men, leaving Sơn inside rather than dragging him along.
- Diễn xuất: The invitation is delivered as a demand that will outlast this retreat, not a defeated snarl.
- Điểm nét: Hold enough street depth to show departure without a new car-start close-up.
- Chất liệu: Road dust and dark car surfaces contrast with the wet interior from which Sơn watches.
- Âm nổi: Engine and tire movement begin only as the car withdraws, under the meeting instruction.
- Nhịp diễn: End with Dũng genuinely leaving and Sơn holding his position, so the next task is a choice.

**Clip 6 · L · MARKET_REAR · M**
- Đầu: Thành seated safely at the rear exit, one glasses arm bent.
- Hành động: Mây acknowledges the cost of her absence and restores the patrol roster.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Lần này chị giữ đủ người ở đây.
- Cuối: Thành taking the replacement roster, Sơn listening nearby.

- Ý đồ: Make restoring protection an acknowledgement of cost instead of another empty tactical assurance.
- Bố cục: Frame Thành seated safely, damaged glasses visible, Sơn present but not the center of the apology.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây hands the replacement roster to Thành without demanding that he reassure her.
- Diễn xuất: She speaks directly and quietly, he accepts responsibility while still recovering from fear.
- Điểm nét: Keep her look toward him and the practical roster visible together, no reaction insert.
- Chất liệu: The bent glasses and temporary rear-exit seat keep the consequences materially present.
- Âm nổi: The market begins ordinary work at a distance, leaving the line without heroic scoring.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow her admission a beat, then finish on Thành taking the actual task, not a prolonged promise.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Let market noise end before establishing night lighthouse wind and Bà Hai already waiting. No second market confrontation.

### CẢNH 31: Bà Hai Ở Hải Đăng
*Thời gian truyện: D6 night*
Bà Hai muốn lấy lại Sơn, anh đặt điều kiện gặp trực tiếp Hạ.

**Câu hỏi:** Bà Hai muốn trả Hạ hay chỉ muốn lấy Sơn và Kiên về tay mình?
**Móc vào:** Bà gọi về nhà từ nơi cao gió lạnh, lời thân tình đi cùng Dũng canh lối.
**Điểm bẻ:** Bà không biết Hạ đang ở đâu, Sơn giữ bản sao và buộc bà chấp nhận gặp công khai.
**Gieo / trả:** Bà cần Sơn tới mức phải ngăn Dũng; sự lệ thuộc của trùm được hé trước cao trào.

**Clip 1 · OPEN · LIGHTHOUSE · W**
- Đầu: Bà Hai and Dũng on the gallery, Sơn emerging from the stairs.
- Hành động: Sơn stops outside Dũng's reach with one copied ledger page.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Về với má. Chuyện này má lo.
- Cuối: Copied page in Sơn's right hand, stair route behind him clear.

- Ý đồ: Let the invitation home compete with the visible fact that Dũng can block the stairs.
- Bố cục: Keep Bà Hai, Dũng and the stair emergence in the same wind-exposed wide frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn stops outside reach, copy in right hand and route behind him kept open.
- Diễn xuất: She sounds familiar rather than ceremonial; he must resist the bodily habit of obeying her.
- Điểm nét: Keep the paper unmistakably thin, not the red original ledger suddenly appearing in his hands.
- Chất liệu: Violet silk and rough iron rail move differently in the wind, making shelter conspicuously absent.
- Âm nổi: Her soft voice stays close despite the gale, without a supernatural whisper treatment.
- Nhịp diễn: Let him reach his chosen stopping point before the invitation, giving distance emotional meaning.

**Clip 2 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Bà Hai facing Sơn across the gallery.
- Hành động: She offers to clear his old debts if he brings Kiên under her control.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Đưa Kiên tới. Má lấy lại người cho con.
- Cuối: Sơn listening without stepping closer.

- Ý đồ: Expose her need for Kiên while she continues to present control as motherly protection.
- Bố cục: Hold the established gallery relation, with the gap between them as important as their faces.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She offers terms without stepping closer, and Sơn refuses to fill the space she leaves.
- Diễn xuất: Her reassurance should be almost habitual, then sharpen slightly on the demanded delivery.
- Điểm nét: Keep attention on whether he moves, not a decorative push toward the sea.
- Chất liệu: Her composed clothing remains ordered against the wind, his ordinary jacket less protected.
- Âm nổi: Wind stays consistent under the offer, with no fresh threat motif or distant invented vehicle.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the promised rescue its persuasive beat before the condition becomes clear.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Sơn listening without stepping closer.
- Hành động: Sơn asks whether she knows Hạ's holding place, Bà Hai cannot answer.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Má bán cô ấy mà không biết đi đâu?
- Cuối: Bà Hai avoiding the question, Sơn looking toward the stairs.

- Ý đồ: Make the question expose the limit of her supposed maternal knowledge.
- Bố cục: Retain precisely the preceding medium composition and distance rather than switching to a revealing close-up.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn asks from the same position; Bà Hai avoids answering by holding her ground and gaze.
- Diễn xuất: His use of má should carry disappointed recognition, while her first hesitation is minute.
- Điểm nét: Keep both faces readable so the missing answer belongs to her, not narration.
- Chất liệu: Wind touches her sleeve and his jacket while neither uses weather as a performance distraction.
- Âm nổi: Let the question finish cleanly, then leave only the already-established wind for her silence.
- Nhịp diễn: Do not rush his turn toward the stairs; the unanswered gap is the scene's new fact.

**Clip 4 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · I**
- Đầu: Copied ledger page held above the gallery rail.
- Hành động: Sơn shows the signature and folds the copy back into his pocket.
- Thoại: Sơn, ngoài khung hình: Bản gốc có người giữ. Đụng tôi cũng vô ích.
- Cuối: Folded copy returned to Sơn's inner pocket, no book left behind.

- Ý đồ: Show that he keeps the evidence boundary even while still emotionally caught by her.
- Bố cục: Use one detail plane for copied paper, signature shapes, pocket and gallery rail below.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He displays only the page already brought, then folds and returns it to his own pocket.
- Diễn xuất: His hands stay measured; the fold is a refusal to leave anything within her reach.
- Điểm nét: Keep the copy thin and original absent, with no legible names generated on paper.
- Chất liệu: The light page catches wind against dark jacket cloth, demanding a steady grip.
- Âm nổi: Paper creases and his off-screen explanation remain clear without an artificial threatening bass.
- Nhịp diễn: The showing is brief; spend the end on the page being securely back with him.

**Clip 5 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Dũng taking a step toward Sơn, Bà Hai raises one hand.
- Hành động: She stops Dũng and accepts a public exchange at the harbour.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Má tới. Con giữ lời.
- Cuối: Dũng stopped behind Bà Hai's hand, Sơn by the stairs.

- Ý đồ: Reveal that her remaining power depends on stopping Dũng, not simply ordering Sơn.
- Bố cục: Frame raised hand, Dũng's halted step and Sơn's stair position in the same medium setup.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng starts forward once; her hand interrupts him before he closes the usable gap.
- Diễn xuất: She accepts with a trace of calculation under tenderness, Dũng resenting the check but obeying.
- Điểm nét: Keep the spatial stop visible rather than substituting a tight bracelet insert.
- Chất liệu: The left jade bracelet remains on the raised wrist, sleeves lifting naturally in the gallery wind.
- Âm nổi: One halted footstep and her short acceptance carry the power change, no added ring or chime.
- Nhịp diễn: Let viewers see she needs this agreement before her final instruction tries to sound absolute.

**Clip 6 · L · LIGHTHOUSE_BASE · W**
- Đầu: Mây waiting below the tower where she monitored the meeting.
- Hành động: Sơn rejoins her and reports the exchange will include both parties.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Họ sẽ tới cảng. Phải thấy Hạ trước.
- Cuối: Two figures leaving by the same open main path.

- Ý đồ: Return the solitary family confrontation to the shared rescue responsibility below.
- Bố cục: Use the base-wide frame to show Mây waiting and the same main path leading away.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn rejoins her without needing support yet, both leaving by the route already established.
- Diễn xuất: He reports facts tersely, withholding the private wound rather than reciting the entire meeting.
- Điểm nét: Keep both figures and the open exit readable, no return to Bà Hai inside this clip.
- Chất liệu: Cool night stone and moving grass contrast with the gallery's intimate bargaining.
- Âm nổi: Their footsteps take over from tower wind as the next clinic room tone approaches.
- Nhịp diễn: One report, one departure, finishing away from the tower so the scene truly advances.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Introduce clinic room tone over footsteps leaving the tower. The clinic meeting remains the same night.

### CẢNH 32: Đường Rút Cho Người Sống
*Thời gian truyện: D6 night*
Kế hoạch cứu người có hỗ trợ, Tê không phải mẹo giả chết.

**Câu hỏi:** Kế hoạch có còn cứu người khi cơ thể và lời hứa không chịu theo đồng hồ?
**Móc vào:** Mẫu thuốc được khóa lại, mối nguy được nhận biết chứ không được biến thành công cụ thần kỳ.
**Điểm bẻ:** Linh bác sự chắc chắn, Mây chốt đường rút thay vì một mẹo thắng.
**Gieo / trả:** Chuẩn bị tuyến cứu Sơn và vai trò an toàn của người dân, giảm giải thích lại giao kèo.

**Clip 1 · J · CLINIC · M**
- Đầu: Linh examining the sealed sample carton without opening it.
- Hành động: Mây asks him to stand by near the planned exchange, outside the danger area.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Ông giúp phần cứu người. Không ra giữa cảng.
- Cuối: Linh putting the sealed carton into locked evidence storage.

- Ý đồ: Turn knowledge of Tê into a duty to prepare care rather than a clever method to exploit.
- Bố cục: Keep Linh, sealed carton and Mây in the same medium clinic arrangement.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He examines only the unopened packaging, then places it in the existing locked storage.
- Diễn xuất: Mây separates his medical role from the danger zone, Linh attentive without eager complicity.
- Điểm nét: Keep the seal readable as intact; do not reveal contents or invented preparation details.
- Chất liệu: Green curtain and worn white coat provide the ordinary clinic context for a frightening substance.
- Âm nổi: Cupboard latch and neutral ventilation under a clear sentence are enough, without a medical alarm cue.
- Nhịp diễn: The carton leaves useable reach before discussion continues, establishing a boundary through action.

**Clip 2 · CONTINUE · CLINIC · M**
- Đầu: Linh putting the sealed carton into locked evidence storage.
- Hành động: Sơn explains that Dũng uses Tê to take people alive, Linh listens carefully.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Hắn muốn giữ người sống để ép khai.
- Cuối: Linh recording that risk on the emergency note.

- Ý đồ: Make the enemy's preference for living leverage feel more dangerous than a death threat.
- Bố cục: Preserve the same medium view with all three people remaining in their established places.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn explains without handling the sample, Linh recording the risk in his existing emergency note.
- Diễn xuất: His matter-of-fact knowledge suggests familiarity he is ashamed to possess, not expertise to admire.
- Điểm nét: Keep Linh listening and Sơn speaking readable together; no illustrative cutaway to an application device.
- Chất liệu: Paper, cloth and tired faces stay ordinary, refusing a glamorous laboratory aesthetic.
- Âm nổi: His voice stays dry over the clinic bed, with a small note-taking sound afterward.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the implication of being kept alive land before Linh responds about uncertainty.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · CLINIC · M**
- Đầu: Linh recording that risk on the emergency note.
- Hành động: Linh rejects any guaranteed waking time, Mây crosses out the tentative recovery estimate on the existing tactical sketch, and Sơn accepts the uncertainty.
- Thoại: Bác sĩ Linh (@Bác sĩ Linh), nói trong hình: Gặp thuốc thì đưa về ngay. Đừng chờ phút nào.
- Cuối: Sơn nodding, Mây with the crossed-out recovery estimate on the tactical sketch, route plan still available.

- Ý đồ: Remove the comfort of a precise rescue clock while preserving a practical path forward.
- Bố cục: Keep the same three-person medium composition, emergency note and sketch only supporting the faces.
