- Vị trí / chuyển động: Linh rejects certainty; Mây visibly withdraws the assumption while Sơn acknowledges the limitation.
- Diễn xuất: Linh speaks from responsibility, not mystique, and Sơn's nod should contain real apprehension.
- Điểm nét: Hold their listening reactions without cutting to a countdown or imagined heartbeat graphic.
- Chất liệu: Clinic practical light makes the paper plan less authoritative than living faces.
- Âm nổi: The warning is delivered plainly, no rising timer sound or sudden monitor beeps.
- Nhịp diễn: Leave a brief silence after the instruction, making uncertain recovery part of the risk.

**Clip 4 · CUT · CLINIC · I**
- Đầu: Simple harbour map on the desk, two protected exits marked by shapes.
- Hành động: Mây assigns rescue extraction to the van and civilians to evacuation only.
- Thoại: Mây, ngoài khung hình: Dân rút trước. Người của tôi giữ hai đầu.
- Cuối: Van route separated from the police approach, map complete.

- Ý đồ: Distinguish the route that brings a person out from the route that brings police in.
- Bố cục: Use the detail shot for the existing map, with two exits and separate path shapes legible.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây follows the van extraction route with one finger, keeping civilian movement outside the contact area.
- Diễn xuất: Her hand is economical, indicating one priority rather than rehearsing the whole operation again.
- Điểm nét: Keep both route shapes in the same focal field, no animated arrows or readable tactical labels.
- Chất liệu: Creased paper and the desk edge give the diagram a finite, imperfect scale.
- Âm nổi: A finger sliding on paper supports the off-screen instruction, clinic room tone unchanged.
- Nhịp diễn: Explain the separation once, finishing on the clear rescue path rather than more positions.

**Clip 5 · CUT · CLINIC · M**
- Đầu: Sơn facing Mây across the plan.
- Hành động: He agrees to withdraw if Hạ cannot be seen directly at the meeting.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Không thấy Hạ, không trao gì hết.
- Cuối: Mây's finger on the abort route, Sơn acknowledging.

- Ý đồ: Let Sơn accept a condition that might stop him from reaching the woman he wants to save.
- Bố cục: Frame him across the plan from Mây, with Linh present rather than disappearing between takes.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây holds the abort route with a finger while Sơn gives his agreement without touching it.
- Diễn xuất: His pause should reveal the cost of promising withdrawal, then settle into a deliberate answer.
- Điểm nét: Keep his eyes and her fixed finger readable, avoiding a heroic isolated portrait.
- Chất liệu: The bandage on his left wrist remains visible beside the plan he cannot control.
- Âm nổi: Quiet paper and ventilation stay beneath the line; do not add confirmation radio chatter.
- Nhịp diễn: The assent comes after consideration, leaving enough silence to believe he means to honor it.

**Clip 6 · L · CLINIC · W**
- Đầu: Linh placing ordinary monitoring equipment in his bag.
- Hành động: Mây radios the agreed positions while Sơn steps back from the plan.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Ưu tiên đưa cả hai người ra.
- Cuối: Medical bag ready, route map with Mây, Sơn unarmed.

- Ý đồ: Translate the limits just agreed into a prepared response, not another explanation of the bargain.
- Bố cục: Keep medical bag, map holder and unarmed Sơn together in one practical wide setup.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Linh packs his existing equipment while Mây confirms roles, Sơn stepping back to leave space.
- Diễn xuất: They work with sober concentration, without the triumphant rhythm of a guaranteed victory montage.
- Điểm nét: Keep the bag and map destinations clear while faces remain useful, no gear catalogue inserts.
- Chất liệu: Ordinary equipment in a worn bag supports care rather than miracle medicine.
- Âm nổi: Bag handling, radio response bed and her single instruction remain restrained and motivated.
- Nhịp diễn: Finish preparations without repeating their earlier positions; the next memory should interrupt readiness emotionally.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Flower-shop shutter sound introduces the memory after the current radio check. No repeated medical explanation.

### CẢNH 33: Quá Khứ: Một Lời Hứa Chưa Xong
*Thời gian truyện: PAST one month earlier night*
Hạ đòi lời nói thật, bông hoa chỉ thuộc đêm quá khứ.

**Câu hỏi:** Một lời nói thật có đủ để Hạ vẫn mở cửa cho Sơn không?
**Móc vào:** Cửa chừa một khoảng cho anh đứng lại tự chọn.
**Điểm bẻ:** Sơn thôi hứa chắc sẽ quay lại, Hạ chỉ xin một bữa sáng bình thường.
**Gieo / trả:** Bông hoa chỉ ở quá khứ, lời hẹn nhỏ tăng ý nghĩa cho việc mở đồng hồ ở52.

**Clip 1 · J · FLOWER · W**
- Đầu: Hạ lowering half the shop shutter, Sơn still inside.
- Hành động: She leaves a gap for him and waits rather than filling the silence.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Mai anh còn quay lại không?
- Cuối: Half-closed shutter behind Sơn, Hạ at the counter.

- Ý đồ: Give Hạ the power to leave an opening while asking for honesty instead of reassurance.
- Bố cục: Use the shutter gap and counter in the wide composition, Sơn still on the inside edge.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She stops the shutter halfway, then waits without walking toward him to fill the distance.
- Diễn xuất: Her question is direct and tired of evasion, not a foreknowledge of coming tragedy.
- Điểm nét: Keep the unused opening visible alongside her face and his hesitation at the doorway.
- Chất liệu: Warm shop bulbs, wet pavement beyond and shutter slats make the possible departure tangible.
- Âm nổi: Shutter rattle settles before the question, leaving the ordinary shop room tone.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the unclosed doorway hold the choice after she speaks, rather than filling it with movement.

**Clip 2 · CUT · FLOWER · M**
- Đầu: Sơn with both gloves on, looking at Hạ directly.
- Hành động: He admits the job is dangerous instead of promising an effortless return.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Anh không biết. Anh không muốn nói dối nữa.
- Cuối: Hạ listening, Sơn no longer touching the door handle.

- Ý đồ: Make an honest uncertain answer more intimate than another promise to return safely.
- Bố cục: Hold both faces with Sơn still gloved and close to the established door position.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: His hand leaves the handle as he admits uncertainty, Hạ staying where she chose to wait.
- Diễn xuất: The confession should feel unpracticed; he does not soften it with a reassuring smile afterward.
- Điểm nét: Keep his exposed expression primary while her listening remains readable, no sudden focus trick.
- Chất liệu: Black gloves and warm flower colors hold the tension between his work and her ordinary room.
- Âm nổi: Speak over a low steady street bed, no score instructing the audience to forgive him.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the first admission time to be heard, then the refusal to lie as a smaller second breath.

**Clip 3 · CUT · FLOWER · I**
- Đầu: Hạ holding a single white lily beside Sơn's jacket.
- Hành động: She tucks the stem into his breast pocket and checks it stays upright.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: One lily in his breast pocket, Hạ's hands withdrawn.

- Ý đồ: Make the flower an ordinary act of care that does not excuse the dangerous job.
- Bố cục: Use the stable detail view for one stem, his breast pocket and Hạ's hands.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She tucks the single lily in once, checks it upright and removes her hands.
- Diễn xuất: Her care is practiced florist attention, not ceremonial blessing; his stillness permits it.
- Điểm nét: Keep stem and pocket in the same plane, without showing an extra flower or falling petals.
- Chất liệu: Cream petal against black cloth and the slightly damp stem give the gesture physical specificity.
- Âm nổi: Cloth and stem touch lightly, shop room tone continuing without a romantic music cue.
- Nhịp diễn: The placement is simple; hold the empty space her hands leave after it is secure.

**Clip 4 · CUT · FLOWER · M**
- Đầu: Sơn holding the still-closed watch at chest height.
- Hành động: Hạ touches the lid once, asking for an ordinary future rather than a symbol.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Về rồi mình ăn sáng. Chỉ vậy thôi.
- Cuối: Her hand leaving the closed watch, Sơn putting it away.

- Ý đồ: Make the promised future small enough to feel possible and painful to postpone.
- Bố cục: Frame the closed watch between their faces at chest height, both still near the doorway.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ touches only the shut lid once; Sơn receives the request and puts it away.
- Diễn xuất: She speaks as someone planning breakfast, not delivering a symbolic prophecy about time.
- Điểm nét: Keep eyes primary and the closed case readable, never reveal its inner photograph or dial.
- Chất liệu: Smooth metal and florist-worked fingertips contrast without glamour or exaggerated shine.
- Âm nổi: A faint existing mechanical tick may remain beneath ordinary shop sound, no amplified heartbeat.
- Nhịp diễn: Her simple request should arrive without buildup, followed by his inability to answer in more words.

**Clip 5 · CONTINUE · FLOWER · M**
- Đầu: Her hand leaving the closed watch, Sơn putting it away.
- Hành động: Sơn gives a small honest nod, Hạ leans her forehead briefly against his shoulder.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Two figures close without an embrace performed for the camera.

- Ý đồ: Allow a brief moment of mutual trust that rests on the honest answer just given.
- Bố cục: Preserve the same medium frame, door relation and body distance from the closed-watch exchange.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He nods once; she leans her forehead to his shoulder without pulling him into an embrace.
- Diễn xuất: The contact is accepted awkwardly and gently, neither actor presenting grief toward the lens.
- Điểm nét: Keep both profiles and the small contact readable, with no new lens or rack reset.
- Chất liệu: His dark lapel and her scarf remain in their established positions, flower undisturbed.
- Âm nổi: Breathing and distant wet-street traffic are enough; no speech, added sob or musical climax.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the contact arrive after the nod and end before it becomes a posed farewell tableau.

**Clip 6 · L · FLOWER_FRONT · W**
- Đầu: Sơn outside the shop, Hạ inside at the shutter.
- Hành động: She lowers the shutter fully after he leaves, no return to present inside this clip.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Shutter closed, empty pavement in warm street light.

- Ý đồ: Close the ordinary shop without pretending either character knows this is their last night.
- Bố cục: Keep pavement, shutter and Sơn's departure direction in the same exterior wide frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He clears the opening, she lowers the shutter fully once, leaving no return glimpse inside.
- Diễn xuất: Her pace is practical end-of-day work, with hesitation expressed only by the moment before closure.
- Điểm nét: Hold the closing boundary and then the empty pavement; do not chase his departing face.
- Chất liệu: Warm street reflection lies across wet paving as the shutter removes the shop's interior light.
- Âm nổi: Metal slats rattle and settle, then rain and street bed remain for the coming dawn cut.
- Nhịp diễn: The shutter completes the action; remaining time belongs to the empty place rather than another farewell.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Match the closed shutter lines to the cabin frame at dawn, then carve the seventh tally once.

### CẢNH 34: Vạch Thứ Bảy
*Thời gian truyện: D7 dawn*
Bằng chứng giao nhận rõ, Sơn giữ bản sao cho cuộc trao đổi.

**Câu hỏi:** Khi bằng chứng đã an toàn, Sơn còn đem gì ra đổi và có gì để mất?
**Móc vào:** Vạch thứ bảy hoàn tất hạn nhưng chưa hoàn tất món nợ.
**Điểm bẻ:** Bản gốc rời tay anh, chỉ còn bản sao và thân mình đi gặp hai phe.
**Gieo / trả:** Đồng hồ gửi Mây giữ như một mong muốn sẽ quay lại lấy, không như thư tuyệt mệnh.

**Clip 1 · MATCH · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: Exactly six marks on the frame, first dawn light across the wood.
- Hành động: Sơn adds only the seventh mark and puts the carving tool away.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Ngày bảy. Lần này tôi ở lại để khai.
- Cuối: Seven distinct marks, tool on the shelf.

- Ý đồ: Make the final tally a commitment to accountability rather than a threat against himself.
- Bố cục: Use the stable detail composition to show six old grooves and room for exactly one new mark.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn adds the seventh line, lowers the established tool and puts it on the shelf.
- Diễn xuất: The hand is steady with effort; no trembling blade pose or aggressive gouging performance.
- Điểm nét: Keep count and new pale wood legible, without an imagined calendar overlay.
- Chất liệu: Fresh wood dust catches the first daylight, older marks staying darker in the same frame.
- Âm nổi: One carving scrape, tool contact and the low narration replace any ticking countdown.
- Nhịp diễn: Finish the single addition early enough to read seven, then keep natural hand and wind movement.

**Clip 2 · CUT · CABIN · M**
- Đầu: Mây opened evidence satchel beside the cabin table contains the black ledger and original USB, while red ledger and hardware token are beside Sơn.
- Hành động: Mây lays out black ledger and original USB as Sơn brings red ledger and token alongside them, then they seal the originals together and leave only the preview envelope apart.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Bản gốc đi trước. Anh cầm bản sao.
- Cuối: Originals sealed with Mây, only a copy envelope before Sơn.

- Ý đồ: Separate protected originals from the vulnerable person who will carry only an excerpt.
- Bố cục: Use the medium table view to distinguish two bindings, USB sleeve, token and copy envelope.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây and Sơn assemble the existing originals into custody, leaving the thin preview apart.
- Diễn xuất: Their careful checking should feel like a relinquishment, not a repetitive evidence display for the audience.
- Điểm nét: Keep black and red books distinct without requiring readable seals or paperwork text.
- Chất liệu: Cloth binding, leather, plain sleeves and used table grain make each item physically different.
- Âm nổi: Small paper and seal sounds stay behind Mây's short line, no repeated upload chime.
- Nhịp diễn: One coordinated packing operation ends with only the copy before Sơn, the meaningful subtraction.

**Clip 3 · CUT · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: Sơn passing the small scarf fragment to Mây's evidence bag.
- Hành động: She stores both pieces in separate sleeves inside the same bag.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Both scarf pieces with Mây, sleeve edges visible.

- Ý đồ: Give the two scarf fragments a shared custody before they can later return to Hạ.
- Bố cục: Frame the small fragment, separate long remainder and open evidence bag together.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn passes the short piece once; Mây places each in its own existing sleeve.
- Diễn xuất: Hands handle the cloth with deliberate care, without stroking it into a sentimental repeated gesture.
- Điểm nét: Keep matching torn edges recognizable but separate, no magically repaired scarf or changed size.
- Chất liệu: Frayed blue silk contrasts with clear sleeves and coarse cabin wood.
- Âm nổi: A subdued sleeve rustle and pine wind replace dialogue, making care visible rather than explained.
- Nhịp diễn: Let separation and common bag become clear, then withdraw both sets of hands naturally.

**Clip 4 · CUT · PORCH · M**
- Đầu: Sơn holding his closed watch while Mây prepares to leave.
- Hành động: He asks her to keep it safe during the exchange, she accepts it.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Giữ giúp. Tôi muốn tự lấy lại.
- Cuối: Closed watch in Mây's right hand, Sơn's belt chain empty.

- Ý đồ: Make the watch handover a request to return for something, not a final bequest.
- Bố cục: Keep both faces and the closed watch together on the porch, no inner-dial insert.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn passes the complete watch and chain into Mây's right hand, leaving no second chain hanging.
- Diễn xuất: He asks with an ordinary hopeful insistence, she accepts without pity or promises she cannot keep.
- Điểm nét: Keep the closed lid and her receiving hand readable while their eyes carry the request.
- Chất liệu: Dented metal and bare hands remain modest under dawn light, the case never opening.
- Âm nổi: One small chain contact is enough beneath his line; no ticking from the stopped watch.
- Nhịp diễn: The acceptance should follow the stated wish to reclaim it, finishing on custody rather than mourning.

**Clip 5 · L · POST · W**
- Đầu: Mây handing the sealed originals to an assigned officer at the station.
- Hành động: Thành records the handover while copies remain with the local team.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Signed handover receipt with Thành, originals departing under escort.

- Ý đồ: Move the originals beyond the upcoming confrontation so the rescue cannot erase their evidence.
- Bố cục: Use the station wide view to include Mây, Thành, the assigned officer and one departing route.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây completes the sealed handover while Thành records it, local copies remaining on their side.
- Diễn xuất: They verify before releasing, with no congratulatory handshake or feeling the whole case is finished.
- Điểm nét: Keep originals and retained receipt physically distinct, without legible official writing.
- Chất liệu: Plain sleeves, tired desk surfaces and morning light keep the transfer unglamorous.
- Âm nổi: Paper acknowledgement and ordinary departure footsteps replace a procedural success montage sound.
- Nhịp diễn: The originals actually leave before the shot ends, making the transfer irreversible and visible.

**Clip 6 · CUT · POST · M**
- Đầu: Confirmed road team on the station radio, Mây beside Thành.
- Hành động: The team reports it will take position before the meeting, not during a later rescue.
- Thoại: Bộ đàm, qua thiết bị: Hai đầu đường đã có người.
- Cuối: Thành marking the confirmed positions, Mây acknowledging.

- Ý đồ: Confirm a real support presence while leaving the human exchange still uncertain.
- Bố cục: Frame Mây beside Thành at the radio with the already-used route map visible.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Thành marks only the reported road positions, Mây listening before acknowledging.
- Diễn xuất: Relief is limited to coverage of roads, not certainty about Hạ or the two leaders.
- Điểm nét: Keep their listening and a small position mark readable, no cutaway to invented convoys.
- Chất liệu: Desk wear and ordinary radio casing preserve the local team's scale.
- Âm nổi: The compressed confirmation sits over the station fan, with no additional operators talking over it.
- Nhịp diễn: One confirmation changes the map, then leave a breath showing the actual risk remains elsewhere.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Radio confirmation yields to coffee-stall glass sounds before the morning safety briefing. Orders here assign new practical tasks.

### CẢNH 35: Những Việc Nhỏ Của Thị Trấn
*Thời gian truyện: D7 morning*
Thị trấn chuẩn bị bằng các nhiệm vụ an toàn khác nhau.

**Câu hỏi:** Một thị trấn có thể giúp cứu người bằng việc bình thường nào?
**Móc vào:** Nhận còng mới và dọn xe cho thấy kế hoạch thành công việc thật.
**Điểm bẻ:** Mỗi người làm một nhiệm vụ, Sơn để đôi găng lại rồi ngồi nhìn biển.
**Gieo / trả:** Cho thấy dân đã rút và đường cứu hộ có thể dùng, tránh đám đông làm phép cứu nguy.

**Clip 1 · J · STALL · W**
- Đầu: Mây beside a harbour sketch, Bảy and Thành listening.
- Hành động: Thành gives Mây two fresh pairs of cuffs, she assigns the van to the rear lane and orders civilians off the pier.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Chiều nay đóng sạp sớm. Không ai xuống cầu.
- Cuối: Three people each taking their own route away from the table.

- Ý đồ: Turn help from the town into bounded jobs, not a speech summoning a heroic crowd.
- Bố cục: Keep three named people and the harbour sketch inside the wide coffee-stall frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Thành passes two fresh cuff pairs; Mây directs Bảy toward the rear lane and then disperses them.
- Diễn xuất: She is firm about keeping civilians away, both men listening as workers with specific tasks.
- Điểm nét: Keep the handover readable enough to account for later cuffs without a dedicated equipment insert.
- Chất liệu: Everyday glasses, table stains and practical work clothing keep the briefing embedded in town life.
- Âm nổi: Her one instruction sits over the opening-stall bed, no cheers or martial rhythm.
- Nhịp diễn: Hand over equipment first, assign routes second, end as the three separate into their jobs.

**Clip 2 · CUT · VAN_REAR · W**
- Đầu: Bảy van already parked beyond the market wall, rear space mostly cleared, one final empty crate near the clean mattress and Linh beside the open door.
- Hành động: Bảy removes the last empty crate while Linh secures the already-positioned clean mattress and existing medical bag, leaving the stretcher approach clear.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Xe sạch rồi. Đường cũng trống.
- Cuối: Van ready behind cover, rear doors opening toward the safe lane.

- Ý đồ: Make the eventual rescue vehicle usable through visible ordinary preparation.
- Bố cục: Use the van's parking position and the protected lane together in the wide setup.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Bảy clears the remaining obstruction while Linh secures the clean response space already being prepared.
- Diễn xuất: Both work with familiar competence, not frantic montage speed or jokes about the coming danger.
- Điểm nét: Keep rear doors and clear approach route visible along with the ready mattress.
- Chất liệu: Washed metal, clean fabric and the empty crate space separate this use from hauling fish.
- Âm nổi: A crate movement and door hardware sound remain practical under Bảy's short report.
- Nhịp diễn: Finish on a space someone can actually be carried into, avoiding repeated shots of the same equipment.

**Clip 3 · CUT · MARKET_ROOF · M**
- Đầu: Thành on the market roof with repaired glasses and radio.
- Hành động: He checks sightlines to both pier approaches and confirms the radio works.
- Thoại: Thành (@Phó Thành), nói trong hình: Tôi thấy cả hai đầu.
- Cuối: Binoculars lowered, radio clipped at his collar.

- Ý đồ: Let Thành regain agency by checking what he can genuinely see from above.
- Bố cục: Frame him with the existing roof edge and both approach roads readable below.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He checks each road through binoculars once, lowers them and clips the radio securely.
- Diễn xuất: Concentration has a small physical cost through repaired glasses, but he does not play incompetence.
- Điểm nét: Keep his eyeline linked to the roads without an internal point-of-view cut or floating map.
- Chất liệu: Tape on the glasses and wind against his shirt retain the previous day's consequences.
- Âm nổi: Wind and a clean radio test carry the action, not a surveillance-machine sound effect.
- Nhịp diễn: Two measured checks, one confirmation, then return attention to the real view ahead.

**Clip 4 · CUT · CABIN · M**
- Đầu: Sơn already wearing the black suit, left wrist bandage exposed.
- Hành động: He folds both black gloves into the canvas bag and leaves the bag in the cabin.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Gloves inside the bag on the bed, Sơn with bare hands.

- Ý đồ: Show the present-day refusal of the gloves as a settled choice rather than a new costume reveal.
- Bố cục: Use the medium cabin setup with the bag on the bed and bare hands in open view.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn folds the pair into the canvas bag once and leaves it where it is.
- Diễn xuất: He should not linger admiring leather or rehearse the gesture again; resolve has replaced the earlier hesitation.
- Điểm nét: Keep both gloves accounted for without implying he hides one on his body.
- Chất liệu: Soft old leather against worn canvas differs from the fresh left wrist bandage.
- Âm nổi: One quiet fold and bag rustle play over the cabin wind, with no narrator required.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete the practical placement and let his empty hands establish what he will carry forward.

**Clip 5 · L · STREET · T**
- Đầu: Sơn leaving the hill path for the main road.
- Hành động: He walks openly past shutters being secured for the afternoon.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn approaching the sea-facing coffee table.

- Ý đồ: Let walking openly register as exposure earned by the earlier decision to stay.
- Bố cục: Track laterally in the established direction with secured shutters remaining part of his route.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He keeps a steady ordinary pace from hill path toward Bảy's table, not a center-street strut.
- Diễn xuất: His shoulders carry tension despite composure; no knowing looks to unseen admirers or heroic smile.
- Điểm nét: Keep his bare hands and bandaged left wrist readable without pushing into an extra detail shot.
- Chất liệu: Closed shutters and bright coastal light make a public road feel newly unprotected.
- Âm nổi: Footsteps and shutter work from the evacuation dominate, no slow-motion or anthem cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow an uninterrupted approach that ends at the table, without replaying his departure from the cabin.

**Clip 6 · CUT · STALL · M**
- Đầu: Sơn seated facing the sea, Bảy carrying coffee.
- Hành động: Bảy sets the glass down and leaves Sơn the open view.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Chỗ này nhìn biển được rồi.
- Cuối: Coffee glass on Sơn's right, sea in front of him.

- Ý đồ: Make facing the sea an emotional change that Bảy notices without explaining the whole arc.
- Bố cục: Frame Sơn toward the water, Bảy entering with the single glass at his right.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Bảy sets coffee down once and leaves the sightline open rather than joining a long conversation.
- Diễn xuất: His line is observation with quiet care, Sơn receiving it without needing a reply.
- Điểm nét: Keep Sơn's face, glass and actual open sea related in the medium depth.
- Chất liệu: Sunlit glass rim and faded awning make the ordinary seat feel newly chosen.
- Âm nổi: One glass touch gives way to sea wind, with no symbolic watch ticking under the moment.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the placement finish before the remark, then hold his new direction of attention.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: The coffee glass settles, the approaching white-car engine begins before the street cut. Kiên arrives only once.

### CẢNH 36: Khách Hàng Áo Trắng
*Thời gian truyện: D7 afternoon*
Đối mặt Kiên, buộc kẻ giữ người chứng minh Hạ có mặt.

**Câu hỏi:** Kiên có thực sự cho Sơn thấy Hạ mà vẫn giữ được quyền định đoạt cô không?
**Móc vào:** Xe trắng tới nơi Sơn đã chọn nhìn biển, xâm phạm khoảng yên mới có.
**Điểm bẻ:** Hạ khẳng định tự đi được, Kiên biến cuộc trao đổi thành yêu cầu nhận tội thay.
**Gieo / trả:** Cửa xe và tay nắm trở thành thước đo quyền tự do của Hạ, dẫn tới vạch an toàn ở cảng.

**Clip 1 · J · STREET · W**
- Đầu: White car stopping across from the stall, Sơn already seated.
- Hành động: Kiên steps out with two unnamed guards, walks to Sơn's table.
- Thoại: Kiên (@Vũ Kiên), nói trong hình: Anh làm tôi tìm lâu quá.
- Cuối: Kiên standing opposite Sơn, car kept in view behind him.

- Ý đồ: Let Kiên enter the ordinary place Sơn has only just learned to occupy peacefully.
- Bố cục: Keep the white car, seated Sơn and Kiên's route between them together in the wide street frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên steps out once and walks directly to the table, guards staying in their existing supporting positions.
- Diễn xuất: His ease should intrude on Sơn's quiet without a grand villain introduction or theatrical pause.
- Điểm nét: Maintain the car in usable background view because Hạ is the immediate stake, not just transport.
- Chất liệu: White cloth and polished car surfaces catch harsh afternoon light against the humble awning.
- Âm nổi: Incoming engine resolves into the same idling bed; his greeting has no musical punctuation.
- Nhịp diễn: Arrival is economical, ending with him opposite Sơn rather than replaying a second approach.

**Clip 2 · CUT · STALL · M**
- Đầu: Kiên seated across from Sơn, no handshake offered.
- Hành động: He admits the first trap was meant to silence Sơn, then states why he kept Hạ.
- Thoại: Kiên (@Vũ Kiên), nói trong hình: Anh trốn được. Tôi giữ Hạ để anh quay lại.
- Cuối: Sơn listening without giving him the copy envelope.

- Ý đồ: Make Kiên explain control as a business adjustment, while Sơn refuses to reward the explanation.
- Bố cục: Use the medium table frame with an unoffered handshake space and car direction still understood.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên remains seated, Sơn keeps the copy envelope outside his reach without clutching it dramatically.
- Diễn xuất: Kiên speaks easily until naming Hạ; Sơn's smallest attention change reveals the leverage works.
- Điểm nét: Keep faces and unused table space readable, no imagined flashback illustrating the first trap.
- Chất liệu: Tailored white sleeve and worn coffee table make the unequal resources visible without added jewelry details.
- Âm nổi: His line sits over the subdued stall bed and car idle, without a phone effect.
- Nhịp diễn: The failed trap is brief context; holding Hạ is the new pressure that should land last.

**Clip 3 · CUT · WHITE_CAR · O**
- Đầu: Rear car window lowered, Hạ visible inside with Kiên outside.
- Hành động: Hạ meets Sơn's eyes and deliberately rests her hand on the inner door handle.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Em tự đi được.
- Cuối: Hạ's fingers on the handle, Sơn seeing the gesture from outside.

- Ý đồ: Give Hạ an act of self-definition the instant she becomes physically visible again.
- Bố cục: Frame from the established observer side through the lowered rear window, handle and her face both readable.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She rests her hand on the inner handle, Kiên staying outside on her left with his right hand controlling the door area.
- Diễn xuất: She meets Sơn directly, speaking as a capable person rather than asking to be carried away.
- Điểm nét: Keep her eyes and handle hand within useful depth; the glass edge must not obscure the gesture.
- Chất liệu: Grey dress and plain car upholstery contrast with Kiên's white sleeve at the window edge.
- Âm nổi: Her voice remains natural through the open window, with only the established engine beneath it.
- Nhịp diễn: Let recognition register before the hand touches the handle, then let the assertion remain unanswered.

**Clip 4 · CUT · STREET · M**
- Đầu: Kiên closing the rear car window, Sơn beside the table.
- Hành động: Sơn refuses a private transfer and specifies the open harbour exchange.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Cảng cá. Thấy cô ấy bước ra rồi mới trao.
- Cuối: Window closed, Sơn still standing outside the car's reach.

- Ý đồ: Make Sơn insist that proof of life must become a visible movement toward freedom.
- Bố cục: Keep the closing rear window and the safe public space between Sơn and Kiên legible.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên closes the window with his right hand, Sơn staying outside reaching distance rather than pursuing it.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn states the condition steadily, showing the effort of not grabbing at the first sight of her.
- Điểm nét: Retain the car window as a closing boundary without a separate shot of Hạ trapped behind glass.
- Chất liệu: Window reflections return gradually over the grey interior, not an abrupt opaque digital wipe.
- Âm nổi: The motor sound is small under his clear instruction, no threatening lock sound added afterward.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the window finish closing, then end on Sơn holding the condition rather than pleading.

**Clip 5 · CUT · STALL · M**
- Đầu: Kiên returning toward Sơn's table before leaving.
- Hành động: Kiên leaves a confession blaming Sơn and Bà Hai alone, trying to turn Sơn into the disposable witness who excludes him from the account.
- Thoại: Kiên (@Vũ Kiên), nói trong hình: Ký anh với bà ta làm. Đừng kéo tôi vào.
- Cuối: Blank confession folder placed on the table, Sơn not touching it.

- Ý đồ: Make the demanded confession reveal what Kiên wants bought beyond the ledger.
- Bố cục: Frame the existing folder on the table between their separate hands, car still logically behind Kiên.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên leaves the folder once; Sơn neither opens nor signs it and does not surrender the copy.
- Diễn xuất: Kiên treats the coercion as routine paperwork, his practiced smile hiding impatience at resistance.
- Điểm nét: Keep the closed folder and Sơn's refusal of contact readable without printing legible confession text.
- Chất liệu: Plain paper and immaculate cuff reinforce how ordinary the demand is to Kiên.
- Âm nổi: One folder contact and his low instruction are enough, no dramatic pen-click not established in the scene.
- Nhịp diễn: The condition arrives after the apparent agreement, ending with the object refusing to become a handshake.

**Clip 6 · L · STREET · W**
- Đầu: Kiên getting into the front passenger seat, Sơn remaining by the stall.
- Hành động: The white car drives toward its temporary lodging, not directly out of town.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: White car receding, confession folder still unopened on the table.

- Ý đồ: Leave the new demand physically behind after its owner removes himself.
- Bố cục: Use the wide street view to connect Kiên entering the passenger side and the untouched table.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: The white car departs toward town lodging, Sơn staying at the stall rather than following it.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn watches the retreat with held urgency, not relief, because Hạ is still inside.
- Điểm nét: Keep the confession folder visible as the car loses size, no internal cut to departing faces.
- Chất liệu: Afternoon glare on white bodywork gives way to the darker awning over the abandoned paperwork.
- Âm nổi: Car engine recedes under ordinary stall sound, with no second ignition or invented radio call.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the car genuinely clear the immediate space before attention returns to the folder left there.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: White-car engine recedes under the rear-stall discussion. Keep the car gone and the confession folder with Mây.

### CẢNH 37: Điều Kiện Không Đổi
*Thời gian truyện: D7 afternoon*
Mây và Sơn điều chỉnh kế theo điều kiện mới, không kể lại kế cũ.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn có giữ điều kiện cứu Hạ khi Kiên buộc anh nhận thêm tội không?
**Móc vào:** Phong bì không mở khiến lời đòi của Kiên vẫn hiện diện giữa họ.
**Điểm bẻ:** Hai người tách bản sao khỏi giấy nhận tội, cùng nhận ra một lối phụ chưa thể kiểm soát kín.
**Gieo / trả:** Tín hiệu đặt phong bì phải được ghi nhớ bằng vị trí và im lặng, không diễn lại nhiều lần.

**Clip 1 · L · STALL_REAR · M**
- Đầu: Mây and Sơn behind the stall with the unopened confession folder.
- Hành động: Mây identifies the new trap, Sơn agrees the folder will remain blank.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Hắn cần người nhận tội thay.
- Cuối: Folder closed in Mây's hand, Sơn's hands empty.

- Ý đồ: Give Mây the uncomfortable task of naming what Sơn's urgency might make him accept.
- Bố cục: Keep both partners behind the stall with the unopened folder occupying their shared table edge.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây takes custody of the folder, Sơn leaves his hands empty instead of arguing for a shortcut.
- Diễn xuất: She is direct without scolding; he recognizes the trap but must feel the price of resisting it.
- Điểm nét: Keep her face and the closed folder readable, avoiding an extra close-up of imagined written terms.
- Chất liệu: Awning shade removes the white car's glare, leaving ordinary paper between two tired people.
- Âm nổi: The receding engine can fade under her line, then only canvas and distant stall activity remain.
- Nhịp diễn: Name the trap once, ending on custody of the folder rather than another explanation of Kiên.

**Clip 2 · CUT · STALL_REAR · I**
- Đầu: Copy envelope open between them, only selected ledger pages inside.
- Hành động: They mark it as a preview, originals already with the investigator.
- Thoại: Sơn, ngoài khung hình: Tôi chỉ đưa bản xem trước.
- Cuối: Preview pages separated from the blank confession folder.

- Ý đồ: Separate the limited preview they will offer from the false admission they will never sign.
- Bố cục: Use the detail frame to show the open copy envelope and closed confession folder apart.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They verify the selected pages already present, keeping originals absent and the folder untouched.
- Diễn xuất: Their hands communicate agreement through clear separation, not a staged secret-agent flourish.
- Điểm nét: Keep paper thickness and different containers legible, with no readable signatures or invented security marks.
- Chất liệu: Thin preview sheets and the heavier folder create a concrete visual distinction.
- Âm nổi: Paper edges moving against the table support Sơn's off-screen sentence, with no digital confirmation sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Finish with preview and confession separated, making the refusal visible without rehearsing the whole exchange.

**Clip 3 · CUT · STALL_REAR · M**
- Đầu: Mây beside the confirmed road map, Sơn looking toward the harbour.
- Hành động: Mây assigns the remaining road officer to the main cliff-road turnoff; the narrow service footpath is a separate route below the wall.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Chặn đường chính lên tháp. Báo ngay nếu hắn đổi hướng.
- Cuối: Main cliff road marked as covered, Sơn acknowledging; service footpath remains a separate gap to watch.

- Ý đồ: Expose a specific spatial limitation so later escape feels like exploitation, not police forgetting a door.
- Bố cục: Keep Mây and Sơn above the existing map, main road and thin service route both visible.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She assigns the remaining officer to the main turnoff, leaving the lower footpath a distinct route to watch.
- Diễn xuất: Her concentration includes the limitation; Sơn understands it without a reassuring nod that dismisses the risk.
- Điểm nét: Keep her indicating hand and the two routes legible without readable labels or animated overlays.
- Chất liệu: Creased paper and a narrow drawn path make resources feel finite rather than omniscient.
- Âm nổi: Her short radio-facing instruction stays over muted town sound, no redundant chorus of confirmations.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the covered road clarity, then let the separate footpath remain the small unfinished edge.

**Clip 4 · CONTINUE · STALL_REAR · M**
- Đầu: Main cliff road marked as covered, Sơn acknowledging; service footpath remains a separate gap to watch.
- Hành động: Sơn agrees to stop if Hạ is not released from the car.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Không thả người, tôi không bước tiếp.
- Cuối: Mây closing the map, both understanding the abort signal.

- Ý đồ: Make Sơn agree to a limit that conflicts with the urge to rush toward Hạ.
- Bố cục: Preserve the exact preceding medium framing, map position and two-person eyelines.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He accepts the release condition while Mây closes the map, neither moving closer to soften it.
- Diễn xuất: His agreement comes after a restrained breath, suggesting a promise he knows will be difficult.
- Điểm nét: Keep his face and her closing hand readable, without a rack or new camera angle.
- Chất liệu: The folder remains where it was, an unaccepted alternative at the edge of the practical plan.
- Âm nổi: His line is unhurried and low, with canvas movement audible after it ends.
- Nhịp diễn: The condition is stated once, then held through the map closure without another rehearsal.

**Clip 5 · CONTINUE · STALL_REAR · M**
- Đầu: Mây closing the map, both understanding the abort signal.
- Hành động: Mây gives him the rescue signal: lay the envelope down and step away.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Đặt xuống. Lùi lại. Người tôi sẽ vào.
- Cuối: Envelope flat on the table as a rehearsal, nobody repeating the action twice.

- Ý đồ: Plant the later rescue signal through one ordinary object placement that can be recognized instantly.
- Bố cục: Keep the same medium setup as the closed-map agreement, envelope within the existing table area.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây demonstrates the flat placement once; Sơn does not copy it back as another performance.
- Diễn xuất: She is precise rather than ritualistic, he watches with the attention of someone depending on her response.
- Điểm nét: Keep her release of the envelope visible, not a new close-up or slow-motion hand study.
- Chất liệu: The thin envelope lies flat on worn wood, without adding a symbol or readable mark.
- Âm nổi: One paper contact punctuates the short instruction; existing ambience keeps the action natural.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow the placement to settle before the final words, then stop demonstrating and preserve the result.

**Clip 6 · CUT · STALL_REAR · I**
- Đầu: Sơn's bare right hand picking up the preview envelope.
- Hành động: He leaves the confession folder with Mây and takes only the copy.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Copy envelope in right hand, left wrist bandaged, no weapon.

- Ý đồ: Leave Sơn carrying only the preview and his own vulnerable body into the next phase.
- Bố cục: Use the stable detail frame for his sound right hand, bandaged left side and separate folder.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He takes the preview once, leaving Mây with the confession; no hidden weapon or extra paper changes hands.
- Diễn xuất: The hand is controlled, not shaking for emphasis, while the bandage keeps cost visible.
- Điểm nét: Hold the actual handoff plane sharp, with faces outside the detail frame rather than forced in.
- Chất liệu: Used paper edges, bandage cloth and bare fingers distinguish him from the black-glove memory next.
- Âm nổi: Small envelope lift and quiet cloth movement lead into the memory's rain, no new ticking.
- Nhịp diễn: End after the preview leaves the table so the following bare palms can answer its burden.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Match present Sơn bare hand holding the copy to young Sơn empty palms under the awning. No glove-box reveal until 38.4.

### CẢNH 38: Quá Khứ: Đôi Găng Đầu Tiên
*Thời gian truyện: PAST twenty-five years earlier rainy evening*
Ơn nuôi dưỡng biến thành quyền sở hữu qua một món quà.

**Câu hỏi:** Lòng biết ơn đã biến thành sự giam giữ từ lúc nào?
**Móc vào:** Bàn tay trắng của Sơn trẻ đối diện bàn tay có bằng chứng của anh hôm nay.
**Điểm bẻ:** Tô cơm thành công việc, đôi găng thành nghĩa vụ giữ cửa, Sơn hiện tại chọn cất chúng.
**Gieo / trả:** Ký ức làm việc từ chối Bà Hai ở cảng có giá tình cảm, không thêm một thông tin hồ sơ.

**Clip 1 · MATCH · SILK_REAR · W**
- Đầu: Young Sơn empty bare palms below the silk-shop awning, young Bà Hai watching from within.
- Hành động: She offers shelter, Sơn enters without a bag or trade tools.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Vào ăn trước đã.
- Cuối: Young Sơn under the awning, rain behind him.

- Ý đồ: Make the remembered kindness believable enough that rejecting Bà Hai later will hurt.
- Bố cục: Match the region of empty young palms into the wide awning view, young Bà Hai already sheltered.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She offers entry, he moves only as far as cover, without inventing childhood or extra possessions.
- Diễn xuất: He is hungry and wary, she practical and attentive rather than visibly plotting a future crime.
- Điểm nét: Keep palms and their relative shelter readable, no impossible morph between old and young faces.
- Chất liệu: Rain-dark cheap clothes and the dry awning establish the immediate value of what she gives.
- Âm nổi: Rain remains outside the shelter, her invitation direct and unornamented beneath it.
- Nhịp diễn: Let him hesitate before accepting cover, giving the kindness a small real cost in trust.

**Clip 2 · CUT · SILK · M**
- Đầu: Young Sơn seated with a bowl, Bà Hai across the table.
- Hành động: He begins to eat only after she moves the bowl closer.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Bowl near young Sơn, Bà Hai's hands withdrawn.

- Ý đồ: Show the first permission to receive care before later gifts acquire obligations.
- Bố cục: Keep the bowl and both young faces within the same medium table relation.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She nudges the existing bowl nearer, withdraws her hands, he begins eating only afterward.
- Diễn xuất: He watches for refusal before the first mouthful, while she lets him have the space.
- Điểm nét: Keep food and eyes readable without a sentimental insert of starving hands or oversized portions.
- Chất liệu: Steam and ordinary ceramic stand against the dry silk-shop interior and his damp sleeves.
- Âm nổi: A bowl scrape and modest eating sounds play against rain, no warm sentimental music swell.
- Nhịp diễn: The wait before eating carries the change, then end after he accepts that she will not reclaim it.

**Clip 3 · MATCH · SILK · M**
- Đầu: Later season, same table with folded silk instead of the bowl.
- Hành động: Bà Hai teaches him to fold a bolt and listen to the shop bell.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Nghe hết. Nói ít.
- Cuối: Neatly folded silk between them, young Sơn looking toward the door.

- Ý đồ: Let useful teaching become a route into belonging before it hardens into control.
- Bố cục: Match the familiar table composition across the stated time ellipsis, bowl replaced by folded silk.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She guides one existing fold and directs his attention to the shop bell, not an entire training montage.
- Diễn xuất: He is eager to do it correctly, she approving without the coldness the viewer now expects.
- Điểm nét: Keep fold and listening faces readable in the same medium plane, no internal cut to the bell.
- Chất liệu: Dry work cloth and tidier shirt placement mark time passed without changing their identities.
- Âm nổi: Silk movement settles before the familiar instruction, bell only within its established shop context.
- Nhịp diễn: One fold becomes neat, one attention shift follows, allowing the lesson to sound ordinary then restrictive.

**Clip 4 · CUT · SILK · I**
- Đầu: A glove box open on the teak desk.
- Hành động: Bà Hai places new black gloves in young Sơn's bare palms.
- Thoại: Bà Hai, ngoài khung hình: Từ nay con giữ cửa sau.
- Cuối: Gloves resting across both palms, not yet worn.

- Ý đồ: Reveal the gloves as a gift whose condition is spoken at the same moment.
- Bố cục: Use the single detail setup for open glove box, her hands and his bare palms.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She lays the pair across both palms once, he receives without putting them on yet.
- Diễn xuất: His hands accept carefully, unaware of how the gesture will later be understood.
- Điểm nét: Keep both gloves accounted for and fingertips clear, with no age drift in the young hands.
- Chất liệu: New leather contrasts with the older teak desk, modest packaging rather than luxury gift staging.
- Âm nổi: Soft leather and a box edge sound beneath her off-screen instruction, no ominous flourish.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the gift register first, then the assigned duty changes its meaning before the shot ends.

**Clip 5 · CUT · SILK · M**
- Đầu: Young Sơn by the rear door, Bà Hai at the desk.
- Hành động: He realizes his sleeping mat has been moved inside the locked stockroom.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Chỗ ở dần thành chỗ không được đi.
- Cuối: Young Sơn looking at the stockroom lock, Bà Hai watching him.

- Ý đồ: Make the cost of shelter emerge as a physical arrangement he cannot ignore.
- Bố cục: Keep young Sơn at the rear door and Bà Hai at the desk in the medium setup.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He notices the existing sleeping mat beyond the stockroom lock, without testing or opening a new door.
- Diễn xuất: His expression shifts from wanting approval toward recognizing dependence, while she quietly watches.
- Điểm nét: Keep his eyeline to the lock understandable without an insert or camera move revealing another room.
- Chất liệu: Teak, stockroom iron and the simple mat belong to the same practical place she first welcomed him.
- Âm nổi: Shop room tone continues under the older narrator, no lock slam or invented threat voice.
- Nhịp diễn: The realization should come after the look, with no speech added to explain his young expression.

**Clip 6 · MATCH · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: Present-day folded gloves in the canvas bag, Sơn's bare hands beside them.
- Hành động: Sơn zips the bag closed and leaves it on the bed.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Bag closed on the bed, bare hands leaving the frame.

- Ý đồ: Make the present hand capable of leaving the gift behind rather than reenacting the first acceptance.
- Bố cục: Match to the existing folded pair in the cabin bag, bare older hands positioned beside it.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He closes the zip once and leaves the bag on the bed, keeping hands ungloved.
- Diễn xuất: The movement is calm but final, not a ceremonial rejection thrown at the memory.
- Điểm nét: Keep both gloves inside until the zip covers them, no spare pair or hand disappearing into a pocket.
- Chất liệu: Worn canvas and older leather replace the remembered new gift box under current-day light.
- Âm nổi: Zipper sound finishes naturally, then cabin wind prepares the harbour cut.
- Nhịp diễn: Do not repeat the glove-folding from35.4; this shot closes the bag on a settled choice.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Present-day bag zipper finishes under the cut to the harbour overview, then sea wind takes over. Gloves stay in the cabin.

### CẢNH 39: Bản Đồ Lúc Hoàng Hôn
*Thời gian truyện: D7 sunset*
Thiết lập một lần địa lý và vị trí lực lượng cho toàn cao trào.

**Câu hỏi:** Địa hình nào có thể cứu hoặc làm hỏng cuộc trao đổi?
**Móc vào:** Một toàn cảnh rõ cho người xem tự dự đoán nguy cơ trước nhân vật.
**Điểm bẻ:** Khán giả thấy đường chính được giữ, lối bộ là khe hở, Sơn đứng giữa hai xe đang tới.
**Gieo / trả:** Tuyến xe cứu hộ và cửa kỹ thuật được ghi bằng không gian, không cần thuyết minh lần nữa.

**Clip 1 · L · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: Whole harbour in one view, market on landward side, lighthouse road uphill west.
- Hành động: Mây checks the protected landward junction while Thành takes the roof post.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Pier, market lane and uphill service path all readable in frame.

- Ý đồ: Give viewers enough geography to anticipate where protection ends and escape might begin.
- Bố cục: Use the full harbour width with market landward and uphill service path west in the same view.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây checks the junction while Thành reaches his roof post, each following a different established route.
- Diễn xuất: Their work is focused rather than triumphant; no one gestures as if giving an audience tour.
- Điểm nét: Keep routes and distances legible through depth instead of cutting to map fragments.
- Chất liệu: Concrete apron, boat ropes and low market structures form a finite recognizable arena.
- Âm nổi: Sea and work sounds persist without crowd noise, since evacuation is already underway.
- Nhịp diễn: Read the layout before concentrating on individuals, making later movement a consequence of this place.

**Clip 2 · CUT · HARBOUR_REAR · M**
- Đầu: Van behind the market wall, rear lane clear to the clinic.
- Hành động: Mây confirms Linh's protected position and a clear stretcher path.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Đưa người qua lối này. Không qua chợ.
- Cuối: Van and stretcher path clear, Mây heading back to the pier.

- Ý đồ: Make the rescue route feel real by showing what must remain unobstructed.
- Bố cục: Frame the van behind cover and the lane to clinic with Mây, Bảy and Linh inside that relation.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây confirms the clear stretcher path, then leaves them protected rather than pulling them toward the pier.
- Diễn xuất: Her instruction carries care for their limits; they respond through readiness, not spoken heroics.
- Điểm nét: Keep rear-door opening and route width visible, no repeated inventory of the medical bag.
- Chất liệu: Wall shadow, clean mattress edge and ordinary van metal distinguish this space from the exposed apron.
- Âm nổi: Her line passes over quiet van and distant harbour sound, no clinical monitor anticipation.
- Nhịp diễn: The route is checked once, ending as Mây heads back and the support team stays where needed.

**Clip 3 · CUT · MARKET_ROOF · O**
- Đầu: Thành looking down at two officers covering the harbour junction.
- Hành động: He confirms both arrival roads are watched and civilians have left.
- Thoại: Thành (@Phó Thành), nói trong hình: Đủ vị trí. Chợ đã rút hết.
- Cuối: Empty public pier below, officers concealed at the junction.

- Ý đồ: Show what Thành can verify while leaving the exchange itself outside his control.
- Bố cục: Use his established observation side with both road officers and the empty public pier below.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He checks each approach once and reports the cleared civilian area without scanning theatrically everywhere.
- Diễn xuất: He speaks with regained steadiness, repaired glasses still a reminder of yesterday's limits.
- Điểm nét: Keep the roads readable rather than manufacturing sharp tiny faces through impossible binocular reflections.
- Chất liệu: Wind on roof metal and taped frames connect this post to the earlier preparation.
- Âm nổi: Radio confirmation is brief, with wind remaining at its existing level rather than dropping for suspense.
- Nhịp diễn: Finish his check and let the empty pier hold the unease of a place waiting for people.

**Clip 4 · CUT · HARBOUR · T**
- Đầu: Sơn entering from the market side, envelope in his right hand.
- Hành động: He stops on the agreed painted seam, facing both approaches.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn on the seam, sea behind him, open retreat toward land.

- Ý đồ: Place Sơn as a willing exposed participant, not a returning gunfighter.
- Bố cục: Track along his established entry from market side, keeping sea behind and retreat landward.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He carries the copy in his right hand, reaches the painted seam and stops once.
- Diễn xuất: He steadies himself without presenting the bandage as weakness to the unseen parties.
- Điểm nét: Keep the seam and both approach directions readable, avoiding an orbit that reverses the arena.
- Chất liệu: Black suit against plain concrete shows how visible he has chosen to become.
- Âm nổi: Measured footsteps and envelope cloth contact sit over rope and sea ambience.
- Nhịp diễn: The walk is uninterrupted, ending on the agreed position so it is not repeated when cars arrive.

**Clip 5 · CUT · HARBOUR · C**
- Đầu: Sơn at the seam, wind moving his jacket naturally.
- Hành động: He looks once toward Mây's covered position and steadies his left wrist.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn's gaze back toward the road, both hands visible.

- Ý đồ: Let the quiet check of his ally expose dependence beneath outward composure.
- Bố cục: Keep close on Sơn from the established landward camera side, both hands fitting the lower frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He looks once toward Mây's covered place and supports the left wrist without a new prop.
- Diễn xuất: His relief at her presence is smaller than a smile, followed by renewed attention to the road.
- Điểm nét: Hold his eyes, with the bandage readable but not isolated by a rack or cut.
- Chất liệu: Coastal wind moves the same black jacket, skin texture remains natural in the fading light.
- Âm nổi: Breath and existing harbour bed are sufficient, no stopped-watch ticking or heartbeat added.
- Nhịp diễn: One look away, one return ahead, leaving him ready rather than looping anxious glances.

**Clip 6 · J · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: Empty arrival roads beyond Sơn, two engines heard off screen.
- Hành động: Black and white headlights appear from different landward roads.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Both headlights approaching the junction, neither car stopped yet.

- Ý đồ: Make incoming threat audible before the visible arena fills with the two sides.
- Bố cục: Hold the wide harbour setup with Sơn in the established place and both roads readable.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Headlights enter their respective roads toward the junction, neither vehicle parking yet.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn stays where he chose to stand, attention dividing without frantic head movement.
- Điểm nét: Keep both approaches readable at once; do not cut between headlights or move into a drone view.
- Chất liệu: Last daylight still defines concrete edges while headlights gain contrast, not full night instantly.
- Âm nổi: Two distinct but ordinary engines rise from their actual directions, without racing or tire squeals.
- Nhịp diễn: Let sound create expectation, headlights answer it, and parking remain entirely for the next scene.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep the two approaching engines into the arrivals. The next shot shows parking, not a repeated approach from the road.

### CẢNH 40: Hai Chiếc Xe
*Thời gian truyện: D7 sunset*
Hai phe tới một bãi trao đổi, Hạ có vị trí và hướng thoát rõ.

**Câu hỏi:** Hạ có thể tự bước từ xe tới vạch an toàn hay vẫn chỉ là món hàng?
**Móc vào:** Hai xe dừng theo hai hướng đã biết, vị trí sống còn thay cho màn giới thiệu lại.
**Điểm bẻ:** Cô ra khỏi xe bằng chân mình nhưng bàn tay Kiên vẫn giữ lại.
**Gieo / trả:** Một bàn chân đổi hướng báo lựa chọn của Hạ, đặt câu hỏi ngay trước Kiên đổi luật ở41.

**Clip 1 · CUT · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: Black car reaching the east landward apron, Sơn on the center seam.
- Hành động: Black car parks on the east side of the landward apron, Bà Hai steps out.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Black car east, Bà Hai and Dũng beside it, Sơn still on the seam.

- Ý đồ: Let Bà Hai arrive inside geography already understood rather than restage her authority.
- Bố cục: Keep the east apron, black car and Sơn on the seam in the wide landward frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: The black car parks once, Bà Hai exits with Dũng beside that vehicle, staying east.
- Diễn xuất: Her composure is maintained against the open exposure, Dũng scanning rather than strutting forward.
- Điểm nét: Keep their position relative to Sơn clear, with no fresh close-up introduction of bracelet or face.
- Chất liệu: Low sun catches violet silk against dark paint, while existing concrete seam anchors the distance.
- Âm nổi: One engine settles to idle and one door movement, no repeated approach sound from39.6.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete parking and emergence economically; end before either party advances toward the exchange point.

**Clip 2 · CUT · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: White car approaching the west apron, black car already parked in background.
- Hành động: White car stops on the west apron, Kiên opens the rear door for Hạ.
- Thoại: Kiên (@Vũ Kiên), nói trong hình: Người đây. Phần anh đâu?
- Cuối: White car west, Hạ still seated in the open rear doorway.

- Ý đồ: Make the price of Hạ's visibility appear with the opening of one ordinary car door.
- Bố cục: Keep the west car and black car already parked behind it, preserving the opposite positions.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên opens the rear door with his right hand, leaving Hạ seated until her next action.
- Diễn xuất: His line sounds transactional, while she watches the available space rather than awaiting a cue from him.
- Điểm nét: Keep door opening and her seated silhouette legible without an internal face insert.
- Chất liệu: White paint, grey seat and the shadow inside the car contrast with the exposed apron.
- Âm nổi: Engine idles without restarting, door seal releases once, his sentence remains unhurried.
- Nhịp diễn: The opening reveals the person before his demand tries to reduce her to a delivery.

**Clip 3 · CUT · HARBOUR · M**
- Đầu: Hạ seated at the white car threshold, Kiên holding the door.
- Hành động: She steps out on her own and studies the landward escape lane.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Hạ beside the car, thin sandals on concrete, landward lane to her right.

- Ý đồ: Give Hạ the first self-directed movement in the exchange and make its direction useful.
- Bố cục: Use the medium frame from the established landward side, car threshold and safe lane related.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She places both thin-sandaled feet on concrete and stands by herself, Kiên staying at her left.
- Diễn xuất: She gathers breath without collapsing, reading the route she may need rather than looking only at Sơn.
- Điểm nét: Keep eyes and door handle region readable; no close detail of bare feet replacing her decision.
- Chất liệu: Grey dress catches wind after leaving the protected car interior, sandals stay consistent.
- Âm nổi: A small sandal contact and cloth movement register under the ongoing engine bed.
- Nhịp diễn: Let stepping out finish once, then the held look toward land becomes the new information.

**Clip 4 · CUT · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: All five positions visible around the marked seam.
- Hành động: Both leaders approach but stop on opposite sides of Sơn, keeping Hạ west.
- Thoại: Bà Hai, ngoài khung hình: Con giữ người kiểu đó hả Kiên?
- Cuối: Bà Hai east, Kiên and Hạ west, Sơn center, landward route open.

- Ý đồ: Turn the separate arrivals into an unequal negotiation whose geometry viewers can track.
- Bố cục: Hold all five main positions, sea behind and open landward lane, within one wide setup.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: The leaders advance only a few steps and stop on opposite sides, with Kiên keeping Hạ west.
- Diễn xuất: Bà Hai's line sounds like a criticism of handling property, revealing her own assumption as well.
- Điểm nét: Keep east and west identities readable through costume and spacing, not facial close-ups inside this take.
- Chất liệu: Violet, white, grey and black clothing divide the plain concrete without extra colored lighting.
- Âm nổi: Her voice carries over the same idling engines and ropes, no second establishing music cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the advance conclude before the line lands, ending on stable positions ready for changed terms.

**Clip 5 · CUT · HARBOUR · M**
- Đầu: Sơn holding the preview envelope low, Kiên beside Hạ.
- Hành động: Sơn asks Kiên to let Hạ cross to the marked safe line.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Cho cô ấy qua đây.
- Cuối: Sơn pointing to the safe line with empty left hand.

- Ý đồ: Make Sơn ask for a verifiable release rather than simply trust another promise.
- Bố cục: Frame him opposite Kiên and Hạ, with the marked safe line clearly connected to his gesture.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Right hand keeps the preview low; the bandaged empty left hand indicates the line with a modest movement.
- Diễn xuất: He asks firmly without stretching injured fingers into a heroic pose or appealing to the crowd.
- Điểm nét: Keep his face, left bandage and requested destination readable in the same medium setup.
- Chất liệu: Thin envelope stays distinct from the absent original books and any confession folder.
- Âm nổi: His short instruction sits above engines, with no added gasp or unseen officer response.
- Nhịp diễn: Make the condition simple enough to judge, then leave the waiting space after it for their answer.

**Clip 6 · CUT · HARBOUR · C**
- Đầu: Hạ looking from Sơn to the safe line, Kiên gripping her arm.
- Hành động: Hạ makes a small landward weight shift that turns her left shoulder, testing Kiên right-hand grip on her LEFT upper arm without a sudden pull.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Hạ eyes and left shoulder angled landward, Kiên RIGHT hand still controlling her LEFT upper arm at the frame edge.

- Ý đồ: End on Hạ testing a possible escape rather than waiting for the men to settle ownership.
- Bố cục: Keep a tight view of her face and left upper arm, Kiên's right-hand grip visible at the edge.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: A small landward weight shift reaches her left shoulder, testing his hold without a second camera move.
- Diễn xuất: Her eyes calculate, breath controlled; Kiên's pressure answers without a graphic squeeze or new threat.
- Điểm nét: Hold her eyes and the existing grip sufficiently readable, not a rack down to an unseen sandal.
- Chất liệu: Grey sleeve and his white cuff mark whose hand holds whom, with wind moving the loose hair.
- Âm nổi: Only cloth, breathing and established engines remain; do not add dialogue to explain the attempt.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the shift be slight but unmistakable, ending with her landward intent still blocked.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep both engines idling under the first new demand. Do not re-establish the same five positions again.

### CẢNH 41: Cuộc Trao Đổi Đổi Luật
*Thời gian truyện: D7 last light*
Kiên phá điều kiện, Hạ làm Sơn chọn người thay vì giao kèo.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn có chấp nhận mua tự do của Hạ bằng một lời khai giả?
**Móc vào:** Tờ nhận tội được đưa ra sau khi hai bên đã tới điểm trao đổi.
**Điểm bẻ:** Hạ từ chối cách đổi người đó, Sơn đặt bản sao xuống và chọn ở lại.
**Gieo / trả:** Trả tín hiệu đặt phong bì ở37; đẩy lựa chọn đạo đức sang hành động.

**Clip 1 · CUT · HARBOUR · M**
- Đầu: Kiên holding Hạ just short of the safe line with his RIGHT hand on her LEFT upper arm, Sơn opposite with his own preview envelope; no confession folder here.
- Hành động: Kiên verbally demands that Sơn sign the confession before Hạ is released, changing the agreement without presenting another physical folder. His RIGHT hand keeps Hạ LEFT upper arm, LEFT hand still concealed.
- Thoại: Kiên (@Vũ Kiên), nói trong hình: Ký trước. Tôi mới thả.
- Cuối: Kiên condition unanswered, Hạ still held just short of the safe line, Sơn opposite with the preview copy; no second folder.

- Ý đồ: Make the changed condition feel like a deliberate theft of the agreement, not another negotiation recap.
- Bố cục: Keep Kiên nearer the lens, Hạ restrained beside him, Sơn readable opposite with the preview envelope still his own.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên delivers the condition without extending a second folder; RIGHT hand retains Hạ LEFT upper arm, LEFT hand stays concealed.
- Diễn xuất: Kiên delivers the short demand as a routine condition, Hạ jaw tightens before Sơn answers.
- Điểm nét: Stay on Kiên controlling face and the unanswered distance to Sơn; no folder or document insert is needed.
- Chất liệu: White suit catches the last sky light, grey dress stays matte against the concrete.
- Âm nổi: Let the idling white car sit under the line, with a small sleeve movement at Hạ held arm.
- Nhịp diễn: The offer travels slowly, then stops unanswered; keep that unfinished distance alive through the end.

**Clip 2 · CUT · HARBOUR · M**
- Đầu: Bà Hai east of Sơn, Dũng a pace behind her.
- Hành động: She orders Sơn to bring Kiên closer in exchange for her protection.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Đưa nó về phía má.
- Cuối: Dũng edging around Sơn's retreat path, Bà Hai watching.

- Ý đồ: Expose the maternal bargain as another command over Sơn's body and loyalties.
- Bố cục: Place Bà Hai east in the medium foreground, Dũng just behind, Sơn across her eyeline.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Bà Hai stays planted; Dũng edges around the clear retreat lane without stepping through Sơn.
- Diễn xuất: Her gentleness contains expectation rather than warmth, and Dũng watches for permission to advance.
- Điểm nét: Hold Bà Hai's eyes and raised chin, retaining Dũng's approach legibly within the medium depth.
- Chất liệu: Her violet silk and left jade bracelet catch small dusk highlights without looking newly polished.
- Âm nổi: A soft silk sleeve movement precedes the order; avoid a villainous musical accent.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the order room, then let Dũng's measured repositioning supply the threat after her voice stops.

**Clip 3 · CUT · HARBOUR · C**
- Đầu: Hạ's arm held by Kiên, eyes fixed on Sơn.
- Hành động: She tells Sơn not to accept blame for Kiên, then makes herself face the safe lane.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Đừng ký. Em không đổi bằng chuyện đó.
- Cuối: Hạ standing upright despite Kiên's grip, Sơn hearing her.

- Ý đồ: Give Hạ an independent refusal that changes what Sơn is willing to exchange.
- Bố cục: Frame Hạ close, Kiên's gripping RIGHT hand on her LEFT arm visible at the edge.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ squares her free shoulder toward the safe lane while remaining within the same grip.
- Diễn xuất: She speaks through controlled breath, looking at Sơn rather than pleading upward toward Kiên.
- Điểm nét: Prioritize her eyes and the held arm in the same natural focal plane, no glamour blur.
- Chất liệu: Keep grey cotton slightly creased and wind-touched hair irregular, without adding fresh injuries or tears.
- Âm nổi: The sea and a faint sleeve strain remain beneath her line; no gasping chorus.
- Nhịp diễn: Begin with the cost of being held, let her refusal settle, then finish on her deliberate upright posture.

**Clip 4 · CUT · HARBOUR · M**
- Đầu: Sơn facing Kiên and the safe line, preview copy envelope in his RIGHT hand, LEFT wrist bandaged; no confession folder in this space.
- Hành động: He places his copy envelope flat on the seam and steps back once.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Tôi ở lại. Để cô ấy đi.
- Cuối: Envelope on concrete, Sơn one full step behind it.

- Ý đồ: Turn Sơn's promise into an observable choice before the police signal is acted upon.
- Bố cục: Keep the seam and Sơn preview envelope within the medium lower frame, Hạ blocked path beyond.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn bends only enough to place the COPY, then takes exactly one full backward step.
- Diễn xuất: He addresses Hạ's release with level restraint, accepting exposure without performing a martyr pose.
- Điểm nét: Keep his bare RIGHT hand and envelope placement clear; LEFT wrist bandage must remain unchanged.
- Chất liệu: Paper rests on rough concrete with a slight wind lift, never multiplying into another ledger.
- Âm nổi: Use one flat paper contact and a single foot scrape, leaving dialogue unhurried and dry.
- Nhịp diễn: Separate the placement from the retreat by a breath, but do not repeat either gesture to fill ten seconds.

**Clip 5 · CUT · HARBOUR · O**
- Đầu: Mây behind cover with a clear view of the laid-down envelope.
- Hành động: She signals the officers forward slowly while watching Hạ's blocked path.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Tiếp cận. Chưa có lối cho con tin.
- Cuối: Two officers advancing behind cover, Mây tracking Kiên's hand.

- Ý đồ: Show Mây beginning a constrained response because the hostage still blocks a safe approach.
- Bố cục: Observe from her covered shoulder, seam and Kiên's gripping hand in the established harbour direction.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây gives one compact advance signal, staying behind cover until the officers have moved.
- Diễn xuất: Her voice stays low and professional; eyes track the hostage hand rather than Sơn's apparent bravery.
- Điểm nét: Keep her near profile readable enough for the line and the hostage zone identifiable beyond.
- Chất liệu: Cover and jacket edges stay practical and worn, with no new tactical equipment appearing.
- Âm nổi: Radio transmission is brief and diegetic, with advancing shoes muted beneath existing engine noise.
- Nhịp diễn: A short observation precedes the order, then the visible officers move only as far as their cover permits.

**Clip 6 · CUT · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: Dũng blocking Sơn's right side, Kiên pulling Hạ back toward the white car.
- Hành động: Sơn shifts between Hạ and Dũng as the exchange breaks apart.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn shielding Hạ's path, Dũng within arm's reach.

- Ý đồ: Make the exchange physically fail before anyone narrates that it has failed.
- Bố cục: Use the wide as a map: Dũng near Sơn's right, Kiên drawing Hạ west, landward route behind.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn shifts once into the threatened gap, keeping empty hands visible and his bandaged LEFT wrist guarded.
- Diễn xuất: Hạ resists the pull with her shoulders, Dũng watches Sơn's hands, Kiên concentrates on retreat.
- Điểm nét: Maintain enough depth to read all four positions, rather than finding a new dramatic close-up.
- Chất liệu: The envelope stays on the center seam, white car west and black car east remain fixed anchors.
- Âm nổi: Cloth tension and sandals scraping replace dialogue, while both engines keep the same subdued presence.
- Nhịp diễn: The single sidestep closes the space; finish with Dũng already within reach, not approaching twice.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep breath and road engines. Cut from Sơn shielding the path to Dũng reaching him; the envelope is already on the ground.

### CẢNH 42: Bóng Ma Ngã Xuống
*Thời gian truyện: D7 dusk*
Dũng dùng Tê giữ người sống, Sơn không tự làm giả chết.

**Câu hỏi:** Ai sẽ quyết định số phận Sơn khi anh mất khả năng chống đỡ?
**Móc vào:** Bà Hai nhận ra thiết bị trong tay Dũng trước khi Sơn khuỵu.
**Điểm bẻ:** Dũng vượt ý Bà Hai dùng Tê, làm lộ giới hạn tình mẫu tử của bà.
**Gieo / trả:** Tê được nhận biết đúng; không cần lừa phản diện bằng cái chết giả.

**Clip 1 · CUT · HARBOUR · H**
- Đầu: Dũng within arm's reach of Sơn, Kiên west of them with Hạ.
- Hành động: Dũng reaches to restrain Sơn while Kiên drags Hạ away from the safe line.
- Thoại: Dũng (@Dũng), nói trong hình: Má nói giữ mày sống.
- Cuối: Dũng holding Sơn's jacket sleeve, Hạ pulled west toward the car.

- Ý đồ: Convert the broken agreement into immediate bodily danger without inventing a fresh attacker.
- Bố cục: Keep restrained handheld medium-wide from the same landward side, Hạ west behind Sơn's shoulder line.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng closes his hand around Sơn's jacket sleeve, Kiên retains RIGHT-hand control of Hạ's LEFT arm.
- Diễn xuất: Dũng says the line with possessive certainty; Sơn's response is bracing, not a theatrical attack.
- Điểm nét: Hold the held sleeve and Sơn's face within one readable field; do not cut to a device macro.
- Chất liệu: Sơn's black lapel pulls under real tension, bandage remains clean and on the LEFT wrist.
- Âm nổi: A compact jacket tug punctuates Dũng's words, with no exaggerated impact or electronic sting.
- Nhịp diễn: Let contact happen early, then show the opposing pulls accumulate while positions remain geographically stable.

**Clip 2 · CUT · HARBOUR · M**
- Đầu: Bà Hai watching Dũng press close to Sơn.
- Hành động: She sees the distinctive Tê device in Dũng's hand and tells him to stop.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Dũng. Má chỉ bảo giữ người.
- Cuối: Bà Hai stepping forward, device partly hidden by Dũng's sleeve.

- Ý đồ: Let Bà Hai recognize a limit being crossed before Sơn loses his strength.
- Bố cục: Frame her watching Dũng close to Sơn, keeping the partly obscured device a secondary edge detail.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She takes one involuntary step forward and raises her hand, without instantly reaching both men.
- Diễn xuất: The first word lands sharper than her usual sweetness; the remaining phrase tries to recover command.
- Điểm nét: Favor Bà Hai's recognition, not the device mechanism; its distinctive shape need only be recognizable.
- Chất liệu: Left jade bracelet catches the remaining light as her sleeve lifts, no sudden age or outfit change.
- Âm nổi: Her interrupted breath and one slipper step precede the command; leave device operation inaudible and undescribed.
- Nhịp diễn: The recognition is quick, the attempted stop arrives just late, and the step ends unresolved.

**Clip 3 · CUT · HARBOUR · C**
- Đầu: Sơn's face as he braces against Dũng, Hạ beyond his shoulder.
- Hành động: His grip weakens after contact with the fictional device, no application detail shown.
- Thoại: Hạ, ngoài khung hình: Sơn!
- Cuối: Sơn losing balance, Hạ turning toward him.

- Ý đồ: Hold the frightening moment when Sơn knows his body is no longer obeying him.
- Bố cục: Keep a close-up on Sơn, Dũng's shoulder defining contact and Hạ blurred beyond his shoulder.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: His balance shifts inside the existing restraint; do not show a technique, dose, puncture or new wound.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn tries to maintain eye contact, then his eyelids and grip lose coordination without a staged seizure.
- Điểm nét: Stay on his face through Hạ's off-frame call; no cutaway, orbit or artificial pupil effect.
- Chất liệu: Skin texture and sweat remain natural, collar tension softens instead of becoming instantly corpse-grey.
- Âm nổi: His breath remains audible under Hạ's single call, with the harbour bed continuing unchanged.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow the weakness to arrive unevenly across one attempt to brace, ending before the actual descent.

**Clip 4 · CUT · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: Sơn folding at the knees beside the copy envelope.
- Hành động: He lowers against a mooring bollard, Dũng holds his jacket instead of letting him strike the ground.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn seated limp against the bollard, visibly breathing.

- Ý đồ: Keep Sơn visibly alive and vulnerable, avoiding the visual language of a completed death.
- Bố cục: Show bollard, center seam and both men's full balance in a stable wide from the same axis.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng controls the jacket as Sơn folds down against the bollard, lowering rather than dropping him.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn cannot arrest the movement but still breathes; Dũng is intent on possession, not concern.
- Điểm nét: Retain chest movement and Dũng's supporting hold within the wide; do not seek a death-like eye insert.
- Chất liệu: Bollard and concrete retain their established surfaces, with no new blood, debris or shattered objects.
- Âm nổi: Use cloth dragging against the bollard and one soft shoe shift, not a heavy body-impact effect.
- Nhịp diễn: The descent happens once and ends seated; the rest of the take registers helplessness through breathing.

**Clip 5 · CUT · HARBOUR · M**
- Đầu: Bà Hai reaching the bollard, Sơn breathing but unable to stand.
- Hành động: She recognizes Tê immediately and orders Dũng to give him room.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Nó còn sống. Tránh ra cho nó thở.
- Cuối: Bà Hai between Sơn and Dũng, her hand on Sơn's shoulder.

- Ý đồ: Show that Bà Hai knows the drug and is forced to face what her own order enabled.
- Bố cục: Keep Bà Hai beside the seated Sơn, Dũng beyond her shoulder, their separation readable in medium framing.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She puts a hand on Sơn's shoulder and occupies the gap between him and Dũng.
- Diễn xuất: Her line is factual before it is maternal; concern should not erase the authority she still claims.
- Điểm nét: Hold her face and Sơn's breathing shoulder together, with no rack to a symbolic tear.
- Chất liệu: Jade remains on LEFT wrist and violet sleeve lies against the black suit without wardrobe drift.
- Âm nổi: Breathing is slightly clearer now, her words above it, with no heart monitor invented on the pier.
- Nhịp diễn: Give recognition no mystery pause; let her protective movement become the complicated emotional beat afterward.

**Clip 6 · CUT · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: Kiên west with Hạ, Bà Hai beside Sơn at center.
- Hành động: Kiên demands Sơn and the copies while keeping his RIGHT hand on Hạ LEFT upper arm. Hạ plants both sandals and turns her free shoulder toward the inner apron, resisting the pull back to the white car without breaking his hold.
- Thoại: Kiên (@Vũ Kiên), nói trong hình: Tôi cần cả người lẫn sổ.
- Cuối: Hạ still held west of center, sandals planted; preview envelope remains untouched on the center seam where Sơn placed it.

- Ý đồ: Preserve Hạ's agency through resistance rather than an implausible trip to the center envelope.
- Bố cục: Use the wide to keep Kiên and Hạ west, Bà Hai beside Sơn center, envelope still on its seam.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ plants both sandals and turns her free shoulder inward against Kiên's RIGHT-hand grip, without escaping.
- Diễn xuất: Kiên demands both assets while Hạ's refusal remains physical and silent, no added heroic quip.
- Điểm nét: Keep the opposing body directions readable instead of hunting faces with focus changes.
- Chất liệu: Grey hem presses against her planted sandals, COPY envelope remains separate and untouched on concrete.
- Âm nổi: A shoe scrape and taut fabric give her resistance weight under Kiên's single demand.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the pull meet a clear stop; end on a contested hold rather than performing another release attempt.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: The envelope remains on the center seam. Keep idling engines and sea under the contested ownership; police commands begin only at 43.3.
