### CẢNH 43: Không Ai Còn Là Món Hàng
*Thời gian truyện: D7 dusk*
Hai phe tranh quyền giữ Sơn, lực lượng vào khi thấy hành vi khống chế.

**Câu hỏi:** Bà Hai bảo vệ Sơn vì thương hay vẫn muốn sở hữu anh?
**Móc vào:** Hai phe tranh quyền giữ người trong lúc Sơn không thể tự chọn.
**Điểm bẻ:** Bà ngăn Kiên lấy Sơn nhưng cuộc khống chế đã đủ để Mây vào.
**Gieo / trả:** Người giữ luật tách khỏi hai phe coi người là món hàng.

**Clip 1 · MATCH · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: Preview envelope still on the center seam where Sơn placed it, Bà Hai beside the collapsed Sơn, Kiên west of them holding Hạ.
- Hành động: Bà Hai refuses to hand Sơn to Kiên and pulls Dũng away from his demand.
- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Con tao. Không giao thêm lần nữa.
- Cuối: Bà Hai shielding Sơn's side, Kiên forced to keep Hạ close.

- Ý đồ: Make Bà Hai's protection emotionally real while keeping its possessive language visible.
- Bố cục: The wide retains the preview at center seam, Sơn at bollard, Kiên and Hạ west.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Bà Hai shields Sơn's side and draws Dũng back from Kiên's attempted claim, not across the hostage lane.
- Diễn xuất: She speaks as a mother who still calls a person hers; do not frame this as absolution.
- Điểm nét: Keep enough depth that Sơn's inability to choose and Hạ's continuing restraint remain present together.
- Chất liệu: Violet silk moves in the same dusk wind; no halo-like new light or cleansing rain.
- Âm nổi: Her slipper scrape and the idling engines remain ordinary, allowing the contradiction to carry the line.
- Nhịp diễn: The refusal is immediate; the unresolved spacing after it invites Kiên's answer rather than a sentimental hold.

**Clip 2 · CUT · HARBOUR · M**
- Đầu: Kiên tightening his hold on Hạ, facing Bà Hai.
- Hành động: He reminds her she sold both people and signals his guard toward Sơn.
- Thoại: Kiên (@Vũ Kiên), nói trong hình: Bà bán rồi. Giờ muốn lấy lại sao?
- Cuối: Guard moving toward center, Kiên stepping toward the white car.

- Ý đồ: Let Kiên puncture Bà Hai's self-justification while exploiting the opening she has left.
- Bố cục: Frame Kiên and restrained Hạ medium, Bà Hai on his opposing eyeline, car west behind.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: His RIGHT hand stays on Hạ's LEFT upper arm while his other hand signals the guard inward.
- Diễn xuất: He sounds irritated that an agreed commodity is being reclaimed, not surprised by her morality.
- Điểm nét: Stay with his practiced face as the smile thins, with the guard's direction readable at the edge.
- Chất liệu: White jacket has the same creasing from the pull, grey dress remains unchanged and no weapon appears early.
- Âm nổi: Use a soft shoe reposition and paper rustle already on the apron; no new offscreen gun sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the accusation a clipped rhythm, then use his first backward step to carry threat toward the car.

**Clip 3 · J · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: Mây coming out of landward cover, officers behind her.
- Hành động: She activates the harbour floodlight and orders everyone to release their grips.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Bỏ người ra. Công an đây.
- Cuối: Harbour lit steadily, Mây advancing along the safe line.

- Ý đồ: Change control of the space through a visible police action rather than a magical rescue arrival.
- Bố cục: A wide reveals Mây emerging from landward cover, officers behind, both cars in fixed positions.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She switches the established floodlight once and advances along the safe line after it brightens.
- Diễn xuất: Her command is clear and forceful without triumph; every armed person remains a live concern.
- Điểm nét: Keep the lamp change readable across the full space, never whitening the frame into a flash transition.
- Chất liệu: Blue dusk yields to the same floodlight on concrete, preserving skin detail and established shadow directions.
- Âm nổi: One switch or relay click, followed by her command and controlled footfalls, no action-music reset.
- Nhịp diễn: Start in the existing dim state, let the lamp settle, then advance only far enough to establish intervention.

**Clip 4 · CUT · HARBOUR · O**
- Đầu: Thành on the roof, white car and service stair visible below.
- Hành động: He spots Kiên using the car as cover to reach the uphill maintenance path.
- Thoại: Thành, qua thiết bị: Hướng tây. Lối kỹ thuật sau xe trắng.
- Cuối: Kiên and Hạ moving behind the white car toward the stair.

- Ý đồ: Give the audience a concrete route of escape at the moment Kiên exploits it.
- Bố cục: Use Thành's roof-side observation angle, white car below and the western service stair beyond.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên uses the car as cover while retaining Hạ; Thành stays at his observation post and transmits.
- Diễn xuất: Thành reports direction before emotion, recognizing a changing problem rather than merely shouting danger.
- Điểm nét: Favor the route and paired movement below; do not pull focus into an unnecessary full face of Thành.
- Chất liệu: Roof edge and car paint catch the new floodlight, service stair remains darker but legible.
- Âm nổi: Radio compression carries the short report over shoes moving behind the idling car.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the car briefly occlude the pair, then reveal where they reappear so the report resolves the geography.

**Clip 5 · CUT · HARBOUR · H**
- Đầu: Dũng trying to pull Sơn behind the black car, Mây within reach.
- Hành động: Mây and an officer separate Dũng from Sơn without dropping the immobile man.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Đưa Sơn về xe cứu hộ.
- Cuối: Sơn supported by the officer, Dũng withdrawing behind a crate.

- Ý đồ: Make Sơn's extraction competent while showing why Mây cannot chase every threat at once.
- Bố cục: Keep medium-wide handheld at bollard height, Mây and assisting officer approaching from landward side.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They separate Dũng from Sơn and transfer his weight to the officer without dropping his head or torso.
- Diễn xuất: Mây's urgency is practical, Dũng relinquishes position reluctantly and keeps searching for a way back.
- Điểm nét: Keep the support points and seated man's breath readable, not a montage of isolated fighting hands.
- Chất liệu: Sơn's LEFT bandage remains visible, the black car stays east and no fish crate becomes a body receptacle.
- Âm nổi: Short commands, cloth shifting and supported steps replace heavy blows or a crowd cheer.
- Nhịp diễn: The extraction progresses once from held to supported; Dũng's withdrawal finishes toward the already present crate.

**Clip 6 · L · HARBOUR_SERVICE · W**
- Đầu: Kiên and Hạ at the foot of the narrow maintenance stair behind the apron.
- Hành động: He blocks the lower gate with a tipped cart and forces her upward.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Cart blocking the lower gate, pair ascending the service stair.

- Ý đồ: Turn the escape into an obstacle that will cost the rescuers real time.
- Bố cục: Frame the lower service gate and first stair together, keeping harbour direction behind them.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên tips the existing cart across the lower gate before pushing Hạ upward, never releasing her unguarded.
- Diễn xuất: Hạ studies the narrowing route rather than collapsing; Kiên becomes hurried and less polished.
- Điểm nét: Keep the cart obstruction and departing pair sharp enough to understand in one wide setup.
- Chất liệu: Metal gate and cart wheels remain the same objects, no new padlock or unexplained barrier.
- Âm nổi: Cart wheel scrape and gate clang are brief, then shoes climb into the thinner stair echo.
- Nhịp diễn: The obstruction lands once and stays; finish with upward progress so the next team has something specific to clear.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Police commands continue over rescue movement and the service-route cut. No reset to a second stand-off.

### CẢNH 44: Lối Thoát Bị Chặn
*Thời gian truyện: D7 early night*
Kiên lên tháp qua lối phụ, tuyến cứu Sơn và truy bắt tách rõ.

**Câu hỏi:** Lực lượng có tới Hạ trước khi Kiên khóa được đường lên tháp?
**Móc vào:** Xe bị khóa đường, Kiên chọn lối bộ hẹp chứ không biến mất bằng ô tô.
**Điểm bẻ:** Hai tuyến cứu người tách ra; Sơn bất động, Hạ phải tự tạo thời gian.
**Gieo / trả:** Trả bản đồ39, giữ một đường truy đuổi đọc được trên hình.

**Clip 1 · J · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: Officers securing the two cars and the public pier, no civilians in the fight.
- Hành động: Mây directs one team to clear the blocked service gate, another to secure witnesses.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Một tổ lên tháp. Giữ người còn lại.
- Cuối: Cars secured, service-gate team starting to lift the cart.

- Ý đồ: Show the intervention narrowing to distinct tasks instead of an undefined harbour brawl.
- Bố cục: Use the wide to separate secured cars, the public pier and the team at the service gate.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây points one group toward the western obstruction and another toward witnesses, civilians absent from danger.
- Diễn xuất: Her authority remains focused; officers acknowledge through movement rather than a chorus of repeated commands.
- Điểm nét: Keep all task routes in one readable depth, with Mây not blocking the view of their departure.
- Chất liệu: Floodlight remains steady on cleared concrete and parked cars, no new smoke or chaos dressing.
- Âm nổi: Order, restrained radio-squelch and measured boots form the rhythm; do not add scattered shots, extra reply lines or shouting crowds.
- Nhịp diễn: Begin with control already forming, then send each team on its own route without re-enacting the initial rush.

**Clip 2 · CUT · HARBOUR_REAR · M**
- Đầu: Sơn supported upright on the stretcher, Linh beside him.
- Hành động: Linh checks his breathing as Bảy guides the stretcher into the van.
- Thoại: Bác sĩ Linh (@Bác sĩ Linh), nói trong hình: Vào xe. Tôi theo dõi ở đây.
- Cuối: Sơn on the secured mattress, Linh inside, Bảy by the rear door.

- Ý đồ: Keep the audience connected to Sơn's physical condition while the pursuit moves elsewhere.
- Bố cục: Frame Linh beside the supported stretcher, van rear doors and Bảy's guiding position clearly visible.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Linh stays by the breathing man while Bảy guides the stretcher into its secured mattress position.
- Diễn xuất: Linh's reassurance is addressed to the patient, Bảy concentrates on the door clearance, no comic reaction.
- Điểm nét: Hold the supported body and guiding hands in the same medium depth, not a diagnostic insert.
- Chất liệu: Clean mattress contrasts with worn van metal; bandage LEFT and black suit remain identical to the pier.
- Âm nổi: Stretcher wheels and a restrained latch sound carry the move, with breathing above the distant harbour commands.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete one careful transfer and settle the body before cutting away; do not repeat an exterior arrival afterward.

**Clip 3 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE_SERVICE · W**
- Đầu: Hạ at the top of the service stair, Kiên behind her with a handgun kept low.
- Hành động: She slows at an old hatch, keeping him talking while studying the latch.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Lối này không ra đường lớn đâu.
- Cuối: Hạ beside the hatch, Kiên turning to listen for pursuit.

- Ý đồ: Make Hạ use familiarity with the tower before she can reach any physical advantage.
- Bố cục: The wide includes the hatch, Hạ ahead and Kiên immediately behind with handgun LOW in LEFT hand.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên's RIGHT hand holds Hạ's LEFT upper arm; she slows at the hatch without turning into the weapon.
- Diễn xuất: She delivers the warning as practical knowledge, masking how closely she is assessing the latch.
- Điểm nét: Keep hatch and the two body positions readable, avoiding a glamour close-up that hides the gun hand.
- Chất liệu: Grey cotton brushes old painted metal, Kiên's white suit begins catching dust from the confined route.
- Âm nổi: Old metal complaining under a hand and thin stair echo under her line, no magical lock mechanism.
- Nhịp diễn: A small hesitation buys time; end on Kiên listening behind them while Hạ has noticed the useful latch.

**Clip 4 · CUT · HARBOUR_SERVICE · M**
- Đầu: Thành and an officer at the still-blocked lower gate.
- Hành động: They cut the cart's tangled securing rope and radio the delay.
- Thoại: Thành, qua thiết bị: Lối phụ bị chặn. Đang mở.
- Cuối: Cart partly lifted, space just opening at the gate.

- Ý đồ: Make the delay measurable by progress on the exact obstruction already shown.
- Bố cục: Frame Thành and an officer at the same cart and lower gate from its landward side.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: One officer clears the tangled securing rope while Thành holds the route clear and reports the delay.
- Diễn xuất: Thành keeps his voice functional, frustration present only in the speed of one glance upstairs.
- Điểm nét: Favor the opening gap and real clearance gained, not an instructional close-up of cutting technique.
- Chất liệu: Cart wheels stay planted until slack allows a partial lift, with no unexplained extra chains.
- Âm nổi: Rope strain, cart scrape and compressed report distinguish the work from an arbitrary clock sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Begin blocked, gain only a partial opening, end with the next step possible but not yet completed.

**Clip 5 · CUT · HARBOUR_REAR · O**
- Đầu: Dũng behind a crate seeing the stretcher enter Bảy's van.
- Hành động: He follows the cleared stretcher path as Mây turns back from the pier.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Dũng moving along the wall, Mây noticing him before the van.

- Ý đồ: Establish how Dũng finds the van so his return feels earned and threatening.
- Bố cục: Observe from behind his established crate, van path ahead and Mây turning from the pier.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng follows the visible stretcher route along the wall, staying on the same side of the lane.
- Diễn xuất: His attention is fixed on reclaiming Sơn, while Mây's quick look shows she has noticed the approach.
- Điểm nét: Keep the path and both endpoints legible instead of obscuring everything in shallow darkness.
- Chất liệu: Crate edge stays a foreground boundary, floodlight spill fades naturally toward the protected rear lane.
- Âm nổi: His boots soften near the wall, van sounds provide the direction he follows, no stealth-music cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Let him commit to the route once; finish before confrontation so Mây's blocking action remains the next new beat.

**Clip 6 · L · VAN · C**
- Đầu: Sơn's eyes open on the van mattress, lamp beside his face.
- Hành động: He tries to move his bandaged left hand but cannot yet lift it.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Tôi nghe được. Chưa giúp được ai.
- Cuối: Breathing visible, left hand still on the mattress.

- Ý đồ: Make helpless awareness replace the old fantasy that Sơn controls every situation.
- Bố cục: Close on his face at mattress level, his bandaged LEFT fingers only at the lower edge if naturally framed.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He attempts a small lift without moving the rest of his body; the hand settles where it began.
- Diễn xuất: Eyes stay alert and frustrated, breath remains present, narration is internal with visible lips at rest.
- Điểm nét: Keep face and the nearest finger movement plausibly within the same shallow plane, no impossible eye reflection.
- Chất liệu: Warm van lamp reveals tired skin without corpse colouring, black collar retains the same damp creases.
- Âm nổi: Breathing and low radio murmur support the single internal line, with no heartbeat countdown.
- Nhịp diễn: A genuine attempt occupies the take, followed by recognition of its failure; avoid repeated finger testing as padding.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep Sơn breathing unchanged across the cut to Linh beside him. Hạ route returns only in its next dedicated shot.

### CẢNH 45: Những Người Đang Chờ
*Thời gian truyện: D7 night continuous*
Dựng song song chăm sóc Sơn và Hạ trì hoãn Kiên.

**Câu hỏi:** Hạ có giữ được một khe cửa cho người cứu mình?
**Móc vào:** Kiên lo khóa cửa mà không thấy tay Hạ giữ lại cơ hội.
**Điểm bẻ:** Cô giấu kẹp vào chốt trong khi Mây giữ Sơn sống và chặn Dũng.
**Gieo / trả:** Gieo kẹp bị phát hiện48.5; không cho Mây biết trước thông tin này.

**Clip 1 · CUT · VAN · M**
- Đầu: Sơn lying on the van mattress with eyes open, Linh at his right side.
- Hành động: Linh steadies Sơn's head and keeps watching his breathing, no miracle timer or instant cure.
- Thoại: Bác sĩ Linh (@Bác sĩ Linh), nói trong hình: Đừng cố đứng. Cứ thở với tôi.
- Cuối: Linh seated beside Sơn, lamp kept steady.

- Ý đồ: Put care and uncertainty on screen without inventing a medical countdown or miracle intervention.
- Bố cục: Use a stable medium two-shot of Linh at Sơn's RIGHT side and the supported head.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Linh steadies the head and observes the existing breathing, keeping his bag where it was established.
- Diễn xuất: He speaks slowly enough for Sơn to follow; concern appears in sustained attention rather than alarm acting.
- Điểm nét: Stay on patient and caregiver together, not an unintroduced monitor, vial or diagnostic display.
- Chất liệu: Lamp remains warm and constant, clean bedding and worn van panels keep the improvised setting honest.
- Âm nổi: Linh's voice sits close to breathing, distant radio remains low, no new beeps or resuscitation effects.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the instruction guide one calmer breath; the uncertainty remains after the line instead of an instant visible cure.

**Clip 2 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE_SERVICE · M**
- Đầu: Hạ beside the stuck maintenance hatch, Kiên pulling at its wheel.
- Hành động: Keeping the handgun low in his LEFT hand, Kiên briefly releases his RIGHT-hand grip on Hạ LEFT upper arm to work the hatch wheel, his body blocking the narrow stair retreat. Hạ gives only the next necessary instruction, buying time while her free RIGHT hand nears her hair clip.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Chốt dưới trước. Ba tôi làm vậy.
- Cuối: Kiên occupied with the wheel using RIGHT hand, LEFT-hand handgun low; Hạ boxed beside the hatch with her free RIGHT hand near the clip.

- Ý đồ: Show Hạ buying seconds through selective knowledge, with Kiên focused on the wrong problem.
- Bố cục: Frame hatch wheel and both faces medium, Kiên's LEFT-hand weapon kept low and clear of her body.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên briefly releases his RIGHT grip to turn the wheel, LEFT-hand gun kept low, his body blocking her stair retreat.
- Diễn xuất: Her practical tone conceals calculation, Kiên resents needing instruction from the person he controls.
- Điểm nét: Keep their faces and the wheel relation readable, without shifting into a mechanical how-to insert.
- Chất liệu: Old hatch paint and white jacket sleeves show contact with the confined space, not new wounds.
- Âm nổi: Wheel resistance and a brief metal creak punctuate her measured words, letting silence buy more time.
- Nhịp diễn: Give only the next instruction, then wait for Kiên's effort; do not solve the whole hatch in this one shot.

**Clip 3 · CUT · HARBOUR_SERVICE · W**
- Đầu: Lower gate now open, two officers starting up the narrow service stair.
- Hành động: Thành sends their position and asks the lighthouse team to keep Kiên talking.
- Thoại: Thành, qua thiết bị: Đã thông. Tổ một lên lối phụ.
- Cuối: Officers already above the gate, Thành moving after them.

- Ý đồ: Pay off clearance of the lower barrier while preserving the remaining distance to Hạ.
- Bố cục: The wide starts at the same gate now open, stair route rising from the frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Officers pass the cleared cart in order, Thành follows after sending their position rather than overtaking.
- Diễn xuất: Their urgency is disciplined; Thành's concise report sounds like progress, not a premature rescue announcement.
- Điểm nét: Keep the first stair turn visible so forward movement has a destination and does not vanish offscreen aimlessly.
- Chất liệu: The displaced cart stays beside the gate, no longer magically in its original blocking position.
- Âm nổi: Boots change from concrete to metal, radio compression carrying only the actual clearance update.
- Nhịp diễn: Begin after the last obstacle movement, send the team upward once, finish with Thành entering the same route.

**Clip 4 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE_SERVICE · I**
- Đầu: Hạ's hair clip concealed in her right palm near the inner latch.
- Hành động: As Kiên pushes the hatch with his RIGHT hand and keeps the LEFT-hand handgun low, Hạ wedges her existing silver clip into the inner latch gap with her free RIGHT hand. The device only prevents full closure; no new door or tool is introduced.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Clip holding the latch short of fully closing behind them.

- Ý đồ: Give Hạ a small decisive action the captor does not notice and the audience can remember.
- Bố cục: Use the locked detail on her free RIGHT palm and the inner latch, not her whole body.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: With Kiên pushing the hatch using his RIGHT hand, Hạ places the existing clip from her free RIGHT palm.
- Diễn xuất: Her hand is precise without displaying triumph, no pause to admire the trick or look into camera.
- Điểm nét: Hold the clip and latch in one sensible focal plane; no magnified diagram or new lock part.
- Chất liệu: A little chipped paint defines the gap, the clip retains the same silver finish seen in the video.
- Âm nổi: A tiny metal tick is almost lost beneath the hatch creak, without an explanatory music accent.
- Nhịp diễn: One concealed placement changes the outcome; end with the latch just short of closing, never show a second attempt.

**Clip 5 · CUT · VAN · M**
- Đầu: Mây at the van door, Linh at Sơn's side.
- Hành động: She tells Sơn the teams are moving and returns his closed watch to his right hand.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Người của tôi đang lên. Anh cứ thở.
- Cuối: Closed watch in Sơn's right palm, Mây's hands free.

- Ý đồ: Make Mây offer concrete reassurance based on the team she can actually hear.
- Bố cục: Frame her at the van door beside Linh and Sơn, with enough room for the watch handover.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She returns the same CLOSED watch into Sơn's RIGHT palm, his LEFT bandage untouched.
- Diễn xuất: Mây's concern is private but her words remain factual; Sơn registers the weight without opening the lid.
- Điểm nét: Keep handover and both faces readable, with no insert that implies the photograph inside has been revealed.
- Chất liệu: The engraved closed lid catches warm lamplight, no ticking or second chain introduced.
- Âm nổi: Her line and a light metal touch accompany breathing; radio stays a separate restrained background layer.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the object settle in his palm before her hand withdraws, ending on a complete return rather than a sentimental freeze.

**Clip 6 · J · VAN_REAR · W**
- Đầu: Mây stepping out of the van, Dũng approaching along the wall.
- Hành động: She closes one rear door behind herself and blocks his approach.
- Thoại: Dũng (@Dũng), nói trong hình: Để tao đưa nó về cho má.
- Cuối: Mây between Dũng and the van, open retreat lane behind her.

- Ý đồ: Make Mây choose the threatened doorway before Dũng can reach the patient again.
- Bố cục: Use the wide to keep van rear, wall approach and the open retreat lane in one geography.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She steps out, closes ONE rear door and puts herself between Dũng and the remaining opening.
- Diễn xuất: Her posture is economical and balanced; Dũng's demand sounds like obedience curdled into ownership.
- Điểm nét: Keep both opponents and the protected doorway readable, rather than cutting to reaction shots within the take.
- Chất liệu: Van metal and the same loose cart remain practical obstacles, no fresh weapon or defensive equipment appears.
- Âm nổi: One door latch and Dũng's approaching boots precede his line, with breathing muffled inside the van.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete the door movement once, then let the held distance make the next advance dangerous.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: The van-door latch leads directly into Dũng first advance. Do not introduce him arriving a second time.

### CẢNH 46: Mây Giữ Cửa
*Thời gian truyện: D7 night continuous*
Mây khống chế Dũng bằng còng mới, Sơn trở lại từng chút.

**Câu hỏi:** Mây có giữ được người bệnh mà không biến thành người đánh đổi người?
**Móc vào:** Dũng tới đúng vệt cáng vừa qua, không xuất hiện ngẫu nhiên.
**Điểm bẻ:** Cô nhận hỗ trợ, khống chế Dũng đúng nhiệm vụ, Sơn bắt đầu cử động.
**Gieo / trả:** Trả hai còng mới35; kết thúc mối đe dọa tay sai trước đỉnh tháp.

**Clip 1 · CUT · VAN_REAR · H**
- Đầu: Mây between Dũng and the van doors, loose cart to her left.
- Hành động: Dũng rushes for the van door, Mây angles a loose cart into his direct path.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Anh không đưa ai đi nữa.
- Cuối: Cart between them, Dũng off balance, Mây beside its handle.

- Ý đồ: Turn the established cart into a simple barrier, not an extravagant winning stunt.
- Bố cục: Keep restrained medium-wide from one side of the lane, van behind Mây and cart to her left.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng advances toward the remaining door, Mây angles the existing cart across that direct path.
- Diễn xuất: She delivers the refusal through concentration, not a triumphant taunt; he is surprised only for a beat.
- Điểm nét: Retain feet, cart and doorway in view so the collision of intentions stays readable.
- Chất liệu: Cart wheels skip lightly on concrete, old cut cuff remains on Dũng's RIGHT wrist at this point.
- Âm nổi: A wheel rattle and sharp shoe stop support the line, without a heavy punch or metallic explosion effect.
- Nhịp diễn: The cart turns once and stops his line; end with both still active, leaving the next countermove unperformed.

**Clip 2 · CONTINUE · VAN_REAR · H**
- Đầu: Cart between them, Dũng off balance, Mây beside its handle.
- Hành động: Dũng pushes the cart aside, Mây steps clear toward the arriving officer.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Mây beside the officer, Dũng facing two people rather than one.

- Ý đồ: Show Mây accepting support rather than insisting on a solitary victory.
- Bố cục: Continue the exact handheld setup and focal range from the cart obstruction, no new angle at the seam.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Dũng shoves the cart aside while Mây clears its path toward the officer already arriving.
- Diễn xuất: She checks the officer's position with one glance, Dũng realizes his route is now confronted by two people.
- Điểm nét: Do not reset focus to a new face; let the existing medium-wide hold cart, steps and officer arrival.
- Chất liệu: The moved cart stays at the side reached by the shove, no reset to its prior diagonal position.
- Âm nổi: Wheels scrape continuously through the seam, followed by controlled boots, no new score beat.
- Nhịp diễn: Advance directly from the prior stop; finish when the two-person boundary has formed, not after beginning restraint.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · VAN_REAR · H**
- Đầu: Mây beside the officer, Dũng facing two people rather than one.
- Hành động: Mây and the officer control Dũng's arms against the van side without a head strike.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Dũng restrained standing, both arms controlled.

- Ý đồ: Make the restraint a result of cooperation, preserving both the patient boundary and Mây's role.
- Bố cục: Keep the same handheld axis and distance as the preceding continuous shot.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây and the existing officer bring Dũng's arms under control against the van side, keeping his head clear.
- Diễn xuất: Dũng's resistance decreases through lost leverage, while Mây stays intent rather than furious.
- Điểm nét: Hold bodies and contact points together, with no technical wrist insert or hidden impact cut.
- Chất liệu: The van side flexes only slightly under ordinary weight, no new dent or blood appears.
- Âm nổi: Cloth strain, short exertion and one dull panel contact carry the action, never a neck-cracking sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete one controlled movement to a standing restraint; leave cuff application to its separate next shot.

**Clip 4 · CUT · VAN_REAR · I**
- Đầu: A fresh intact pair of Mây's issued cuffs in her hands.
- Hành động: She secures Dũng's wrists together and hands him to the officer.
- Thoại: Mây, ngoài khung hình: Niêm phong cả chiếc cũ trên tay hắn.
- Cuối: Dũng cuffed to himself under escort, old cut cuff still separately visible.

- Ý đồ: Distinguish the new lawful restraint from the broken cuff Dũng has carried back.
- Bố cục: Lock the detail on Mây's fresh cuffs and Dũng's controlled wrists, existing officer maintaining position.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She secures the new pair once, while the old cut ring is kept visibly separate for logging.
- Diễn xuất: Hands work efficiently without a victory flourish; Dũng no longer dictates when anyone leaves.
- Điểm nét: Keep fresh intact chain and old cut component visually distinct, no sudden usable loose cuff end.
- Chất liệu: Steel is worn but clean, RIGHT-wrist old ring condition stays consistent with the earlier cut chain.
- Âm nổi: One concise cuff closure and her off-frame instruction, not repeated ratcheting for dramatic emphasis.
- Nhịp diễn: Let closure finish before transfer to escort; end on a settled controlled position rather than performing another arrest.

**Clip 5 · CUT · VAN · M**
- Đầu: Sơn still on the mattress, watch closed in his right hand.
- Hành động: His fingers begin moving, Linh helps him sit partway without letting him stand.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Tôi cử động được rồi.
- Cuối: Sơn propped against a support, left wrist still weak.

- Ý đồ: Let returning movement feel incremental and uncertain instead of a switch back to action-hero strength.
- Bố cục: Frame Sơn supported on the mattress with Linh close enough to prevent an impulsive rise.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: RIGHT fingers tighten slightly around the CLOSED watch, Linh assists only a partial seated position.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn's relief is restrained by effort, and Linh watches the movement without celebrating a complete recovery.
- Điểm nét: Keep his face and supported torso readable, LEFT bandage still weak and not suddenly doing the lift.
- Chất liệu: Warm lamp and rumpled black shirt remain unchanged, no whitening skin or sudden athletic posture.
- Âm nổi: A careful breath and mattress shift accompany his short line, no triumphant music swell.
- Nhịp diễn: Begin with one small successful movement, progress only to the stated supported sit, and stop short of standing.

**Clip 6 · L · VAN_REAR · M**
- Đầu: Dũng under escort beside the van, Mây checking her radio.
- Hành động: He realizes Sơn remains alive and can testify, stops asking to take him.
- Thoại: Dũng (@Dũng), nói trong hình: Má tưởng giữ được nó mãi.
- Cuối: Dũng led toward the custody vehicle, Mây returning to the van.

- Ý đồ: Let Dũng understand he has lost the right to move Sơn anywhere.
- Bố cục: Use a medium view of escorted Dũng with Mây by the radio and van behind the established lane.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He pauses within the officer's control, then accepts movement toward the custody vehicle.
- Diễn xuất: His line is a bitter recognition of Bà Hai's failed ownership, not a new threat or sequel promise.
- Điểm nét: Stay with his face until he turns back toward the road, avoiding an unnecessary cut to sleeping Sơn.
- Chất liệu: Fresh custody cuffs remain distinct, old cut ring is no longer being treated as a usable restraint.
- Âm nổi: Escort footfalls and a faint cuff clink replace the earlier struggle; radio remains practical background.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the sentence end before he resumes walking, so his departure clears the scene rather than restarting conflict.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Custody footsteps recede, incoming van breathing introduces seated Sơn. His watch remains closed and silent.

### CẢNH 47: Tỉnh Lại
*Thời gian truyện: D7 night continuous*
Sơn chấp nhận đi cùng Mây trong vai trò hỗ trợ, không tự lao đi.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn có chấp nhận được cứu và đi sau người khác?
**Móc vào:** Bàn chân chạm sàn mà cơ thể chưa đứng vững.
**Điểm bẻ:** Anh chấp nhận vị trí hỗ trợ, Mây dẫn đường thay vì thả anh tự lao đi.
**Gieo / trả:** Khẳng định cái giá Tê còn tồn tại suốt cuộc tiếp cận.

**Clip 1 · J · VAN · M**
- Đầu: Sơn seated with support, Mây at the doorway, Linh watching him.
- Hành động: Linh checks that he can follow instructions and move both feet.
- Thoại: Bác sĩ Linh (@Bác sĩ Linh), nói trong hình: Chậm thôi. Anh chưa khỏe lại.
- Cuối: Sơn still seated, feet on van floor, Linh close enough to support.

- Ý đồ: Keep the cost of Tê visible just as the audience wants Sơn to get moving.
- Bố cục: The medium holds seated Sơn, Linh beside him and Mây at the same van doorway.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Linh checks response and foot movement without adding a medical procedure or inviting a sudden stand.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn wants to hurry but has to concentrate on following the simple instruction; Linh stays matter-of-fact.
- Điểm nét: Hold all three at their existing depth, letting Sơn's effort be read through posture and eyes.
- Chất liệu: LEFT bandage remains visible against dark clothes, closed watch stays with his RIGHT hand.
- Âm nổi: His breathing and feet reaching the van floor are audible under Linh's line, no recovery sting.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow a small adjustment to succeed, then end with support still necessary rather than announcing that he is cured.

**Clip 2 · CONTINUE · VAN · M**
- Đầu: Sơn still seated, feet on van floor, Linh close enough to support.
- Hành động: Sơn tells Mây about the lower hatch Hạ described in the past.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Hạ biết cửa dưới ban công.
- Cuối: Mây drawing the service hatch below the gallery on her notepad.

- Ý đồ: Make Sơn contribute knowledge while accepting that someone else controls the rescue.
- Bố cục: Continue the exact medium van composition, with Mây and Linh remaining where the preceding shot left them.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn indicates the remembered lower hatch verbally while Mây draws only its relation to the gallery.
- Diễn xuất: He speaks with effort but precision, not a fresh monologue about their romance or his expertise.
- Điểm nét: Keep the exchange and Mây's sketching hand readable without resetting the lens or inserting a map close-up.
- Chất liệu: Notepad remains the existing one, watch stays CLOSED and his LEFT wrist does not suddenly support his weight.
- Âm nổi: Pencil movement and the continuing van bed accompany the short line, no imagined Hạ voice replay.
- Nhịp diễn: Let him finish the one useful fact, then register Mây taking it in before the next decision.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · VAN · M**
- Đầu: Mây drawing the service hatch below the gallery on her notepad.
- Hành động: Mây receives confirmation that the main door is accessible, decides to lead that approach herself.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Đi với tôi. Ở phía sau tôi.
- Cuối: Mây closing her notepad, Linh continuing to support Sơn.

- Ý đồ: Move leadership to Mây without making Sơn passive or repeating the whole tactical plan.
- Bố cục: Stay in the same medium setup and focal range across the continuous seam.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây receives the existing radio confirmation, closes her notepad and states that Sơn remains behind her.
- Diễn xuất: Her firmness is protective and professional; Sơn's assent should be a small visible acceptance, not defiance.
- Điểm nét: Keep the listening faces in the established plane, no focus reset toward an invented radio display.
- Chất liệu: Notebook returns to her same pouch, lamp direction and Linh's supporting position remain unchanged.
- Âm nổi: A brief radio-squelch click motivates the received update beneath the established instruction, without adding another spoken line.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the decision follow the received information, then leave a short beat for Sơn to accept its terms.

**Clip 4 · L · VAN_REAR · W**
- Đầu: Mây helping Sơn step down, Linh beside his weaker left side.
- Hành động: Sơn tests his balance against the van door instead of running.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn standing with support, right hand holding the closed watch.

- Ý đồ: Make standing itself an obstacle so the next journey has physical stakes.
- Bố cục: Use the wide to include van floor height, Linh on Sơn's weaker LEFT side and Mây beside him.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They help him descend once; he tests balance against the van instead of launching forward.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn hides embarrassment more than pain, Mây waits for a stable footing without an impatient pull.
- Điểm nét: Keep all supporting contact and both feet visible so no edit conceals an impossible recovery.
- Chất liệu: CLOSED watch stays in his RIGHT hand, bandage never transfers sides and jacket remains the same black suit.
- Âm nổi: A shoe reaches concrete, mattress creak recedes and a careful exhale replaces dialogue.
- Nhịp diễn: Spend the time on one genuine balance adjustment, ending upright with help still available.

**Clip 5 · CUT · MOTORBIKE · M**
- Đầu: Motorcycle stationary, engine off, two helmets on the seat.
- Hành động: Sơn pockets the closed watch, both fasten their helmets, then Mây takes the front seat and Sơn the pillion seat.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Mây riding position, Sơn seated behind, both helmets fastened.

- Ý đồ: Turn practical preparation into proof that the rescue is controlled, not reckless flight.
- Bố cục: Frame the stationary motorcycle and both people medium, helmets on the seat at the start.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn pockets the same CLOSED watch, both secure helmets, Mây takes controls and Sơn the rear place.
- Diễn xuất: He moves cautiously around the bike, she checks his readiness without treating him as a child.
- Điểm nét: Keep hands and helmet fastenings naturally within the medium frame; do not insert riding instructions.
- Chất liệu: Black suit and LEFT bandage remain visible, olive jacket does not reappear and no second watch is introduced.
- Âm nổi: Helmet straps and fabric are quiet foreground sounds; engine remains OFF until the next shot.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete the necessary preparation once, ending seated and ready rather than starting the journey prematurely.

**Clip 6 · CUT · MOTORBIKE · W**
- Đầu: Both seated on the stationary motorcycle, helmets already fastened.
- Hành động: Mây starts the engine, confirms the route by radio, departs slowly with Sơn holding the rail.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Chúng tôi lên cửa chính.
- Cuối: Motorcycle leaving left to right, Sơn seated steadily behind her.

- Ý đồ: Let Mây carry both responsibility and literal control of the journey.
- Bố cục: The wide holds the already seated pair and a clear left-to-right departure route.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She starts the stationary motorcycle, gives the short radio report and leaves gently with Sơn holding the rear rail.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn stays behind her and does not reach for the controls; Mây sounds focused, not exhilarated.
- Điểm nét: Keep the whole departure readable, no drone lift or speed-ramp replacing the established view.
- Chất liệu: Both helmets remain fastened, LEFT bandage protected and the watch now stays inside Sơn's jacket.
- Âm nổi: Ignition occurs only once, settles into the same engine note that will bridge to the road.
- Nhịp diễn: Pause only long enough to confirm the route, then move out continuously without replaying helmet preparation.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep the same motorcycle ignition after helmets are secured. Cut to the road with movement still left to right.

### CẢNH 48: Hai Lối Lên Tháp
*Thời gian truyện: D7 night continuous*
Hai tổ tiến tới, Hạ giữ lối phụ hé, Sơn không tự đi một mình.

**Câu hỏi:** Một hành động nhỏ của Hạ có đủ biến lối bị khóa thành lối vào?
**Móc vào:** Hai lối lên cùng một tầng, không hai cuộc truy đuổi rời rạc.
**Điểm bẻ:** Tổ phụ tìm thấy kẹp, Mây và Sơn đồng thời tới cửa chính.
**Gieo / trả:** Trả kiến thức hải đăng15–16 và kẹp45.4.

**Clip 1 · CUT · CLIFFROAD · T**
- Đầu: Same motorcycle already on the lower cliff road, travelling left to right.
- Hành động: Mây rides uphill at a controlled speed while Sơn braces against the rear rail.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Motorcycle approaching the lighthouse bend, both helmets still on.

- Ý đồ: Make the climb feel purposeful while preserving Sơn's limited condition and Mây's leadership.
- Bố cục: Track laterally with the same motorcycle moving left to right uphill, no aerial detour.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây holds a measured line, Sơn braces on the rear rail rather than leaning forward as a second rider.
- Diễn xuất: His concentration appears in rigid shoulders and careful breathing; her attention remains on the road.
- Điểm nét: Keep both helmeted profiles and the road bend readable with the same tracking distance.
- Chất liệu: Existing headlamp supplies road light, dark sea remains below, no new rain or cinematic spray appears.
- Âm nổi: The established engine note continues, joined by tires and wind, not a fresh chase-music track.
- Nhịp diễn: Maintain momentum through one visible bend, ending near the lighthouse approach without adding a near-crash.

**Clip 2 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE_BASE · W**
- Đầu: Motorcycle stopped at the main entrance, engine switched off.
- Hành động: They dismount and leave both helmets on the seat before entering.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Both on foot at the main door, helmets left on the motorcycle.

- Ý đồ: Carry the approach directly into an organized entry rather than another heroic arrival.
- Bố cục: Use a wide that includes parked bike, main iron door and the first interior stair.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They dismount after the engine is off, place both helmets on the seat and enter on foot.
- Diễn xuất: Mây keeps an eye on Sơn's balance, he accepts the slower pace without repeating a protest.
- Điểm nét: Keep feet, helmets and doorway within one readable setup so nothing vanishes between the two actions.
- Chất liệu: Same motorcycle stays outside, helmets remain together on its seat, no new vehicle or weapon appears.
- Âm nổi: Engine ends cleanly, helmet contact and iron-door wind take over as local sounds.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete the transition from seated to entering once, leaving the staircase ascent to its next shot.

**Clip 3 · L · LIGHTHOUSE_STAIR · T**
- Đầu: Mây leading up the spiral stair, Sơn a step below.
- Hành động: He climbs using the rail, pauses once to regain balance, Mây waits.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Both below the final landing, no overtaking or running.

- Ý đồ: Let every gained step show that Sơn is participating without pretending to be physically restored.
- Bố cục: Track smoothly along the existing spiral route with Mây one step ahead and the rail continuously visible.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn uses the rail, stops at one step to steady himself, Mây waits and resumes with him.
- Diễn xuất: He is frustrated by the delay but not theatrically collapsing; she reads the pause and stays calm.
- Điểm nét: Keep their changing height and the next landing in focus enough to follow, not alternating face inserts.
- Chất liệu: Bandage remains LEFT, black suit creases at the careful grip, no instant drying or costume change.
- Âm nổi: Uneven but controlled steps echo against the stairwell, wind enters through existing slits.
- Nhịp diễn: One pause breaks the upward rhythm, then progress returns; do not repeat the hesitation at each stair.

**Clip 4 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE_SERVICE · M**
- Đầu: Kiên testing the gallery hatch behind him, Hạ facing the sea wall.
- Hành động: Kiên tests the latch with his RIGHT hand while keeping the handgun low in his LEFT. The existing hair clip prevents full closure. He gives up, regrips Hạ LEFT upper arm with his RIGHT hand and moves her upward.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Service hatch still ajar, Kiên LEFT-hand handgun low and RIGHT hand again holding Hạ LEFT upper arm; Hạ has seen the gap remain.

- Ý đồ: Reward Hạ's concealed action by making the failed closure visible to her and the audience.
- Bố cục: Frame Kiên testing the hatch medium, Hạ facing the inner sea wall within his control.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên tests the latch with RIGHT hand while keeping LEFT-hand gun low, then regrips Hạ LEFT upper arm before moving her upward.
- Diễn xuất: Hạ watches without revealing satisfaction; Kiên interprets the resistance as old machinery, not a plotted trick.
- Điểm nét: Keep the narrow persistent gap readable, avoiding a new macro mechanism or a miraculous opening by itself.
- Chất liệu: The same clip prevents the same latch seating, paint and garment dust remain as established.
- Âm nổi: One incomplete metal click and Kiên's impatient breath explain the failure without a narrator.
- Nhịp diễn: Let him try the closure only once, then force Hạ onward, leaving the ajar state for the following discovery.

**Clip 5 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE_SERVICE · I**
- Đầu: An officer's hand reaching the same hatch from below, Thành behind him.
- Hành động: The officer recognizes the existing clip and holds the hatch open. After the first bodies clear the gap, Thành retrieves the silver clip into his RIGHT palm and follows the same route quietly.
- Thoại: Thành, qua thiết bị: Con tin để cửa cho mình.
- Cuối: Hatch held open by the officer, recovered clip in Thành RIGHT palm, Thành following through the gap.

- Ý đồ: Make a tiny survival decision become an actual route for other people.
- Bố cục: Hold the detail on the already ajar latch, an assisting hand reaching from below, Thành behind.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: The officer holds the hatch open after entry; Thành retrieves the existing clip once the first bodies have cleared the gap.
- Diễn xuất: Their restraint contrasts with Kiên's earlier urgency, and Thành's report carries recognition rather than praise.
- Điểm nét: Keep clip and latch legible in one plane, with background bodies only as spatial context.
- Chất liệu: No new tool opens the hatch; the recovered clip stays in Thành RIGHT palm, not simultaneously still in the latch.
- Âm nổi: Careful metal contact and low radio compression replace loud entry sounds that would contradict their quiet approach.
- Nhịp diễn: Begin with the gap Hạ left, complete one retrieval after clearance, end with hatch held open and clip safely recovered.

**Clip 6 · L · LIGHTHOUSE_STAIR · M**
- Đầu: Mây and Sơn at the last stair corner, Kiên heard above.
- Hành động: Mây announces herself and asks him to move away from Hạ.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Kiên. Tôi lên nói chuyện.
- Cuối: Mây at the corner, Sơn one step lower and behind her.

- Ý đồ: Place Mây at the threshold of conversation while Sơn visibly remains behind her.
- Bố cục: Use the medium at the last main-stair corner, gallery light above and Sơn one step lower.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây stops before exposing the whole group, announces herself and holds the corner after speaking.
- Diễn xuất: Her tone is steady enough to invite a response; Sơn listens rather than answering Kiên on her behalf.
- Điểm nét: Keep Mây's mouth and Sơn's listening position readable, no cut to the off-screen captor during the line.
- Chất liệu: The main-stair rail and same practical light define the height already climbed, without a new tactical backdrop.
- Âm nổi: Her voice travels up the existing stair echo, with wind and a restrained radio bed beneath it.
- Nhịp diễn: Arrival resolves into a stop, then the announcement changes the scene from movement to negotiation.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep wind and restrained radio traffic through the parallel cut to Bà Hai arrest below. The climb continues off screen.

### CẢNH 49: Chiếc Vòng Ngọc
*Thời gian truyện: D7 night simultaneous with the climb*
Bà Hai bị bắt bởi tổ dưới cảng, trả giá cho việc coi người là hàng.

**Câu hỏi:** Khi quyền sở hữu không còn tác dụng, Bà Hai còn lời nào để nói?
**Móc vào:** Dũng chờ một mệnh lệnh cuối mà bà không còn đưa ra được.
**Điểm bẻ:** Bà tự tháo vòng nhưng không thể dùng quan hệ mẹ con để xóa việc bán người.
**Gieo / trả:** Kết tuyến Bà Hai bằng mất quyền, không thêm một đoạn xin tha.

**Clip 1 · CUT · HARBOUR · W**
- Đầu: Bà Hai on the stone bench under the floodlight, one officer in front of her.
- Hành động: She looks toward the custody road off screen and sets her unlit holder down, unable to give another order.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Holder on bench, Bà Hai's hands resting on her knees.

- Ý đồ: Show Bà Hai's authority draining through the ordinary act of having nothing useful to order.
- Bố cục: The wide isolates her on the established stone bench, an officer present and custody road off frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She sets the unlit holder down and keeps both hands on her knees rather than signaling again.
- Diễn xuất: Her composure remains practiced, but the delay before releasing the holder reveals the lost habit of control.
- Điểm nét: Let the distance around her be readable; do not push into a tear close-up in this establishing beat.
- Chất liệu: Violet silk and jade remain on her LEFT wrist, floodlight stays functional rather than reverential.
- Âm nổi: Holder touching stone is a small clear sound amid distant custody footsteps, without mournful scoring.
- Nhịp diễn: Use one completed placement, then let her still living posture register defeat without freezing the image.

**Clip 2 · CUT · HARBOUR · M**
- Đầu: Dũng pausing under escort within sight of the bench.
- Hành động: He waits for an order, Bà Hai cannot give him one that changes anything.
- Thoại: Dũng (@Dũng), nói trong hình: Má còn muốn con giữ ai nữa?
- Cuối: Dũng facing the road again, Bà Hai looking at her bracelet.

- Ý đồ: Make Dũng ask for an instruction his loyalty can no longer produce.
- Bố cục: Frame escorted Dũng medium with Bà Hai visible on the bench in the opposing depth.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He pauses only within the escort's allowance, then turns back toward the custody road.
- Diễn xuất: The line carries fatigue and an unwanted need for her approval, not a new violent challenge.
- Điểm nét: Stay on the exchange of looks without magnifying either person into a melodramatic reaction insert.
- Chất liệu: Fresh custody cuffs remain secure; the old cut component is evidence, not hanging ready for another trick.
- Âm nổi: Escort shoes halt briefly, a small cuff sound sits below his words, and the harbour continues normally.
- Nhịp diễn: Give Bà Hai the opportunity not to answer; Dũng's turn away finishes this relationship beat.

**Clip 3 · CUT · HARBOUR · I**
- Đầu: Jade bracelet on Bà Hai's left wrist.
- Hành động: She removes it deliberately and places it beside the unlit holder.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Bracelet and holder together on stone, both wrists bare.

- Ý đồ: Reduce the symbol of ownership to an object that no longer protects its owner.
- Bố cục: Lock the detail on her LEFT wrist and the same bench surface beside the unlit holder.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She removes the jade bracelet once and puts it down, keeping both bare wrists visible afterward.
- Diễn xuất: Hands are deliberate rather than trembling theatrically; hesitation may appear before the final release.
- Điểm nét: Keep bracelet, fingertips and holder in a plausible common plane without ornate reflections or inscriptions.
- Chất liệu: Jade keeps its existing colour and wear, violet cuff stays the same, no duplicate bracelet appears.
- Âm nổi: A restrained jade-on-stone contact is enough; do not add a ceremonial chime or score swell.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the fingers leave the object fully, ending on the two ordinary objects and empty wrists.

**Clip 4 · CUT · HARBOUR · M**
- Đầu: Bà Hai standing before the officer with bare wrists.

- Thoại: Bà Hai (@HaiNgoc), nói trong hình: Nó là con tôi nuôi.
- Cuối: Cuffs secured, Bà Hai looking toward the lighthouse.

- Ý đồ: Keep the claim of motherhood present without allowing it to function as a defence against responsibility.
- Bố cục: Frame her standing medium before the assisting officer, bench and surrendered objects behind.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She offers bare wrists for the cuffs, then looks toward the lighthouse after they are secured.
- Diễn xuất: Her sentence sounds like a fact she wants to regain power, not an appeal the scene endorses.
- Điểm nét: Keep face and cuffed hands within readable framing, avoiding an isolated beauty shot of the arrest.
- Chất liệu: Bracelet stays on the bench and never returns to her LEFT wrist; outfit remains immaculate but no longer commanding.
- Âm nổi: One cuff closure follows ordinary procedure, her voice clear above the same floodlight-and-sea environment.
- Nhịp diễn: The arrest happens once, and the glance toward the tower follows rather than interrupts it.

**Clip 5 · CUT · HARBOUR · C**

- Hành động: The officer records her words without accepting them as an excuse.
- Thoại: Cán bộ hỗ trợ, ngoài khung hình: Vậy bà sẽ khai cả chuyện đã bán người.
- Cuối: Bà Hai lowering her gaze away from the tower.

- Ý đồ: Let the law acknowledge her words while refusing to turn them into absolution.
- Bố cục: Use the close-up on Bà Hai listening, officer remains a voice outside this frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She lowers her gaze after the officer's factual response, with no reach toward the bench or bracelet.
- Diễn xuất: The response lands as an unwanted boundary; she contains the reaction rather than producing a confession speech.
- Điểm nét: Hold her eyes and the slight loss of chin height, no cut to a symbolic object during the line.
- Chất liệu: Bare wrists remain outside or low in frame, powdered face shows natural tired texture rather than sudden tears.
- Âm nổi: The officer's off-frame words are plain and unamplified, with normal harbour noise beneath.
- Nhịp diễn: Do not rush her lowered gaze, but avoid a farewell-like long hold that competes with the active hostage plot.

- Hành động: She walks under escort, leaving the bracelet for the evidence officer.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Empty bench with bracelet in an evidence tray, vehicle door open.

- Ý đồ: Close her physical route without claiming the whole film has ended.
- Bố cục: Use the wide to connect bench, evidence officer and custody vehicle on the protected road.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She walks under escort and leaves the surrendered objects behind; the evidence tray is handled by the established officer.
- Diễn xuất: Her pace is controlled but no longer sets anyone else's pace, and no one salutes her departure.
- Điểm nét: Keep the emptied bench and departing figure readable in the same geography without a new crane move.
- Chất liệu: Bracelet and holder stay together in custody, not carried secretly in her clothing.
- Âm nổi: Footsteps and vehicle preparation remain low and routine; incoming gallery wind can take over for the return upward.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the scene clear its space, then cut before a second emotional farewell or final-car-door flourish.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Incoming gallery wind replaces pier custody sounds before returning to Mây at the main stair entrance.

### CẢNH 50: Đỉnh Hải Đăng
*Thời gian truyện: D7 night continuous*
Mây giữ đối thoại, Hạ nhận ra lối thoát, Sơn không áp sát nòng súng.

**Câu hỏi:** Ai sẽ tạo được khoảng hở khi Kiên còn giữ Hạ và súng?
**Móc vào:** Bố cục cho thấy đường đi khả thi trước khi ai hành động.
**Điểm bẻ:** Mây giữ đối thoại, Hạ thay đổi vị trí có chủ đích, Sơn chờ thay vì lao tới.
**Gieo / trả:** Chuẩn bị cú bật đèn đã thử16.5, không tạo đèn thần kỳ.

**Clip 1 · J · LIGHTHOUSE · W**
- Đầu: Mây at the gallery entrance, Sơn behind her, Kiên holding Hạ near the rail.
- Hành động: Mây stops short and keeps a clear space between herself and the hostage pair.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Cho Hạ đứng riêng. Tôi nghe anh.
- Cuối: Four positions readable, service hatch at Kiên's far side.

- Ý đồ: Give the audience a complete danger map before the last negotiation begins.
- Bố cục: The wide places Mây at the eastern main doorway, Sơn behind, Kiên and Hạ near the southern rail.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây stops before closing the distance, Hạ's service hatch remains on Kiên's far side.
- Diễn xuất: Mây's calm is intentional, Sơn's restraint is visible in his refusal to step past her.
- Điểm nét: Keep all four positions readable with natural depth, rather than isolating the gun before geography exists.
- Chất liệu: Work lamps and sea fill remain low, backup lamp still OFF, white suit already rumpled.
- Âm nổi: Wind moves through the open gallery, her measured line distinct without suppressing the actual sea space.
- Nhịp diễn: The entrance settles into a controlled stop; the offered conversation is the action, not a new advance.

**Clip 2 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Kiên gripping Hạ, handgun low but visible in his other hand.
- Hành động: He asks for a vehicle off the coast road, refusing to surrender.
- Thoại: Kiên (@Vũ Kiên), nói trong hình: Một xe. Không ai theo.
- Cuối: Hạ looking toward the service hatch as Kiên watches Mây.

- Ý đồ: Expose Kiên's shrinking options while keeping the hostage and weapon physically distinct.
- Bố cục: Frame him medium with Hạ, LEFT-hand handgun low and RIGHT hand controlling her LEFT upper arm.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He keeps her between his retreat route and the approaching officers but does not wave the muzzle through her body.
- Diễn xuất: The smooth voice has become clipped, his practical demand revealing fear without a sudden comic collapse.
- Điểm nét: Keep gun hand, controlling hand and face legible in one setup; do not add a separate weapon insert.
- Chất liệu: Dust and creases carry forward on the white suit, grey dress and sandals remain unchanged.
- Âm nổi: His short demand sits above wind and a small grip-induced fabric sound, no cocking or gunfire effect.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the demand come quickly, then show him waiting for an answer he can no longer dictate.

**Clip 3 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Mây staying at the entrance with her hands visible.
- Hành động: She keeps him engaged while an officer reaches the service side unseen by Kiên.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Muốn đi, trước hết bỏ tay khỏi cô ấy.
- Cuối: Kiên facing the main entrance, service side outside his attention.

- Ý đồ: Make negotiation buy the exact attention Hạ needs, not merely pause action for dialogue.
- Bố cục: Use medium coverage from the established side of the axis, Mây facing the held pair.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She remains at the entrance with visible hands while the existing officer reaches the service side beyond Kiên's attention.
- Diễn xuất: Her line offers a first achievable action, no taunt about his weakness or certainty he will not shoot.
- Điểm nét: Favor Mây's speaking face while preserving Kiên's eyeline toward her; do not rack to the hidden officer.
- Chất liệu: Stable practical lighting keeps the service side less prominent without making the space unreadably black.
- Âm nổi: Wind and restrained footfalls remain motivated; her voice is firm enough to hold Kiên's attention alone.
- Nhịp diễn: Give him time to process the condition, then end with his attention still on the main entrance.

**Clip 4 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · C**
- Đầu: Hạ recognizing Sơn's bare hands and bandaged left wrist behind Mây.
- Hành động: She gives him the same small downward glance used beside the old hatch.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn following Hạ's eyeline toward the backup-light cable.

- Ý đồ: Let recognition of Sơn's changed hands become a shared practical understanding, not another romantic speech.
- Bố cục: Close on Hạ's face with Sơn behind Mây on her eyeline, no new spatial crossing.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ makes one small downward glance toward the existing backup-light cable, Sơn follows it without stepping forward.
- Diễn xuất: Her fear turns into concentration; his response is a barely perceptible readiness, not an exaggerated nod.
- Điểm nét: Keep the glance legible through eye movement and head angle, without an impossible reflected close-up of the cable.
- Chất liệu: LEFT wrist bandage and bare hands remain visible where framed; no lily or recovered scarf appears yet.
- Âm nổi: Only existing wind and a faint cable rattle, no whisper or extra cue line added.
- Nhịp diễn: The exchange should take one look, then a quiet recognition beat, leaving the physical move for later.

**Clip 5 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE_SERVICE · I**
- Đầu: Thành below the gallery at the tested backup-light lever.
- Hành động: He sees the open route and waits for Mây's radio cue instead of touching anything accidentally.
- Thoại: Thành, qua thiết bị: Tôi ở đèn dự phòng. Chờ lệnh.
- Cuối: Thành's hand near but not on the lever.

- Ý đồ: Show that the light will be a coordinated action using established equipment.
- Bố cục: Lock the detail on Thành's hand near the tested lever below the gallery.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He waits beside the lever without touching it, keeping clear of the officer's established service route.
- Diễn xuất: His radio report is controlled and quiet, not anticipation acted as a grin or accidental switch.
- Điểm nét: Keep hand and lever in the same plane; no new dashboard, timer or glowing control panel.
- Chất liệu: Old lever paint and adjacent wall retain their earlier appearance, backup remains OFF at this point.
- Âm nổi: Radio compression and faint gallery wind explain the location, without electrical activation before the cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Hold the hand ready through the short report; readiness is the new information and no action is pre-performed.

**Clip 6 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Hạ angled toward the inner wall, Kiên still holding her upper arm.
- Hành động: She asks to steady herself, shifting them one step away from the rail.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Cho tôi vịn tường. Tôi chóng mặt.
- Cuối: Hạ nearer the inner wall, Kiên pulled slightly off his preferred position.

- Ý đồ: Move Hạ into a safer geometry through a plausible request before the distraction comes.
- Bố cục: Frame Hạ and Kiên medium with the inner wall and remaining distance to the rail readable.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She shifts one step toward the wall under his RIGHT-hand hold; his LEFT-hand gun stays low and away.
- Diễn xuất: Her dizziness is useful restraint rather than a fainting performance; Kiên adjusts because he still needs her upright.
- Điểm nét: Keep their feet or weight change implied clearly within the medium composition, no covert escape hidden by a cut.
- Chất liệu: Grey fabric brushes masonry, white cuff remains at her LEFT upper arm, no new tears or injury.
- Âm nổi: One sandal adjustment and her measured request sit against the continuing wind.
- Nhịp diễn: Let Kiên allow exactly one step, ending with altered balance but the grip still intact for the next scene.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep the established gallery wind and faint cable rattle. Backup light turns on only on the cue at 51.1.

### CẢNH 51: Tay Không Giữ Người
*Thời gian truyện: D7 night continuous*
Hạ tạo khoảng hở, Sơn cứu cả kẻ thù, Mây bắt Kiên.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn sẽ cứu Hạ rồi để Kiên ngã, hay dừng hẳn cách sống cũ?
**Móc vào:** Khoảng hở do phối hợp tạo ra chứ không nhờ Kiên bỗng bất tài.
**Điểm bẻ:** Hạ tự thoát, Sơn chọn kéo Kiên vào, Mây hoàn tất bắt giữ.
**Gieo / trả:** Theo dõi súng TAY TRÁI của Kiên rơi vào trong trước hành động cứu.

**Clip 1 · MATCH · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Hạ near the inner wall, Kiên holding her arm, Mây still at the entrance.
- Hành động: Mây gives the short radio cue, the backup lamp turns on toward the sea, not into anyone's eyes.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Bật đèn. Giữ cửa phụ.
- Cuối: Kiên looking toward the unexpected lamp, Hạ already near the inner wall.

- Ý đồ: Create an earned distraction from a coordinated cue, not a blinding magical flash.
- Bố cục: Keep the medium on the existing positions, Hạ inner wall, Kiên beside her and Mây at the main door.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây gives the single cue and the tested lamp turns seaward; nobody crosses into a new position prematurely.
- Diễn xuất: Mây stays composed, Kiên's brief attention shift comes from unexpected coordinated activity at his service side.
- Điểm nét: Do not pull focus or cut to the lever; the lamp change is visible as steady spill across the same composition.
- Chất liệu: Backup light starts OFF and settles ON, preserving natural faces and the established working lamps.
- Âm nổi: Her cue, one relay click and continued wind suffice; no explosive light sound or heroic music accent.
- Nhịp diễn: The cue arrives promptly, the light settles, and Kiên's glance creates only a short usable window.

**Clip 2 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · H**
- Đầu: Kiên's attention shifted toward the lamp, Hạ beside the inner wall.
- Hành động: Hạ turns out of his loose grip and steps behind the doorway toward Mây.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Hạ behind Mây's line, Kiên no longer holding her.

- Ý đồ: Let Hạ complete the escape she prepared by changing her own position.
- Bố cục: Keep restrained handheld medium-wide on the same gallery side, doorway and Mây visible as her destination.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: With Kiên's attention shifted, Hạ turns her LEFT upper arm free from his RIGHT hand and steps behind the doorway.
- Diễn xuất: Her movement is purposeful and compact, not an elaborate fighting technique or a victorious pose.
- Điểm nét: Keep both the releasing grip and completed step readable without hiding contact in a close-up.
- Chất liệu: Grey dress, sandals and loose hair stay consistent, scarf still absent and no new weapon appears in her hand.
- Âm nổi: A sharp breath and sandal steps are enough, gunfire and impact sounds remain absent.
- Nhịp diễn: The single opening produces one escape movement; end with her clearly behind Mây's protective line.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · LIGHTHOUSE · H**
- Đầu: Hạ behind Mây's line, Kiên no longer holding her.
- Hành động: Kiên lunges after Hạ with his freed RIGHT arm, his LEFT-hand handgun held low toward empty inner concrete. Sơn checks the reaching RIGHT arm with his sound RIGHT arm. In Kiên stumble, his LEFT grip loosens and the handgun slips onto the inner gallery floor, away from Hạ.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Kiên stumbling backward toward the rail, Sơn off balance beside him; handgun already on the INNER gallery floor, nobody holding it.

- Ý đồ: Keep the weapon's loss legible before the story asks Sơn to save Kiên.
- Bố cục: Continue the exact handheld gallery setup from Hạ's escape, no camera or focus reset.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên reaches with freed RIGHT arm, LEFT-hand gun low; Sơn checks the reaching arm, the gun slipping inward during Kiên stumble.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn checks the reach using his sound RIGHT arm, visibly limited by the bandaged LEFT wrist.
- Điểm nét: Keep both men's upper bodies and the inner floor in the existing readable field, avoiding a technical disarming insert.
- Chất liệu: The single handgun lands on inner concrete and stays there, without duplication in Kiên's hand or near Hạ.
- Âm nổi: One restrained metal contact and foot scrape mark the changed control, not a shot or a weapon flourish.
- Nhịp diễn: Progress directly from the previous escape; finish on imbalance and the already dropped gun, leaving the rescue undecided.

**Clip 4 · CONTINUE · LIGHTHOUSE · H**
- Đầu: Kiên stumbling backward toward the rail, Sơn off balance beside him; handgun already on the INNER gallery floor, nobody holding it.
- Hành động: Kiên heel catches the bottom bar of the existing railing and his shoulders pitch against the upper rail, both feet still on the inner gallery. Sơn catches the jacket with his sound RIGHT hand and brings their weight inward rather than letting Kiên pitch farther. Both settle onto the inner floor; the handgun stays where it fell, beyond their hands.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Giữ lấy tay tôi.
- Cuối: Both men on the INNER gallery floor, feet away from the edge; handgun still on the same inner concrete spot, no second release or weapon in either hand.

- Ý đồ: Make Sơn's moral choice visible without giving him implausible strength after Tê.
- Bố cục: Continue from the same handheld angle with rail, feet and the dropped gun's inner-floor position readable.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Kiên catches the lower rail while still inside, Sơn uses his sound RIGHT hand on the jacket to bring their weight inward.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn chooses to help before his expression relaxes; Kiên briefly needs the man he tried to own.
- Điểm nét: Keep both feet inside the gallery and bodies below the point of a full over-edge fall, no dangling one-arm hoist.
- Chất liệu: Gun remains on the same inner concrete spot, bandage stays LEFT and the rail neither breaks nor invents new damage.
- Âm nổi: A fabric strain, rail contact and shared exhale carry the move, with no loud heroic impact.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the choice be a fraction before the pull; settle both onto the inner floor once without repeating the rescue.

**Clip 5 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · W**
- Đầu: Released handgun on the inner floor, both men down and Hạ safely by the door.
- Hành động: Mây secures the handgun, an officer controls Kiên, Hạ steadies Sơn.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Kiên, hết đường giữ người rồi.
- Cuối: Kiên restrained under officer control, handgun secured as evidence.

- Ý đồ: Transfer control to the law as soon as the physical danger is contained.
- Bố cục: The wide re-establishes the inner floor, dropped gun, officer with Kiên and Hạ by the safe doorway.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây secures the single handgun while the existing officer restrains Kiên, Hạ steadies Sơn instead of joining the arrest.
- Diễn xuất: Mây's line closes the threat, Hạ concentrates on Sơn's condition, Kiên remains a person under custody rather than a defeated prop.
- Điểm nét: Keep the three simultaneous responsibilities readable at separate positions, no rapid internal cuts between them.
- Chất liệu: The weapon stays inside and becomes evidence, no toss into the sea or fresh restraint gadget appears.
- Âm nổi: Clear words, controlled movements and ordinary metal handling replace the earlier struggle sounds.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete custody and care in parallel, ending with each person in a stable role before the emotional quiet begins.

**Clip 6 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Sơn seated against the inner wall, Hạ beside him, Mây a few paces away.
- Hành động: Mây checks they are safe and gives them a moment while maintaining custody of Kiên.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Hạ and Sơn seated together, clear gallery space between them and Kiên.

- Ý đồ: Give the rescued people space without pretending the consequences have disappeared.
- Bố cục: Frame Hạ and seated Sơn medium against the inner wall, Mây and controlled Kiên in the established background.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây checks their safety and steps back only far enough to permit privacy while maintaining the scene.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn's breath remains effortful, Hạ's attention is immediate and grounded, neither starts a celebration speech.
- Điểm nét: Keep foreground faces readable while background custody remains identifiable, not melted into an empty romantic void.
- Chất liệu: Grey dress and black suit retain ordeal creases, backup lamp stays steady and the watch remains closed inside his pocket.
- Âm nổi: Breathing and wind gradually become clearer as police commands lower, no new sentimental score cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Let care replace struggle in one unhurried beat, ending seated so the watch action can begin only in the next shot.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Only breathing and the steady backup lamp remain. Cut to the quiet watch scene without replaying the restraint.

### CẢNH 52: Lần Đầu Mở Đồng Hồ
*Thời gian truyện: D7 night continuous*
Trả lời lời hẹn bằng một hành động duy nhất, khăn được trao lại có nguồn.

**Câu hỏi:** Thứ đã dừng giữa hai người có còn tiếp tục được không?
**Móc vào:** Sau tiếng động lớn là một vật nhỏ chưa từng mở trong phim.
**Điểm bẻ:** Đồng hồ mở lần đầu và chạy lại, nhưng Sơn vẫn còn lời phải khai.
**Gieo / trả:** Trả lời lời hẹn06 bằng hành động, giữ im lặng thay lời giải thích.

**Clip 1 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Hạ and Sơn seated safely against the inner wall, officer with Kiên out of focus beyond.
- Hành động: Sơn takes the same closed watch from his inside pocket and shows Hạ the dented lid.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Nó dừng đêm anh chạy khỏi em.
- Cuối: Closed watch between them, his bandaged left hand resting on his knee.

- Ý đồ: Introduce the object as a difficult ordinary admission rather than another grand reveal.
- Bố cục: The medium keeps seated Hạ and Sơn close to the inner wall, officer and Kiên only as soft custody context.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn takes the same CLOSED watch from his inside pocket with RIGHT hand, LEFT bandage resting on his knee.
- Diễn xuất: His line contains regret without a speech; Hạ listens to what he says rather than watching for a trick.
- Điểm nét: Keep lid and their faces in a plausible medium plane, avoiding an anticipatory glimpse inside the closed case.
- Chất liệu: Dented silver remains the same object, no new repair, chain or photograph lying separately on their knees.
- Âm nổi: The lid is silent here, sea wind softened by the inner wall and breathing under his line.
- Nhịp diễn: Let him show the unopened lid once, then leave the pause for Hạ's recognition before the actual opening.

**Clip 2 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · I**
- Đầu: Sơn's right thumb at the closed watch clasp.
- Hành động: He opens the lid for the first time in the film, revealing cracked glass and Hạ's small photograph.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Watch open, stopped hands and photograph clearly visible without text.

- Ý đồ: Pay off the first opening with clarity and restraint after fifty-one scenes of withholding.
- Bố cục: Lock the detail on Sơn's RIGHT thumb, the shut clasp and Hạ's supporting presence at the edge.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He releases the lid once; the cracked glass and small photograph appear only as the case opens.
- Diễn xuất: His hand hesitates briefly from emotional weight, not a tremor designed to delay an ordinary latch forever.
- Điểm nét: Hold clasp, glass and photograph on the same small plane, no lens change or mirror-like eye reflection.
- Chất liệu: The case starts CLOSED, glass has the established crack, the single watch's hands remain stopped.
- Âm nổi: One small clasp click, softened wind, no ticking until it actually resumes in the following shot.
- Nhịp diễn: The opening occupies the first part, then allow a still-living hand adjustment that lets the audience see what was hidden.

**Clip 3 · CONTINUE · LIGHTHOUSE · I**
- Đầu: Watch open, stopped hands and photograph clearly visible without text.
- Hành động: Hạ steadies the open case while Sơn winds the crown once.
- Thoại: Hạ, ngoài khung hình: Nó vẫn còn sửa được.
- Cuối: Second hand moving again, both hands steady around the single watch.

- Ý đồ: Let the future resume through shared handling instead of an explanatory declaration.
- Bố cục: Continue the exact locked detail and focal plane from the newly opened case.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ steadies the SAME open watch while Sơn winds once with his sound RIGHT hand, no repeated opening.
- Diễn xuất: Her off-frame line is quiet and practical, leaving room for the implication without naming it.
- Điểm nét: Do not rack to faces or reset the case's angle; retain the exact crack and photo orientation at the seam.
- Chất liệu: Hands are stopped at first and move only after the single winding, with no second watch or new engraved message.
- Âm nổi: A gentle winding sound leads into resumed mechanical ticking, wind remains subdued and dialogue unforced.
- Nhịp diễn: Let winding finish before ticking becomes apparent, then end on ongoing seconds rather than replaying the turn of the crown.

**Clip 4 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · C**
- Đầu: Both listening to the newly audible ticking, no one looking into camera.
- Hành động: Sơn exhales into a small unforced smile, Hạ stays close without speaking.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn looking at Hạ, open watch lower in frame.

- Ý đồ: Give the smile weight because it follows something finally heard, not because music orders emotion.
- Bố cục: Close on the two faces at their established seated angle, open watch kept low in the frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn exhales and finds one small smile; Hạ stays close without reaching for another object.
- Diễn xuất: Relief is mixed with fatigue and unfinished responsibility, no ecstatic laugh or simultaneous tears on cue.
- Điểm nét: Keep their eyes and the nearest watch edge naturally readable, never simulate ticking reflected in their pupils.
- Chất liệu: Natural skin texture remains tired, grey dress and open black collar carry the same wrinkles.
- Âm nổi: The resumed ticking is heard close against breathing, distant sea wind continues without a score swell.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow the smile to arrive late from listening, then leave it incomplete enough that the next legal choice remains possible.

**Clip 5 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Mây holding the two previously sealed scarf sleeves beside them.
- Hành động: She returns the documented long scarf to Hạ and keeps the small fragment with the case file.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Phần còn lại của mày đây.
- Cuối: Long scarf in Hạ's hands, short fragment still sealed with Mây.

- Ý đồ: Return something Hạ owns through a documented human gesture, not a symbolic miracle.
- Bố cục: Frame Mây beside the seated pair with both existing evidence sleeves visible in the medium.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She gives Hạ only the long remainder, keeping the short fragment sealed with the file.
- Diễn xuất: Mây's warmth is familiar, Hạ receives the cloth without instantly changing clothes or using it as a weapon.
- Điểm nét: Keep the two sizes distinguishable and the handover legible, no isolated glamour shot of scarf fluttering.
- Chất liệu: Grey dress stays on Hạ, the long torn edge matches the fragment, sleeve count does not multiply.
- Âm nổi: Evidence sleeve rustle and one soft cloth movement sit under Mây's plain line.
- Nhịp diễn: The long section changes owner once, Mây withdraws with the small piece and the scene remains in the same night.

**Clip 6 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Hạ wrapping the long scarf loosely over the same grey dress.
- Hành động: Sơn closes the watch and looks toward Mây waiting by the stairs.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Tôi còn một việc phải nói hết.
- Cuối: Hạ still in grey dress with scarf, watch closed in Sơn's hand.

- Ý đồ: Let renewed intimacy lead directly to Sơn's unfinished responsibility instead of canceling it.
- Bố cục: Medium coverage keeps Hạ wrapping the returned long scarf over the SAME grey dress beside Sơn.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn closes the one watch and turns his attention toward Mây's waiting position by the stairs.
- Diễn xuất: He speaks with acceptance, Hạ's response is attentive rather than a fresh fear of abandonment.
- Điểm nét: Keep faces and the final closure readable without making a new watch-reveal insert.
- Chất liệu: Grey cotton remains beneath blue scarf, the closed watch stays in his RIGHT hand, bandage still LEFT.
- Âm nổi: The small lid closure joins softened wind; ticking may remain faint but should not become a recurring theme cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow the last private gesture to finish, then let his gaze toward the doorway start the next irreversible choice.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Renewed ticking continues briefly under the custody footsteps, then falls away naturally. Do not reopen the watch.

### CẢNH 53: Người Không Chạy Nữa
*Thời gian truyện: D7 night continuous*
Sơn tự nhận trách nhiệm, Hạ chấp nhận thay vì cản luật.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn có giữ quyết định chịu trách nhiệm ngay khi có thể về cùng Hạ?
**Móc vào:** Người vừa cứu anh cũng là người cần còng anh.
**Điểm bẻ:** Anh tự bước tới Mây; Hạ buông tay áo vì hiểu đó là lựa chọn của anh.
**Gieo / trả:** Khép hành trình không chạy bằng chủ động đưa tay, không một lời khẩu hiệu.

**Clip 1 · L · LIGHTHOUSE · W**
- Đầu: Mây waiting at the gallery doorway, Hạ and Sơn standing with care.
