- Hành động: Sơn approaches Mây on his own, keeping his hands where she can see them.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Tôi đi với cô.
- Cuối: Sơn in front of Mây, Hạ one step behind.

- Ý đồ: Make surrender the next voluntary action, not an arrest sprung on the rescued man.
- Bố cục: Use the wide to connect seated area, Mây at the doorway and Hạ just behind Sơn.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn stands carefully, approaches Mây with visible empty hands and no attempt to pass her toward an exit.
- Diễn xuất: He is tired but definite; Mây receives the choice without triumph and Hạ watches rather than obstructing.
- Điểm nét: Keep his entire approach and Mây's stationary position readable so the choice is unmistakably his.
- Chất liệu: LEFT bandage, black suit and Hạ's grey dress with returned scarf remain continuous.
- Âm nổi: His measured steps and short line sit over steady backup-light ambience, without a moralizing musical cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Give the rise one careful effort, then a direct approach that ends before the cuffing action belongs to the next shot.

**Clip 2 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Hạ beside Sơn before he reaches the doorway.
- Hành động: She asks if this is his choice, he answers without blaming her.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Anh tự chọn chứ?
- Cuối: Hạ releasing his sleeve deliberately.

- Ý đồ: Let Hạ test that he owns this decision instead of speaking for him.
- Bố cục: Frame Hạ beside Sơn medium before he reaches the doorway, her hand lightly on his sleeve.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She asks once and deliberately releases the sleeve, leaving him room to answer and continue.
- Diễn xuất: Her concern is adult and searching, not an impulsive attempt to overrule Mây or bargain away the case.
- Điểm nét: Keep her face and the releasing fingers in the same composition, with Sơn's eyeline close enough for the question.
- Chất liệu: Returned scarf stays over the grey dress, no clean white blouse or new sentimental object appears.
- Âm nổi: One sleeve movement and her clear question are enough, sea wind remains present and unstylized.
- Nhịp diễn: Let release follow the question rather than answer it for him, ending on the space she chooses to give.

**Clip 3 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · M**
- Đầu: Sơn holding both hands forward, left wrist still bandaged.
- Hành động: Mây applies the cuffs carefully while an officer documents the condition of his wrist.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Ừ. Lần này là anh chọn.
- Cuối: Cuffs fitted without pressing the bandage, Sơn standing upright.

- Ý đồ: Join his acceptance to the careful reality of custody, avoiding either punishment spectacle or easy forgiveness.
- Bố cục: The medium includes Sơn's offered wrists, Mây and Hạ at their established relative positions.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây fits the intact remaining cuffs without pressing the LEFT bandage, existing officer documents the condition.
- Diễn xuất: Sơn answers Hạ without looking away from the consequence; Mây's care does not become reluctance to do her job.
- Điểm nét: Keep hands and speaking face readable, not an extreme insert that hides his voluntary posture.
- Chất liệu: One intact pair closes, no old broken cuff returns and no wrist injury changes sides.
- Âm nổi: A single subdued closure follows his answer, without repeated clicks, score hit or crowd reaction.
- Nhịp diễn: The answer and custody form one unhurried action; end upright, settled and still visibly choosing to remain.

**Clip 4 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE · I**
- Đầu: Closed watch in Sơn's cupped right hand, cuff chain below.
- Hành động: Hạ places it safely into his breast pocket with his consent.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: One closed watch in his breast pocket, her hand withdrawn.

- Ý đồ: Allow Hạ one ordinary act of care that respects the decision just made.
- Bố cục: Lock the detail on his cupped RIGHT hand holding the CLOSED watch, cuff chain lower in frame.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: With his consent she puts the same watch into the breast pocket and withdraws her hand completely.
- Diễn xuất: The gesture is practical and restrained, not a covert release, key exchange or promise to wait forever.
- Điểm nét: Keep pocket opening, closed lid and fingers within one believable plane, no invented inscription or photo reveal.
- Chất liệu: Black jacket and chain remain the same, LEFT bandage is never moved to make the composition prettier.
- Âm nổi: Soft cloth and metal touching cloth replace dialogue, with steady gallery wind beyond.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete one careful placement, then let the empty hand remain naturally at rest without a held freeze.

**Clip 5 · L · LIGHTHOUSE_STAIR · W**
- Đầu: Kiên already under escort farther down the stairs, group behind him separated.
- Hành động: Mây escorts Sơn, Thành supports Hạ, nobody overtakes on the narrow stair.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Both escorted groups descending in order, gallery empty behind.

- Ý đồ: Show safe coordinated departure after the moral decision without staging another finale.
- Bố cục: Use the wide down the spiral stair with Kiên's escorted group already lower and separated.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây escorts Sơn in order, Thành supports Hạ, no one overtakes or crosses through another body.
- Diễn xuất: Their attention is on footing and procedure, not looking back to perform a final emotional tableau.
- Điểm nét: Keep both groups' spacing readable along the existing spiral, without a new crane or overhead impossible angle.
- Chất liệu: Hạ remains in grey dress and scarf, Kiên's white suit stays rumpled, Sơn's LEFT bandage is retained.
- Âm nổi: Measured footsteps and handrail contact replace dialogue; backup light continues behind them rather than resetting.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the whole line descend continuously, ending with the emptied gallery above rather than repeating the start of descent.

**Clip 6 · CUT · LIGHTHOUSE_BASE · W**
- Đầu: Group emerging from the main doorway, backup beam moving over the sea.
- Hành động: Hạ pauses to see her father's lighthouse working, then joins the others.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Để đèn sáng tới khi mọi người về.
- Cuối: Hạ walking toward the waiting vehicle, light continuing behind her.

- Ý đồ: Give Hạ her father's place back briefly, then let the group continue toward the consequences.
- Bố cục: Frame the main doorway and waiting vehicle wide, the working beam already moving across the sea.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ pauses just long enough to see the light, then joins Mây and the others instead of separating for a speech.
- Diễn xuất: Her line is a quiet request for safety, not an announcement that the whole story is solved.
- Điểm nét: Keep her pause and renewed steps readable against the existing main-door geography.
- Chất liệu: Grey dress and blue scarf remain unchanged, beam comes from the established backup rather than a new lamp.
- Âm nổi: Wind outdoors opens the sound field, her voice ordinary within it, vehicle preparation low in the background.
- Nhịp diễn: A short pause within ongoing departure supplies the beat; finish moving toward the vehicle, not frozen before the tower.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Incoming station kettle and fan introduce the later interview after Hạ clothing change. Do not show the descent twice.

### CẢNH 54: Lời Khai Của Hạ
*Thời gian truyện: D7 late night*
Hạ kể các lựa chọn mình đã làm và cung cấp chứng cứ mới.

**Câu hỏi:** Hạ sẽ được nhớ như con tin hay người đã quyết định để lại dấu?
**Móc vào:** Lời khai bắt đầu từ quyền được nghỉ của người sống sót.
**Điểm bẻ:** Cô làm rõ dấu khăn, video và kẹp rồi chọn việc về nhà của mình.
**Gieo / trả:** Biến điều khán giả thấy thành sự thật điều tra, không diễn lại cảnh bị giữ.

**Clip 1 · J · POST · W**
- Đầu: A later station scene, Hạ already changed into borrowed white blouse and jeans.
- Hành động: Mây brings tea, Thành starts a fresh witness record at a separate desk.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Mệt thì nghỉ. Tao vẫn ở đây.
- Cuối: Hạ seated freely with scarf on chair, tea before her.

- Ý đồ: Begin Hạ's account with restored choice rather than immediately demanding another performance of her ordeal.
- Bố cục: The wide establishes the later station scene, separate witness desk, tea and Hạ in changed clean clothes.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây brings the cup within reach, Thành starts the record at the existing desk without crowding her.
- Diễn xuất: Hạ considers whether to speak before taking the cup, Mây offers a genuine option to pause.
- Điểm nét: Keep her free seating and open route from the chair readable, distinguishing this room from captivity.
- Chất liệu: White blouse and jeans appear only after the stated change, long blue scarf lies on the chair.
- Âm nổi: Kettle and fan are ordinary background sounds, Mây's line stays conversational rather than therapeutic narration.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the offered pause be felt, then settle into an interview with an active witness instead of a second rescue scene.

**Clip 2 · CUT · POST · I**
- Đầu: Small scarf fragment sealed between Mây and Hạ.
- Hành động: Hạ explains she tore it on the flower-shop latch to leave a trace for anyone searching.
- Thoại: Hạ, ngoài khung hình: Tao xé lúc bị đưa ra cửa.
- Cuối: Hạ pointing to the torn edge without touching the seal.

- Ý đồ: Reframe the torn scarf as evidence of Hạ's own decision, not merely Sơn's keepsake.
- Bố cục: Use the locked detail on the SHORT fragment sealed between the two women, her finger near the edge.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She indicates the torn part through the sleeve without opening or touching the seal.
- Diễn xuất: Her off-frame explanation is exact and composed, avoiding a dramatic reenactment of the abduction.
- Điểm nét: Keep sleeve, torn weave and pointing fingertip in one sensible plane, no impossible enlargement of fibres.
- Chất liệu: The small fragment remains distinct from the LONG scarf on the chair, with no new intact duplicate.
- Âm nổi: A light sleeve rustle precedes her line, fan continues, no remembered captor voices or flashback effects.
- Nhịp diễn: Make the identification one clear beat; do not repeat the tearing action on screen after it has been explained.

**Clip 3 · CUT · POST · M**
- Đầu: Thành showing Hạ the saved video on a tablet.
- Hành động: She identifies the ferry emblem and the meal-delivery rhythm she used to count days.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Còi tàu tới trước bữa tối. Ngày nào cũng vậy.
- Cuối: Thành adding the identifiable transport detail to the statement.

- Ý đồ: Confirm a lived detail that turns an earlier clue into usable testimony without pretending the audience just discovered it.
- Bố cục: Frame Hạ and Thành with the saved video on the existing tablet between them.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She points out the ferry emblem and habitual sound timing while Thành notes the specific transport detail.
- Diễn xuất: Her certainty comes from repetition endured, not a clever-detective smile; Thành listens rather than finishing her words.
- Điểm nét: Keep her speaking face primary, tablet readable as a recorded image without requiring generated legible labels.
- Chất liệu: Clean clothes remain ordinary and the captured room is confined to the tablet, no extra Hạ body appears.
- Âm nổi: Tablet audio may stay muted beneath her line, with only the actual station fan and pencil present.
- Nhịp diễn: Let recognition move quickly into the new factual detail, avoiding an extended replay of the entire video.

**Clip 4 · CUT · POST · C**
- Đầu: Hạ holding her own empty palms above the table.
- Hành động: She describes deciding to keep the service hatch open when Kiên took her upward.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Tao chỉ cần họ vào được sau tao.
- Cuối: Mây listening without interrupting, Hạ's hands relaxing.

- Ý đồ: Give Hạ authorship of the door decision without glorifying the danger she survived.
- Bố cục: Close on her face and relaxed empty hands at table height, Mây remains her off-axis listener.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She opens her palms as she explains the single practical aim of keeping access available.
- Diễn xuất: Her voice is controlled with a trace of fatigue; no triumphant claim or sudden collapse into staged tears.
- Điểm nét: Hold her eyes and nearest fingertips within natural depth, keeping focus on a person deciding what to tell.
- Chất liệu: No prop is added to demonstrate the latch, and the clean blouse does not acquire new damage.
- Âm nổi: Only her line and a quiet breath break the fan bed, no hatch noises replayed from the tower.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow one pause before the reason, then let the hands settle; this is testimony, not another suspense set-piece.

**Clip 5 · CUT · POST · I**
- Đầu: Hair clip recovered from the hatch, now in its own sealed bag.
- Hành động: Thành photographs Hạ identifying it and adds the recovery location.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Clip bag and station record together, chain of custody intact.

- Ý đồ: Anchor the rescue trace in a recoverable object and finish its evidentiary route.
- Bố cục: Lock the detail on the SAME silver clip sealed separately beside the station record.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Thành documents Hạ identifying the clip, leaving the sleeve closed and recovery note together.
- Diễn xuất: Visible hands behave carefully and routinely; nobody holds the item aloft like a trophy.
- Điểm nét: Keep clip shape and the photographic record legible as forms, not readable generated text or a new device montage.
- Chất liệu: The clip is no longer in the hatch or Hạ's hair, sealed sleeve count stays fixed.
- Âm nổi: A camera shutter and paper placement are small sounds, without electronic discovery chimes or another voice line.
- Nhịp diễn: One identification and record completes the chain; end ready to continue the interview rather than opening a new mystery.

**Clip 6 · L · POST · M**
- Đầu: Interview ended, Thành stopping the recorder in the background.
- Hành động: Hạ asks to see her father's house the following morning instead of asking only about Sơn.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Mai đưa tao về nhà ba nhé.
- Cuối: Mây placing the old family-house key on the table.

- Ý đồ: Give Hạ a forward-looking request that belongs to her life beyond Sơn.
- Bố cục: Medium coverage keeps Hạ and Mây at the interview table, Thành ending the record in the background.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây puts the existing family-house key down within Hạ's reach after the statement ends.
- Diễn xuất: Hạ asks simply, with relief mixed with uncertainty about entering an old home, not a declaration of endless waiting.
- Điểm nét: Keep key placement subordinate to Hạ's speaking face; no focus flourish turns it into a secret clue.
- Chất liệu: Scarf remains on the chair or with her, clean clothes and documented clip stay separate.
- Âm nổi: Recorder stop and a light key contact frame her line, with kettle and fan continuing normally.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the official activity finish, then the personal request arrive; end on the offered key, not a new farewell.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Match the central placement of the house key on Hạ table to the recorder on Sơn interview table, then begin a different account.

### CẢNH 55: Tên Của Những Cánh Cửa
*Thời gian truyện: D7 late night*
Sơn nhận trách nhiệm cụ thể, không coi cứu Hạ là xóa tội.

**Câu hỏi:** Sơn có nói hết cả phần không giúp anh được tha thứ?
**Móc vào:** Không còn bí danh che giữa người khai và hành vi đã làm.
**Điểm bẻ:** Anh gọi tên địa chỉ và người xác nhận, ký vào trách nhiệm riêng.
**Gieo / trả:** Cứu Hạ không xóa tội; không thêm cảnh bạo lực minh họa lời khai.

**Clip 1 · MATCH · INTERVIEW · M**
- Đầu: Recorder centered on the interview desk, Sơn seated on the other side.
- Hành động: Mây checks he understands the interview and starts the recording.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Anh muốn khai từ việc nào?
- Cuối: Recorder light steady, Sơn facing Mây.

- Ý đồ: Make Sơn initiate an account that can cost him, even after the rescue earned sympathy.
- Bố cục: Frame him across the interview desk with recorder centered and Mây listening, distinct from Hạ's prior table.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây confirms readiness and starts the recorder once, Sơn remains seated instead of performing a confession entrance.
- Diễn xuất: He meets her eyes without the guarded half-smile of the opening, she remains professional and attentive.
- Điểm nét: Hold both faces in medium depth, keeping the recording light a modest factual indicator.
- Chất liệu: Black suit and LEFT bandage persist, closed watch stays secured in the pocket rather than being displayed.
- Âm nổi: A single record-button click precedes Mây's question; the room is quieter than the public station.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow the question to create a genuine choice, then end with Sơn ready to answer, not already reenacting his past.

**Clip 2 · CONTINUE · INTERVIEW · M**
- Đầu: Recorder light steady, Sơn facing Mây.
- Hành động: Sơn names the first address and year, choosing facts instead of his nickname.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Địa chỉ đầu tiên. Tôi nhớ người mở cửa.
- Cuối: Mây writing an address in the private record, Sơn remaining seated.

- Ý đồ: Replace the nickname's mystique with a specific address and a remembered human consequence.
- Bố cục: Continue the exact medium interview setup, paper and recorder remaining where the previous frame left them.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn names the first address and year while Mây writes, no imaginary door appears in this room.
- Diễn xuất: He speaks plainly with effortful precision, avoiding a stylized killer's recital or dramatic stare into camera.
- Điểm nét: Maintain the existing focal plane and composition across the seam, allowing writing and listening without a new insert.
- Chất liệu: Paper remains private and unreadable to camera, pen and recorder are the same objects.
- Âm nổi: Voice and pencil carry the beat, no rain or gunshot flashback soundtrack invading the testimony.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the factual words land before Mây's note finishes, and leave a small breathing space for what comes next.

**Clip 3 · CUT · INTERVIEW · C**
- Đầu: Sơn looking at the paper rather than at his reflection in the window.
- Hành động: He states that fear and debt do not excuse what he did.
- Thoại: Sơn (@Son), nói trong hình: Không ai bắt tay tôi làm. Tôi phải chịu.
- Cuối: His bare hands flat on the table, no watch used as a prop.

- Ý đồ: Keep responsibility from becoming another elegant line that excuses the speaker.
- Bố cục: Close on Sơn reading the paper, his face angled away from the window reflection.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He keeps bare hands flat instead of reaching for the closed watch or touching his scar.
- Diễn xuất: The admission is uncomfortable and direct, with no self-pity smile and no demand that Mây forgive him.
- Điểm nét: Stay on eyes and the nearest resting hand, not an ornamental focus shift into the reflective glass.
- Chất liệu: LEFT bandage remains real, jacket creases remain, no symbolic prop or fresh injury is added.
- Âm nổi: The line is dry in the quiet room, followed by a normal breath rather than a musical absolution cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Keep the sentence short, then let him remain answerable in the silence instead of concluding the whole interview.

**Clip 4 · CUT · INTERVIEW · M**
- Đầu: Mây separating verified facts from items needing investigation.
- Hành động: She asks for names of people who can confirm the account and records each answer.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Ai còn sống để xác nhận chuyện này?
- Cuối: Two evidence columns on paper without readable text, Sơn cooperating.

- Ý đồ: Make the account lead outward to verification rather than stay inside Sơn's personal mythology.
- Bố cục: Medium coverage includes Mây's two evidence columns, Sơn opposite and the existing recorder.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây separates verified facts from new leads and asks for people who can confirm them.
- Diễn xuất: She does not soften the question because he helped Hạ, Sơn listens without defending his reputation.
- Điểm nét: Keep her mouth and working hands readable together; columns are shapes, never a demand for readable document text.
- Chất liệu: The same paper and pen remain on the desk, no additional evidence suddenly arrives to validate him.
- Âm nổi: A page turn and precise question continue the room tone, with no courtroom-like dramatic sound treatment.
- Nhịp diễn: The question should open the next necessary step quickly, preserving procedural momentum after the emotional admission.

**Clip 5 · CONTINUE · INTERVIEW · M**
- Đầu: Two evidence columns on paper without readable text, Sơn cooperating.
- Hành động: Sơn gives the next contact and signs the statement after reading it.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Statement signed, pen set aside once, hands withdrawn.

- Ý đồ: Show commitment becoming a signed record rather than another promise to do better.
- Bố cục: Continue the same medium framing, camera axis and paper position from Mây's verification question.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn supplies the next contact, reads the statement and signs once before setting the pen down.
- Diễn xuất: His concentration is ordinary and sober; the small fatigue in the LEFT wrist remains without obstructing the signature.
- Điểm nét: Keep his RIGHT signing hand and face within the established field, no handwriting macro or heroic pen close-up.
- Chất liệu: One pen, one statement and the same bandage remain, no new dramatic document wording appears.
- Âm nổi: Paper movement and one pen contact finish the exchange, no extra spoken line or recorder beep is improvised.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow the read before the signature, then the hand withdraws; do not repeat signing from another implied angle.

**Clip 6 · L · HOLDING · W**
- Đầu: Sơn seated in the holding room after the interview, corridor light outside.
- Hành động: He closes his eyes and rests with the wrist bandage visible, no new speech.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn finally sleeping, closed watch secured in his pocket.

- Ý đồ: Let rest follow accountability instead of treating unconsciousness as redemption.
- Bố cục: Use a stable wide of the holding bench, barred window and corridor light beyond.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Sơn settles carefully, closes his eyes once, and stops trying to manage what happens outside.
- Diễn xuất: Exhaustion replaces vigilance gradually; no smile implies that his consequences have vanished.
- Điểm nét: Keep the room's ordinary proportions and his resting body readable, avoiding a prison-martyr close-up.
- Chất liệu: LEFT bandage and black suit remain, the one CLOSED watch is secured in his pocket and never opened again.
- Âm nổi: Ceiling fan, distant corridor steps and breathing replace dialogue, with no separate sad music track.
- Nhịp diễn: Let posture soften during the take, then cut from living rest rather than holding a frozen final tableau.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Carry the station fan from the quiet holding room into morning evidence handover. Do not replay the statement or signature.

### CẢNH 56: Sổ Tiền Thành Hồ Sơ
*Thời gian truyện: D8 morning*
Bằng chứng được xác minh và đời sống thị trấn bắt đầu trở lại.

**Câu hỏi:** Thị trấn sẽ trở lại nhịp sống thường bằng những việc gì?
**Móc vào:** Một chiếc ghế và chiếc kính mới thay lời ca ngợi người hùng.
**Điểm bẻ:** Hồ sơ chuyển giao xong, ghế quán dựng lại, người trực trở về lịch bình thường.
**Gieo / trả:** Đây là nối việc trở lại, nhịp gọn chứ không một đoạn kết bi tráng nữa.

**Clip 1 · J · POST · W**
- Đầu: Investigator reviewing the sealed originals against Thành's handover record.
- Hành động: Mây delivers the remaining local witness files for the same case.
- Thoại: Bộ đàm, qua thiết bị: Đã khớp sổ với xưởng và chuyến tàu.
- Cuối: Complete evidence handover recorded, local copies retained.

- Ý đồ: Resolve the evidence route with verification, not a celebratory broadcast that retells the plot.
- Bố cục: The wide connects investigator, originals, handover record and Mây's remaining witness files.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She delivers the local files while the existing record is checked against the sealed originals.
- Diễn xuất: People work with relief but no applause, treating the case as continuing responsibility beyond one dramatic night.
- Điểm nét: Keep the relationship of original sleeves and local copies readable without trying to show legible account figures.
- Chất liệu: Black and red books remain distinct, original USB and token do not merge into one object.
- Âm nổi: One concise radio confirmation sits over paper and station fan, not a news theme or public cheer.
- Nhịp diễn: Move directly through the final handover, ending complete but not framing it as another emotional end of the film.

**Clip 2 · CUT · STALL · M**
- Đầu: Bảy returning an overturned chair to its regular place, Thành nearby.
- Hành động: They reopen the stall together, without retelling the whole fight.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Giờ bán cà phê lại được chưa?
- Cuối: Chair upright, first clean glass on the counter.

- Ý đồ: Return the town to an ordinary transaction rather than another retelling of the fight.
- Bố cục: Frame Bảy and Thành at the coffee counter with the familiar chair and first clean glass.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They restore the chair and open the stall, keeping the work small and habitual.
- Diễn xuất: Bảy's question contains weary practical humour, Thành's response can stay in a look rather than extra dialogue.
- Điểm nét: Keep both at conversational medium depth, without isolating the chair like a sacred relic.
- Chất liệu: Counter, chair and glass are the same worn everyday items, no new flowers or hero portrait appears.
- Âm nổi: Chair feet on concrete and a glass placed normally create a lighter rhythm after the interview quiet.
- Nhịp diễn: Let work proceed through the line, finishing ready to serve instead of pausing for laughter or a curtain-call reaction.

**Clip 3 · L · MARKET · W**
- Đầu: Mây walking through the reopening fish market.
- Hành động: She checks on sellers who evacuated and thanks them for keeping the routes clear.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Cảm ơn mọi người đã đưa dân ra.
- Cuối: Mây with two unnamed sellers at the repaired side gate.

- Ý đồ: Show communal repair through checking people, not praising Sơn as the town's saviour.
- Bố cục: Use the wide along the reopened market lane, Mây meeting the two existing unnamed sellers near the gate.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She checks the cleared passage and thanks the sellers while they resume normal handling of their stalls.
- Diễn xuất: Her gratitude is sincere but brief; the sellers remain workers with lives rather than a cheering audience.
- Điểm nét: Keep the route and activity readable in ordinary depth, no spotlight isolating Mây in the crowd.
- Chất liệu: Gate stays repaired in its existing place, carts and tubs return to functional positions rather than fight poses.
- Âm nổi: Market voices and normal water sounds resume beneath her thanks, replacing the night command bed.
- Nhịp diễn: Walk and conversation should carry forward together, ending as the sellers get back to business.

**Clip 4 · CUT · CLINIC · M**
- Đầu: Linh at the clinic sink after filing his medical notes.
- Hành động: He hangs the emptied response bag to dry and sits down at last.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Bag drying above the bench, Linh resting with a cup of water.

- Ý đồ: Let Linh's private fatigue provide a human consequence without restarting the medical suspense.
- Bố cục: Frame the clinic sink, bag hook and bench medium, Linh already finished with his notes.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He hangs the emptied response bag to dry and finally sits with water within reach.
- Diễn xuất: A small release in his shoulders is enough, no collapse, tremor or haunted stare to camera.
- Điểm nét: Keep bag placement and seated figure in one composition, avoiding another medical-object montage.
- Chất liệu: Bag is the same used at the van, now emptied; no timer, amber antidote or new blood-stained item appears.
- Âm nổi: Water at the sink settles into ventilation and a quiet cup contact, with no dialogue or lingering emergency tone.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the practical task complete first, then allow one ordinary breath of rest before moving on.

**Clip 5 · CUT · POST · M**
- Đầu: Thành wearing replacement glasses, Mây at the case board.
- Hành động: They remove the emergency duty roster and restore the normal shift plan.
- Thoại: Thành (@Phó Thành), nói trong hình: Đêm nay trực lại như cũ hả chị?
- Cuối: Emergency roster filed away, ordinary patrol map visible.

- Ý đồ: Use the return of a normal shift to show the emergency has ended without claiming the case is over.
- Bố cục: Frame Thành and Mây at the ordinary patrol map with the temporary roster in hand.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They remove and file the emergency assignment, restoring the regular map rather than erasing evidence.
- Diễn xuất: Thành's question is half relief, half readiness to work; Mây need not answer with a moral speech.
- Điểm nét: Keep his replacement glasses and the map relation readable at medium distance, no generated text close-up.
- Chất liệu: New intact glasses appear here after the taped pair, paper roster stays one distinct sheet.
- Âm nổi: Paper filing and ordinary station sounds sit below the short line, no radio alarm interrupts the calm.
- Nhịp diễn: Keep this a brisk administrative beat, not a new round of farewells for every supporting character.

**Clip 6 · L · STREET · W**
- Đầu: Hạ at the station doorway with the family-house key, Mây joining her empty-handed.
- Hành động: They walk toward the harbour houses instead of the crime-scene pier.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Về nhà trước. Chuyện khác tính sau.
- Cuối: Both walking side by side in morning light.

- Ý đồ: Carry Hạ toward her own home so recovery becomes action rather than a waiting-room image.
- Bố cục: Use the wide at the station doorway, Hạ holding the family key and Mây joining empty-handed.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They start toward the harbour houses together, leaving the crime-scene direction behind.
- Diễn xuất: Hạ's apprehension is quiet and forward-facing, Mây's familiarity offers company without taking over.
- Điểm nét: Keep their shared direction clear, not alternating isolated reaction shots that stall the walk.
- Chất liệu: White blouse, jeans and long scarf remain the same, key stays with Hạ after the interview handover.
- Âm nổi: Ordinary morning street sound replaces case-room fan, Mây's line light enough to belong to friends.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the invitation become actual walking immediately, ending in motion toward the next threshold.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep ordinary town morning sound from the walk to the family-house threshold. Market-stall planning happens after the home entry.

### CẢNH 57: Chỗ Của Hạ
*Thời gian truyện: D8 morning*
Hạ có mục tiêu đời sống riêng và bắt đầu thực hiện.

**Câu hỏi:** Hạ có bắt đầu sống trước khi biết bao giờ Sơn trở lại?
**Móc vào:** Một không gian trống được đo bằng lựa chọn riêng.
**Điểm bẻ:** Cô nhận nhà và đo chỗ làm sạp hoa cho chính mình.
**Gieo / trả:** Mục tiêu mở hoa được thực hiện, tách đời Hạ khỏi việc chỉ chờ.

**Clip 1 · CUT · HA_HOME · W**
- Đầu: Hạ and Mây at the family-house doorstep, key in Hạ's right hand.
- Hành động: Hạ unlocks her father's house, Mây remains outside until invited.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Vào đi. Nhà tao mà.
- Cuối: Door open, Hạ inside her own threshold.

- Ý đồ: Let Hạ claim the right to invite someone into her own space again.
- Bố cục: Frame the old house threshold wide, key in her RIGHT hand and Mây remaining outside.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ opens the existing lock once and crosses first, then invites Mây rather than being led inside.
- Diễn xuất: The line has a small reclaiming humour through fatigue, not a declaration that all trauma is gone.
- Điểm nét: Keep opening door and Hạ's first step readable with Mây's respectful pause in the same field.
- Chất liệu: Key remains the one from the station, no new house furnishing or welcoming relative appears.
- Âm nổi: A normal key turn and door hinge sit under her invitation, with harbour morning sounds beyond.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the threshold change ownership emotionally in one entry, no second door-opening shot afterward.

**Clip 2 · CUT · HA_HOME · M**
- Đầu: Hạ before an old workbench with a lighthouse photograph.
- Hành động: She wipes a clear space and sets her own bag down, claiming the room.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Clean patch on the workbench, her bag beside it.

- Ý đồ: Make inhabiting the house a concrete first task, not another memorial scene.
- Bố cục: Use medium framing around the existing workbench and lighthouse photograph with Hạ alone.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She wipes one useful space and sets her own bag down there, leaving the rest unfinished.
- Diễn xuất: Her attention is practical, a brief look at the photograph can inform the work without becoming a grief performance.
- Điểm nét: Keep cleared patch and face naturally visible, avoiding a shrine-like focus pull to the photograph.
- Chất liệu: Dust stays where she has not wiped, ordinary bag and worn bench remain materially specific.
- Âm nổi: Cloth against wood and the bag settling are enough, no remembered father voice or music cue.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete the modest clearing, then finish on a usable space rather than trying to tidy the whole house in ten seconds.

**Clip 3 · L · STALL_SITE · W**
- Đầu: Empty strip beside the fish-market entrance, no finished flower stall yet.
- Hành động: Hạ measures a modest place for a folding table, Mây holds the tape end.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Tao mở ở đây. Sáng người ta đi qua đông.
- Cuối: Space marked with chalk, both facing the future stall position.

- Ý đồ: Translate Hạ's earlier wish into a choice of dimensions and a workable place.
- Bố cục: The wide shows the empty market strip, both ends of the tape and existing entrance traffic beyond.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ measures while Mây holds the other end, keeping the future stall modest and absent from the present image.
- Diễn xuất: Hạ is engaged in making something viable, Mây listens as a friend instead of an approving official.
- Điểm nét: Keep marked ground and the two people's roles legible in depth, not a premature finished-shop fantasy.
- Chất liệu: Chalk mark, tape and bare space remain ordinary; no flower display materializes before construction.
- Âm nổi: Tape extending and morning market chatter support the line, with no triumph fanfare.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the measure reach its chosen edge during speech, then end on an actual decision about the space.

**Clip 4 · CUT · STALL_SITE · M**
- Đầu: Hạ at the chalk mark, Mây holding the other end of the tape.
- Hành động: Mây asks whether Hạ wants to wait until the case ends, Hạ keeps measuring.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Tao mở cho tao sống trước đã.
- Cuối: Tape extended to the agreed edge, Hạ making her own decision.

- Ý đồ: Make Hạ's independence a working decision rather than an inspirational slogan delivered to camera.
- Bố cục: Medium coverage keeps her at the chalk mark, Mây holding the same tape end.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She keeps measuring while answering the question about waiting, without dropping the practical task for a speech.
- Diễn xuất: The answer is plain and certain, with a trace of humour toward her friend, not defiance aimed at Sơn.
- Điểm nét: Keep her face and tape hand within one setup, allowing Mây's listening reaction without a new cut.
- Chất liệu: Tape stays at the same edge and grey-to-white wardrobe change is already complete, no new symbolic prop appears.
- Âm nổi: Tape movement and normal market bed continue under the short line, making it part of a real conversation.
- Nhịp diễn: Speak within work, then mark the agreed edge; finish with the project advancing rather than holding for applause.

**Clip 5 · CUT · HA_HOME · I**
- Đầu: One white lily from her father's small yard vase on the windowsill.
- Hành động: Hạ trims the stem and wraps only this flower in plain paper.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Single wrapped lily in Hạ's hand, remaining vase undisturbed.

- Ý đồ: Choose one personal farewell object without restarting the whole flower-shop romance.
- Bố cục: Lock the detail on the single white lily at the existing home windowsill and Hạ's hands.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She trims and wraps only that stem, leaving the remaining vase untouched.
- Diễn xuất: Hands move with a florist's everyday familiarity, not as if preparing a funeral offering.
- Điểm nét: Keep stem, paper and fingers within a plausible detail plane, no new written message needs to be legible.
- Chất liệu: Plain paper, natural petal imperfections and the same window light make the flower ordinary and specific.
- Âm nổi: Paper folds and a quiet stem contact replace dialogue, with home breeze and harbour sounds still present.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete one wrap once, ending with a single flower ready to carry rather than several duplicate bouquets.

**Clip 6 · J · HARBOUR_WALK · W**
- Đầu: Hạ and Mây reaching the public walkway, transport engine heard near the station.
- Hành động: Hạ stays on the safe side of the walkway with the flower rather than running into the road.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Tao đứng đây. Ảnh sẽ thấy.
- Cuối: Hạ visible from the transport lane, Mây beside her.

- Ý đồ: Let Hạ choose a safe visible place for the farewell instead of repeating a desperate chase.
- Bố cục: The wide connects public walkway, tree line and transport road without placing either woman in traffic.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ stays beside Mây on the safe side, holding the already wrapped lily where the window can see her.
- Diễn xuất: Her line is confident but restrained, choosing to be seen rather than demanding the vehicle stop.
- Điểm nét: Keep their relation to the road clear, no long-lens compression suggesting they are directly in its path.
- Chất liệu: One lily remains in plain paper, scarf and clean clothes unchanged, no sudden crowd gathers to see Sơn off.
- Âm nổi: The approaching transport engine motivates her attention, ordinary morning sea sound remains beneath it.
- Nhịp diễn: Arrival at the chosen place completes before she speaks, then stay ready without rehearsing a wave.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Keep the approaching transport engine into Sơn window-side view. Hạ already holds the single lily; do not wrap it again.

### CẢNH 58: Qua Ô Cửa Xe
*Thời gian truyện: D8 late morning*
Chia tay bằng một ánh nhìn, không diễn lại nhiều lần.

**Câu hỏi:** Hai người có thể chia tay mà không cần lời hứa dễ dàng?
**Móc vào:** Ô kính xe tạo khoảng cách không thể rút ngắn bằng chạy theo.
**Điểm bẻ:** Một bông hoa, một cái gật; Hạ quay về công việc, Sơn đi tiếp bằng tên mình.
**Gieo / trả:** Đây là cuộc chia tay duy nhất, các cảnh sau không tổ chức thêm lượt tiễn.

**Clip 1 · J · TRANSPORT · M**
- Đầu: Inside the prisoner transport, Sơn in olive jacket after a documented clothing change.
- Hành động: Thành sits opposite with the transfer file, signals the public walkway ahead.
- Thoại: Thành (@Phó Thành), nói trong hình: Bên phải. Cạnh hàng cây.
- Cuối: Sơn turning toward the side window, Thành giving him space.

- Ý đồ: Place the farewell inside the unavoidable movement of custody, not a private romantic escape.
- Bố cục: Frame Sơn by the RIGHT mesh window, Thành opposite with the existing transfer file.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Thành gives the brief direction and leaves visual space for Sơn to turn toward it.
- Diễn xuất: His courtesy is discreet, Sơn's anticipation held in a small turn rather than an impulsive lunge.
- Điểm nét: Keep the window direction and opposing seats clear, no confusing mirror reversal of right and left.
- Chất liệu: Olive jacket has returned only after the stated clothing change, LEFT bandage and closed watch remain consistent.
- Âm nổi: Transport engine and tires form a steady bed, Thành's line concise without radio chatter over it.
- Nhịp diễn: The direction arrives once, Sơn begins to look, and the vehicle continues rather than pausing conveniently for a speech.

**Clip 2 · CUT · TRANSPORT_VIEW · O**
- Đầu: View from Sơn's window, Hạ at the public walkway with one wrapped lily.
- Hành động: She lifts the flower only to chest height as the vehicle approaches.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Hạ holding one lily against her chest, eyes on the moving window.

- Ý đồ: Make one small flower gesture carry the farewell from Hạ's side.
- Bố cục: Use the established moving view through Sơn's RIGHT mesh window toward the safe walkway.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ lifts the single wrapped lily only to chest height, Mây remains beside her outside traffic.
- Diễn xuất: Her eyes follow the passing window with steadiness, no running, repeated waving or staged sobbing.
- Điểm nét: Keep the roadside distance believable through the mesh, do not make a perfect unobstructed glamour portrait.
- Chất liệu: Plain paper and one lily remain unchanged, morning light touches her white blouse naturally.
- Âm nổi: Engine is filtered by the window, sea and walkway sounds stay modest beyond it, no extra dialogue.
- Nhịp diễn: The lift finishes as the vehicle passes, leaving the recognition to the opposite shot rather than repeating the gesture.

**Clip 3 · CUT · TRANSPORT · C**
- Đầu: Sơn beside the mesh window, closed watch chain secured inside his jacket.
- Hành động: He acknowledges Hạ with one small nod, keeping his cuffed hands low.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Sơn's nod finished, eyes still following her.

- Ý đồ: Let Sơn receive the farewell without mistaking it for release from what lies ahead.
- Bố cục: Close on him beside the mesh, cuffed hands low and the chain secured inside the olive jacket.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He gives one small nod and lets his eyes continue following Hạ outside the frame.
- Diễn xuất: Gratitude and loss coexist, with no smile claiming that the prison journey is a formality.
- Điểm nét: Keep face and mesh edge readable, avoiding an invented reflected Hạ portrait or a new watch insert.
- Chất liệu: Bandage remains LEFT, watch lid stays CLOSED, jacket and chain do not migrate to a different pocket.
- Âm nổi: Road sound and a contained exhale are enough, no swelling song or second internal monologue.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow the nod to be brief and completed, then hold living attention as her position passes out of sight.

**Clip 4 · L · STREET · W**
- Đầu: Transport already beyond Hạ, both women on the walkway.
- Hành động: Hạ lowers the flower and turns toward her chosen stall site.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Both women walking back toward the market, vehicle continuing away.

- Ý đồ: Return Hạ to her own direction immediately after the one farewell has happened.
- Bố cục: Use the wide with transport already beyond them, walkway and route toward the market both clear.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She lowers the flower and turns with Mây toward the chosen stall site, not back toward the departing vehicle.
- Diễn xuất: The turn is deliberate and a little difficult, not instantly cheerful and not another collapse into grief.
- Điểm nét: Keep their first returning steps readable in the same geography, without a new farewell reverse angle.
- Chất liệu: The same single flower stays in her hand, no petals scatter or symbolic object falls behind her.
- Âm nổi: Engine recedes while ordinary market sound begins to reclaim the scene, no dialogue added.
- Nhịp diễn: Lower, turn and walk form one forward beat; cut before another look back can create a second goodbye.

**Clip 5 · CUT · TRANSPORT · M**
- Đầu: Sơn facing forward again as the town thins outside.
- Hành động: Thành opens the destination transfer file, the journey continues without another farewell.
- Thoại: Sơn, lời dẫn ngoài hình: Tôi rời đi bằng tên mình.
- Cuối: Sơn seated quietly, file open in Thành's lap.

- Ý đồ: Name Sơn's changed relationship to responsibility once, after the farewell image is already gone.
- Bố cục: Medium interior returns to Sơn facing forward, Thành across with the destination file.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Thành opens the file while Sơn settles back into the journey rather than searching for another view of Hạ.
- Diễn xuất: Narration stays internal and plain, visible mouth at rest, no proud smile at the line.
- Điểm nét: Keep the ordinary seated posture and file in the existing depth, avoiding another close-up of cuffs or watch.
- Chất liệu: Olive jacket, LEFT bandage and closed watch position remain fixed, no new destination sign is generated.
- Âm nổi: His short voice-over rides the same engine note and paper movement, no musical statement of redemption.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the line finish while the vehicle keeps moving; leave the next image to complete the exit from town.

**Clip 6 · CUT · CROSSROAD · W**
- Đầu: Transport approaching the crossroad where the coach once stopped.
- Hành động: It takes the inland road and leaves the sea visible after it exits frame.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Empty crossroad, transport sound receding inland.

- Ý đồ: Close the outward journey geographically without creating another performance of parting.
- Bố cục: The wide frames the same crossroad as the opening, sea visible beyond the southern side.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Transport takes the inland branch and leaves the composition, nobody stepping out or looking back.
- Diễn xuất: If Sơn is visible at all he remains part of the moving vehicle, not a superimposed memory figure.
- Điểm nét: Keep the road choice readable in natural depth, no drone pull or time-lapse as the vehicle exits.
- Chất liệu: Crossroad sign remains unreadable, no new grave, message or flower appears beside the road.
- Âm nổi: Engine recedes inland and sea wind remains after it, with no extra concluding narration.
- Nhịp diễn: Use one continuous departure, ending on an empty but living crossroad rather than a frozen postcard.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Let the inland vehicle engine recede into cabin wind after the transport has left the picture.

### CẢNH 59: Căn Nhà Còn Mở Cửa
*Thời gian truyện: D8 afternoon*
Thị trấn giữ ký ức nhưng tiếp tục sống, Hạ bắt tay làm sạp.

**Câu hỏi:** Ký ức có thể đi cùng một đời sống đang làm việc tiếp không?
**Móc vào:** Bảy vạch được giữ nhưng không khắc thêm một dãy chờ mới.
**Điểm bẻ:** Khung cửa cũ thành phần dưới quầy mới, căn nhà tiếp tục cho thuê.
**Gieo / trả:** Dùng việc đóng bàn nối sang cửa hàng nhiều năm sau, không biến nhà thành đền.

**Clip 1 · L · CABIN · W**
- Đầu: Cabin door open, Bảy carrying a repair box, Mây with the final rental inventory.
- Hành động: They check the now-empty room together rather than preserving it as a shrine.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Chiều nay tui sửa cửa. Nhà còn cho thuê.
- Cuối: Empty bed, cleared table, repair box by the threshold.

- Ý đồ: Keep the cabin part of a working town rather than a monument reserved for Sơn.
- Bố cục: The wide establishes Bảy with repair box and Mây doing the final inventory in the emptied room.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They check the bed and table, then turn naturally toward the door to be repaired this afternoon.
- Diễn xuất: Bảy's practical line refuses a shrine without dismissing what happened, Mây accepts the ordinary future use.
- Điểm nét: Keep room proportions and threshold readable, not a mournful push toward an absent person's bed.
- Chất liệu: Cleared table, empty bed and existing repair box carry the material change, no sentimental objects left behind.
- Âm nổi: Wood creak and box placement accompany conversation, pine wind present without a sad musical reprise.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete the inventory as work, ending ready to repair rather than pausing as if this were the final scene.

**Clip 2 · CUT · CABIN · I**
- Đầu: Seven marks on the damaged doorframe, no eighth mark.
- Hành động: Mây photographs the marks once for the file, then puts the camera away.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Seven marks still in wood, Mây's phone lowered.

- Ý đồ: Preserve the fact of seven days without starting a new ritual of waiting.
- Bố cục: Lock the detail on the SAME damaged frame with exactly seven marks and Mây's phone.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She documents the wood once, lowers the phone and leaves the marks unaltered.
- Diễn xuất: Her hands are professional and gentle, not hovering over a potential eighth cut or secret message.
- Điểm nét: Keep tally depth and phone relation clear, avoiding readable screen text or a second overlapping photograph.
- Chất liệu: Seven marks remain seven, no knife, hair clip or new scratched row is introduced.
- Âm nổi: One camera sound and the phone returning to rest are enough, with ordinary cabin wind.
- Nhịp diễn: One record completes the task; end with the wood still part of a door being repaired, not an altar.

**Clip 3 · J · PORCH · W**
- Đầu: Hạ arriving with a folded market table, Bảy already outside.
- Hành động: She asks to use the porch to prepare flower buckets while the stall site is finished.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Cho con mượn chỗ làm cái sạp nhé.
- Cuối: Folded table on porch, Bảy clearing space for it.

- Ý đồ: Bring Hạ into the cabin through work she has chosen, not through waiting for its former occupant.
- Bố cục: Use the wide porch with Bảy already outside and space for the folded table she carries.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Hạ brings the existing market table, asks to prepare buckets, and sets the folded table down once.
- Diễn xuất: Her energy is practical and a little hopeful, Bảy responds by clearing useful space rather than giving a blessing speech.
- Điểm nét: Keep table weight, her footing and the cleared porch area readable, no sudden finished stall appears.
- Chất liệu: White blouse, jeans and scarf remain day-eight clothes, table still unfinished and empty of sale flowers.
- Âm nổi: Table legs touch wood with a modest knock, porch wind and daytime voices keep the work alive.
- Nhịp diễn: Let arrival turn directly into placing the work down, avoiding a slow tour of the cabin as a memory location.

**Clip 4 · CUT · PORCH · M**
- Đầu: Folded table on the porch, Bảy at one end, Hạ holding an empty bucket.
- Hành động: Bảy unfolds the table, Hạ checks its balance with an empty flower bucket.
- Thoại: Ông Bảy (@Ông Bảy), nói trong hình: Nhà này gió. Làm chân bàn chắc vào.
- Cuối: Stable table, one empty bucket upright on it.

- Ý đồ: Make support between ordinary people tangible through a table that must actually stand.
- Bố cục: Frame Bảy at one table end, Hạ with the empty bucket, both in medium depth.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He unfolds the existing legs and she checks balance with the one bucket, no elaborate carpentry demonstration.
- Diễn xuất: Bảy's line is dry practical advice, Hạ responds through adjusting her stance rather than another spoken joke.
- Điểm nét: Keep supporting hands and the table's level readable without cutting to new tools or angles.
- Chất liệu: Worn hinges and an unpainted leg keep the table plausible, bucket stays empty and unique.
- Âm nổi: Leg hinges, a light wobble and bucket contact supply gentle rhythm under the line.
- Nhịp diễn: One unfold and one balance check complete the beat; do not repeat the wobble just to fill the take.

**Clip 5 · CUT · PORCH · M**
- Đầu: Mây holding the old damaged doorframe strip removed during repair.
- Hành động: Hạ asks to keep the small marked strip for the underside of her future counter.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Để dưới quầy. Nhắc mình đã đi qua.
- Cuối: Marked wood strip in Hạ's hands, new doorframe behind them.

- Ý đồ: Move the marks into a useful future object while keeping their past meaning modest.
- Bố cục: Medium framing includes Mây with the removed old strip, Hạ beside the work table and repaired frame behind.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: Mây passes the documented strip only when Hạ asks to keep it beneath the counter.
- Diễn xuất: Hạ's line is matter-of-fact remembrance, not a promise to count years until Sơn returns.
- Điểm nét: Keep wood strip and faces readable, avoiding a glamour close-up that turns the marks into a supernatural token.
- Chất liệu: Old seven-mark strip is distinct from the new doorframe, repair occurs this afternoon consistently with the earlier line.
- Âm nổi: Old wood touching her hands and work sounds beyond the line retain a practical environment.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete one transfer, then let their attention return toward fitting the table rather than dwelling on an absent man.

**Clip 6 · L · PORCH · W**
- Đầu: Three people working around the unfinished flower table.
- Hành động: They sand and fit its legs in natural afternoon light, no one adding another tally.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Table standing straight, sawdust brushing away from its surface.

- Ý đồ: Use collective work as a time bridge, not one more farewell tableau.
- Bố cục: The wide holds the three people around the same unfinished table, porch and repair area recognizable.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: They sand and fit the already established legs, brushing dust away until the table stands straight.
- Diễn xuất: Concentration and small cooperative adjustments replace dialogue, no one stops to look toward the road for Sơn.
- Điểm nét: Keep work relationships and the increasingly level surface readable without internal montage or speed changes.
- Chất liệu: Natural sawdust and moving pine shadows retain texture, seven-mark strip is kept for underside use, not re-scratched.
- Âm nổi: Soft sanding and wood contact continue as a real work bed, no sentimental reprise required.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the surface become ready through steady activity, ending on the object that can match the finished counter years later.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: Match the newly sanded table surface to the finished flower counter years later, with a clear morning/time change and older faces.

### CẢNH 60: Buổi Sáng Có Người Về
*Thời gian truyện: LATER many years after the case*
Kết bằng đời sống Hạ đã xây và một người trở lại.

**Câu hỏi:** Người trở lại có thể bước vào đời sống Hạ đã tự xây?
**Móc vào:** Sạp hoa đang sống tốt trước tiếng xe đò tới.
**Điểm bẻ:** Hạ nhận ra một bàn tay trần và mở chỗ ngồi bình thường.
**Gieo / trả:** Trả tư thế nhìn biển và chuyến xe đầu; không đồng hồ thứ hai hay phản diện mới.

**Clip 1 · MATCH · FLOWER_STALL · W**
- Đầu: Finished flower counter years later, older Hạ arranging fresh lilies.
- Hành động: She opens the shutters of a working shop, old marked wood discreetly fixed underneath.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Shop open, Hạ behind the counter, morning customers blurred beyond.

- Ý đồ: Establish that Hạ has built a full working life before the returning man enters it.
- Bố cục: Match the finished counter surface to the earlier table, then keep older Hạ and shop opening in the wide.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She opens the shutters and arranges the existing lilies as practiced morning work, customers only blurred background.
- Diễn xuất: Her ease contains years of ownership, no permanent grief pose or expectation directed at the coach stop.
- Điểm nét: Keep counter and shop depth readable, with old marked wood discreetly underneath rather than staged for a reveal.
- Chất liệu: Older face and hair remain distinct from day eight, cream blouse, green apron and same blue scarf consistent.
- Âm nổi: Shutters, wrapping and ordinary market morning sounds establish continuity of life, not a return-theme fanfare.
- Nhịp diễn: Begin with work already underway, finish open for business; the later coach sound has not yet interrupted her.

**Clip 2 · CUT · FLOWER_STALL · M**
- Đầu: Older Mây stopping outside the shop with her helmet in hand.
- Hành động: She buys one ordinary bunch and exchanges a familiar look with Hạ.
- Thoại: Mây (@May), nói trong hình: Chiều ghé ăn cơm nhé.
- Cuối: Flowers in Mây's hand, Hạ handing back change.

- Ý đồ: Show enduring friendship as an ordinary purchase rather than a recap of every intervening year.
- Bố cục: Medium coverage holds older Mây outside with helmet in hand and Hạ behind her counter.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: One bunch changes hands and Hạ gives change, without either person explaining prison time or the case.
- Diễn xuất: Their familiar ease lives in a small look, Mây's invitation casual and specific to an everyday meal.
- Điểm nét: Keep faces and the transaction readable, no focus flourish on silver hair as an aging announcement.
- Chất liệu: Mây's increased silver and Hạ's mature features stay consistent, helmet remains in hand and flowers remain one purchase.
- Âm nổi: Paper and coins are small sounds under the line, shop and market bed continues naturally.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the exchange finish at working pace, with no hug, ceremonial pause or new promise to wait.

**Clip 3 · J · STALL · W**
- Đầu: Older Bảy behind the same coffee counter, coach engine heard off screen.
- Hành động: He looks toward the crossroad, leaves a clean glass ready on the sea-facing table.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: One empty chair at the sea-facing table, coach sound closer.

- Ý đồ: Prepare a place for someone through Bảy's habitual hospitality, not through supernatural foreknowledge.
- Bố cục: Use the wide coffee counter and familiar sea-facing table, older Bảy moving at his own slower pace.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He hears the arriving coach, looks toward its direction and leaves a clean glass by the empty chair.
- Diễn xuất: His response is ordinary attention to potential business, not a knowing smile that confirms a plot twist.
- Điểm nét: Keep the empty chair and counter connected spatially, no new photograph or memorial detail on the table.
- Chất liệu: Older hands and same moustache remain natural, one clean glass appears, existing table position does not reverse.
- Âm nổi: Coach engine begins off screen within established town geography, glass contact remains a small foreground sound.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the engine motivate his glance, then complete the simple preparation without holding for an implied reveal.

**Clip 4 · CUT · CROSSROAD · M**
- Đầu: Coach door open, only an older man's jacket, bag and hands framed below shoulders.
- Hành động: The man steps down once, same repaired watch chain visible inside the jacket, coach departs.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Man standing with bag in left hand, right hand bare at his side.

- Ý đồ: Repeat the opening arrival with a changed hand and age, while keeping identity just outside full view.
- Bố cục: Frame below the older man's shoulders in medium, coach door, canvas bag and ground readable.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: He steps down once, bag in LEFT hand, RIGHT hand bare, coach leaves without a second disembarkation.
- Diễn xuất: His movement is older and measured, neither swaggering professional nor a suddenly frail caricature.
- Điểm nét: Keep the same repaired watch chain briefly readable inside the jacket, never show or add another watch.
- Chất liệu: Faded olive fabric and older hand texture carry the years, no fresh wound, gun or disguise appears.
- Âm nổi: One footfall and the departing engine echo the first scene naturally, with no voice-over naming the man.
- Nhịp diễn: Complete the step and settle his balance, ending with him present in the town rather than beginning the approach to the shop here.

**Clip 5 · L · FLOWER_STALL · C**
- Đầu: Hạ hearing the coach fade outside, one lily still in her hand.
- Hành động: She looks toward approaching footsteps, recognition arriving gradually without a run.
- Thoại: —
- Cuối: Hạ setting the single lily on the counter, eyes at the doorway.

- Ý đồ: Let Hạ recognize a possible return without abandoning the life already shown around her.
- Bố cục: Close on older Hạ at the counter, one lily held before the approaching footsteps reach the doorway.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: She stops wrapping, looks toward the entrance and sets the one stem down, never runs.
- Diễn xuất: Recognition arrives unevenly across breath and eyes, a small disbelief before warmth, not an instant tearful grin.
- Điểm nét: Stay on her face through the change, keeping the lily only as a lower-frame reference, no rack to the unseen visitor.
- Chất liệu: Mature features, green apron and worn blue scarf remain continuous; no second watch emerges from a drawer.
- Âm nổi: Coach fades into sea wind and incoming footsteps, with no new score forced over the recognition.
- Nhịp diễn: Allow the face to change late, then complete the stem placement so the final shot begins from a settled invitation.

**Clip 6 · CUT · FLOWER_STALL · M**
- Đầu: From inside the shop, Hạ behind the counter, a bare hand entering the doorway edge.
- Hành động: The arriving hand rests on the counter without a new watch, Hạ makes room for him.
- Thoại: Hạ (@Ha), nói trong hình: Hôm nay ngồi nhìn biển với em nhé.
- Cuối: Hạ facing the unseen visitor, one lily between them, daylight beyond the door.

- Ý đồ: End with room being made for an ordinary shared morning, not a new contract or final twist.
- Bố cục: From inside the shop, frame Hạ behind the counter and only the visitor's bare hand at the doorway edge.
- Vị trí / chuyển động: The hand rests once on the counter, Hạ clears space beside the single lily without handing over a new object.
- Diễn xuất: She speaks with mature ease carrying remembered feeling, the unseen man need not answer or reveal his face.
- Điểm nét: Keep her eyes and arriving hand spatially related, no reverse angle or final hidden-identity montage inside the take.
- Chất liệu: One lily, one bare hand and existing counter remain, no duplicate watch, prison token or extra note appears.
- Âm nổi: Footsteps stop at the threshold, sea wind continues under the last line, title and any fade belong to editing.
- Nhịp diễn: Let the invitation complete and the composition stay naturally alive; do not add an embrace, return journey or sequel threat.

Âm nối sang cảnh sau: End on Hạ recognition with continuing sea wind. Any title and final fade are added only in the edit.
